Arrangements
Only Lan Qing, Huo Ze and a few other Jiuli tribesmen were left in the Jinxiu Palace.
Maybe it was because they had the same blood flowing in their veins. Although it was the first time they met, Luo Zheng could feel a sense of closeness.
Luo Zheng would definitely take good care of these people since Third Grandpa had entrusted them to him.
"Brother Luo Zheng, can you really make Jiuli rise?" Lan Qing asked with her watery eyes.
Lan Qing had heard of the glory of Jiuli.
At that time, they didn't have to hide like now. Every time it was the Day of Sacrifice, there would be tens of thousands of people coming to worship.
If First Grandpa was angry, the whole mother world would be shaken …
That kind of glory only existed in Lan Qing's imagination. If only Brother Luo Zheng could really do it.
She was the youngest and the most naive.
However, Huo Ze and the others didn't think so.
It wasn't that they didn't believe in Luo Zheng's strength and talent, but they felt that such a promise was impossible to fulfill.
What kind of person was Chi You?
In the end, he still lost to the You Xiong Clan …
You Xiong and Shennong Clans weren't any weaker than Jiuli at its peak. Even the Heavenly Palace didn't dare to fight against them. Luo Zheng alone was simply wishful thinking.
"Sister Qing, there are some words you should listen to. The rise of Jiuli, haha … It's just a fool's dream," a young man beside Lan Qing said.
Huo Ze frowned and scolded, "Xian Shi!"
This time, Li Lu brought a few Jiuli tribesmen who were the best in the remaining Jiuli tribe. Xian Shi was one of them.
"Did I say something wrong? What kind of person was the Great Chief? In the end, he still lost to Ji Xuanyuan! Although Luo Zheng is powerful, he can only lead Jiuli to rise among our peers? If this isn't a dream, then what is? " Xian Shi continued, "He only relied on some luck to be a little powerful in the Nirvana Realm!"
"Shut your stinky mouth! Don't forget Brother Luo Zheng's identity! "Huo Ze's face was completely cold.
On the way back, Chi Yi had told them that Luo Zheng's bloodline was even purer than theirs.

"So what if he has the status? Now that Jiuli has fallen to such a state, what can identity prove? "Xian Shi retorted stubbornly.
"Then do you think that Senior Qiu is willing to take us in and even help us get the tickets to the Chaotic Origin Great World because of my grandfather?" Huo Ze's eyes were filled with flames as he stared at Xian Shi.
In the eyes of these superpowers, Jiuli was already worthless. The glory of the past could only be used as a memory. It was completely worthless!
The reason why they could enter the Chaotic Origin Great World through the Heaven Palace was not because of Li Lu. Instead, it was because of Luo Zheng!
"Remember! In the future, Luo Zheng will be our young master! "Huo Ze said fiercely.
After being reprimanded by Huo Ze, Xian Shi's face was still filled with dissatisfaction. "You want me to acknowledge him as Young Master? On what basis? Unless he's stronger than me! "
With that said, the aura in his body surged, and a statue of a savage beast floated in front of him. "Divine Wilderness Vicious Eagle!"
Along the way, Xian Shi had been listening to Lan Qing and Huo Ze talk about how strong that 'Heaven Traverser' in the Paramita was. Now that he finally saw him, he felt that he was merely so-so. He wanted to compete with him and directly took out his Paramita Token.
The Divine Wilderness Vicious Eagle was also a high-grade Paramita Token in the 13 Layers Heaven. The moment this item was taken out, a vicious aura burst out and filled the entire Splendid Palace.
Before Huo Ze and the others could stop him, Xian Shi had already turned into a gust of wind and charged towards Luo Zheng. He slapped down with his palm.
"Heaven Opening Palm!"
Being the grandson of Li Lu, Xian Shi had an extremely high talent. This palm of his had drawn out the Paramita Energy of the Divine Wilderness Vicious Eagle. It was a total of 900 Divine Jun!
If this palm were to land, not to mention a person, even a mountain would be split into two.
"Xian Shi, don't mess around!"
"Stop!"
Lan Qing, Huo Ze, and the others' expressions changed. Although they knew that Xian Shi had an eccentric temperament, Li Lu had warned him many times along the way, so he should stop. They didn't expect that he would cause trouble the moment he stayed.
Lan Qing and the others had seen Luo Zheng's strength in the Paramita, but the Paramita and the Mother World were two different things. Whether he could block this strike was still unknown.
"700 Divine Jun. It's not bad in the 13 Layers Heaven …"
Luo Zheng's expression was calm. He didn't even move his feet. With a raise of his hand, the Stone Sealing Solution was absorbed into the bones of his right arm. Then, he gently clasped his hand and accurately grabbed Xian Shi's palm.
"Pu!"
The Splendid Palace shook slightly. The ground beneath Luo Zheng's feet cracked. The originally powerful strike was silently dissolved.
Xian Shi's palm was tightly gripped by Luo Zheng, and he couldn't pull it out!
When Huo Ze and the others saw this scene, they also secretly gasped a breath of cold air.
On the way here, Chi Yi had said that if Luo Zheng entered the 13 Heavens of the Paramita, his strength would definitely surpass his peers. At that time, Xian Shi snorted coldly. Huo Ze and the others also felt that this evaluation was a bit exaggerated. Now, it seemed that Chi Yi had underestimated Luo Zheng.
It was hard for them to imagine that anyone of the same level could take Xian Shi's full-force strike with one hand and come out unscathed.
After Huo Ze finished speaking, he hurriedly said to Luo Zheng, "My brother Xian Shi doesn't know how to be polite. Brother Luo Zheng, please forgive him."
Luo Zheng casually released his hand. Then, he looked at the ceiling of the Splendid Palace and said, "We are all brothers of the same clan. I will naturally understand. Our Li Tribe is already like this. We have no other choice but to work hard. Since Third Grandfather has entrusted you all here, I only have one request." His eyes were burning as he said, "Don't let me see you give up on yourselves."
After these words, the gazes of Huo Ze, Xian Shi, and the other Jiuli tribesmen suddenly became complicated.
In the small world where the Jiuli tribe was hiding, the shadow of the You Xiong tribe had never disappeared. Whether it was the elders or the juniors, they all seemed to have lost the courage of the Jiuli tribe and had been giving up on themselves.
And Luo Zheng was alone in the Heaven Palace, but he had never forgotten that he was a member of the Jiuli tribe. That confidence was almost engraved in his bones. Compared to him, they gradually felt inferior.
"Anyway, I believe that Brother Luo Zheng can do it!" Lan Qing's little nose twitched as she said with a serious face.
"I also believe," Huo Ze said.
"Our Jiuli tribe will definitely get better!" The other Jiuli tribesmen also echoed.
After Qiu Yinhe sent Li Lu away, he turned back and said to Luo Zheng and the others, "I will first send you to the Greater One Mountain. Everyone, please follow me."
It was the first time that these Jiuli tribesmen had left the tribe leader and elders. Facing Qiu Yinhe, they were still a little nervous and followed behind without saying a word.
Standing on the giant sword that Qiu Yinhe took out and looking at the Dragon City below and the giant mountain in the distance, the Jiuli tribesmen were all curious.
They had been curled up in that small world since they were born. Although the mother world was huge, they had never set foot in it. This was also the first time they went out and the first time they saw the scale of a super force.
After returning to the Heart Flow Sword Sect, Qiu Yinhe helped them apply for an independent courtyard and settled the six Jiuli tribesmen there.

Treatment
These people from the Jiuli tribe did not need to cultivate the Heart Sword, so they did not need to go out.
Most of the time, they placed their Sun Souls in the Paramita Realm and cultivated there.
When he first met Lan Qing, this little girl from the Jiuli tribe was still worried about 2,000 Soul Elixirs.
This showed that the remnants of the Jiuli tribe did not have enough money. It was not easy for them to raise their Sun Souls to the 13th Heaven.
On the second day after the six people from the Jiuli tribe stayed, Luo Zheng summoned them to the Paramita Realm.
Lan Qing and Huo Ze were still confused, not knowing what Luo Zheng wanted to do.
However, Luo Zheng said, "You are all brothers and sisters of my tribe. Take these as a greeting gift."
Then, he took out 30 Soul Crystals from the Sumeru Space. Each of the six people received five Soul Crystals.
Huo Ze, Lan Qing, Xian Shi, and the others could not help but tremble when they felt the pure aura of the Soul Crystals.
Although they knew that Luo Zheng's physical body could almost do anything in the Paramita Realm, and it was not difficult for him to get some good Paramita tokens and Soul Crystals, they were still shocked when he casually took out 30 Soul Crystals and gave them away.
The last time they entered the Temple of Heaven, the Soul Elixirs they paid were collected by the elders of the tribe who sold a Paramita token to Ear Mouse.
The 30 Soul Crystals in front of them were worth 30 million Soul Elixirs, and 30 million Soul Elixirs could not buy so many Soul Crystals!
"Young Master … this greeting gift is too precious," Huo Ze said as he stared at the Soul Crystals.
Although he wanted to refuse, any Sun Soul who cultivated in the Paramita Realm could not resist this temptation. After all, the Soul Crystals were almost equivalent to the cultivation of the Sun Soul.
"When I first entered the Realm of the Gods, I also wanted to find my people, but the Realm of the Gods was too big. At that time, my cultivation was too low and I couldn't find them. After that, I stayed in the Heavenly Palace to cultivate until now. Even if I have some achievements, it is nothing to give to my people." Luo Zheng said, "If the other brothers and sisters of the Jiuli tribe have any difficulties, you can tell me. I will do my best."
If he wanted to rebuild the Jiuli, Luo Zheng would need to pay more. Compared to this, it was not worth mentioning.
After giving them the Soul Crystals, Luo Zheng went back to his courtyard.... Consort Yan Concubine was asking for him.

The last time Luo Zheng left the Taishi Palace, Imperial Concubine Yan had already given him the order to enter the palace. This time, there was naturally no need to use the Mind's Eye to break through this fog.

Luo Zheng had just walked to the entrance of the Tai Di Palace when he heard Feng Ge shouting from inside, "Get out of my way! I Me Translation, I to Me to Me! Translation
Inside the side palace, chains made of golden flames wrapped around Feng Ge's arms and legs.
No matter how hard Feng Ge struggled, these chains locked him tightly. He could not move at all.
Several servants in the palace were constantly trying to dissuade them, and their expressions were very gloomy.
"How can this be?" Luo Zheng asked with a frown.
The palace maid replied, "Concubine Yan used several methods to save Her Highness Feng Ge, but the results weren't great. She's been clamoring to leave all day long, and just now, she angered Concubine Yan into leaving."
"The results were not good …"
It was hard to cure the problem of the soul.
Mo Yijian had not returned to the Heart Flow Sword Sect since he used the Forbidden Technique.
When he and Feng Ge had just returned, Consort Yan was confident. Luo Zheng also thought that it would not be a problem to cure Feng Ge. He did not expect that there would be no progress.
Feng Ge was still struggling. However, this was not the Nirvana Realm. She could not do whatever she wanted.
The moment Luo Zheng appeared in front of her, her angry eyes suddenly froze. Her hysterical emotions quickly calmed down. At the same time, she asked for help, "Luo Zheng, you're here! Take me out of here! My mother wants to kill me! "
"Consort Yan is your mother. Why would she want to kill you?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"She wants to kill me!" Feng Ge said with certainty.
At this moment, Concubine Yan's voice sounded behind Luo Zheng. "I've been trying to find a way to clean up the messy memories in her head, but a considerable amount of memories have merged into her Memory Fire and become a part of her."
It was not difficult for Consort Yan to clean up her memories. However, the pure person's memories had merged with Feng Ge. If she removed those memories, it would be the same as removing Feng Ge himself. Feng Ge naturally thought that Consort Yan wanted to kill him. In fact, Consort Yan wanted to kill Hu Yun's memories.
However, from Feng Ge's perspective, that was what she thought.
"Consort Yan, why did you ask me to come?" Luo Zheng asked.
Although Luo Zheng had controlled the Land of Reincarnation, he could not compare with someone like Consort Yan in terms of the soul. He did not think that he could be of any help.
"The memory removal has to be done when she is awake. However, the messy memories that do not belong to her are hidden in the deepest part of her soul fire. I can't dig them out," Consort Yan said.
After Feng Ge became a pure person, he did not recognize anyone but Luo Zheng. After thinking about it, Consort Yan could only ask Luo Zheng for help.
Consort Yan originally wanted to ask Luo Zheng to come as soon as possible. However, she thought that Luo Zheng had just left the Nirvana Realm and waited for a few days.
"I will do my best if I can," Luo Zheng promised.
Luo Zheng was partly responsible for Feng Ge's situation.
However, Luo Zheng could not stop her if she insisted on entering the Temple of Heaven.
However, it was only possible to leave the dark realm alive because of Feng Ge.
After Luo Zheng agreed, Consort Yan left the side palace for a while and brought back three old men.
After the three old men each took a position, a large formation on the ground began to flicker.
When the light appeared, Feng Ge, who had just calmed down, was frightened again. "Luo Zheng, they want to kill me again! Help me kill them! "
This fear was instinctive. The pleading look on her face softened Luo Zheng's heart.
Luo Zheng sat in front of Feng Ge and asked, "Who are you?"
"I, I, I am Feng Ge!" Feng Ge replied.
Luo Zheng shook his head and said, "You are not Feng Ge. If you were, you wouldn't be afraid."
"I am not Feng Ge?" She was confused by Luo Zheng's words. "Then who am I?"
"You are Hu Yun, a creature of the dark realm, and I destroyed your clan! All the pig-faced monsters died at my hands, "Luo Zheng said seriously.
Hu Yun's memory only occupied a small part. If Luo Zheng did not mention it, Feng Ge would not remember it. He had verified this in the dark realm.
But when Luo Zheng mentioned it, that part of Hu Yun's memory swarmed out. How could the hatred of exterminating a clan dissipate so easily?
Feng Ge's beautiful face was full of malevolence, as if he wanted to swallow Luo Zheng. He roared, "You dare to exterminate my clan! I will kill you! "
At this time, Luo Zheng said to Consort Yan, "Consort Yan, now!"
Consort Yan was overjoyed when she saw this scene. How could she miss this opportunity?

Coercion
Provoking Feng Ge in the Nirvana Realm was definitely not a wise thing to do.
Now that they were in the Taishi Palace, there was nothing to worry about.
Under Luo Zheng's constant provocation, the memories of "Hu Yun" hidden deep in Feng Ge's heart were constantly exposed, and even occupied a dominant position in Feng Ge's body.
"Puny humans, you will be ground into minced meat by the Blood Council sooner or later, I …"
At this moment, Feng Ge's voice changed. The bloodline power in her body was ignited, and traces of golden flames were about to burst out. Her inner world was also releasing energy, which was a sign of danger.
"Concubine Yan, be careful, she is going to explode her inner world!" Luo Zheng reminded.
Just exploding her inner world wouldn't hurt Luo Zheng and Concubine Yan, but in this way, Feng Ge would be completely crippled.
"Ha …"
Concubine Yan seemed to be prepared. She sneered and gently brushed Feng Ge's abdomen with her other palm. The energy in Feng Ge's abdomen quickly dissipated.
Then she flicked Feng Ge's head with her jade fingers, releasing rays of light that invaded Feng Ge's fine black hair.
Feng Ge seemed to be in more pain, holding his head and screaming.
"Now!" Concubine Yan said loudly.
The three elders closed their eyes, and the power of the formation became stronger. Concubine Yan's hand that was on Feng Ge's head suddenly pulled, and rays of white light were pulled out of Feng Ge's head.
"Whoosh!"
Concubine Yan pulled the white light and retreated quickly. She stepped into the center of the formation and suddenly pressed down.
All the white light gathered at one point, forming a milky white flame.
Luo Zheng stared at the white flame and raised his eyebrows slightly. "Memory fire?"
The white flame in front of him was very dim, but it still exuded a fierce aura. It should be the memory fire that Hu Yun left in Feng Ge's soul. Concubine Yan had pulled it out. Luo Zheng had never heard of such a method.
"Quick! Seal his memory fire! "Concubine Yan ordered.
As a Pure One, Hu Yun must have preserved some precious memories in his memory fire. Not to mention other things, just the things about the Blood Council were very valuable. She wanted to leave these memories for Donghuang.

Although he was unable to seal that wisp of memory flame, he was finally able to extract that damned memory.
Feng Ge's memory was not missing. It was just that after Hu Yun's memory was mixed in, it became extremely chaotic.
After the chaotic problem was solved, Feng Ge's memory would return to normal.
Concubine Yan flicked her finger, and the two flame chains disappeared. She walked forward and helped Feng Ge up. Then she smiled at Luo Zheng. "Thanks to you. I tried several times before, but that thing is very cunning. I couldn't get it out."
"It's nothing. Concubine Yan, you're too polite," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
"I'll take Feng Ge to rest first. It's not easy for you to come to Taishi Palace. Do you want to see your friend?" Concubine Yan stared at Luo Zheng and asked with a smile.
She purposely emphasized the word 'friend'.
Concubine Yan could see that Han Liusu and the others had an unusual relationship with Luo Zheng.
She was also an experienced person. Concubine Yan was not the only concubine in this Taishi Palace of the East Emperor. It was normal for a man to have three wives and four concubines.
What Concubine Yan really cared about was Luo Zheng's wife, Ning Yudie. When she was in contact with Han Liusu, she had secretly inquired about her.
Feng Ge was the legitimate daughter of the East Emperor. If she could really marry Luo Zheng, the status was still very important.
"Okay." Luo Zheng nodded.
Since he had come to Taishi Palace, he naturally had to pay a visit.
In the dark domain, Feng Ge had mentioned that something strange happened to Han Liusu, which caused a fire in Taishi Palace. Later, Feng Ge specially arranged a palace for her.
The purple Deviant Fire she had was called "Extortion Fire." Only the bloodline of the divine bird, Bi Fang, could obtain such a flame.
Concubine Yan found a palace maid and took Luo Zheng to the Qianlian Palace, which was north of Taishi Palace.
In the open space in front of the Qianlian Palace, dozens of people were pointing at the side.
In the middle of the open space, a tall woman was constantly moving and dodging, and behind her, a young man was chasing.
This young man and the tall woman had just escaped into the Sea of True Meaning not long ago and had not integrated with the Nirvana token. Their cultivation was about the same level, but the young man's unique skill was obviously much more powerful than the tall woman's!
The tall woman did not dare to fight head-on and could only keep retreating.
"The open space here is only a hundred feet wide. You know how to run, but no matter how you run, you can't avoid defeat!" The long sword in the young man's hand was like a drizzle, sprinkling toward the tall woman.
Seeing this, the tall woman's eyes suddenly burst with a fierce look. This time, instead of retreating, she moved forward. The blood-red long sword in her hand turned and stabbed suddenly. It was a fighting style that would hurt both sides!
"Sister Xun! Don't fight head-on! "
"If we lose, we will leave Taishi Palace!"
Han Liusu and Mu Ning saw this scene and suddenly turned pale.
The tall woman was Xun, and the man who challenged her was a direct descendant of Taishi Palace, called Chunyuan.
Under the protection of Feng Ge, Han Liusu, Mu Ning and Xun received more resources than many direct descendants. Especially after Han Liusu's Bi Fang bloodline was found, Concubine Yan even specially gave them a side palace.
This kind of treatment was difficult to obtain even for the direct descendants of Taishi Palace. The three women naturally attracted the jealousy of these direct descendants.
If Feng Ge had always been in Taishi Palace, it would have been fine. She was the most outstanding among the direct descendants of Greater One Mountain's younger generation, and also received the Eastern Emperor's favor. Who would compete with her?
But during this period of time, Feng Ge had been absent, and the news that she was trapped in the Nirvana Realm had spread in Taishi Palace. Many people felt that Feng Ge would not come back.
After a long time, these direct descendants began to have thoughts.
This time, they instigated Chunyuan to anger the three women. Xun's personality was the most unyielding.
How could Xun not see through these direct descendants' methods? But the three women dodged several times in exchange for endless provocation and coercion. Under such circumstances, they could only choose to fight back.
Xun's strength was not as good as Chunyuan's, and as soon as they fought, she was at a disadvantage.

Chapter 2997
Translator: EndlessFantasy Translation Editor: EndlessFantasy Translation
Trouble!
Chun Yuan panicked when he saw the deadly sword moves.
When two powerful people were fighting, it would be hard for one side to turn the situation around.
He should have defeated Xun easily, but now he was forced to retreat by Xun's fierce attacks.
"What's going on? That woman is weaker than Chun Yuan. Chun Yuan can't defeat her!"
"We are going to make a fool of ourselves today!"
"This woman is crazy. She doesn't care about her life at all. She is cheating!"
The direct descendants of the Taishi Palace exclaimed.
Mu Ning was similar to Xun in terms of personality, so she understood Xun's judgment.
Hearing the comments of the direct descendants, she retorted, "Fights between powerful people are always fought with their lives on the line. You have never experienced it, yet you say it's cheating. How ridiculous!"
The direct descendants of the Taishi Palace would only understand the cruelty of this world after they entered the Nirvana Realm.
Even in the early stage of the Nirvana Realm, they might not understand. After all, they would be protected by the temple after they reached the Fourth Heaven.
Like Feng Ge, who was fearless after reaching the Thirteenth Heaven, she only understood the feeling of death when she entered the Dark Zone.
Whoosh!
Chun Yuan kept retreating, but his footsteps gradually became stable. He flipped his sword and turned it into nine sword shadows.
"Nine World Mantra Sword!"
The Nine World Mantra Sword was a supreme sword scripture, and only the direct descendants of the Taishi Palace could cultivate it. Even the disciples who hadn't entered the Nirvana Realm could unleash its great power.
Although Xun had cultivated the Sword Doctrine before, she was used to using a spear most of her life. She had to start over in the Taishi Palace.
She didn't defeat Chun Yuan in the first round, so she didn't have another chance!
"Break World!"
"Origin World!"
"Orchid World!"
Chun Yuan fought steadily. His every move was like a textbook. There were almost no flaws.
Xun couldn't do it even if she wanted to. Occasionally, she would stab with her sword, but it was easily deflected by Chun Yuan. She could only retreat step by step.
Seeing that Chun Yuan had gained the upper hand, the direct descendants cheered.
"That's it, Chun Yuan!"
"If you defeat this woman, they will pack up and leave!"
"With his talent, it's a joke if he stays in the Taishi Palace!"
Xun bit her lips, and her expression became extremely focused.
Although she had done her best, her opponent's sword style was so perfect. Such an opponent was not someone she could defeat at her current level.
"Swift Realm!"
"Realm Crossing!"
Chun Yuan's sword aura became more and more ferocious, like a monster that was slowly opening its mouth to reveal its fangs.
"World Extermination!"
He swung his sword at Xun's head, and Xun's face darkened.
"Smoke! Just admit defeat! "
"The Crown Prince's Palace is no big deal. I'll just leave!"
Han Liusu and Mu Ning were also extremely anxious, loudly urging.
However, Xun was clearly aware that she was unable to counterattack or even dodge this attack. If she was struck by this attack, then she would probably be heavily injured even if she didn't die!
Just as the sword was about to reach Xun, a finger quietly reached over and clamped down on the sword's edge.
Under the pressure of the two fingers, cracks began to appear on the edge of the sword like a spider web. Chun Yuan's sword was actually crushed by the two fingers!
Xun didn't look back, but when she saw the pair of hands, a smile bloomed on her face. She turned around and hugged Luo Zheng.
When Han Liusu and Mu Ning saw Luo Zheng suddenly appear, their faces lit up with joy and they quickly gathered around.
"Who are you! How dare you break my sword! "
Chun Yuan's face became extremely ugly when he saw his sword being crushed by a single finger.
Luo Zheng glanced at him indifferently and asked, "Xun, why are you fighting with them?"
When he rushed over, Xun was already retreating in defeat. Looking at their fierce battle, it was definitely not as simple as just exchanging pointers.
Xun's personality was independent and she didn't want Luo Zheng to stand up for her. She calmly said, "It's nothing."
On the side, Mu Ning said, "These people want to force us out of the Crown Prince's Palace. They've been constantly provoking us these days!"
With this, Luo Zheng understood in his heart.
"Our Crown Prince's Palace only accepts direct descendants. How can the three of them stay here to cultivate?"
"And they're taking up our resources …"
"…"
Those direct descendants also said one after another.
Luo Zheng's cultivation was far higher than these people, but these direct descendants were not afraid of him. Who would dare to attack them in the Crown Prince's Palace?
But what they didn't know was that Luo Zheng defeated Feng Ge a few months ago here.
After listening to the complaints of these direct descendants of the Crown Prince's Palace, Luo Zheng smiled gently and said to Xun, "In that case, we'll leave."
Han Liusu and Mu Ning were originally cultivating in Dragon City, but they were "invited" by Feng Ge. Luo Zheng didn't care about the resources of the Crown Prince's Palace now, so there was no need to force them to stay here.
The three women had been with Feng Ge for some time, so they had some feelings for him. Now that Feng Ge was not here, they had nothing to worry about. Naturally, they were willing to leave with Luo Zheng.
"Okay, let's go back and pack up. We'll leave here," Mu Ning said and glared fiercely at those direct descendants.
Just as Luo Zhenghe and the others were about to come over, Chunyuan said, "Wait, you broke my Nine Words Sword and you want to leave just like that?"
As a direct descendant of the Crown Prince's Palace, he felt superior in the entire Taiyi Heavenly Palace.
Moreover, the direct descendants were divided into distant and close ones. Chun Yuan's mother was Concubine Qin, who had a high status in the Crown Prince's Palace. Chun Yuan's brother was from the Land of Sword Grief and was very strong.
Although Chun Yuan had just entered the Nirvana Realm, he was quite respected in the Crown Prince's Palace.
Just now, he was about to defeat Xun, but Luo Zheng suddenly appeared and broke his Nine Words Sword. How could Chun Yuan allow Luo Zheng to leave?
"Then what do you want? You want me to compensate you with a sword? "Luo Zheng showed an interesting smile.
Chun Yuan sneered and said, "Do I look like someone who cares about a sword? You can break my sword at will because your cultivation is higher than mine. Then, my brother's cultivation is higher than yours. Can he also break your sword at will? "
Chun Yuan had not even stepped into the Nirvana Realm. Naturally, he could not challenge Luo Zheng.
However, his brother Chun Xuan happened to be in the Crown Prince's Palace. Chun Yuan had just used a voice transmission to inform his brother. Now that Chun Xuan was on his way, he naturally did not want to let Luo Zheng go.
Chun Yuan was not even 20 years old. Why would Luo Zheng care about a child?
Just as he was about to leave with Xun's hand, another person came in from the entrance of the Qianlian Palace.
This person's skin was dark and his arms were unusually long. His eyes were as sharp as a poisonous snake.
As soon as he stepped through the gate of the Qianlian Palace, he did not budge an inch and blocked Luo Zheng's path. There was a trace of coldness and disdain on his black face. "Chunyuan, who crushed your sword?"
This person was Chun Yuan's brother, Chun Xuan.
"He's the one who crushed my sword! He is not from the Crown Prince's Palace! "
Chun Yuan pointed at Luo Zheng and said. At the same time, he was observing Luo Zheng's expression.
Chun Yuan originally thought that Luo Zheng's expression would be ugly. One had to know that his brother had entered the Sword Grief Ground and was an expert who had surpassed the Chaotic Origin Realm!

Chaotic Origin Realm expert was one realm higher than Luo Zheng. Such a person should have a decent cultivation and self-restraint.

Chapter 2998
Resisting head-on

Chapter 2999
Not Heavy Mountain
