There might be a Nirvana Realm token that could overturn the Heavenly Palace, but it would not appear in the Thirteenth Heaven.
"Damn it!"
Chun Xuan flew into a rage out of humiliation. His black hair stood straight up.
A primitive and unique power was emitted from his body.
As this power spread out, a melodious sound of a bell rang out ….
"Brother has finally summoned his bloodline!"
"Brother Chun Xuan's bloodline is very pure. If he can summon the Donghuang Bell, it will be very terrifying!"
"…"
Chun Xuan flicked his blood-colored long sword and spat out the word "suppress". Then, the long sword swept past the two green jade statues. Immediately, two large bell phantoms fell straight down.
"Clang!"
"Clang!"
The two large bell shadows covered the green jade statues. In the blink of an eye, the two green jade statues turned into countless fingertip-sized fragments.
"Hmph!"
After Chun Xuan suppressed them with his bloodline, he looked at Luo Zheng again. The scene that he saw almost made him faint.

Chapter 3002
Consort Qin
Luo Zheng's finger once again pressed down on the three chess pieces on the chessboard.
The 15th, 16th and 17th gigantic Green Jade Beast sculptures were instantly activated by Luo Zheng. The three gigantic Green Jade Beasts began charging towards Chun Xuan.
Every time a gigantic Green Jade Beast descended, their strength and fleshy body would grow once more.
The strength of these three gigantic Green Jade Beasts was comparable to ordinary Chaotic Origin Realm experts. Their fleshy body was slightly weaker.
Even though Chun Xuan was a Chaotic Origin Realm expert, it was still slightly difficult for him to deal with them.
He was practically gritting his teeth and squeezing out the bloodline in his body. The blood sword seemed to contain endless energy. It transformed into a huge bell before smashing down on the gigantic Green Jade Beast.
"Kuang!"
"Kuang kuang!"
"Kuang …"
The 15th gigantic Green Jade Beast was still easily killed by Chun Xuan.
But the 16th gigantic Green Jade Beast actually withstood a single attack from the Eastern Emperor Bell. Many cracks appeared on its body. Only when Chun Xuan attacked it a second time did this gigantic Green Jade Beast shatter.
And the 17th gigantic Green Jade Beast only had a single crack after being attacked by the Eastern Emperor Bell. One could see how strong its fleshy body was!
Those imperial guards who were watching the battle from afar inhaled a breath of cold air when they saw this scene.
"Isn't the descending jade sculptures too terrifying?"
"Chun Xuan activated his Paramita Token and bloodline yet he could not shatter them?"
"Can he really make these jade sculptures stronger?"
Such a technique was simply unheard of.
Not just those imperial guards, even the direct disciples of the Supreme Lineage Palace had ashen expressions.
In their eyes, Chun Xuan who was unparalleled was simply a joke right now. Currently, he could not even touch the sleeves of Luo Zheng. He was completely exhausted from dealing with these jade sculptures. This outcome had completely exceeded their mental defenses.
Especially Chun Xuan. His expression was pale. His mouth opened and closed. He did not know what to say.
Chun Xuan was puzzled too.
How could a Paramita Realm expert from the Color Realm be so difficult to deal with?
If he were to lose in the hands of Luo Zheng today, he would become a huge laughingstock.
Due to the anger in his heart, he crazily squeezed out his Nirvana token and bloodline power. The Donghuang Bell silhouettes came crashing down madly. After smashing seven times, he finally smashed the fifteenth statue into pieces.
"He is indeed a Chaotic Origin expert …" Luo Zheng nonchalantly said.
The power that Chun Xuan unleashed was already close to two thousand Godly Jun, and with the addition of the illusory image of the Eastern Emperor Bell, the power was even stronger.
Without relying on his Civilization Tool, Luo Zheng could not defeat Chun Xuan.
It was no wonder that the Chaotic Origin Realm was known as the Chaotic Origin Rebirth Realm. After stepping into this realm, it was as though the cultivator had been reborn.
Luo Zheng began to look forward to the Chaotic Origin Great World.
"However, the seventeenth and eighteenth Green Jade Beasts should be enough to defeat you …"
As Luo Zheng spoke, he pressed his finger on the chessboard. Two more chess pieces were pushed out by Luo Zheng.
The green jade spirit told Luo Zheng that this green jade chessboard was a small chessboard. It could only hold 81 chess pieces. The limit of the green jade spirits that Luo Zheng could summon was 91. To summon so many green jade spirits, the amount of divine crystals he had to consume was an astronomical figure!
If the Green Jade Civilization ascended to the Desire Realm and removed the suppression of the Color Realm, it could bestow Luo Zheng an even bigger chessboard. That chessboard had 19 horizontal lines and could send down a total of 361 Green Jade Lifeforms!
"Guang, guang, guang, guang …"
Two huge Green Jade Beasts that were close to three meters long were running on the bluestone path of the Taishi Palace.
Chun Xuan had spent quite a bit of his Paramita Energy to deal with the previous three Green Jade Beasts. At this moment, he was showing signs of fatigue.
What was supporting Chun Xuan now was his reputation as a direct descendant of the Eastern Emperor!
He no longer hoped to defeat Luo Zheng. Facing the incoming Green Jade Beasts, he frantically absorbed God Crystals to replenish the energy of his Paramita Talisman.
The Paramita Talisman could be replenished with God Crystals. But bloodline energy could not be replenished. If he wanted to defeat these two Green Jade Beasts, he could only crazily squeeze out his bloodline energy.
"Shatter, shatter, shatter …"
Chun Xuan roared as he charged towards the two Green Jade Beasts.
Many Eastern Emperor Bell phantoms came crashing down along with his blood-colored sword. This divine ability that had never failed unleashed a terrifying suppression.
This path within the Taishi Palace was already in a mess. But the two Green Jade Beasts seemed to be forged from iron. Facing the suppression of the Eastern Emperor Bell phantoms, only cracks could be seen on the surface of their bodies. They were not greatly affected.
Just as Chun Xuan was attacking, one of the Green Jade Beasts abruptly charged forward and grabbed at Chun Xuan.
The Green Jade Beasts did not have any unique divine abilities. They relied on their own strength and fleshy body.
Previously, Chun Xuan had dodged the attacks of the Green Jade Beasts several times. But as the Green Jade Beasts continued growing stronger, Chun Xuan could not evade this grab!
"Shua! Hong! "
After grabbing Chun Xuan, the Green Jade Beasts smashed him onto the ground.
Under the tremendous force, the surrounding ground trembled. The walls by the two sides of the palace shattered. Ning Yudie, Xun, and the other girls including those direct descendants were shaken to the point that they could not stand stably. They fell onto the ground.
The fleshy body of a Chaotic Origin Expert was extraordinarily tough. If it were an ordinary Paramita Realm expert, he would have been smashed into pieces.
But Chun Xuan had withstood it just like that!
"You all …"
Chun Xuan still wanted to resist. But just as he was about to stand up, the other Green Jade Beasts waved its huge palm and slapped down on Chun Xuan once again.
"Hong, hong, hong …"
Under the attacks of the two Green Jade Beasts, Chun Xuan used his Chaotic Origin Recreated Body to resist!
'What a tough body …'
Luo Zheng smacked his lips. Even if he used the Sealing Stone Solution to protect his body, it was still hard for him to resist against the attacks of the two Green Jade Beasts.
Chun Xuan was grumbling inwardly.
His Chaotic Origin Recreated Body had reached the brink of exhaustion. If he withstood a few more attacks, the toughness of his fleshy body would be greatly reduced. He would be smashed into a pile of meat paste by the Green Jade Beasts!
But what could he do? Admit defeat? Beg for mercy?
Being a direct descendant of the Crown Lineage Palace and the pride of the Land of Sword Grief, how could he admit defeat to a disciple of the Heaven Palace?
Driven by his extremely strong self-esteem, Chun Xuan was actually unable to take this step.
Just as Chun Xuan was in despair, two specks of ice crystals mysteriously appeared by his side.
The moment these two specks of ice crystals appeared, they struck the bodies of the two Green Jade Beasts.
The originally emerald green Green Jade Beasts turned snow-white. They turned into two Green Jade Ice Sculptures that stood stiffly at their original position.
A girl wearing a white gauze dress appeared by the side of Chun Xuan. The moment she appeared, the temperature of the entire Crown Lineage Palace dropped by several degrees.
One could see her extending her slender and white fingers before lightly brushing against the two ice sculptures.
"Hua la!"
The two ice sculptures shattered into pieces on the ground.
"Mother …" Chun Xuan called out when he saw this girl.
"Mother!" Chun Yuan also ran over from afar.
"Where did this wild man come from? How dare he behave atrociously in my Crown Lineage Palace?"

Chapter 3003
Who was important?
The woman in white was Consort Qin, who had a high status in the Taishi Palace.
Chun Xuan was fighting fiercely in the Qian Palace. The passing palace maids saw it and went to the Ice Cloud Hall to inform Consort Qin.
Only then did Consort Qin hurriedly rush over to take a look. How could she not be angry when she saw that her son was being beaten up by the two jade sculptures?
When the guards saw Consort Qin, they exchanged glances with each other and left quietly.
The other consorts were easy to talk to, but Consort Qin's face was extremely cold and she liked to vent her anger on others.
Her son was beaten by two giant jade statues for a long time, and these guards also watched from the side for a long time.
These guards were powerless to stop a fight of this level, but if Consort Qin wanted to blame them, they didn't have a chance to refute, so they simply slipped away.
Hearing Consort Qin's words, Luo Zheng's face showed helplessness.
Sure enough, like mother, like son. Chun Xuan's mother and Chun Xuan had the same personality. She didn't distinguish between right and wrong and wanted to denounce him.
"I had an agreement with Chun Xuan to teach him. I don't know where Your Excellency's' behaving atrociously 'came from?" Luo Zheng retorted.
"Mother, this person came and crushed my sword. He clearly came to the Taishi Palace to make trouble. He must be punished," Chun Yuan complained to his mother.
After Consort Qin heard her youngest son's words, a cold light flashed in her eyes. "I said you were behaving atrociously, so you are behaving atrociously. There is no room for you to refute in the Taishi Palace!"
After saying that, she snorted coldly, and with a snap of her fingers, pieces of ice crystals floated toward Luo Zheng.
These ice crystals could freeze and shatter the jade statues when they touched them. One could imagine how low the temperature was.
Seeing the ice crystals floating towards Luo Zheng, the direct disciples of the Taishi Palace all had happy expressions on their faces.
Most of these direct disciples were proud and arrogant people. After all, they more or less had a trace of the East Sovereign's bloodline in their bodies. They had always looked down on the disciples outside the Taishi Palace.
As soon as Luo Zheng came in, he taught Chun Xuan a lesson. They couldn't accept it in their hearts.
It was as if Chun Xuan was not the only one who was defeated, but the entire direct line of descent of the Crown Prince Palace was defeated.
Driven by such thoughts, they couldn't wait for Luo Zheng to die quickly.
Consort Qin's cultivation was naturally not something that Chun Xuan could compare with. Wouldn't it be easy for her to kill Luo Zheng?
Chun Xuan and Chun Yuan also looked at Luo Zheng as if they were looking at a dead man. This guy was going to die soon, but he still didn't know it. There was no fear on his face. Indeed, the ignorant were fearless!
However, the two brothers soon realized that something was wrong.
There was no fear on Luo Zheng's face. Instead, a strange smile appeared on his face.
"Luo Zheng!"
"Be careful of the ice crystals!"
Xun, Mu Ning, and Han Liusu anxiously reminded him.
They didn't expect that Consort Qin would appear out of nowhere even though Luo Zheng had clearly won!
The three of them had met this Consort Qin once before. That time, Han Liusu couldn't control her flames properly and caused a fire in the Crown Prince Palace. It just so happened that Consort Qin happened to pass by. Hence, she acted to extinguish the fire that Han Liusu set.
Consort Qin originally wanted to teach Han Liusu a lesson, but she was stopped by Consort Yan.
At that time, they knew that Consort Qin wasn't someone to be trifled with. But who would have thought that she wanted to take away Luo Zheng's life the moment she appeared!
When those two ice crystals floated in front of Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng didn't retreat or dodge. Instead, he extended his finger and lightly tapped on those ice crystals.
The ice crystals that contained an aura of extreme frost disappeared just like that. It was as if they had never appeared in this world.
"Ah …"
The two brothers Chun Yuan and Chun Xuan and the direct line of descent of the Crown Prince Palace opened their mouths wide.
Even if Luo Zheng could defeat Chun Xuan who was at the Chaotic Origin Realm, he shouldn't be able to easily neutralize Consort Qin's sacred art right?
Those two ice crystals might seem ordinary, but they weren't something that Luo Zheng could withstand!
Consort Qin's expression didn't change. She merely said with a cold tone, "Consort Yan, since you have arrived, why are you still hiding by the side and playing tricks?"
A watery red figure stealthily appeared by the side of Luo Zheng. Consort Yan had an indistinct smile on her face, "I've given Luo Zheng the palace token to enter the Crown Prince Palace. Naturally, he can walk freely in the Crown Prince Palace. What Consort Qin said about him behaving atrociously is too much."
"Breaking my son's sword and injuring my son's body. For a mere disciple of the Celestial Palace to do this, what is this if not atrocious?" The cold light in Consort Qin's eyes kept trembling.
Even when facing Consort Yan, Consort Qin didn't back down.
At this moment, Mu Ning who had rushed to the side of Luo Zheng frowned. She said, "That youngest son of yours is racking his brains to chase us out of the Crown Prince Palace. He even nearly killed Xun. Fortunately, Luo Zheng stopped him and allowed him to avoid this calamity. Your eldest son is even worse. He wants to bully Luo Zheng as a Chaotic Origin Realm expert. Since he can't defeat Luo Zheng, he wants his mother to step in. Your family is really reasonable!"
"Those who aren't part of the same family will never enter the same family. The reason lies with her family." Han Liusu added.
Xun merely narrowed her eyes. Her eyes were filled with vigilance. She stood quietly by the side of Luo Zheng without saying a word. She only snorted coldly.
Consort Qin was ridiculed by these two girls. That pair of elegant eyes of hers became even more furious.
She felt that she was in a high position. What qualifications did these lowly people have to argue with her?
"What a sharp tongue. You are seeking death!" Consort Qin clenched her fist, causing many ice crystals to appear in the sky. These ice crystals rapidly turned into a snowstorm that swept towards Luo Zheng and Han Liusu.
The ice crystals she executed could freeze the space eternally. As long as one came into contact with the ice crystals, he would die without a doubt.
Seeing that the snowstorm was about to engulf them, Consort Yan waved her hand, causing layers of golden flames to rise.
"Hua …"
This Golden Crow God Flame lightly flickered, causing the snowstorm to dissipate.
"Consort Qin is too agitated today. Please return to the Ice Cloud Hall to rest." Consort Yan said solemnly.
"Consort Yan! Are you really going to protect these few servants? "Consort Qin angrily shouted. That originally beautiful face of hers had become slightly distorted.
The Crown Prince Palace might seem peaceful and peaceful, but these concubines were fighting in the dark.
Consort Qin and Consort Yan had always been on bad terms. This time, Chun Xuan and Chun Yuan were at a disadvantage. Yet, Consort Qin was actually protecting several outsiders. How could Consort Qin accept this?
"Servants?" The corners of Consort Yan's mouth curled up slightly, full of ridicule. "First, they are disciples of the Greater One Heavenly Palace, not servants as you said. Second, they are Feng Ge's friends."
"Friends …" Consort Qin sneered and was about to retort.
Consort Yan interrupted her again and continued, "Third, Luo Zheng is much more important to the Greater One Heavenly Palace than you and me, a mere concubine!"
She still had to rely on Luo Zheng to crack the [True Comprehension Chapter].
From this point of view, Luo Zheng was indeed more important to the Greater One Heavenly Palace than the concubines of the Crown Prince Palace.
When Consort Qin heard this, her heart skipped a beat. More important than them?
What was the background of this kid?
But in the end, she couldn't swallow this anger. What's more, Consort Yan was probably deceiving her.
Consort Qin still said in a cold voice, "No matter what you say today, if you don't hand them over, I will not let this go!"

Chapter 3004
Summon
Consort Qin's eyes flashed with a dangerous luster.
In terms of strength, she was not necessarily weaker than Consort Yan, but it was very unwise to fight with Consort Yan in the Taishi Palace.
The key was that Consort Yan had just said that Luo Zheng was even more important to the Taiyi Heavenly Palace than her and Consort Yan …
If there was really something at stake and Donghuang punished her, her status in the Taishi Palace would plummet.
Consort Yan was still calm and unruffled in the midst of chaos, with a faint smile on her face, which was quite charming.
She had already dug a big hole and was waiting for Consort Qin to make a move.
The hearts of the disciples of the Heavenly Palace were also pounding.
It was not a big deal for the disciples of the Heavenly Palace to fight in the Taishi Palace.
If two high-ranking consorts fought, it would be a big deal.
Luo Zheng and the others did not say a word. He could roughly guess that Consort Yan dared to provoke Consort Qin because she was certain of something.
"Dong …"
Just then, there was a melodious bell ringing from the depths of the Taishi Palace.
Consort Yan, Consort Qin, and the disciples of the Taishi Palace all looked in that direction when they heard the sound.
Then there was a resounding voice from the depths of the Taishi Palace.
"Summon Luo Zheng for an audience."
This voice resounded through the entire Taishi Palace, and everyone could hear it clearly.
"Ji Jun is awake!" Consort Yan's face revealed a look of surprise.
Donghuang had been in seclusion for too long this time, and the Taishi Palace was full of worry.
Consort Qin's face also changed slightly. Donghuang did not summon anyone after he woke up, but wanted to see Luo Zheng immediately?
"Consort Yan, who is Ji Jun …" Luo Zheng asked.
Consort Yan smiled and said, "It's Donghuang. Quick, Donghuang wants to see you. I'll send you there."
"Send me there?" Luo Zheng glanced at Mu Ning and the others.
Consort Qin was narrow-minded. If Consort Yan left, what if she lost her mind and vented her anger on them?
Consort Yan did not understand Luo Zheng's concerns? She said carelessly, "Don't worry, this is the Taisei Palace. Although some people are unreasonable, their brains are not damaged. Naturally, they won't do things that seek death."
Donghuang wanted to see Luo Zheng as soon as he woke up. Even a fool would know the value of Luo Zheng.
No matter how stupid Concubine Qin was, she couldn't really do anything to Mu Ning and the other girls. Besides, the Taishi Palace wasn't big, and a master like Concubine Yan could completely cover it with her divine thoughts.
"Xun, wait for me in the Qianlian Palace," Luo Zheng said to the three women.
Xun, Mu Ning, and Han Liusu had been in the Tai Yi Heavenly Palace for a long time. They knew a lot about the Great One Heavenly Palace, especially the legend of the Eastern Sovereign.
He was the absolute leader of the Heavenly Palace, who had forcefully condensed an orthodox powerhouse of the human race with his own strength!
They also felt honored that such a person wanted to see Luo Zheng as soon as he came out of his Closed Door Meditation.
"En!" Han Liusu and Mu Ning nodded obediently. Xun tilted her head and smiled.
Concubine Yan led the way in front, and Luo Zheng followed.
Chunyuan and Chunxuan were completely dumbfounded, especially Chunxuan.
Donghuang was Chunxuan's father, and he rarely saw him in many years. Luo Zheng was just a disciple of the Heavenly Palace. What did he rely on?
"Mother, what's the background of that kid?" Chunxuan asked.
"How would I know!" Concubine Qin angrily rebuked, "You don't know his background, yet you dare to bully him at will and make trouble for me!"
Concubine Qin's strength was not weaker than Concubine Yan, but after Concubine Yan ascended the throne, she had been favored by the Eastern Sovereign. Concubine Qin had long been at a disadvantage in front of the Eastern Sovereign. If Concubine Yan took this opportunity to complain, her status would probably plummet.
Seeing their mother angry, Chunxuan and Chunyuan trembled with fear.
Chunxuan felt wronged. Concubine Qin also didn't know Luo Zheng's background, but she still wanted to kill him.
He thought so in his heart, but how could he dare to refute his mother?
After Consort Qin snorted coldly, she swaggered away.
Just like what Concubine Yan said, she naturally wouldn't do anything stupid against these three girls.
… …
… …
On the way to the Taidi Palace, Luo Zheng asked something he had been curious about, "Concubine Yan, is there no Empress in the Taidi Palace?"
People in the secular world liked to call themselves Sovereigns and Emperors.
Because a title that was highly anticipated could consolidate one's imperial power, it was the so-called Mandate of Heaven.
But in the world of Martial Arts, strength was everything. No matter how many titles you had, no matter how good you were at deceiving others, you would be kicked off the throne sooner or later if you were not strong enough.
After becoming a True God, one's lifespan would be endless, so this method wouldn't work.
Therefore, except for the four Divine Kingdoms, the big forces Luo Zheng had seen rarely established Divine Kingdoms. After reaching the Divine Realm, they only called themselves Wealthy Families.
But in the mother world, they seemed to have returned to the ways of the secular world …
Concubine Yan smiled slightly and said, "We are different from the Jiuli Clan, Youxiong Clan, and the Divine Farmer Clan. These human clans are united by blood. When the Eastern Sovereign chose the Center Divine Province to establish the Heavenly Palace, his purpose was to gather the scattered human clans in the mother world and merge them into one. Naturally, we need to act according to the Divine Kingdom."
The Seven Mountains and Seven Sovereigns established the Center Divine Province as the legitimate country of the Human Clan, welcoming the shores of the four seas …
It was not easy for the Eastern Sovereign to create another legitimate country of the Human Clan. Over the years, the titles and habits had been used, and naturally, it had become what it was now.
"As for the Empress, of course, there is no such title," Concubine Yan smiled slightly.
It was not that there were no concubines who had fought for this title, especially Concubine Qin. She had mentioned it many times in front of the Eastern Sovereign, but she had been rejected by the Eastern Sovereign.
After Concubine Yan explained to Luo Zheng, the two had reached the depths of the Taishi Palace.
In front of Luo Zheng, there was a three-story pavilion. The construction was exquisite, but it was far less magnificent than the other halls in the Taishi Palace. In comparison, it was too simple.
"Luo Zheng, you can go in." Concubine Yan walked Luo Zheng to the front of the pavilion and stopped.
"Alone?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
Concubine Yan nodded, "The Eastern Sovereign summoned you, so you can go in alone."
Luo Zheng glanced at the simple door of the pavilion, then stepped into the pavilion.
Although the pavilion was small, the internal structure was very elegant. Stepping into it, a faint fragrance assailed his nostrils. He did not know what this fragrance came from, but it calmed his mind.
In the main hall of the pavilion, there was a thin gauze curtain.
When Luo Zheng came closer, the gauze curtain automatically opened to the sides.
In the center of the main hall, there was a simple screen. On the screen, there was an exquisite large bell. This large bell was exactly the same as the Eastern Sovereign Bell that Chun Xuan had created with his bloodline.
"You are Luo Zheng?"
Following a voice, a middle-aged man in a gray robe stepped out from the side of the screen.
This middle-aged man wore a purple golden crown. He had a square face, and his eyes were as sharp as swords. His expression was stern, and when one looked at him, one would feel a great pressure.

Chapter 3005
Your request.
Donghuang sized up Luo Zheng, and a smile appeared on his stern face.
He usually had an extremely serious face, so Luo Zheng naturally didn't know how rare it was for him to smile like this.
As soon as Donghuang smiled, he pointed at Luo Zheng's head.
Using the tip of one's finger as a sword was something that ordinary sword cultivators could do.
However, when the Hand of the Donghuang was released, it was naturally exceptionally sharp.
Luo Zheng could also see that this finger contained the aura of the Eternal True Meaning of Sword Fate, and it was the True Comprehension Chapter.
Seeing that the finger was heading straight for him, Luo Zheng also stretched out a finger. A white spiral shot out from his finger. He also used the True Comprehension Chapter.
Swish!
"Sou!"
Swish …
Luo Zheng swiped with his finger, but Donghuang's finger moved slightly. It was actually a horizontal chop.
Luo Zheng flicked his finger and twisted it, instantly disintegrating Donghuang's finger.
In the blink of an eye, the two of them had exchanged more than ten moves.
Donghuang suppressed his power to an extremely low level and dealt with it purely with the Sword Fate Eternal True Meaning.
On the other hand, Luo Zheng's True Comprehension Chapter was slightly more than Donghuang's. His finger aura was a bit sharper, suppressing Donghuang in every aspect. In the end, Donghuang was forced to stop.
Then Donghuang said, "I've been in the Nirvana Realm for too long this time, so I left a part of my soul here. If there's any important news in the Heavenly Palace, Qiu Yinhe and the others would report it to my part of my soul. After fusing with this part of my soul, you really scared me."
Luo Zheng pretended to know nothing and asked, "What scared Donghuang?"
"I have been immersed in the [True Comprehension Chapter] for many years, and I once wanted to give up. In the end, I only managed to decipher a little more than a hundred words. Your bone age is only a little more than a hundred years old, but your understanding of deciphering Sanskrit has already surpassed mine. This really surprises me." Donghuang's expression was very sincere.
Donghuang was an expert who had established a proper human lineage, and he pursued the ultimate Dao wholeheartedly.
It was the same for deciphering Sanskrit and swordsmanship.
The True Comprehension Chapter had troubled him for far too long, and it was only in recent years that he had made some progress.
However, after a hundred characters, the difficulty increased exponentially. It was unknown how long it would take to completely decipher the two Sanskrit tablets.
"You flatter me, Donghuang," Luo Zheng replied.
Unexpectedly, Donghuang shook his head and said, "Qiu Yin River can coincidentally lead you to the Heavenly Palace. It is a great fortune for the Heavenly Palace. May I ask you, are you confident that you can decipher the True Comprehension Chapter?"
Luo Nian had already deciphered it …
Although Luo Zheng thought so in his heart, he would not give such an answer.
Instead, he put on a troubled expression and said, "The structure of the two Sanskrit characters in the True Comprehension chapter is very special. Finding the correct answer among thousands of possibilities is indeed too difficult. However, I have found a trace of a pattern in the dark, so it is possible to solve all of them."
How could Luo Zheng know how to decipher Sanskrit?
He had heard it from Luo Nian and was just spouting nonsense in front of Donghuang.
After meeting Qiu Yin River, Luo Zheng knew that he would meet Donghuang sooner or later.
If he wanted to revive Jiuli, the Taiyi Heavenly Palace must be the most suitable helper for him. After all, the Heavenly Palace also stood on the opposite side of You Xiong and Shennong.
Since Donghuang had a request, Luo Zheng would naturally comply. However, he could not tell Donghuang that this thing was too simple for Luo Nian to decipher at a glance.
"Found a trace of a pattern? What is the pattern? "Donghuang asked.
Deciphering the True Comprehension Chapter was very important, and Donghuang was also very interested in how to decipher it. After all, he had spent too much effort on the two Sanskrit tablets.
"This …" Luo Zheng hesitated. "I can't tell the pattern. It is completely a trace of spiritual connection in deciphering."
Deciphering Sanskrit was originally a mysterious matter. It was not possible for Donghuang to ask Luo Zheng to describe it accurately. Otherwise, all the Sanskrit in the world could be quickly deciphered.
If it was not too rude, Donghuang would have looked through Luo Zheng's memory …
"Fine." Donghuang waved his hand. "Qiu Yin River reported last time that you have broken the True Comprehension Chapter to more than 150 words. Have you made any progress?"
"During this time, I have been cultivating in the Nirvana Realm. I didn't have much time, but I have already deciphered more than 200 words," Luo Zheng replied.
Donghuang's eyes lit up again.
He heard Luo Zheng say that it was possible to completely decipher it, but this time could be long or short. If it was short, it could be short. If it was short, it could be short. If it was long, it could be thousands or tens of thousands of years …
This kid could decipher 50 more words if he took the time. At this speed, it might not take too long for him to completely decipher it.
"Can I take a look?" Donghuang asked.
Luo Zheng did not refuse.
He had to personally ask Donghuang about Jiuli. The True Comprehension Chapter was originally used as an exchange. It only depended on whether Donghuang had the courage to do so.
Luo Zheng took out a pen and paper and started to write.
After a short while, the True Comprehension Chapter had already landed on the paper.
Donghuang took it and looked at it carefully. As he read it, the admiration on his face grew stronger and stronger.
After looking at it for a while, he carefully folded the paper and said, "It is Chiyou's blessing to have a grandson like you. Unfortunately, alas …"
When Jiuli was destroyed, the Heavenly Palace was very nervous. Donghuang also felt sorry for them.
When Jiuli was destroyed, one of the four orthodoxies of the human race was gone.
And the Heavenly Palace was obviously not on the side of the You Xiong Clan. Sooner or later, the You Xiong Clan would make a move against the Heavenly Palace.
His former brother Di Jun turned against him. It was the You Xiong Clan's plan.
Hearing Donghuang's sigh, Luo Zheng just stood quietly on the side without saying anything.
"What do you want?" Donghuang suddenly asked.
With the True Comprehension Chapter, all sword cultivators would benefit immensely.
Donghuang was not naive enough to think that Luo Zheng would selflessly give it to the Great Yi Heavenly Palace, so he asked directly.
And when he asked this question, Donghuang also mentioned Jiuli. This meant that he had completely seen through Luo Zheng's thoughts!
Since Donghuang asked directly, Luo Zheng's answer was also very direct, "I want to rebuild Jiuli. I hope the Heavenly Palace can help me."
Donghuang didn't look surprised at all, but he waved his hand and said, "I can help you. Even the entire Heavenly Palace will help you. But not now."
"Then when?" Luo Zheng asked back.
"When the war between them is completely over," Donghuang answered.
Ordinary people might not be able to understand who "they" referred to.
But Luo Zheng went to the Dark Realm and inherited the Weapon of Civilization. He also understood that the super forces in the mother world were basically the representatives of a certain civilization.
"Donghuang, the" they "you mentioned, are they those civilizations in the past?" Luo Zheng asked.
"You know?" This time, Donghuang was really surprised.
A little guy at the 13th Layer of Heaven shouldn't even know about the structure of the Nirvana Realm. How could he know about the existence of those civilizations?
Every once in a while, the Heaven Jiedu would report some important information to Donghuang's divided soul.

Chapter 3006
Departure
"Mn, I've heard of it before." Luo Zheng nodded.
Luo Zheng had naturally heard of it before.
The Green Jade Civilization was once one of the top ten great civilizations. Now, the entire civilization was standing behind him.
Following Luo Zheng's improvement in the Paramita Realm, if the Green Jade Civilization were to join the fray once again, it would definitely bring about a far-reaching impact.
Donghuang stared at Luo Zheng. He revealed a pensive look.
Ever since he established the Great Yi Heaven Palace, he had seen too many young geniuses with monstrous talents.
But there had never been one like Luo Zheng.
After conversing with Luo Zheng for a while, Donghuang discovered that this person was hiding many secrets. It gave him a feeling that he could not see through him.
"You know quite a lot. It's just that your cultivation is too low …" Donghuang said, "But the Chaotic Origin Great World is about to open. To Paramita Realm cultivators, the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction is a threshold that they must cross. You have to grab hold of this opportunity!"
"Luo Zheng has already prepared for everything." Luo Zheng replied.
Actually, Luo Zheng was slightly perplexed in his heart.
From Chun Xuan's performance, the fleshy body of a Chaotic Origin Expert was indeed strong.
But if Luo Zheng absorbed a few more pieces of Sealing Stone and used the Sealing Stone Solution to protect his body, his fleshy body could reach the level of Chaotic Origin Reconstruction. It might even surpass it.
Was the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction really a necessary threshold to reach the Paramita Realm?
Not long later, Luo Zheng came out from Donghuang's pavilion.
The two did not meet for long this time. Donghuang did not bestow him anything either. But Luo Zheng had already gotten what he wanted. That was Donghuang's promise.
There might be many unique treasures and sacred arts in the Heaven Palace. But those were not what Luo Zheng was after. As for fame, he did not care much about it.
After Luo Zheng left, a figure appeared at a corner. Her watery red robe outlined her slim figure. She looked extremely charming. That was Concubine Yan.
"Jijun woke up and didn't want to see me. Instead, he asked to see Luo Zheng," Concubine Yan complained. She was like a little girl in front of Donghuang.
"You know how important Luo Zheng is," Donghuang said as he held Concubine Yan in his arms. "If I can get the Real Comprehension Chapter, I may have a breakthrough in the Seeking Sword Way."
"The Supreme Monarch's Sword-seeking Dao will break through on his own sooner or later. There's no need to rush," Concubine Yan said coquettishly.
Donghuang shook his head and said, "It's too late. This time … something big happened in the Nirvana Realm."
"A major change?" Concubine Yan's expression froze.
"Yes, I'm afraid they can't hold back anymore." When he spoke up to here, Donghuang revealed a worried expression, "It's fine if I become cannon fodder in this era, but it's a pity for the countless living beings in the Center God Province."
In the several continents under the jurisdiction of Taiyi Heavenly Palace, there were countless aristocratic families with a population of more than ten billion. Once Taiyi Heavenly Palace got involved, how could these people protect themselves?
Looking at Donghuang's worried face, Concubine Yan comforted him, "They were born in this era and naturally have the misfortune of this era. If we lose in the end, let alone the Center God Province, even in the entire Chaos, where can they find shelter? Unless they are qualified to escape to the Nirvana Realm! "
The only shelter in the Chaos was the Nirvana Realm.
But in a civilization, how many people could hide in the Nirvana Realm?
"Even if we escape to the Nirvana Realm, I'm afraid we can't escape," Donghuang said again.
"Even the Nirvana Realm can't escape?" Hearing this, Concubine Yan looked surprised.
Donghuang nodded and said, "They want to destroy everything, including the Nirvana Realm."
"That's crazy!" Concubine Yan said with her eyes wide open.
If this Chaos was destroyed in the end, the most powerful people in the Mother World still had a way out, which was to hide in the Nirvana Realm.
This was the privilege of some of the powerful people.
If the Nirvana Realm was destroyed, the consequences would be unimaginable. No wonder Concubine Yan was so agitated.
"Let's not talk about this first. A few days ago, you said you had something important to tell me. What is it?" Donghuang asked.
A few days ago, Concubine Yan was helpless against Feng Ge, so she naturally wanted to borrow Donghuang's power.
But Donghuang had not escaped from the Nirvana Realm, so the divided soul she reported to Donghuang was useless.
Concubine Yan smiled and said, "It's fine now. Feng Ge has recovered."
"Feng Ge? What happened to her? "Donghuang asked curiously. He was still very concerned about Feng Ge's talented little daughter.
"Feng Ge has become a Pure Witch …" Concubine Yan said.
"A Pure Witch?" Donghuang was stunned.
Of course, he knew what a Pure Witch was. She was a powerful person from the Nirvana Realm who dominated a layer of heaven!
"Yes." Concubine Yan nodded.
"How is that possible? A Pure Witch can only target creatures from the Nirvana Realm. How did Feng Ge become one? "Donghuang was even more surprised.
"This is related to Luo Zheng," Concubine Yan said.
"It's related to this kid again …" Ever since Donghuang escaped from the Nirvana Realm, he carefully read the news from the divided soul. Most of the things he was interested in were related to Luo Zheng. This name had appeared in his mind too many times.
"It's a long story. Please listen to me in detail," Concubine Yan said with a smile.
…
…
Luo Zheng returned to the Qianlian Palace. Mu Ning, Xun, and Han Liusu had already packed up and were ready to leave the palace.
After this conflict with Concubine Qin, they no longer needed to stay in the Taidi Palace.
But just as they walked to the entrance of the Qianlian Palace, Feng Ge's figure appeared in front of them.
"You've recovered?" Luo Zheng asked with a smile.
Feng Ge's face was covered with a layer of frost. Her waist was tall and straight, and her blue dress made her look very noble.
In the pair of azure blue eyes on her oval-shaped face, there was a complicated expression constantly changing in them.
The fire of memory itself had a certain degree of plasticity. After Hu Yun's memory was erased, her own memory had been completely restored. Not only that, but what happened in the dark domain was also vivid in her mind.
Thinking about how she followed Luo Zheng like an idiot, she felt unnatural.
"Little Master!"
"Little Master, you've recovered!"
Mu Ning and Han Liusu were still very enthusiastic when they saw Feng Ge. After all, Feng Ge had taught them a lot.
"You're leaving?" Feng Ge's eyes flashed.
Han Liusu nodded and said, "Yes. During this time, thanks to Little Master's teaching, our cultivation has improved by leaps and bounds. But we offended Concubine Qin. I'm afraid that staying in the Taidi Palace will cause you trouble …"
"Concubine Qin? What happened? "Feng Ge frowned.
"During the period when Little Master was unconscious, they …" Mu Ning told Feng Ge about what happened during this period.
After Feng Ge finished listening, her eyes turned cold. She sneered, "Chun Xuan, Chun Yuan … these fellows are quite daring!"
Among the direct disciples of the Taidi Palace, none dared to offend Feng Ge.
Even Chun Xuan, a Chaotic Origin Expert, would take a detour when he saw Feng Ge.

Chapter 3007
Ticket.
Feng Ge had accepted them as disciples on a whim. It was not formal.
But after interacting with them for a period of time, she had developed feelings for them.
Now that she saw Mu Ning and the others leaving, she felt reluctant. Hence, she said to Mu Ning: "There is no need for you to leave."
"But …" Mu Ning said as she looked at Luo Zheng.
Before Luo Zheng could say anything, Feng Ge had already grabbed Mu Ning's hand. At the same time, he stood in front of Xun and Han Liusu. He stared coldly at Luo Zheng: "Even though we are not teacher and disciple, we still have some feelings for each other. You cannot take them away."
Seeing Feng Ge acting like this, Luo Zheng felt both angry and amused. That time, Feng Ge did not have such intentions when he kidnapped them, right?
"I have already offended Consort Qin. I cannot guarantee their safety if they stay in the Tai Shi Palace." Luo Zheng said.
Consort Qin and the others were narrow-minded. They would definitely be filled with resentment. She could not vent her resentment on Luo Zheng. It was highly possible that she would find trouble with them. Luo Zheng naturally would not allow such an accident to happen.
"They won't." Feng Ge said with certainty: "I can guarantee their safety if they stay in the Tai Shi Palace."
"How can you guarantee it? When the Chaotic Origin Great World opens, you will leave the Heaven Palace. By then, how will you protect them? "Luo Zheng retorted.
"I will ask my mother to protect them. I will definitely not let them get hurt." Feng Ge said to Luo Zheng. He then turned to Xun and the others and said with a gentle voice: "I beg of you, can you stay in the Tai Shi Palace?"
The way Feng Ge acted made Luo Zheng feel strange.
Was it because her Memory Fire was damaged, or did it affect her personality?
And when Han Liusu, Mu Ning and Xun saw how Feng Ge acted, they exchanged glances. Only then did Mu Ning say: "Luo Zheng, let's stay in the Tai Shi Palace for now …"
Seeing Feng Ge's sincerity, Mu Ning, Han Liusu, and Xun were somewhat reluctant. Luo Zheng shrugged and finally agreed.
Although he could also give them good cultivation resources, the Taishi Palace's teaching was more systematic. If he could ensure that they were not bullied, the advantages outweighed the disadvantages.
…
…
After returning to the Heart Flow Sword Sect, Luo Zheng sorted out the Nirvana Token he had obtained in the dark domain.
Other than the Weapon of Civilization, the most precious items were the two hearts of accomplices and the statue of the giant green jade beast that Feng Ge had killed with his light.
After a Green Jade creature died, it would turn into a statue like this. This statue could be seen as the corpse of the Green Jade Beast.
After fusing with it, it could greatly strengthen the defense of one's physical body and transform one's body into a green jade body. It could be considered as an auxiliary Nirvana token.
Such Nirvana Tokens were extremely rare. But Luo Zheng could only fuse with three of them. The Strength God Totem was responsible for the explosive power, the Devil Eye of Fear could disturb the mind of the other party during crucial moments, and the Weapon of Civilization was Luo Zheng's most important token.
All three were indispensable. Before entering the Desire Realm, Luo Zheng did not plan on changing his Nirvana Tokens.
The two hearts of accomplices were even more precious.
One of them was given to Luo Zheng by Ling Shuang to the Extreme Celestial Mountain. As for the other, Luo Zheng originally planned to keep it for himself. But after Lin Zhanting knew of this matter, he went with Qiu Yinhe, He Chi, and others to 'forcefully buy' this Nirvana Token.
Such an item was extremely important to a super force. Qiu Yinhe and the others naturally had such foresight.
Sacrificing one's life to kill an apex expert of the other party was a great killing weapon to a super force. But it was not suitable for Luo Zheng either. He would not allow those around him to fuse with it and sacrifice themselves.
Before the opening of the Chaotic Origin Great World, Luo Zheng began his bitter cultivation.
It was not hard to comprehend the Blood Origin Divine Dao. Luo Zheng just had to be given sufficient time. But fusing with it was extremely difficult.
Other than that, Luo Zheng still had to head to the Heart Flow Pagoda to cultivate the Heart Flow Sword Scripture.
But with the company of Ning Yudie and Xi Youqin, time passed by quickly.
Two months later, Mo Yijian finally recovered from his injuries and returned.
Previously, when he executed a secret technique, his soul was severely damaged. He was unconscious for a long time before recovering. The Heart Flow Sword Sect had paid quite a price.
After paying a visit to Luo Zheng, he went into closed-door cultivation.
At this moment, it was only two months before the opening of the Chaotic Origin Great World. Mo Yijian was naturally not willing to miss this opportunity. Naturally, he made use of this time to make up for his shortcomings.
During this period, the atmosphere in the Heaven Palace became quiet.
Almost all the Heaven Palace disciples qualified to step into the 13th Level Heaven were cultivating in seclusion.
The entrance ticket to the Chaotic Origin Great World was very precious. The Heaven Palace was also fighting for more entrance tickets. But in the end, there were still too many monks and too little gruel. Naturally, a portion of them would be eliminated and could not enter the Chaotic Origin Great World. Nobody was willing to be the one eliminated.
It was not just the Heaven Palace. All the superpowers in the Mother World were doing the same.
Even these superpowers were fighting for the entrance tickets openly and secretly. Some of the smaller clans and sects were spending astronomical prices to purchase the tickets through Ear Mouse's black market.
…
…
Black Mountain Sect.
At the peak of the Black Mountain, there was a fishy stench.
Corpses were piled up like a mountain here.
Enormous beetles, vicious wolves, and all sorts of strange creatures.
These creatures were not weak when they were alive. They were at least at the 12th or 13th Level Heaven of the Paramita Realm.
They were surrounding the Black Mountain and engaged in a fierce battle of life and death.
"Ao wu!"
With a final roar of unwillingness, a huge ferocious ape flew up and headed straight for the Evil God.
This savage ape was already heavily injured. But its heart was still branded with deep unwillingness.
As long as it could kill this damnable human, it would be able to obtain the entrance ticket to the Chaotic Origin Great World.
It was just a step away!
Evil Deities looked at the approaching savage ape and revealed a sinister smile.
He extended his hand and lightly pointed. The soul aura in his body suddenly burst out!
This soul aura was as vast as the ocean. As the powerful aura circulated, it surged towards the savage ape.
The moment the savage ape was enveloped by this soul aura, it almost lost its sense of self.
'His Sun Soul … why is it so powerful …'
The mind of the savage ape was still puzzled when it felt a sharp pain coming from its head. At that moment, its consciousness was blasted apart. Its enormous and firm head had already been shattered into pieces.
Stepping on the corpse of this savage ape, Evil Deities finally reached the peak of the Black Mountain.
On the entire Black Mountain, he was the only one standing proudly.
Those villains, evil beings, and vicious beasts within the Black Mountain Sect were all looking up from afar.
"It's too simple." Evil Deities stood at the peak of the Black Mountain. He took out the Black Mountain Token and crushed it. He looked up at the horizon and muttered: "You guys won't be late right …"
On the horizon, a black ship was slowly approaching.
It seemed slow, but in reality, it was moving at an extremely high speed towards the horizon of Ao Xiang.
When the black ship stopped above the Black Mountain, Tu Mi who was wearing a black dress directly descended from the sky. She floated above Evil Deities.
"Don't stand on top of me." Evil Deities said solemnly.
"Don't talk nonsense. Bring it along and follow us."

Chapter 3008
Arrival
The entrance ticket to the Chaotic Origin Great World was made using an extremely ingenious method. It was made by cutting God Crystals into thin pieces.
Around the entrance ticket, there were four small arrays inscribed on it. They absorbed the energy of the God Crystals into the surroundings. As long as any corner was destroyed, the entire entrance ticket would shatter.
The entrance ticket to the Chaotic Origin Great World was too precious. Naturally, many lifeforms would take the risk to imitate it.
Using such an ingenious method to make the entrance ticket was truly helpless.
Evil Deities scrutinized the entrance ticket in his hand.
He had also suffered a lot in the Black Mountain Sect, especially when he had just entered the sect. Some Nirvana Realm powerhouses relied on their own Nirvana Realm tokens to suppress him.
But in this short period of time, he had improved rapidly. Those Paramita Realm experts who once suppressed him were either trampled under his feet and knelt before him or died in the Yellow Springs.
After going through so much, it was all for this little entrance ticket.
He casually kept it into his Sumeru Ring. At the same time, he said: "Is there any way to make a Sumeru Ring for me in the Paramita Realm? I have obtained so many Paramita Tokens, yet I can't bring them away. They are all wasted! "
It wasn't hard to create a Sumeru Space in the Paramita Realm. Those superpowers would usually rear Void Cry Beasts in their Deity Temples. They all had their own unique craftsmanship.
But Evil Deities was all alone in the Paramita Realm. He was like a fiend. Who would forge a Sumeru Ring for him?
He originally wanted to charge into the Deity Temple and force those people to carve a Sumeru Ring for him. But the Deity Temple had restrictions that targeted the Sun Soul. No matter how strong his Sun Soul was, it could not withstand it. Eventually, he could only give up.
Other than some extremely precious tokens that were hidden in the Sun Soul, the rest of the tokens he obtained in the Paramita Realm were all thrown away. It was a pity.
Tumi gave Evil Deities a disdainful look, "Those Paramita Tokens that we found in the Color Realm are all trash. It's not a pity to throw them away. Furthermore, we have no one in the Color Realm."
There were very few people on the black ship. Every one of them was an absolute expert. If not for some special reason, they would not enter the Color Realm.
"You are the trash!" Evil Deities angrily said.
He hated it when others doubted him. Especially during this period in the Black Mountain Sect. Those who dared to doubt him were all dead.
Tumi abruptly turned his head, and a cold light flashed in his eyes under his black hair.
If anyone else dared to speak like this in front of her, they would have died a long time ago. But looking at this arrogant man, Tumi actually resisted the urge to tear him apart.
She clenched her fist in the direction of Evil Deities.
The Evil Deities, who were insufferably arrogant in the Black Mountain Sect, had been squeezed out of shape by Tumi and had no ability to resist at all.
Then Tumi suddenly threw him directly into the black ship, and then the black ship began to speed towards the west.
…
…
Qiu Yinhe lived up to his promise to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng was holding a stack of black cards in his hand. A faint aura of divine crystals was being released from the cards.
This time, when the Chaotic Origin Great World opened, the Heaven Palace did not manage to get many tickets. The tickets were distributed to the various sword sects in the Seven Mountains, and even fewer tickets were distributed to the Heart Flow Sword Sect. It was not easy for Qiu Yin He to get seven tickets.
"The craftsmanship of this ticket is quite exquisite …"
Luo Zheng examined it for a moment before walking towards the independent courtyard opposite.
As the opening of the Chaotic Origin Great World approached, Huo Ze, Lan Qing and the others became more nervous.
That day, Qiu Yin He only said that he would try his best to get the tickets. But they were clear how precious these tickets were.
"Brother, what if the Heaven Palace does not manage to get the tickets?" Lan Qing asked worriedly.
Huo Ze sat on the wooden couch in the courtyard. He shook his head and said: "We can only wait for the Heaven Palace to give us the tickets. What other choice do we have?"
Xian Shi coldly said: "Then Grandpa's hopes have been dashed. We have come here for nothing!"
Seeing Xian Shi's resentful look, Huo Ze reminded him: "Don't forget that the Heaven Palace does not owe our Nine Li anything. Giving us the tickets is a favor. Not giving us the tickets is our duty!"
"But that Luo Zheng …" Xian Shi was still unconvinced.
At this moment, the door to the courtyard was pushed open.
There was a restriction placed in front of this independent courtyard. Ordinary people could not casually open it. The one who entered was definitely Luo Zheng.
"Young Master!" Huo Ze suddenly stood up.
"Big Brother Luo Zheng." Lan Qing smiled.
Luo Zheng felt a headache coming on when he heard this name. But since Huo Ze insisted, he did not correct him.
The Nine Li people looked nervous. Luo Zheng naturally understood why. He smiled and flipped his hand. Black cards appeared in his palm. "You are waiting for this, right?"
Seeing the stack of black cards, Lan Qing, Huo Ze, and Xian Shi's faces lit up with joy.
They had been worried for the past few days. Seeing these tickets, they felt relieved.
After all, the Nine Li people had too little contact with Luo Zheng.
Since Li Lu had personally brought them here, Luo Zheng had to shoulder this responsibility.
Even if Qiu Yin He and the Heaven Palace could not get the tickets, Luo Zheng would buy them through Ear Mouse.
"Young Master …" Huo Ze was full of gratitude and wanted to say something.
Luo Zheng waved his hand, "You are from the same clan as me. You don't have to be so courteous with me. There are still a few days left. We will set off after some preparations."
The tickets to the Chaotic Origin Great World were distributed to the various great sword sects.
It was not easy for the other disciples in the Heaven Palace to get a ticket.
In the Heart Flow Sword Sect, other than Mo Yijian and Luo Zheng, the remaining ten tickets could only be obtained through battles.
Other than that, the battles between the Absolute Formation Sword Sect and the Murdering Sword Sect were also in full swing.
Even the direct disciples had to prove their strength before they could get a ticket.
One month later …
The seven giant airships slowly rose up, leaving the Greater One Heavenly Palace and heading west.
These seven flying ships belonged to the Seven Mountains of the Heaven Palace. The leading flying ship belonged to the Great Yi Mountain. The disciples of the Tai Zhi Palace and the three great sword sects were naturally on it.
This journey to the Chaotic Origin Great World involved crossing several hundred great prefectures. Even if these flying ships could penetrate through space, they still had to pass through the walls between the prefectures. They had to stop for a while before reaching the boundary of the great prefectures. Naturally, they could not travel fast.
Within the spacious and enormous flying ship, there were several hundred compartments.
The disciples of the Heaven Palace were still diligently cultivating during the journey.
The competition within the Chaotic Origin Great World was extremely intense. Not everyone could obtain the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction after entering the Chaotic Origin Great World. The disciples of the Heaven Palace were either tempering their Sun Soul in the Paramita, consolidating their Sword Dao sacred arts or familiarizing themselves with the newly fused Paramita Talisman.
Half a month later, the seven flying ships of the Heaven Palace penetrated through the spatial barrier of the Han Li State and entered a world covered in white snow.
The disciples of the Great Yi Mountain were all on the deck of the flying ship. They were looking down at this world of ice and snow.
Luo Zheng leaned against the side of the ship. He could feel the cold wind blowing against his face.

Chapter 3009
Black Dome
The Snow Drinking Province originally belonged to Li Shan.
Li Mountain had eleven states under its jurisdiction. These states were arranged in a straight line.
Among the eleven provinces, the Snow Drinking Province was the furthest away from Li Mountain.
In the past, when the super organizations formed an alliance to force Li Shan to hand over the Spirit of Chaotic Origin, Li Shan could not withstand the pressure and chose to compromise.
Those superpowers even requested for the Chaotic Origin Great World to be placed in the central region of the Mother World. But Li Shan refused to comply. After several rounds of negotiations, Li Shan chose to give up on the Drinking Snow Province and made it a Public Province. He even placed the Chaotic Origin Great World within it.
For some superpowers, the journey was too long. Hence, some of them had set off half a year ago.
The Great One Heavenly Palace was relatively close, and it only took them half a month to arrive.
The temperature in the Snow Drinking Province was extremely low. If it was an ordinary mortal, they would turn into an ice sculpture in a few breaths of time.
But to Paramita Realm experts, this bit of temperature was naturally normal.
Even in this harsh environment, there were still many large cities built in the Snow Drinking Province.
They were about to reach their destination, and many disciples of the Heavenly Palace looked excited.
They gathered in twos and threes as they discussed the information regarding the Chaotic Origin Great World.
Even though this was the first time these disciples of the Heavenly Palace entered the Chaotic Origin Great World, some seniors of the Heavenly Palace would naturally impart some techniques to them. The reason why they came together was to exchange information.
Although they all belonged to the Greater One Mountain, the four factions of the Greater One Mountain were clearly separated.
The direct disciples of the Supreme Lineage Palace were the most arrogant. They occupied the front row of the flying ship. Among them, a disciple called Chi Yuan spoke with the loudest voice, "Wait till we enter the Chaotic Origin Great World. We, the disciples of the Supreme Lineage Palace, must stick together!"
"But my big brother said that those Chaotic Origin Spirits are scattered all over the place. It would be hard to capture them if they gathered together." Another disciple from the Supreme lineage Palace refuted.
"That depends on the choice. Some of the alien races are very cruel, and they kill people as soon as they enter …"
The Murdering Sword Sect and the Absolute Formation Sword Sect occupied the middle of the airship, while the Heart Flow Sword Sect was at the back, led by Mo Yijian and Gao Qizheng.
The six people from the Nine Li Tribe were also on the flying ship, but their identities were more sensitive, so they did not appear on the deck.
Luo Zheng stood on the side of the ship, letting the wind and snow blow against his face. The feather-sized snowflakes disappeared automatically when they were three inches in front of him.
'This time, the Chaotic Origin Great World is a hurdle that all Nirvana Realm experts have to cross. Luo Yan should be participating right? I wonder if we'll meet this time? "
After being separated from Ming Wei and the others in the Mother World for several years, Luo Zheng actually felt a little sad in his heart as he looked at the vast snowy scenery.
At this moment, a white and jade-like hand stretched out from Luo Zheng's right side and gently caught a snowflake. The snowflake gradually melted into water.
Luo Zheng turned around and saw Feng Ge.
Feng Ge's appearance on the deck of the airship immediately attracted everyone's attention.
However, she had always been as cold as ice. No one dared to approach her, and no one dared to approach her.
"Father told me that you have to be more careful in your journey to the Chaotic Origin Great World." Feng Ge said as he looked at the white patch in the distance.
"Donghuang?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
Feng Ge nodded, "The You Xiong Clan will definitely eliminate you as soon as possible. Even though they can't find you, they are almost certain that you will come to the Chaotic Origin Great World and will definitely target you."
The fleshy body in the Nirvana Realm was a huge threat.
Currently, Luo Zheng could only rampage in the 13 Levels Heaven. But what about the Desire Realm in the future? What if he broke through the Desire Realm and reached a higher level? This was something the You Xiong Clan absolutely did not wish to see.
There were no restrictions in the Chaotic Origin Great World. As long as one entered, they could kill as they wished.
The Heaven Palace and the You Xiong Clan were originally on the verge of falling out. If they determined that 'Heaven Traveler' was a disciple of the Heaven Palace, they could possibly work together with the Golden Crow and Shennong to massacre the disciples of the Heaven Palace. This kind of indiscriminate massacre would ensure that 'Heaven Traveler' was eliminated.
"They don't have such capability right?" Luo Zheng chuckled.
"Anything is possible." Feng Ge said with a straight face.
"Then what about you?" Luo Zheng asked in return.
Feng Ge had similarly appeared in the Nirvana Realm with her fleshy body. Furthermore, she was a Pure Cultivator and belonged to the Nirvana Realm. Her appearance was not blurred.
Those who paid attention to her would definitely pay attention to her.
Furthermore, her identity could not be concealed. She was much more conspicuous than Luo Zheng.
Compared to Luo Zheng, Feng Ge should be more dangerous.
Feng Ge blinked her eyes. Suddenly, she revealed an innocent smile: "You promised to protect me."
Seeing her clean and flawless face, Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat. Although Feng Ge's memories had been restored, the trip to the Dark Domain had obviously affected her personality quite a bit. At the very least, she wouldn't have the attitude of keeping people at arm's length.
But what Luo Zheng did not know was that other than in front of him, Feng Ge was still the same as before.
Because of the matter with Han Liusu and the others, Feng Ge had taught the direct disciples of the Tai Yi Palace a lesson. Hence, even the disciples participating in the Chaotic Origin Great World like Xue Yuan kept quiet out of fear when they saw her.
"Right?" Feng Ge added.
Luo Zheng helplessly nodded, "Of course."
Feng Ge pursed her lips. There was a strand of joy in her eyes. She suddenly pointed to the front and said: "Look."
At this moment, an enormous ring-shaped mountain range appeared in front of them.
Outside the mountain range was a world of ice and snow. Within the mountain range, it was shrouded by a layer of black fog.
The entire mountain range was covered by an enchantment that spanned 1.5 million kilometers. Looking from afar, it looked like a huge black egg.
This was the Chaotic Origin Great World.
Right in front of the Chaotic Origin Great World, there were more than a thousand enormous stone pillars erected. Every stone pillar had the name of a superpower inscribed on it. The seven flying ships of Great Yi Heaven Palace flew towards the stone pillar that belonged to the Heaven Palace. They stopped by the side of the stone pillar.
The flying ships of Great Yi Mountain, Great Gold Mountain, Great Bright Mountain and the other six mountains followed closely behind. They landed neatly beside the stone pillar.
There were also many flying ships parked beside the stone pillars belonging to the other superpowers. Some of the flying ships were covered in layers of snow. It seemed that they had arrived a long time ago.
The disciples of the Seven Mountains of Heavenly Palace gradually came down from the flying ships and gathered together.
The six members of the Nine Li Tribe followed closely behind Luo Zheng.
Gao Qizheng and the others felt puzzled when they saw these six people. These six people did not belong to the Heart Flow Sword Sect. Why were they able to ride a flying ship here?
Even though it was puzzling, no one mentioned this question.
For this expedition to the Chaotic Origin Great World, every mountain had sent an expert to lead the team.
Great Yi Mountain sent Qiu Yinhe. Great Yi Mountain sent Lin Zhanting. The other seven mountains also sent an expert to protect them.

Chapter 3010
Luo Zheng's gaze swept across this vast plaza. On the left side of the plaza, there were many huge figures standing there.
'That is the Li Yuan Clan.'
Luo Zheng had seen the Li Yuan Clan several times in the Paramita World.
Even though he could not see their true bodies, the silhouette of the Sun Soul could outline their figures. Naturally, he could recognize them with a single glance.
The Li Yuan Clan members were all roughly seven to eight meters tall. They stood there like small mountains.
Their bodies were covered in a layer of special golden armor, and their large eyes emitted a strong murderous aura. When they looked around, they were full of disdain for all living beings.
The Li Yuan Clan members were innately huge. Their fleshy bodies were tyrannical. In terms of strength, they were indeed stronger than other clans. Naturally, they were exceptionally arrogant.
Not far from the Li Yuan Clan, there were many Ear Mice sitting there.
These Ear Mice had their faces covered. They did not speak at all. Clearly, they were keeping a low profile.
In the eyes of the world, the Ear Mice were not considered strong. That was because the Ear Mice Clan rarely clashed with other clans. They preferred to settle everything with God Crystals and Soul Pills.
But Luo Zheng's observation in the Paramita World showed that the combat strength of the Ear Mice should not be underestimated.
After all, the best Paramita Token had passed through their hands. The Paramita Token they fused with would naturally not be inferior.
Other than that, there were also the Meng Clan, Sky Wolf Clan, Purple Ape Clan and many others.
Judging from the number of living beings, one could determine the strength of these clans.
The stronger the clan, the more authority they had. Naturally, they would get more tickets.
For instance, the Li Yuan Clan had close to 500 people. The more people there were, the more tickets they would get to enter the Chaotic Origin Great World.
Small clans like the Sky Wolf Clan and Purple Ape Clan only had 20 to 30 people. Their overall strength was naturally weak.
Most super forces could get more than 200 tickets. But this time, the Heavenly Palace Seven Mountains only had 180 plus people. This meant that the Chaotic Origin Great World was at a disadvantage in the allocation of tickets.
After Qiu Yin He entered, he cupped his hands towards the various great clans. After that, he found a corner and indicated for the Heavenly Palace disciples to wait there.
The Heavenly Palace disciples sat cross-legged on the ground. Just like Luo Zheng, they were looking around.
After all, these living beings seated here were their potential competitors.
Not long after the disciples of the Heavenly Palace had taken their seats, another whistling sound came from the horizon.
There were actually dozens of flying ships flying over. One of them was shockingly large, floating in the air like a city. In the center of this flying ship was a huge picture of a bear.
"The people from the You Xiong Clan are here," the disciples of the Heavenly Palace murmured.
Huo Ze, Lan Qing, and the others had been following Luo Zheng silently. However, when they saw the city-like airship, their faces slightly changed.
They had never seen the You Xiong Clan in the real world. This was their first time coming into contact with them.
In their minds, they already saw the You Xiong Family as a terrifying enemy. Now, seeing such a situation, they naturally felt pressured and frightened.
Especially Lan Qing, she subconsciously squeezed towards Luo Zheng.
Seeing Lan Qing's nervous appearance, Luo Zheng's lips gently moved. He used a true essence sound transmission to persuade her, "The You Xiong Clan is also a human race like us. Don't be afraid."
"But they killed so many of our people," Lan Qing responded with a true essence sound transmission.
"A debt of blood must be paid with blood. Fear can't solve the problem," said Luo Zheng.
The path of cultivation was like walking on a single-plank bridge. No matter how high one stood, one had to get rid of the fear at the bottom. Otherwise, one would fall.
One's conscience was very important.
"But they are very powerful," said Lan Qing.
"You will become more powerful," said Luo Zheng with a faint smile.
Lan Qing lowered her head and pondered for a while. When she raised her head, a faint determination appeared in her pretty eyes.
In fact, Lan Qing was a very optimistic girl. However, the environment she grew up in made her think of the You Xiong Clan as a terrifying enemy.
Luo Zheng also knew that a few words from him wouldn't be able to solve the fear of the You Xiong Clan. However, he only needed to prove that the You Xiong Clan wasn't invincible. That would be enough, and the fear in their hearts would be easily solved.
After those airships landed, the people of the You Xiong Clan flew over from the stone pillars.
The leader of the group was an old man with a straight back. The old man's eyes were shining, and every move of his was vigorous and vigorous. The old man also cupped his hands toward those big clans, then led the people of the You Xiong Clan to a corner.
Not long after the people of the You Xiong Clan arrived, the people of the Jinwu Clan, the Minyue Clan, and the Divine Farmer Clan arrived one after another.
During these years, the human race, led by the You Xiong Clan, had been expanding.
The human race, which had seemed to be a bit weak before, now seemed to be catching up with the other super clans. If these super clans joined forces, they might even be stronger than the Liyuan Clan.
The leader of the Jinwu Clan was Lady Phoenix.
She walked at the front, swaying her slim waist slightly. A pair of pure golden wings fluttered with her, giving out a breathtaking charm.
All the creatures on the scene focused their eyes on Phoenix.
Luo Zheng frowned slightly when he saw Phoenix.
Back then at the Guan Shan Prefecture, he was almost killed by this woman's disillusionment projection. Who would have thought that they would meet again.
But this was ultimately the entrance of the Chaotic Origin Great World. With Qiu Yinhe and the other elders protecting him, there was nothing to be afraid of.
The Phoenix Woman walked around in a circle and walked over with a cold smile on her face.
Seeing this, Qiu Yinhe took a step forward and blocked her path. He cupped his hands at Phoenix and said, "Lady Phoenix, I wonder if there's anything you want to teach me?"
Phoenix's gaze landed on Feng Ge. She chuckled, "Nothing much. I just want to see the traitor of our Jinwu who is about to undergo the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction. Time really flies."
Hearing this, Feng Ge's expression sank slightly. He stood up and said coldly, "Say that again!"
Those years in the Phoenix Nest were memories that Feng Ge did not want to recall. Yet Phoenix actually took the initiative to provoke him. Feng Ge was naturally furious.
"Phoenix, watch your words." Qiu Yinhe warned with an unhappy expression.
Everyone came here for the Chaotic Origin Great World, not to stir up trouble.
Phoenix Maiden did not care about Qiu Yinhe. She continued: "I am not wrong. Your mother is a traitor. Naturally, you are a traitor too. It is fine if you were to hide in the Heavenly Palace like your mother. But since you dared to come to the Chaotic Origin Great World, you should be prepared not to return."
Feng Ge's expression darkened. Just as he was about to speak, Luo Zheng suddenly stopped Feng Ge and said, "Let's not bicker with these winged birdmen."
Phoenix originally did not notice Luo Zheng. But when she saw Luo Zheng, she recalled the scene at the Guan Shan Prefecture. Her expression sank, "It's you?"
Her disillusionment projection had allowed Luo Zheng to escape several times. Naturally, Luo Zheng left a deep impression on her.

Chapter 3011

Target

This person was wearing a black cloak. His entire body smashed into the ground, preventing others from seeing his appearance.

Human Emperor Fuxi!
Fuxi had disappeared from the Mother World for a long period of time.
Nobody knew where he went. There were even some who claimed that he had fallen somewhere in the Primal Chaos.
Who would have thought that he would appear on the day the Chaotic Origin Great World opened!
When Fuxi crawled up from the ground, everyone discovered that he was hugging a wine jar. That handsome face of his was in a daze. He actually looked drunk.
He slowly walked towards Phoenix. He completely ignored Phoenix and the experts of the You Xiong Clan and Divine Farmer Clan behind her.

Qiu Yinhe revealed a respectful expression as he cupped his hands towards Fuxi.
Qiu Yinhe and Lin Zhanting were bewildered. They did not know what Fuxi was trying to do.
Who would have guessed that Fuxi did not care about Qiu Yinhe at all. After bypassing Qiu Yinhe, he took a step forward. His figure floated in front of Luo Zheng like a ghost. He placed one hand on Luo Zheng's shoulder and wrapped it around Luo Zheng's neck. He said, "Little Brother, we meet again!"
Luo Zheng had felt how powerful this man was when he was at the lowest level of the Primal Chaos.
Now that Luo Zheng's cultivation had reached the Paramita 13 Heavens realm, his Sun Soul had become more condensed and sharper. But Fuxi still gave him an unfathomable feeling.
"What a coincidence." Luo Zheng indifferently replied.
"Coincidence your fart. I have been waiting for you. But you are too slow. You only started the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction now. How long do you want me to wait?" Fuxi asked.
"Why are you waiting for me?" Luo Zheng revealed a puzzled look.
"Of course, I am waiting for an appropriate time to kill you and replace you." Fuxi chuckled.
Even though he was smiling, those drunken eyes of his revealed a strand of seriousness. Luo Zheng felt his heart palpitating slightly. This fella did not seem to be joking.
Luo Zheng naturally did not know of Fuxi's long-cherished wish …
After Fuxi guessed the intention of the Ancient God, he had completely treated Luo Zheng as his target.
With Luo Zheng's growth speed, he was already extremely monstrous. But in the eyes of Fuxi, he was still too slow.
When the others heard the crazy words of Fuxi, they were dumbfounded since they did not know the inside story.
With Fuxi's strength, he could crush Luo Zheng to death with a single finger. Why would he wait for Luo Zheng to grow before killing him?
Even if Luo Zheng truly grew, could he become a powerful existence like Fuxi in the future?
Phoenix and the others were also puzzled. Fuxi had suddenly interfered, and she did not know what to do next.

Chapter 3012
Fuxi's voice was not loud, but every living being present heard it clearly. The entire place turned silent.
Even though Li Shan had contributed the Snow Drinking Prefecture, it was ultimately closer to Li Shan.
And the experts from the Golden Crow Clan, You Xiong Clan, Li Yuan Clan, and the other superpowers had yet to arrive.
They were clear of the price of disobeying Fuxi.
Phoenix's expression turned ugly.
Her goal was to give the Heaven Palace a show of strength and dampen their spirit.
The news that came from the Paramita made the Golden Crow and You Xiong Clansmen slightly depressed. That 'Tian Hang' had been targeting their superpowers time and time again, yet they could not do anything to him.
She did not expect that Fuxi would personally appear.
"Human Emperor said that everything will be fine outside the Snow Drinking Prefecture?" Phoenix gritted her teeth and asked.
If Di Jun of Jinwu, Di Hongshi of You Xiong, and Lie Shan of Shennong were here, they might not be afraid of Fuxi.

Fuxi believed that Luo Zheng was the candidate chosen by Ancient God Primordial. Naturally, he would not allow Luo Zheng to die so easily.
Of course, Fuxi also knew that Luo Zheng would not be killed so easily. He was only advising Phoenix and the others not to have any evil thoughts.
Phoenix's expression changed. You Xiong, Min Yue, Shennong Clan, and the other leaders also had ugly expressions.
As for the many junior clansmen and disciples, they all had strange expressions.

Much less becoming an opponent of Fuxi, experts at the level of Qiu Yinhe and Phoenix could still insta-kill them.
They were all from the 13 Opposite Heavens, so why was the gap between them so huge?
At this time, another giant flying ship arrived from the horizon.

Feng Ge's eyes kept turning, and his heart was filled with surprise.
