This was similar to Feng Ge's situation. Even though Feng Ge had the bloodline of the Golden Crow, even though he was born in the Phoenix Nest, he did not grow a pair of huge golden wings on his back like Phoenix.
There were a total of four to five hundred people descending from Li Mountain. It was slightly more than the Li Yuan Clan.
A majority of them were half-human and half-human. They dragged a long tail behind them. Close to a third of them looked no different from humans.
Just as the people of Li Mountain landed, a human girl walked out from their midst.
Luo Yan looked left and right before finally landing on the Heaven Palace. With a single glance, she found Luo Zheng.
"Big Brother!"
Luo Yan rushed over to Luo Zheng and hugged Luo Zheng.
Her longing for Luo Zheng was not any lesser than Luo Zheng. She had predicted that Luo Zheng would come for the opening of the Chaotic Origin Great World this time. Now that her wish was fulfilled, she was naturally overjoyed.
"Yan 'er!"
Even though Luo Zheng knew that Luo Yan was safe in Li Mountain with Ming Wei's protection, she was still a young girl who had just entered Li Mountain. She might not have a good life.
But now, it seemed that his worries were unnecessary.
"Hey, you didn't say anything weird to my big brother right?" Luo Yan suddenly stretched her head out and glared at Fuxi.
Luo Yan knew some things about Fuxi. She was indeed worried that Fuxi would do something bad to her big brother.
It was unknown what Fuxi owed Luo Yan. Or did Luo Yan have something that could be used against Luo Yan? Luo Yan actually dared to reprimand Fuxi so brazenly.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was slightly shocked.
This was a Mortal Emperor …
Including Feng Ge, Ling Shuang, Qiu Yinhe, and others. They were all sweating for Luo Yan.
This little sister of Luo Zheng was truly daring …
But those from the Nuwa Clan remained calm as if they were used to this.
As for Fuxi's reaction, it was beyond everyone's expectations. The drunkenness on his face was swept away, and he actually answered submissively, "No, of course not!"
"If you dare to talk nonsense to my big brother, I won't let you off," Luo Yan said fiercely as she waved her small fist.
Not to mention the others, even Luo Zheng was dumbfounded by this scene.
Fuxi was called the Mortal Emperor because it was a symbol of strength and status.
As one of the most powerful people in the Mother World, his majesty was unquestionable. But in front of Luo Yan, he actually acted so submissively. What on earth had happened?
Qiu Yinhe and the others were also extremely shocked.
That was the Mortal Emperor Fuxi …
Qiu Yinhe, Lin Zhanting, and the others had already seen that Luo Yan's status in the Nuwa Clan wasn't low. She had been surrounded by people all the way from the flying boat.
Even if her status was high, how high could it be?
To be able to reprimand the Mortal Emperor like this, and the Mortal Emperor didn't dare to talk back?
But such an inconceivable scene had happened. If they hadn't seen it with their own eyes, they would have found it hard to believe.
Being threatened by a little girl like this, Fuxi also felt embarrassed. He said, "Alright, I don't need to maintain order here. The people of Mount Li can return to their positions …"

"He actually ran away just like that. How hateful!"

Chapter 3013

"I've never heard of you having a younger sister," Ling Shuang's voice drifted over.

Concubine Yan once told Feng Ge that Luo Zheng was a remnant of Jiuli.
Of course, a dilapidated super force could not be compared to the Heavenly Palace.
In front of Luo Zheng, Feng Ge was always proud because of his status. This was human nature.
Not only Feng Ge, but also Ling Shuang.
Now, it seemed that they were completely wrong. Luo Zheng was probably not just a remnant of the Jiuli Tribe.
Based on this situation, these two siblings were very likely to be the direct descendants of Chi You. They might even be one of Chi You's most important descendants …
"Actually, I don't know what happened either," Luo Zheng touched his head and smiled helplessly. In reality, Luo Yan's status in Mount Li was probably much higher than his own in the Heavenly Palace!
After the people of Mount Li had all arrived, everyone still sat in place and waited.
At this moment, more flying boats continued to arrive.
Some of them were super forces, and some were from other small clans. In the corners of this open space, more and more people gathered.
According to the unspoken convention, the top experts of these super forces would not personally lead the team.
For example, the lords of the Seven Mountains of the Heavenly Palace did not come. The experts of the You Xiong Clan and the Li Yuan Clan also did not come personally.
However, when most of the clans of the mother world had arrived, a large black ship suddenly appeared in the sky.
When this large ship appeared, the expressions of Qiu Yinhe, Phoenix, and the leaders of the super forces changed several times.
"Black ship?"
"Why are they here!"
"It has been thousands of years since the Black ship trained new people, right?"
"Could it be that they have found another heaven-defying monster?"
The Black ship was a notorious force in the mother world.
They did not ally with any forces, nor did they compromise with any forces.
At the same time, they would plunder everywhere. No matter how powerful the forces were, even powerful existences like the Li Yuan Clan and the You Xiong Clan, they had also been plundered by them. It could be said that they had made countless enemies.
Even if all the forces in the mother world hated them, they were still active in the mother world.
In history, the Black ship had only escaped from the mother world once. At that time, the Black ship offended the Ear Mouse Clan and snatched a Nirvana token from the Thirtieth Layer of Heaven. The Ear Mice erupted with terrifying power for the first time and spent a lot of money to invite the super forces of the entire mother world to besiege the Black ship.
But even if all the apex experts of the mother world acted, they only managed to kill one person. The Black ship avoided the limelight in the Primal Chaos for several hundred years. When they returned, they destroyed an important branch of the Ear Mice. At the same time, they kept harassing the caravans of the Ear Mice, killing them every time they saw them.
The Ear Mice were just merchants in the mother world. They could not fight and kill all day long. Furthermore, the Black ship did not have a fixed force in the mother world. Once they were forced into a corner, they would escape.
Being forced into a corner, the Ear Mice could only make peace with the Black ship. They even sent a huge gift to settle this matter.
The exact number of people in the Black ship had always been a mystery. But it was certain that those who could enter the Black ship were all supreme experts of the mother world.
Occasionally, the Black ship would train new people.
But those who were chosen by the Black ship were all monstrous monsters.
These years, the Black ship did not participate in the Chaotic Origin Great World. Who would have thought that they would personally come this year.
After that Black ship flew above everyone's head, a woman wearing a black dress jumped down and landed firmly on the ground.
This woman's skin was as white as paper. Her face was as cute as a doll. Her pair of big eyes were even more captivating. But if one was sensitive, they would be able to smell a dense Death Qi from her body.
Shen Tu!
Phoenix, Qiu Yinhe, and the leaders of the other great clans revealed a look of dread.
An expert on the level of Shen Tu was much stronger than Qiu Yinhe and the others. She was an expert on the same level as the Mountain Lords of the Seven Mountains and the Five Elements Envoy of You Xiong. According to convention, such an expert was not allowed to enter the Snow Drinking Province.
The moment Shen Tu appeared, Fuxi, who had originally disappeared, returned.
"I could smell the stench of corpses on your body from afar. What are you doing in the Snow Drinking Province? Scram! "Fuxi said while fanning his nose.

Chapter 3014

"If you don't leave, don't blame me for being impolite." Fuxi threatened.

Today, the people who came to the Snow Drinking Province were all top forces of the Origin World. The disciples and clansmen of these forces were also one in ten thousand. They could all be called monstrous geniuses.

Relying on their heaven-defying talent, Evil Deities grew rapidly and even fused with many Divine Daos in exchange for a bargaining chip to leave his inner world.
Such an existence was destined to not submit to anyone!
Although Fuxi was powerful, compared to Evil Deities, he was not as strong as the Creator Luo Zheng.
He did not even compromise with the Creator, so how could he compromise with Fuxi?

Dao Refinement Body Splitting was divided into two steps. First, choose a creature to establish the Soul Lodging Lands. Then, let this creature leave the Inner World and use resources to cultivate it. When the time was right, take away its consciousness and easily make it a part of oneself.

After showing the petal in front of the stone door, he pressed the ticket into the seal on the side of the stone door. When the ticket shattered, he flew in.
Following closely behind was a silver-haired wolfman from the Celestial Wolf Clan. The number on his petal was' 2 '. After this silver-haired wolfman completed his verification, he rapidly entered.
The living beings present followed their numbers one after another. Their speed was getting faster and faster.
In just an hour, more than 6,000 people had entered.
"Number 7,000." That girl in front of the stone door shouted.
Feng Ge looked at Luo Zheng calmly and said, "I'm going in."
Then, Feng Ge's figure floated up and landed lightly in front of the stone door. He stepped inside.
Then came the eight thousandth place …
Nine thousand …
Ten thousand …
Twelve thousand …
Thirteen thousand …
A small portion of the living beings in this area had already left.
"10,3352!"
The woman in front of the stone gate finally called out Luo Zheng's number.
Luo Zheng smiled at Ling Shuang and the six members of the Li Clan. "I will wait for you inside."
After he finished speaking, he leaped with everyone and landed in front of the stone door.
Just as he handed over the flower petal, that woman from the Nuwa Clan softly said: "Yan 'er did not venture deep into the Chaotic Origin Great World. After entering the stone door, head right. She will be waiting for you there."
There was a hint of blame in the woman's voice.
She did not care about Luo Zheng's background. She only knew that Luo Yan was waiting for this disciple of the Heaven Palace to delay her cultivation. Naturally, she would feel unhappy.
How could Luo Zheng not understand her tone? He smiled faintly before pressing down the ticket in front of the talisman.
"Ka."
After the ticket broke into pieces, Luo Zheng stepped inside.
At the instant he entered the stone door, Luo Zheng was enveloped by a black fog. The surrounding scenery turned dark.
Of course, this darkness was still very different from the Black Domain.
The Black Domain was absolute darkness. And within the Chaotic Origin Great World, it was covered by layers of black fog.
At the same time, Luo Zheng felt a powerful hindering force.
This hindering force was very strange. It felt as though he had fallen into a quagmire. The surrounding air became very viscous and wrapped around his four limbs.
'This hindering force … is emitted by this black fog?' Luo Zheng's expression turned serious.
Luo Zheng tried waving his arm. That fog was everywhere. Naturally, he could not shake off this hindering force. What was even stranger was that he could not move his arm even though he was only using the strength of his main body.
After trying to muster 30 Deity Jun strength, he was finally able to resist the hindering force of the fog. Only then did he manage to move his arm.
'Even the fine fog has such strength. This place is truly unfathomable …' Luo Zheng thought in astonishment.
If it was someone who had just entered the Nirvana Realm, he would not be able to move at all. Even if he pushed his strength to the limit, he would only be able to muster 10 Deity Jun strength.
As for existences at the level of True Gods, their bodies would most likely explode from the pressure of the fog!
"Sou …"
Behind Luo Zheng, a Li Yuan Clansman had already entered.
This Li Yuan Clansman sized up Luo Zheng with a strand of fear in his eyes.
Although he did not know Luo Zheng's strength, he seemed to have attracted the attention of existences at the level of the Human Emperor. Furthermore, that black-clothed youth even came from this kid's Inner World?
It was better not to provoke such a person …
The Li Yuan Clansman, who had always been insolent, took a step forward. In just a few breaths, he had already disappeared into the black fog.
'Luo Yan said she was waiting for me on the right?'
After urging the power of the Nirvana Realm, he walked through the fog.
Although 30 Deity Jun strength was enough to move in the fog, the omnipresent fog still greatly restricted Luo Zheng's movements. Thus, his explosive strength and speed were limited to a certain extent.
Fortunately, this restriction was for everyone. Everyone was on the same starting line, so it was still fair.
After walking to the right for some distance, Luo Zheng only saw large grayish-brown stones. He did not find any trace of Luo Yan.
'What's going on?' Luo Zheng frowned.
Since Luo Yan said she would wait for him here, she would definitely keep her promise.
Now that she had disappeared, did she encounter some trouble?
Luo Zheng could not help but feel worried.
Unfortunately, he did not leave a soul imprint on Luo Yan. Naturally, he could not find her.
After pacing back and forth for a while, with a thought, he had already used his soul imprint to sense the location of Feng Ge. Once he did so, his expression changed.
Feng Ge had just entered the Chaotic Origin Great World not long ago. Yet, he was already more than 40,000 kilometers away from him?
If this was the outside world, Luo Zheng would not be surprised if he could execute Greater Teleportation.
But it was impossible to execute Greater Teleportation in the Chaotic Origin Great World. Under the obstruction of this black fog, it was impossible for him to explode with such speed. How did Feng Ge do it?
Just as Luo Zheng was puzzled, wind started blowing within the Chaotic Origin Great World.
"Hu …"
At the same time, he felt a powerful pushing force on his body.

Chapter 3019
Sea Dragon Clan
Every speck of dust was a tiny individual. They formed this region of black fog that spanned three million kilometers in diameter within the Chaotic Origin Great World.
These specks of dust were extremely heavy. Even living beings would find it hard to walk within them, much less the wind?
The wind that blew within the Chaotic Origin Great World naturally required a stronger force!
As Luo Zheng was swept up by this gale, he finally understood why Feng Ge could travel several tens of thousands of kilometers into the Chaotic Origin Great World in such a short period.
Feng Ge did not enter by herself. Instead, she was swept along by this gale just like him. She did not have the freedom to do so!
If that was the case, Luo Yan would most likely be swept away by this gale …

From this point, it was clear that that woman did not know about this variable.
This gale lasted for an incense worth of time before it gradually subsided.
Luo Zheng also descended steadily from the sky. He landed on a piece of brown rock.
He once again used his soul imprint to sense Feng Ge's position. He discovered that Feng Ge was still about 50,000 to 60,000 kilometers away from him. It seemed that she was similarly swept up by that gale.
Naturally, it was impossible to shorten the distance by a few tens of thousands of kilometers in a short period.
Luo Zheng was not in a rush. After all, everyone had just entered the Chaotic Origin Great World. They would need some time to adapt. There should not be any danger.
He stood on the rock and observed his surroundings. It was a piece of lifeless aura. He could not even see a single plant.
The black fog within the Chaotic Origin Great World was too heavy. To ordinary living beings, this environment was too harsh. Other than the Chaotic Origin Spirit, there should be nothing else here …
'Before I set off, I've heard from Ling Shuang that the Chaotic Origin Spirit likes to hide underground and within rocks. I wonder if there are any Chaotic Origin Spirits within these rocks?'
Thinking of this, he lifted his leg and stomped on the rock beneath him.
"Hua!"
With a crisp sound, fine cracks appeared on the surface of the rock and it collapsed, turning into countless pieces of rubble.
Ling Shuang had once told Luo Zheng that a weak rock would definitely not contain a Chaotic Origin Spirit. If there was a Chaotic Origin Spirit hiding within, the rock would definitely be extremely tough.
Even so, Luo Zheng still rummaged through the stone fragments, yet he did not find any traces of the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
Luo Zheng jumped up and landed on another rock. At the same time, he stomped on it.
"Hua …"
The rock shattered again.
This time, Luo Zheng stopped searching and jumped to the next rock.
After crushing over twenty rocks in a row, all of them turned into fragments. Naturally, he gained nothing.
Luo Zheng was not discouraged.
Currently, he was still at the periphery of the Chaotic Origin Great World. Furthermore, it was not easy to obtain a Chaotic Origin Spirit.
But just as Luo Zheng jumped up and stomped on the twenty-eighth rock, a 'peng' sound rang out. This huge rock did not shatter under Luo Zheng's stomp!
This stomp of Luo Zheng contained the strength of more than a hundred God Catties, yet not a single crack appeared on the surface of the rock.
The edge of his eyes twitched. A strand of joy could be seen deep in his eyes. Was there a Chaotic Origin Spirit hidden within this rock?
Wasn't his luck too good?
After fighting with Chun Yuan previously, Luo Zheng had a certain degree of understanding towards the Chaotic Origin Realm.
Theoretically speaking, the Chaotic Origin Realm did not strengthen one's fleshy body …
The essence of the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction was to fuse the Chaotic Origin Spirit into one's body. And any attacks one received would be borne by the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
The amount of energy the Chaotic Origin Spirit could withstand was not endless. Once the amount of energy exceeded what the Chaotic Origin Spirit could withstand, it would enter a state of fatigue. At that point, the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction Body would lose its effect.
If there was a Chaotic Origin Spirit hidden within this rock, then that Chaotic Origin Spirit would replace the rock to withstand his strength. That was why the rock appeared indestructible. In reality, it was the Chaotic Origin Spirit that was indestructible and not the rock itself.
'Let's see how much energy you can withstand …'
The edge of Luo Zheng's lips curled up. Both his legs abruptly bounced up, causing him to make a round in midair. He then punched downwards with both fists.
"Hong!"
With this strike, Luo Zheng used 500 God Catties of strength.
That huge piece of rock trembled slightly. Yet it still received Luo Zheng's punch.
'Interesting …'
"Hong!"
"Hong!"
"Hong!"
He no longer hesitated. He continued punching down on the rock below.
After punching out seven to eight times, a thin crack formed at the center of the rock.
"Gu gu gu …"
A grayish-brown ball-like object squeezed out from this thin crack. It then bounced to the side.
Even though this was the first time Luo Zheng saw this object, he was certain that it was the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
After receiving multiple attacks from Luo Zheng, the Chaotic Origin Spirit finally could not endure it any longer. It could only escape from the rock.
'Trying to escape? Not a chance! '
Luo Zheng leaped up. He was like a huge eagle spreading its wings as he chased after it.
The pervasive black fog greatly hindered Luo Zheng's speed. But this Chaotic Origin Spirit was not that fast either.

Just as Luo Zheng was getting closer to this little thing, three figures suddenly appeared in front of him. One of them glided along the ground at high speed. He actually grabbed that Chaotic Origin Spirit in his hand.
These three people had scarlet skin. Their bodies were covered in a layer of scales. There was a thick tail dragging behind them. These three were Sea Dragon Clansmen.
"Hehehe, what good luck. Who would have thought that I would be able to pick up a Chaotic Origin Spirit the moment I entered the Chaotic Origin Great World!" That Sea Dragon Clansman revealed a joyous look. The Chaotic Origin Spirit was still struggling futilely in his hand.
There were a total of 200,000 Chaotic Origin Spirits. Not one more or one less. So many people were competing for these 200,000 Chaotic Origin Spirits.
They had just entered the Chaotic Origin Great World and were able to obtain one. Naturally, they were delighted.
The Chaotic Origin Spirit which he chased after for half a day actually fell into the hands of others. Luo Zheng's expression sank slightly, "This Chaotic Origin Spirit is something I dug out from the rock. I'll have to trouble this distinguished one to return it to me."
These three Sea Dragon Clansmen naturally saw Luo Zheng.
Even though they knew that Luo Zheng's identity was extraordinary, this was not the outer world. It was the Chaotic Origin Great World.
If Luo Zheng was really killed, then even if Li Shan's people were watching, they wouldn't be able to intervene. They also wouldn't be able to investigate the three Sea Dragons. This was a rule that Nuwa had personally laid down.

Chapter 3020

But now, it was too late for regrets …
Rong Da did not hesitate. He turned around and fled.

The strength of this hurricane was not something that could be resisted by cultivators with their cultivation. As long as that hurricane blew, who knew where it would end up? This human brat would not be able to do anything to him.
Even though the speed of the two within the black fog was not fast, Luo Zheng was clearly faster than Rong Da.

Chapter 3021

Chapter 3022

Facing Evil Deities, he used this pair of Nirvana Treasures in his first move. It could be said that he did not take Evil Deities seriously.
The Heaven and Earth Dual Slash seemed ordinary, but the Serpent Dragon Soul was attached to it, and it could almost devour all the energy in the world.
Even if it was the Nirvana Power, it could also devour most of it!
However, the moment the cross blade gleams touched the rhombus-shaped fragments, a shocking destructive force exploded from the rhombus-shaped fragments. The dazzling white light penetrated the black fog barrier, and for a moment, Ji Zong's eyes could not see anything!

However, the moment he released his consciousness, a powerful soul pressure attacked him!
Consciousness was the tentacles extended out by the soul. These tentacles were very thin and could extend to an extremely far distance. They could also spread out rapidly. However, their weakness was that they were extremely fragile.
The moment that soul pressure was released, Ji Zong's consciousness had been destroyed. Under the situation where he could not capture the figure of Evil Deities, Ji Zong finally panicked.

During the battle between Evil Deities and Ji Zong, there were two people standing 300 meters away from them.

The curly-haired girl was called Mu Mu. She was a proud daughter of the Divine Farmer Clan. This time, she was placed with high hopes in the Chaotic Origin Great World.
As for the tall and sturdy young man, his origin was rather secretive. Even within the Divine Farmer Clan, only a few people knew of his true identity. His name was only one word, 'Mountain'.
However, Mu Mu preferred to call him 'Little Uncle'.
"Little Uncle, are you really not going to help?" Mu Mu opened her red lips slightly.
Her curly hair covered her appearance. But it was not hard to imagine that under this curly hair was a pair of devastatingly beautiful eyes.
No matter what, the Divine Farmer Clan and the You Xiong Clan were like brothers. Before they set off, the leader of the Divine Farmer Clan had instructed them to support each other in the Chaotic Origin Great World along with the Golden Crow and You Xiong Clans.
Ji Zong was also an important disciple of the You Xiong Clan. Logically speaking, they should help him.
"Since Ji Zong won't die, why don't you let us observe?" Mountain said calmly.
Even though the Soul Absorbing Devil was famous, the two of them did not seem to be afraid of him.
Experts were all proud and aloof. Those who could stand at the top of a superpower were all people of destiny. They did not think that they were inferior to Evil Deities.
"The energy in the hands of that black-robed young man seems to be Dao Fusion Energy. Furthermore, he has fused a lot of Divine Dao Energy …" Mu Mu twirled her hair with one finger, "To be able to fuse Divine Dao to such an extent, this fellow is really not simple …"
"It's just fusing Divine Dao. What's so great about that?" Mountain gently raised his hand. A strand of multi-colored light blossomed from his fingertip. He had fused at least 2,000 types of Divine Dao!
When Mu Mu saw the beautiful luster of the Dao Fusion Energy, the corner of her lips curled up slightly, "Little Uncle is really omniscient and omnipotent …"
"Fusing Dao isn't as difficult as you think. With sufficient time and leisure, you can do it too. The difficult part is to stimulate its power. This black-robed young man has learned this technique from an unknown place. He has stacked more than 100 layers of Dao Fusion Energy. Its power is so great that it can fight against the Paramita Token." Mountain's voice turned solemn as he said this.

This enamel was extremely tough. It directly wrapped Ji Zong's entire body tightly. At a glance, Ji Zong looked like an exquisite porcelain.
