A small race like this could not control their own fate. They could either submit to the You Xiong Clan or be exterminated. Just like that, the Mountain Tomb Race became a vassal of the You Xiong Clan.
Being a vassal had its benefits too.
It was extremely difficult for the Mountain Tomb Race to obtain the entrance ticket to the Chaotic Origin Great World.
Only one or two people from the Mountain Tomb Race could enter the Chaotic Origin Great World to cultivate every few decades or even centuries.
But this time, when the Chaotic Origin Great World opened, the You Xiong Clan casually gave the Mountain Tomb Race three entrance tickets. Naturally, the Mountain Tomb Race was overjoyed.
If not for the You Xiong Clan, this Mountain Tomb Race member would not even be qualified to stand here …
Not only did he not hate the You Xiong Clan for devouring the territory of the Mountain Tomb Race, he was even filled with gratitude.
As long as he contributed to the You Xiong Clan, he would be rewarded handsomely. And these rewards were something he didn't even dare to imagine in the past. They were indeed a superpower!
Before they set off, the You Xiong Clan had already made a list of names.
You Xiong, Golden Crow, Min Yue, Shennong, and the members of the Mountain Tomb Race and a dozen other small clans had all received it. On the list were the disciples they had to pay attention to.
Of which, Ling Shuang, Ye Shang, Chou Xue, Mo Yijian, and a few others from a few other mountains were ranked second.
Feng Ge was ranked first!
The You Xiong Clan had their Deity Temple destroyed, and they were being suppressed in the Paramita World. They had long been searching for a chance to retaliate.
No matter if that 'Tian Xing' was a disciple of the Heaven Palace or not, he would definitely enter the Chaotic Origin Great World to cultivate in the Colored Realm 13 Heavens. Then it was highly possible that he had disguised himself as a disciple of the Heaven Palace to enter.
Hence, the You Xiong Clan would target these outstanding disciples of the Heaven Palace!
Initially, there was only one name on the first page of this list. That was Feng Ge.
Feng Ge appeared in the Thirteen Heavens as a Paramita lifeform. Actually, he had exposed his identity. Naturally, he was the target of the You Xiong Clan.
But just before entering the Chaotic Origin Great World, there was an additional name on the first page of that list. That was Luo Zheng, the lifeform standing in front of the stone pillar.
Taking the opportunity when everyone was shocked by Luo Zheng's ability to be immune to poison, this Mountain Tomb Race member secretly crushed a God Talisman.
The You Xiong Clan had the Xuan Yuan Gouyu to communicate with, but this Mountain Tomb Race member was not qualified to have it.
But when the You Xiong Clan distributed that list, they were well prepared.
There was a God Talisman beside every name. They were of different colors. He crushed the God Talisman corresponding to Luo Zheng.
At the same time when the Mountain Tomb Race member crushed this God Talisman, the soul imprints of the You Xiong Clan, Jinwu Clan, Divine Farmer Clan and Min Yue Clan scattered all over the Chaotic Origin Great World were lightly touched.
At the peak of a huge mountain, a silver-haired young man stabbed his spear into the mountain, fixing himself firmly on the mountain.
Hurricanes were constantly blowing around his body …
Unbelievably, the hurricanes that contained the strength of several thousand God Jun could not blow him away!
Of course, he could not resist such a powerful hurricane. If one were to observe carefully, there were many tiny streams of air flowing around him. It was these streams of air that had almost merged him into the wind.
He allowed the hurricane to forget about him. This was his cultivation method.
Such a powerful hurricane was rare. The silver-haired young man would naturally not miss this opportunity.
At this moment, the silver-haired young man suddenly opened his eyes. He looked into the distance and muttered, "You found that brat called Luo Zheng so quickly? It's a pity he is too far away from me. I'll let you live for a while longer … "

Chapter 3027
Out of the Nest
The hurricane carried the black fog with it, blowing at high speed.
Within the hurricane, there were two Hive Demonesses. They spread their wings and Ao Xiang followed the wind.
They might not be able to go against this powerful hurricane, but they could determine their distance and direction by following the wind.
The Hive Demoness flying in front, Yao Drink's eyes flashed. She said, "Ling Kuang, did you sense it?"
"Mn." The Demoness called Ling Kuang behind her nodded slightly. "If I remember correctly, this soul imprint should have been sent out by a Mountain Tomb Tribesman."
"Ling Kuang's memory is good." Yao Drink laughed. "More importantly, that brat called Luo Zheng is right there."
When she mentioned the name 'Luo Zheng', Yao Drink's tone became colder and more hateful.
Phoenix might not be the strongest in Jinwu, but she was the idol of all the Hive Demonesses.
This brat dared to treat Lady Phoenix in such a way. This was an unforgivable sin.
The You Xiong Clan wanted to kill Luo Zheng, but they would use the elimination method to try and kill 'Tian Xing'. Regardless of whether Luo Zheng was Tian Xing or not, they would still choose to act in the Chaotic Origin Great World.
But because of Phoenix, the Jinwu viewed Luo Zheng with hostility.
"Should we go over?" Ling Kuang asked.
"Of course! It's not far from us, and there should be others joining us. "Yao Drink answered." Right now, the hurricane is still ongoing. Many people can't move in the hurricane, but once it stops, there should be many people approaching the soul imprint. They should be hiding behind something or inside a cave with the target! "
After saying that, Yao Drink spread his golden wings. He adjusted the direction of the wind slightly and flew forward. Ling Kuang did the same.
…
Within the cave.
Those Vicious Ants that flew out of the stone pillars were finally killed by Luo Zheng.
But Chou Xue and the others still did not dare to approach.
There were more than a few dozen Vicious Ants in the stone pillars. Before they were completely wiped out, no one was willing to risk their lives.
The Vicious Ants that were originally emitting red light in the holes of the stone pillars had all shrunk back.
"You're not coming out?"
Luo Zheng looked into one of the holes. It was dark inside and he couldn't see anything.
When he used his Divine Sense to investigate, he discovered that there was something obstructing him within the stone pillar …
Since the fierce ants inside didn't dare to come out, Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't be polite.
He took a step back, and the power of the Mighty God Totem exploded. His power gathered, and he punched the stone pillar.
Boom!
The energy was completely absorbed by the Chaotic Origin Spirit hidden within the stone pillar. The entire stone pillar merely shook slightly before returning to normal. Luo Zheng could not move this stone pillar at all.
Luo Zheng, on the other hand, was not discouraged.
The stronger this stone pillar was, the more Chaotic Origin Spirits were hidden within.
By the time the stone pillar was broken, his harvest would be even greater.
Boom!
Boom!
Boom!
Luo Zheng punched the stone pillar again and again. The Paramita Energy he released was getting stronger and stronger.
Chou Xinghe felt the power from Luo Zheng's punches. He was astonished. He was indeed the champion of the Seven Mountains Party. He wondered to what extent had this brat cultivated his Sword Dao?
Even though Luo Zheng's punches were powerful, it was still insufficient to deal with so many Chaotic Origin Spirits gathered together.
Under his continuous punches, a 'kuang' sound rang out. Lan Qing's Rainbow Stone had landed on the stone pillar.
Luo Zheng turned over. Lan Qing nodded at Luo Zheng with a smile. She then raised her hands, causing the Rainbow Stone to appear in her hands once again.
Even though Lan Qing did not dare to get close to this stone pillar, she could still help Luo Zheng.
Another member of the Celestial Wolf Clan also summoned his Nirvana Realm token. It was a dark gray javelin. This javelin should have been made by a Nirvana Realm race. It wasn't formed naturally, and its entire body was exceptionally exquisite.
Seeing that Lan Qing could help, this Celestial Wolf Clan member naturally did not want to fall behind.
Now that he contributed, he would at least be qualified to get a share of the Chaotic Origin Spirit if he could break the stone pillar apart.
"Xiu –"
The Celestial Wolf Clan member hurled the javelin, causing a series of sharp whistling sounds as it pierced through the air before landing heavily on the stone pillar.
"Ka!"
The stone pillar trembled. Yet this javelin did not even leave a small hole on the stone pillar!
This was the strength of the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
Before the Spirit of Chaotic Origin became fatigued, it would be hard for it to harm even a strand of hair of the other party. The Spirit of Chaotic Origin could help Chaotic Origin Experts absorb any damage. And this stone pillar in front of them was clearly a Chaotic Origin Expert …
Luo Zheng, Lan Qing, and that Celestial Wolf Clan member took turns to attack the stone pillar. When the other living beings saw that the vicious ants were no longer flying out, they became bolder.
Even though they did not have any descending-type Paramita Token, they could still use other methods to participate. Even though the strength of their attacks was not as great, they could still contribute as much as they could.
"Hong!"
"Kuang …"
"Dong dong dong …"
For a moment, all sorts of sacred techniques were unleashed, causing explosions to reverberate within the cavern.
After the group continued attacking for half an incense's time, the vicious ants hiding within the stone pillar could no longer endure it.
Bits of red light started emerging from the fist-sized holes.
Seeing the red light emitted by the vicious ants, Chou Xue and the others became alert once again. They said, "Everyone … be careful … hurry up and run!"
Just as Chou Xue warned the group, all the vicious ants within the stone pillar rushed out in all directions. There were at least a few thousand of them!
The majority of the vicious ants surrounded Luo Zheng. After all, Luo Zheng was the closest to the stone pillar. Naturally, he would be the first target.
But there were still a few hundred vicious ants that spread out and flew towards Chou Xue. Chou Xue only had time to shout out the word 'run' before retreating out of the cavern.
They were not like Luo Zheng who could stand there and allow the vicious ants to tear them apart.
If they were bitten by the vicious ants, they would be finished!
"Sou sou sou sou …"
The Celestial Wolf Clan member, a few other mutated races, and Lan Qing all fled towards the exit.
As long as they escaped to an open area outside the cavern, they might not be afraid of these vicious ants.
But Chou Xue followed the rugged path in the cavern all the way up. He heard the sound of the wind and suddenly had a bad feeling in his heart.
When he reached the exit of the cavern, the hurricane poured in and suddenly blew him to the side of the cavern wall, slamming him hard against the wall.
The hurricane sealed the entrance of the cavern …
With their strength, it was impossible for them to go against the wind and rush out of the cavern.
When Lan Qing and the rest of the foreign races saw this, their hearts sank.

Chapter 3028
"Ah! Ah! "
"This vicious ant has bitten me!"
"Hurry up and retreat!
Following several miserable shrieks, two Moon Spirits and a Celestial Wolf Race clansman were bitten by the huge jaws of the vicious ants.
Their bodies stiffened. They stood rooted to the ground before dying instantly. With a 'putong', their bodies started rotting away.
The remaining few cultivators were greatly startled when they saw this scene.
That Mountain Tomb clansman and Celestial Wolf Race clansman launched a frenzied counterattack against those vicious ants.
Given their strength, they might be able to kill a few vicious ants, but it was like pouring a cup of water on a burning cart of firewood …
It was especially so for this Mountain Tomb clansman. He felt gloomy. If he knew this would happen, he would have found a reason to avoid the hurricane and hide outside. As long as he could take away Luo Zheng's life, everything else would be fine.
Yet he was greedy for that Chaotic Origin Spirit within the stone pillar. Now, he was trapped in a cocoon of his own making. He did not even have a place to escape.
If he were to die like that Moon Spirit, he would truly be wronged.
Chou Xinghe and Lan Qing stood by the entrance of the cave with solemn expressions.
Seeing those vicious ants getting closer, Chou Xinghe finally made his decision.
He pulled Lan Qing behind him and said, "Hide behind me!"
Chou Xinghe could become one of the most outstanding disciples in the Heaven Palace. His strength was not to be underestimated.
He did not care about the lives of the other non-humans. But he could see that the relationship between Lan Qing and Luo Zheng was not ordinary. In this situation, he would naturally protect her.
Chou Xinghe raised his hand and brought out the Paramita Token.
Dozens of shackles extended out from his left hand. These shackles had thin spikes at the front.
The shackles turned around and pierced towards Chou Xinghe's body.
"Pu pu pu pu …"
Those shackles pierced into his chest, Dantian, arms, legs, and even his forehead.
When all the shackles pierced into his body, they continued extending through his meridians, bones, and organs. They had nearly pierced through his entire body.
The light in Chou Xinghe's eyes disappeared. His pupils were dilated. He was like a dead person. His life aura had been completely sealed.
But this dead person's movements had become unprecedentedly nimble!
He pulled out a shiny long sword from his waist, and his figure began to dance like a ghost.
"Big Brother Chou Xinghe …"
In Lan Qing's eyes, Chou Xue's aura changed greatly. He didn't seem like a living being at all, but more like a puppet.
From the perspective of a living being, Chou Xue was now a puppet.
Those chains were also a very rare token in the Nirvana Realm. They were called the Chains of Life and Death.
Chou Xinghe cultivated the True Meaning of Nature and the True Meaning of Eternity of Swordplay at the same time. Hence, he possessed two Nirvana Tokens. He rarely showed the Necklace of Life to others.
When the Life Annihilation Chain was activated, his vitality was completely sealed, and his consciousness was weakened to a negligible state. His entire being was controlled by the Life Annihilation Chain.
In other words, he had become a marionette.
However, the strength of this puppet would greatly increase in a short period of time.
Whether it was speed, reaction, strength …
However, the Life Annihilation Chain also had a huge drawback. It was extremely damaging to the body. After using the Life Annihilation Chain, he would be weakened for a period of time. Even his inner world would be in great turmoil.
In other words, if he failed to kill his opponent with this Nirvana Realm Token, he would be the one to die.
Unless there was no other choice, the Martyrdom of Sorrow would not be used.
Right now, there was no other way. The Martyrdom of Sorrow had no other choice.
"Phew …"
He held a long sword in his hand, and his footsteps were fluttering.
Even the long sword that was thrusting out was erratic, like a clumsy marionette.
However, this erratic long sword was extremely accurate. With a light tap, it pierced a high-speed flying fierce ant into two halves.
The fierce ants that were originally besieging the remaining few foreign tribesmen, upon discovering the Martyrdom of Sorrow's sharp counterattack, all pounced on him.
The chains that pierced into the Martyrdom of Sorrow's body were already connected to his meridians, muscles, and bones.
These chains were constantly stretching, loosening, loosening, and rotating. The Martyrdom of Sorrow's body displayed an incredible body movement. Under the attacks of these flying fierce ants, not a single one touched the Martyrdom of Sorrow's sleeves!
Every time the Martyrdom of Sorrow waved his long sword, he could always cut a few fierce ants into two halves.
Of the five to six hundred fierce ants that followed them, only two to three hundred were left in the blink of an eye. After the bodies of these fierce ants were cut open, they could only lie on the ground in vain, flapping their broken wings.
"Swish swish swish swish …"
The sword rays surged and crisscrossed.
"So powerful!"
"As expected of the best of the best among the superpowers!"
The remaining Celestial Wolf Tribesman, the Moon Spirit Tribesman, and the Mountain Tomb Tribesman all looked at the Martyrdom of Sorrow in disbelief.
In their opinion, the Martyrdom of Sorrow's strength might be slightly stronger than theirs, but the difference was not too great. They might not be a match for the Martyrdom of Sorrow in a one-on-one battle, but the two of them together should be able to take down this disciple of the Celestial Palace.
However, the strength that the Martyrdom of Sorrow displayed was enough to slaughter them all.
The remaining two to three hundred fierce ants were slaughtered by the Martyrdom of Sorrow just like that …
"Ding, ding, ding, ding …"
When the crisis was over, the Chain of Life shook and was pulled out of the Martyrdom of Sorrow's body, disappearing from his left hand.
The Martyrdom of Sorrow regained his consciousness, but his face was full of exhaustion. He could not even walk. Seeing this, Lan Qing went forward to support him.
"Be careful, there might be more fierce ants coming. I'm counting on you guys …" The Martyrdom of Sorrow said as he slowly sat down. He trembled as he took out the divine crystal from his body. He tried to crush the divine crystal and absorb the energy within, but under the exhaustion of his strength, he could not even crush the divine crystal.
When Lan Qing saw this, she helped him do it.
The remaining fierce ants flew over in twos and threes. They were not a threat at all.
Even if the fierce ants were fast, under the situation where there were not enough of them, the few of them could easily kill them.
"I wish to go down and take a look …" Lan Qing said after helping the Martyrdom of Sorrow absorb several hundred divine crystals.
Even though Luo Zheng was not afraid of the ant poison, Lan Qing was still worried.
The Mountain Tomb Clansmen, Celestial Wolf Clansmen, and Moon Spirit Clansmen nodded. Of course, they were not worried about Luo Zheng. Instead, they were worried about the Chaotic Origin Spirit within the stone pillar.
If Luo Zheng killed those fierce ants and used some methods to open the stone pillar, it would be bad for him to take the Chaotic Origin Spirit for himself.
"Let's go down together." The Martyrdom of Sorrow said.
After replenishing the divine crystals, the Martyrdom of Sorrow had not fully recovered his strength, but his movement was not hindered.
The three of them, Lan Qing, and the Martyrdom of Sorrow took a detour through the rugged cavern. Before they entered the cavern, they could hear waves of sword whistling.

Chapter 3029
Queen Ant
Luo Zheng's senses were naturally sharp. When he saw Lan Qing and Chou Martyrdom coming down, he felt as though a huge rock had been lifted off his chest.
Previously, he had seen several hundred vicious ants chasing after Lan Qing. He was worried about her safety.
But Luo Zheng could not help her. After all, he had attracted more vicious ants to attack him. If he were to follow them, those vicious ants would follow suit, and the situation would be worse.
But there was still an existence like Chou Martyrdom among them. And Chou Martyrdom did not disappoint Luo Zheng.
"Xiu xiu …"
Luo Zheng retracted his gaze. His expression became more focused as he sent out several sword lights.
The remaining vicious ants were devoured by his sword lights. Only ant corpses were left in this cavern.
"The vicious ants have been slaughtered by Big Brother Luo Zheng!" Lan Qing clapped her hands and said.
That Mountain Tomb Clansman, Moon Spirit Clansman, and Celestial Wolf Clansman were also filled with joy.
It was especially so for the Mountain Tomb Clansman. Not only did he escape from the calamity, from the looks of it, he could even get a share of the Chaotic Origin Spirit. By the time he got the Chaotic Origin Spirit, those experts from those huge clans would rush over. This scenario was perfect.
Without the vicious ants, there was only that stone pillar left. With everyone working together, they could smash it apart after spending some time.
But just as they were about to approach it, Luo Zheng suddenly felt a strong aura coming from the stone pillar!
Many fiery-red vicious ants tunneled out from the small holes in the stone pillar. These red vicious ants contained a terrifying energy as they charged towards Luo Zheng.
"There are more?"
"These red ants contain a shocking amount of energy!"
"Hurry up and retreat! Retreat! "
Those three non-humans had just come down when they hurriedly retreated back into the passage leading upwards.
There were not many red ants. There were only a few dozen of them.
But Luo Zheng had a serious expression as though he was facing a formidable expert!
When the leading red ant was just a few steps away from Luo Zheng, the bright red color on its body seemed to boil. Under the intense energy reaction, it exploded right in front of Luo Zheng. Explode!
"Hong!"
The shock wave produced by the explosion of the red ants threw Luo Zheng away.
Even Chou Xinghe and Lan Qing, who were further away, were affected by the shockwave. The two of them once again crashed into the mountain wall.
This was only the strength of one red ant. The other red ants obviously didn't plan to let Luo Zheng go. They chased after Luo Zheng in groups.
"I was careless …" Luo Zheng murmured.
If these dozens of red ants exploded on his body at the same time, his body would probably be blasted into pieces.
However, Luo Zheng would only give them one chance. Seeing the red ants rushing forward again, Luo Zheng's mind moved slightly. His arms, face, and chest all turned pitch black.
After finishing his preparations, Luo Zheng even stabbed with his sword backhandedly.
When the sword light cut open an unstable red ant, the red ant exploded again.
Even though the shockwave was still huge, Luo Zheng adjusted his body slightly. He drifted along with the shockwave for a distance before landing firmly on the ground. Once again, he thrust his sword at the red ants that were chasing after him.
Every strike of Luo Zheng's could explode a red ant. Taking advantage of the explosion of these red ants, Luo Zheng would change his position once again.
As he fought and retreated, more and more red ants exploded by Luo Zheng. Not long after, the final red ant exploded, and the cave regained its peace once again …
These vicious ants were too sinister. There was no end to them!
But this time, Luo Zheng did not recklessly approach that stone pillar.
After waiting for a moment, a pair of huge white jaws suddenly extended out from the bottom of the stone pillar. With a slight stir, this huge white jaws opened up a huge hole on the stone pillar.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng, Lan Qing, Chou Xinghe and the others were stunned.
They naturally knew how tough this stone pillar was. Because the Chaotic Origin Spirit was hidden within, they could not break it apart even if they used all their strength.
This pair of huge white jaws simply stirred and the stone pillar broke apart?
"How could this be?"
"What is that …" Lan Qing said with a nervous expression.
"It is the Ant Queen. The Ant Queen is crawling out from within!" Chou Xinghe revealed a solemn expression.
"The Ant Queen is so strong?" Luo Zheng frowned.
Even if everyone worked together, they could not destroy the stone pillar. Did this Ant Queen have the strength of several tens of thousands of Divine Jun?
The habits of ants were similar. Those highly toxic vicious ants should be ordinary worker ants that were responsible for finding food for the Ant Queen. As for those fiery-red ants that could self-explode, they should be soldier ants that were responsible for fighting. They had to ensure the safety of the Ant Queen even if they had to sacrifice themselves.
After Luo Zheng killed the worker ants and soldier ants, the Ant Queen actually crawled out from the stone pillar.
This white Ant Queen was as tall as a human. Its abdomen was huge and translucent. When it crawled out from the bottom of the stone pillar, everyone focused their attention on its huge abdomen.
The eyes of the Celestial Wolf Clan, Moon Spirit Clan, and Mountain Tomb Clan members were flashing with greed.
Many amber-colored crystals were gathered in its translucent abdomen. Naturally, they could see them clearly.
"Chaotic Origin Spirit?"
"So many Chaotic Origin Spirits! They were actually swallowed by it! "
"No wonder it could easily destroy that stone pillar. That stone pillar itself could not withstand a single blow!"
All the Chaotic Origin Spirits were in the stomach of the Ant Queen. It was not the stone pillar that was sturdy but the Ant Queen.
After the white Ant Queen appeared, it did not act rashly.
It dragged its swollen abdomen as it crawled on the ground. That pair of white jaws trembled slightly. The pair of antennae on its head swayed gently as it sensed its surroundings. It was emitting an incomparably ferocious aura.
"It has accumulated so many Chaotic Origin Spirits in its stomach. Even if it did not refine them, it should still be comparable to a Chaotic Origin Expert." Chou Xinghe shook his head with a bitter smile: "Brother Luo Zheng, let's just leave. This thing …"
Chou Xinghe was the clearest about how strong a Chaotic Origin Expert was.
Being the most outstanding disciple of the Heaven Palace, he, Ye Shang and a dozen others had once stayed in the Land of Sword Grief for a period of time.
Not everyone in the Land of Sword Grief was a decisive expert. The majority of them had just reached the Chaotic Origin Realm not long ago. They were not powerful enough to stand on their own in the Heaven Palace.
Ye Shang had once sparred with those who had just reached the Chaotic Origin Realm. But Ye Shang could not even injure a finger of the other party despite using his full strength. Even though it was a challenge across realms, the gap between the heavens and earth had deeply shaken Chou Xinghe's heart. It had planted a seed in his heart – Chaotic Origin Realm experts were undefeatable unless he underwent Chaotic Origin Reconstruction!
But previously, Luo Zheng had broken this convention. By relying on the Weapon of Civilization, he easily suppressed Chun Yuan from the Supreme Lineage Palace. But this matter was only spread within the Supreme Lineage Palace under the control of Concubine Qin. The majority of Heaven Palace disciples did not know about it.
"It isn't at the Chaotic Origin Realm." Luo Zheng stared at the Ant Queen and said: "Even if it is, there must be a way to defeat it!"

Chapter 3030
When the few other humans saw how confident Luo Zheng was, they were naturally delighted.
It was a great harvest for them to be able to get a few Chaotic Origin Spirits.
Chou Xinghe was still worried. After all, he could not help Luo Zheng for the time being after using the Chain of Life.
Just as Chou Xinghe was about to speak, the huge abdomen of the Ant Queen finally moved.
The Ant Queen might look bloated, but the moment it moved, its speed was unbelievably fast. After making an arc on the ground, it charged towards Luo Zheng.
It should have judged that Luo Zheng was the one who posed the greatest threat to it!
"Sou!"
Seeing that the Queen Ant was heading straight for him, Luo Zheng raised his sword and a fighting spirit was brewing in his eyes.
Luo Zheng felt a strand of bewilderment and worry in his heart.
But he was not worried about the Chaotic Origin Spirits …
Even if these Chaotic Origin Spirits greatly strengthened the fleshy body of the Ant Queen, it could only turn the Ant Queen into a sandbag. It might be tough, but it was nothing to be afraid of.
The problem was, where did the strength of those vicious ants come from?
Those vicious ants that contained lethal poison had a strength of several dozens of Deity Jun.
To obtain such strength in the Mother World, one had to fuse with the Paramita keepsake before it was possible. But to erupt with a strength of several dozens of Deity Jun purely with one's fleshy body? Even Luo Zheng who had yet to enter the Paramita Realm could not do so.
And these vicious ants did not have any intelligence. They were simple-minded and did not even have a soul. They could only accept the orders of the Ant Queen to attack the invaders.
And the Ant Queen was clearly intelligent. Through the aura emitted by the Ant Queen, Luo Zheng could determine that its soul was not weak. It must have entered the Paramita Realm through the True Meaning of Nature before and stored the Paramita keepsake …
This Paramita keepsake could grant a few thousand vicious ants a strength of several dozens of Deity Jun. After retracting all the energy, wouldn't its Paramita Energy reach several tens of thousands of Deity Jun?
Seeing the Ant Queen charging towards him, Luo Zheng lightly jumped back. After stepping on the cavern wall, he somersaulted and executed a Thousand Catty Fall. His body seemed to be falling vertically.
He held onto the sword with both hands and stabbed it towards the abdomen of the Ant Queen.
"Kuang!"
The Queen Ant's translucent abdomen looked fragile. Luo Zheng's long sword didn't even leave a mark on it.
And the Chaotic Origin Spirit within its abdomen was flickering with light. It had already absorbed all the energy from Luo Zheng's attack!
"Hua!"
The ant's head and waist were very flexible. The ant queen's head suddenly turned, and a pair of big jaws bit toward Luo Zheng's head.
In the beginning, Luo Zheng had been bitten on the arm by a Highly Poisonous Ferocious Ant. After suffering once, how could Luo Zheng suffer a second time?
Luo Zheng's attack didn't manage to break open the Queen Ant's abdomen. His body floated backwards.
But at this moment, a white ring suddenly rose from the head of the ant queen. As the ring spun, it suddenly shot out rays of white light. The ant corpses that were connected to the white light started moving again!
These ant corpses formed a dense net that closed in on the Queen Ant. And the direction in which Luo Zheng retreated was precisely the direction in which the net was being pulled in.
This set of actions of the Queen Ant was incredibly fast. Luo Zheng was startled. He wanted to dodge, but it was already too late. He fell into the huge net formed by the ant corpses, and his entire body was dragged back!
Seeing that he was getting closer and closer to that pair of white jaws, a seven-colored stream of light with a spiral was shot over.
"Hong!"
The Seven-Colored Stone smashed heavily on the head of the Queen Ant.
This Seven-Colored Stone could attack its target automatically. Its speed was incredibly fast, and its strength was not ordinary. It could be considered a high-grade Paramita Token.
Taking advantage of the moment when the Queen Ant's head leaned back, Luo Zheng escaped from that huge net.
'The Paramita Token fused with this Queen Ant isn't weak, but it is still within an acceptable range. My previous judgment was wrong …' As Luo Zheng distanced himself from the Queen Ant, he pondered.
The strength of those ferocious ants should have been bestowed by the Queen Ant. But the Queen Ant should not be able to bestow strength to all the ferocious ants at the same time. Within a certain period of time, it should only be able to bestow strength to a portion of the ferocious ants!
When Luo Zheng was facing the attacks of those ferocious ants, he discovered that most of them were hovering in the surroundings. Every wave of attacks would only have 45 ferocious ants attacking. The rest of the ferocious ants were just watching a show.
At that time, Luo Zheng was slightly puzzled. But he did not take it to heart.
Now, he understood that the Queen Ant had limited strength. After all, it would be most efficient to concentrate all its strength on a single point.
It's just that those ferocious ants were venomous. As long as they were given a certain amount of strength, it would be sufficient to ensure that their venomous jaws could bite through the prey of the invaders. Hence, the Queen Ant used that Paramita Token 'Ring' to distribute its strength to the other ferocious ants.
With 40 to 50 ferocious ants, each attack would have about 50 to 60 Deity Jun of strength. The limit of this Queen Ant's strength should be about 2,000 Deity Jun …
Even though it was thorny, it was not impossible to take it down!
"Everyone, let's help! If you all continue watching the show, you will not get a share of the Chaotic Origin Spirit! "Lan Qing waved her hand. The Rainbow Stone which she had originally thrown out returned to her hands.
The Moon Spirit Clansmen, Celestial Wolf Clansmen, and Mountain Tomb Clansmen felt their hearts thumping.
Luo Zheng was clearly strong enough to suppress them. If they defeated the Queen Ant, how much Chaotic Origin Spirit they could get would most likely depend on Luo Zheng's preference.
If they did not help, Luo Zheng would have a reason to refuse to share the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
After hearing Lan Qing's words, the three of them no longer felt embarrassed to continue watching by the side. They immediately brought out their Paramita Tokens and began attacking the Queen Ant.
"Zi …"
The Moon Spirit Clansman held a small scarlet-gold bamboo in his hand. Fine lightning bolts shot out from the small golden bamboo, bombarding the Queen Ant.
The hairs on the body of the Celestial Wolf Clansman stood up. He extended his right claw and clawed at the Queen Ant.
Even though those three-patterned claw lights were not weak, they could not break through the defenses of the Queen Ant …
The attacks of the Mountain Tomb Clansman were the most boring. He did not dare to get close to the Queen Ant. Instead, he found some huge rocks in the cave and hurled them at the Queen Ant. But these rocks were as weak as tofu to the Queen Ant. They turned into powder upon impact.
Not only did they not cause any burden to the Queen Ant, they even obstructed the vision of everyone. Lan Qing frowned and angrily shouted: "Stop throwing rocks!"
The Mountain Tomb Clansman glanced at Lan Qing inwardly. He sneered in his heart. Once the reinforcements arrived, don't talk about getting the Chaotic Origin Crystal, all of you would die here!
Even though Lan Qing and the others were attacking, the Queen Ant ignored their attacks. It did not care about them at all.
Even though it did not know how to speak the human language, its intelligence was extremely high. Its judgment was sharp. The only one who could threaten it in this cave was this human young man in front of it. As long as it killed him, the other living beings would become its food.
The Queen Ant continued chasing after Luo Zheng. And Luo Zheng was circling around the Queen Ant.
'This Queen Ant has only devoured the Chaotic Origin Spirit. It has yet to undergo a Chaotic Origin Rebirth. The flaws in its fleshy body should still exist …'

Chapter 3031
Flaws!
Theoretically, there was nothing perfect in this world.
Even those at the Chaotic Origin Realm had flaws.
But given Luo Zheng's current strength, he could not see through the flaws of Chaotic Origin Realm experts.
Back at the Tai Shi Palace, Luo Zheng had once used his Mind Eye to disturb Chun Xuan's mind. But he did not use his True Eye to investigate.
It was not easy to see through the flaws of a fleshy body that had undergone the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction.
But this Ant Queen before him was an exception.
"Sword Pattern Technique!"
"Mind Eye!"
Following that white light, a clear world appeared in front of Luo Zheng.
In this world, everything was filled with lines.
Lan Qing was like that. Chou Xue was like that too.
Through these lines, Luo Zheng could see how strong everyone was.
Even though Chou Xue's fighting strength had plummeted, his aura remained as one as he stood there. His arms were placed in a defensive position, and he was intentionally or unintentionally protecting his flaws.
Chou Xue had never cultivated the Sword Pattern Technique. It was an instinct that he had honed through countless battles.
Other than that, Lan Qing, the Mountain Hill Tribesmen, and the Celestial Wolf Tribesmen had similar flaws …
And in his True Eye, the Ant Queen was a single entity. It was exactly the same as what he saw previously!
"There are no flaws?"
"Impossible!"
The Ant Queen had ultimately not completed the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction. Luo Zheng did not believe that he could not find any flaws.
That pair of white jaws once again pierced towards Luo Zheng. After lowering his body, Luo Zheng leaped up. He kept his sword by his side and did not attack the Ant Queen. Instead, he glared at the Ant Queen.
"What is Luo Zheng doing?" Chou Xue asked curiously.
"He is not attacking anymore. Is he planning to give up?" The Moon Spirit Clansmen frowned.
The Ant Queen relied on the Chaotic Origin Spirit and could not be broken through. But given Luo Zheng's capabilities, it was not a problem for him to escape.
But in this way, they would no longer have any hopes of getting a share of the Chaotic Origin Spirit. Hence, the few Outsiders felt gloomy.
However, they didn't dare to say anything. After all, it wasn't them who were fighting in front, but Luo Zheng.
"Brother Luo Zheng won't give up," Lan Qing said firmly. At the same time, there was a trace of doubt on her face. What was Luo Zheng doing? Looking at how focused he was, he seemed to be observing the queen ant. What was the use of such observation?
Seeing that Luo Zheng kept dodging and staring at the queen ant, Chou Xinghe suddenly thought of something.
As one of the proudest disciples of the Heavenly Palace, it was not difficult for him to obtain a Supreme Sword Scripture. He more or less knew about the Supreme Sword Scriptures in the Heavenly Palace.
"As a member of the Heart Flow Sword Sect, Luo Zheng's main cultivation should be the Heart Flow Sword. Previously, he used the extreme speed of the Nine Flash Diffraction Sword Mantra. Could it be that apart from these two supreme sword mantras, he also cultivated the Sword Pattern Art?"
Every Supreme Sword Scripture represented the pinnacle of swordsmanship. There was no difference in strength between them, only the focus.
To fully comprehend them, one needed to put in a lot of effort. Luo Zheng had just entered the Heavenly Palace. While spending a lot of time on the Nirvana Realm, he actually cultivated so many Supreme Sword Scriptures at the same time. Chou Xinghe was very surprised.
However, what Chou Xinghe didn't know was that Luo Zheng also cultivated the Fusion God Sword Scripture at the same time.
"Flaws …"
"Still nothing?"
Luo Zheng activated the power of the Mind's Eye. The white light in his right eye became stronger and stronger, and the pressure also increased. More and more lines appeared in Luo Zheng's vision. Even the cracks in space began to appear faintly!
"There! There's a trace! "
At this moment, Luo Zheng finally found an insignificant thin line on the queen ant's back.
This thin line was very precious to Luo Zheng!
Taking advantage of the moment when the queen ant turned around, Luo Zheng swept through the air like a bird and landed on the queen ant's abdomen. The sword light suddenly shot out and quickly swept away.
"Weng!"
This sword did not seem powerful. In the eyes of Chou Xinghe and the others, it was like scratching an itch. It did not have any impact on the queen ant at all.
But those amber-colored Chaotic Origin Spirits in the queen ant's abdomen dimmed by a portion!
"What is going on?"
"The Chaotic Origin Spirits have dimmed by so much?"
"That sword should be very ordinary …"
The Celestial Wolf Clan, Mountain Tomb Clan, and Moon Spirit Clan members looked at each other. They could not understand what Luo Zheng had done.
Chou Xinghe had been guessing in his heart for a long time. When he saw this scene, the edge of his lips curled up. It seemed that Brother Luo Zheng had cultivated the Sword Marking Technique to the Great Perfection stage. But thinking about it, given how many Chaotic Origin Spirits were swallowed in the queen ant's abdomen, with Luo Zheng's current strength, he should not be able to see through any flaws unless he had some unique ability.
After the queen ant was stabbed by Luo Zheng, it seemed to be infuriated. As its antennae swayed, the grand circle above its head released a ferocious energy. That bloated body of the queen ant became even more agile as it chased after Luo Zheng relentlessly!
But at the moment it caught up to Luo Zheng, Lan Qing's Rainbow Stone arrived once again, smashing down on the head of the queen ant.
"Hong!"
Taking advantage of the moment when the queen ant was smashed, Luo Zheng turned around and sent out another sword.
"Ka!"
That amber-colored luster of the Chaotic Origin Spirits dimmed by another portion!
According to the records of the < Sword Marking Technique >, even though attacking these lines might not be enough to kill the other party, it was enough to heavily injure the other party.
But the Chaotic Origin Spirits were too heaven-defying. They could replace the fleshy body to endure all the damage.
Hence, Luo Zheng was able to rapidly deplete the Chaotic Origin Spirits by attacking these lines, causing the Chaotic Origin Spirits to rapidly become fatigued.
"Kuang!"
"Ding!"
"…"
Luo Zheng did not fight against the queen ant directly. Instead, he would grab hold of every opportunity to strike.
If the distance between them was not enough, he would strike with his sword.
Even though the movement of the queen ant was fast, with its body structure, it could not protect that thin line. It could only watch as Luo Zheng attacked it. No matter how much it retaliated, it could not do anything to Luo Zheng at all.
After Luo Zheng struck out ten over times, the Chaotic Origin Spirits in the stomach of the queen ant had become fatigued. They turned into small grayish-yellow beads.
At this moment, Lan Qing threw out another Rainbow Stone!
"Hong!"
The Rainbow Stone struck the abdomen of the queen ant, causing it to cave in.
"This queen ant can't hold on any longer!" Lan Qing exclaimed in joy.
"Zi zi zi …"
Under the pain, the pair of antennae of the queen ant clashed against each other, emitting a strange sound. It sounded like a roar.
The other natives also attacked. After losing the protection of the Chaotic Origin Spirits, the defense of the queen ant plummeted. In the blink of an eye, its body was covered in wounds.
Even though the Chaotic Origin Spirits had entered a fatigued state, its Paramita Talisman should not be underestimated.
"Hua!"
But after the Chaotic Origin Spirits entered a fatigued state, it could no longer squeeze out too much energy. Its fleshy body could not withstand it either.
After it activated its Paramita Energy, it charged towards the stone pillar like a wild rhinoceros.
Actually, the stone pillar was not its lair. Its lair was connected to the bottom of the cavern. It extended for several hundred meters. It was a huge maze.
The reason why the queen ant dared to appear was because Luo Zheng had wiped out all its descendants.
Currently, it was filled with regret. If it knew this would happen, it would have holed up underground. Otherwise, it would not be in such a miserable state …
It had never thought that this human young man could actually find its only weakness!
"Pu!"
Just as the queen ant was about to drill into the cavern beneath the stone pillar, Luo Zheng threw out his sword.

Chapter 3032
When the Chaotic Origin Spirit lost its effect, the Ant Queen's defense was not worth mentioning. It was not much stronger than ordinary fierce ants.
But the Ant Queen had a tenacious life force. Even though its head was pierced through, it was still struggling on the ground. Nevertheless, such struggles were futile.
The Mountain Tomb Clansmen, Moon Spirit Clansmen, and Celestial Wolf Clansmen were overjoyed when they saw this. They rushed over to surround the corpse of the Ant Queen before Lan Qing and Chou Xingzhu.
Especially that Mountain Tomb Clansman. He extended his hand and wanted to tear the abdomen of the Ant Queen.
"Mn?"
Luo Zheng who stood in midair snorted. A strand of sinister aura came down.
Luo Zheng was the main culprit who killed the Ant Queen. How could these fellows share the Chaotic Origin Spirit?
After sensing this aura, the three of them froze. Why would they dare to make any unnecessary movements?
In their opinion, the Ant Queen was comparable to a Chaotic Origin Expert. Since Luo Zheng could even deal with the Ant Queen, killing the three of them would not take much effort.
After Luo Zheng descended, he extended his hand and pulled out his sword from the head of the Ant Queen. The three of them hurriedly moved aside.
"Xiu!"
Following a light sword light, the abdomen of the Ant Queen was sliced open. The Chaotic Origin Spirit contained within flowed out. Along with the Chaotic Origin Spirit, many ant eggs also flowed out.
Luo Zheng's sword lightly trembled. Many Chaotic Origin Spirits were plucked out from the abdomen of the Ant Queen. They fell into Luo Zheng's hands.
The three of them followed the movements of these Chaotic Origin Spirits with their eyes. They were silently counting the number of Chaotic Origin Spirits.
They understood how much they were worth. It was up to Luo Zheng to decide how many Chaotic Origin Spirits they could get. But the more Chaotic Origin Spirits there were, the more Chaotic Origin Spirits they would get.
When Luo Zheng counted the Chaotic Origin Spirits, there were a total of over 300 of them!
There was a limit to how many Chaotic Origin Spirits a living being could refine. Most living beings would need about 200 Chaotic Origin Spirits if they wanted to complete the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction. This Chaotic Origin Spirit in the abdomen of the Ant Queen was more than enough for one to complete the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction.
The eyes of the three of them were blazing with fire. If they could swallow the Chaotic Origin Spirits alone, they would not have to risk their lives fighting here.
Lan Qing and Chou Xue were relatively indifferent. As disciples of the Heavenly Palace, they naturally thought that Luo Zheng would be biased towards them.
Luo Zheng calculated in his mind. Naturally, he should keep the biggest share for himself, followed by Lan Qing and Chou. As for the three aliens … he would just send them off with a few each.
But at this moment, on the top of the mountain above the cave, two pairs of golden wings gently flapped.
Ling Kuang and Yao Ying were already floating in the air, and the hurricane had long since stopped.
"The position is correct," Ling Kuang said. "That Mountain Tomb clansman did not move. They are right below us."
Yao Ying closed his eyes and sensed the soul imprint again. Then he said, "Two people from the You Xiong Clan are coming here. Min Yue and Shennong, should we wait for them?"
Of course, they wouldn't underestimate Luo Zheng's strength. The more people there were, the greater the possibility of Luo Zheng being killed.
The corners of Ling Kuang's mouth curled up slightly. That mesmerizing smile was filled with arrogance. "Demon Drink, are you afraid that the kid is too strong?"
The Divine Nest was a unique place. Every Divine Nest Demoness was astonishingly strong. It's just that there were too few demonesses qualified to be nurtured.
Within the same level, the Golden Crow Divine Flame's power was almost absolute. They were not afraid of any opponent. Furthermore, there were two of them.
Yao Ying was straightforward, "Then let's attack!"
Saying that, the wings on their back released golden light. The Golden Crow Divine Flame burned fiercely.
"Weng weng …"
Two golden halos appeared above their heads. They were vibrating intensely. That was the highly condensed Golden Crow Divine Flame.
Following that, the two of them spread out their hands. Many pure golden sharp swords shot out from the halos!
Ling Kuang's index finger lightly pressed down. She opened her mouth and spat out a word, "Go!"
Those pure golden swords shot downwards in unison!
"Xiu xiu xiu xiu xiu …"
Every sharp sword was only the size of a palm. Yet this highly condensed Golden Crow Divine Flame could incinerate almost everything.
If there was still a Chaotic Origin Spirit in this mountain, it might be able to resist for a period of time. But that Chaotic Origin Spirit had already been eaten by that Ant Queen. The entire mountain was no different from an ordinary mountain.
When the sharp swords landed on the mountain, they immediately incinerated the mountain. The fiery-red lava was directly penetrated by the golden sharp swords!
Within the cavern, Luo Zheng was just about to speak when he suddenly felt an intense energy reaction coming from above.
Chou Xingzhu. Lan Qing and the others had also sensed it.
Just as they raised their heads, rays of golden light shot out from the top of the cavern. Many golden sharp swords shot down like raindrops!
"Dodge!"
Chou Xingzhu's reaction was the fastest. He rapidly retreated towards a corner of the cavern.
Lan Qing wanted to dodge as well. But coincidentally, several golden sharp swords landed on the path she was retreating.
Seeing a golden sharp sword just inches away from her, the heat emitted by the golden sharp sword burnt her jet-black hair. She was so frightened that her face turned pale. At this moment, a hand suddenly stretched out and blocked that golden sharp sword. At the same time, that hand pulled Lan Qing to its side.
"Ah …"
Lan Qing naturally knew that the one who helped her block the golden sharp swords was Luo Zheng. But Luo Zheng's hand …
This little bit of Golden Crow God Fire was not enough to activate the Sanskrit scriptures on his body. Golden spirals appeared on his arm, and they easily burned away the flames from the sharp swords.
Lan Qing struggled out from the fear of death. She was still in a daze when she saw this scene. She then heard Luo Zheng saying: "The cavern is about to collapse. You should hide by Chou Xingzhu's side."
Following that, Luo Zheng gently pulled and pushed Lan Qing. He controlled his strength perfectly. Lan Qing's body flew across the sky for a moment before landing by the side of Chou Xingzhu. And Chou Xingzhu's eyesight was accurate. Not a single sword landed at the position he was hiding at.
Comparatively, the three non-humans were in a miserable state.
They were just excitedly waiting for a share of the Chaotic Origin Spirit. Who would have thought that such a calamity would happen?
When those sharp swords landed, the three of them covered their heads and fled. Coincidentally, they were located at a region densely covered by the golden sharp swords.
"Pu!"
One of the golden sharp swords pierced through the head of the Celestial Wolf Clan member. He died on the spot. His hair was ignited, and in the blink of an eye, he was turned into ashes.
The Mountain Tomb Race member felt regret in his heart.
Among these people, he was the only one who knew the truth. He was the one who called these Godly Hive Demonesses over.
But how could he have known that his life was not worth mentioning in the eyes of these people!
Even though this Mountain Tomb Race member tried his best to dodge, he could not avoid the misfortune of death. At the same time, he was pierced through by three golden sharp swords. His fate was exactly the same as that of the Celestial Wolf Clan member.
Instead, it was that Moon Spirit Clan member who had remained silent all this while who reacted the most. His Paramita Talisman had a mystical technique that allowed him to form a transparent membrane.

Chapter 3033
Clash
The golden sharp swords continued descending, corroding the rock layer above the cavern.
When the rock layer was corroded to a certain extent, it would emit a dull cracking sound. The entire rock layer would collapse downwards, covering the entire cavern.
"Hong!"
Luo Zheng, Lan Qing, Chou Xue, and the Moon Spirit Clansman had already retreated to the edge of the cavern to avoid being smashed by the collapsing rock layer.
Given their strength, it was not a big deal even if they were smashed. But they would expose their location, which would be detrimental to their future operations.
After the cavern collapsed, a huge crater was formed.
Yao Drink and Ling Kuang gradually descended.
The pair of wings that contained the Golden Crow God Fire flapped, causing a scalding aura to surge in this crater.
"What … are you all doing!"
The Moon Spirit Clansman leaned by the side with a look of horror.
Even though he had avoided a calamity, the death of the Celestial Wolf Clansman and Mountain Tomb Clansman still frightened him.
The competition in the Chaotic Origin Great World had always been cruel.
Tens of thousands of people would fight for the 200,000 Chaotic Origin Spirits. Killing one meant one less competitor.
According to the records, the clansmen who entered the Chaotic Origin Great World would gather together as soon as possible before clearing the area.
In the end, the majority of those who survived were the clansmen and disciples of those super organizations. Many of the elites that were painstakingly nurtured by the smaller clans would die for unknown reasons.
In order to succeed in the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction, it was a huge test for the clansmen of the smaller clans. The risk they had to take was much higher than those super organizations.
This Moon Spirit Clansman was looking forward to getting a share of the Chaotic Origin Spirits. Just as he was feeling lucky, he actually encountered such a situation. One could imagine how depressed he felt.
Yao Drink glanced at the Moon Spirit Clansman. Her expression did not change.
Outsiders would only be hindrances if they stayed here …
Her index finger tapped lightly at the Moon Spirit Clansman. Under a golden flash, many golden firebirds flew towards the Moon Spirit Clansman.
When the Moon Spirit Clansman saw that, he turned around and leaped up. He wanted to escape from this crater.
Seeing those golden firebirds closing in, he patted on his head, causing a layer of light to sweep out. It enveloped those golden firebirds, causing their speed to decrease significantly.
"Small tricks …"
Yao Drink revealed a look of disdain. She gently pulled at the Moon Spirit Clansman.
It was as if an invisible string was coiled around the leg of the Moon Spirit Clansman, pulling him down into the flock of firebirds.
"Ahhh …"
The screams of the Moon Spirit Clan member only lasted for half a breath's time before they stopped abruptly. His entire body had been burned to a crisp.
Only then did Demon Drink turn his head, his gaze falling on Luo Zheng.
Looking at Luo Zheng's calm expression, Demon Drink was somewhat unhappy. The calmer the opponent, the more difficult it was to deal with.
"You don't seem to be flustered?" Demon Drink asked.
Luo Zheng silently sized up the two Banshees, then immediately said with a smile, "I have fought against your Hive Phoenix before. It's just two Banshees, why should I panic?"
Ling Kuang and Yao Ying frowned.
Even the elites of the Divine Farmer Clan and the You Xiong Clan would feel great pressure when facing her, let alone the two of them.
This Luo Zheng's seemingly patient and prudent character was not inferior to that black-clothed youth.
"Arrogant," Ling Kuang said coldly.
"What you fought against was only Lord Phoenix's Disillusionment Projection, not her full strength," Demon Drink replied with a calm expression.
Luo Zheng's expression did not change, staring at the two Banshees with a hint of a smile in his eyes.
In this kind of life and death confrontation, one's state of mind was very important.
Since Demon Drink had begun to defend herself, it meant that she was at a disadvantage.
In fact, Phoenix's failure to capture Luo Zheng with her Disillusionment Projection had indeed caused a lot of shock in the Hive.
But Luo Zheng was able to escape from Phoenix's hands. On one hand, it was not because he was not afraid of the Golden Crow Divine Fire. On the other hand, it was because of the Hermit Magic of the Nine Li Tribe Chiefs. If it were not for that, he would definitely not have been able to hold on until Qiu Yinhe arrived.
Of course, at that time, Luo Zheng did not know that Qiu Yinhe intended to secretly observe him.
But in any case, Luo Zheng, who had just entered the Nirvana Realm, was able to escape from Phoenix's hands.
Being stared at by Luo Zheng, Demon Drink and Ling Kuang were faintly unnatural.
They could not gain the upper hand verbally, so they did not want to waste time talking.
"Ling Kuang, you take care of those two Heavenly Palace disciples. I'll deal with this guy," Demon Drink said decisively.
Lan Qing and Chou Xinghe stood in a corner. When they heard this, they immediately became nervous.
If he was in peak condition, Chou Xinghe would not be afraid of the Hive Banshees. Even if he faced Ling Kuang and Demon Drink, he was confident that he could escape unscathed.
But after using the Chain of Life, his strength had not completely recovered. He was afraid that he would not be able to fight against them.
Lan Qing's face was filled with anxiety. She gently raised her hands, and the Rainbow Stone appeared in her hands.
If she could not avoid it, she could only fight with her back to the river.
"Whoosh!"
Accompanied by a wave of heat, Ling Kuang had already charged straight towards Lan Qing and Chou Xinghe.
Lan Qing looked very weak. In the Nine Li Tribe, she was also the target of protection from her brothers and sisters.
But at this time, the tenacity she displayed exceeded Chou Xinghe and Luo Zheng's imagination.
She gently threw the Rainbow Stone. The stone dragged a seven-colored light and went straight towards Ling Kuang.
When Ling Kuang saw the Rainbow Stone coming straight towards her, a trace of contempt appeared in her eyes. She gently spread her wings, and her whole body flashed a shadow in the air. The Rainbow Stone drilled through the shadow, unable to touch Ling Kuang at all.
"Die …"
Ling Kuang gently flipped her hand, and a golden spear appeared in her hand. The tip of the spear was suffused with traces of condensed golden flames that seemed to be able to melt everything.
But at the same time that Ling Kuang rushed down, she suddenly heard a whistling sound.
The huge power made Ling Kuang's heart palpitate!
She completely relied on instinct to lower her head. The Rainbow Stone, which had left and returned, swept past her ear. It actually scraped her white ear into pieces, and blood flowed out!
"Swish!"
"Ta!"
Lan Qing caught the Rainbow Stone with a serious expression. "Don't come closer. If you come closer … I won't be polite!"
"Sister Lan Qing, good job," Chou Xinghe encouraged from the side.
The Rainbow Stone seemed to be a very ordinary Nirvana Realm token, but there were many mysteries in it.
This stone was extremely flexible, and it was completely under Lan Qing's control. After it was thrown, it could turn back freely. Lan Qing could also detonate the Nirvana Power in the Rainbow Stone, allowing it to explode with higher speed and power.
But there was a limit to the number of detonations. It could only be detonated three times a day. After that, the Rainbow Stone could no longer be used. After the detonation, the impact of the Rainbow Stone could easily exceed 1,000 Godly Tons.
Ling Kuang saw Chou Xinghe's sickly look, and Lan Qing's strength was not worth mentioning. Naturally, she looked down on them.
But this kind of contempt almost cost her her life.

Chapter 3034
Ling Kuang's eyes looked like they were about to spit out flames. This miscalculation was a great humiliation to her.
Surging killing intent gushed out. Just as Ling Kuang was about to rush towards Lan Qing, a golden flame behind her exploded, followed by a muffled bang.
Ling Kuang turned her head, and a trace of panic appeared in her eyes.
Yao Drink was sent flying, crashing into the cave wall. Luo Zheng was floating in the air. The Golden Crow Flame was rolling around his body, but it didn't cause any damage to him?
"So strong …"
Yao Drink and Ling Kuang had never underestimated Luo Zheng.
The two women broke through the cave. Ling Kuang was responsible for cleaning up the mess, and Yao Drink was responsible for delaying Luo Zheng.
After Ling Kuang killed Lan Qing and the others, the two women would definitely be able to take down Luo Zheng …
But Ling Kuang never expected that Yao Drink wouldn't be able to last one round in front of Luo Zheng.
Demon Drinker was embedded in the rock. There was a hint of fear and anger in his charming eyes. The wings behind him burned and turned the rock into streams of lava.
"Attacking me is good. Attacking my friends, did you ask for my permission?" Luo Zheng stared at the two women. His face was cold.
He originally wanted to test them before attacking heavily, but since they chose to attack Lan Qing and Chou Xue, Luo Zheng could only attack fiercely.
As his voice fell, Luo Zheng took a light step forward. The rock under his feet cracked, and his body shuttled towards Ling Kuang like a shuttle.
"Ling Kuang! Dodge! He's not afraid of fire! "Yao Drink loudly reminded. She had suffered a great loss because of this.
Ling Kuang's Golden Crow Divine Fire was one of the best among all the banshees. When she heard Yao Ying's reminder, she forcefully withdrew the Golden Crow Divine Fire that she was about to spew out. At the same time, she spread her wings and soared into the sky!
On the other side, although Devildrinker had taken a punch from Luo Zheng, this kind of injury didn't affect her too much. She spread her wings and soared into the air. She then said, "Retreat!"
The Golden Crow Clan's greatest reliance was the Golden Crow Divine Fire. If Luo Zheng wasn't afraid at all, they would definitely be at a disadvantage. Retreating was naturally the best option.
"Since you're already here, you might as well stay!"
Luo Zheng's figure flashed. He also flew up and followed Ling Kuang.
Luo Zheng was not as agile in the air as Jinwu, but he was 30% faster than Ling Kuang. He was like a ghost, quickly closing the distance between them.
"Get down!"
Behind Luo Zheng, Yaoyin raised his eyebrows and took out a Paramita Token.
"Heaven Crystal Fall!"
She waved her two fingers in the air, and a series of shiny awls shot towards Luo Zheng.
Yao Yaoyin didn't seek to kill Luo Zheng. She only wanted to force Luo Zheng to retreat and ensure Ling Kuang's safety.
These 'Heaven Crystals' had a strong penetrative power. If Luo Zheng didn't dodge, they could leave behind hundreds of holes on her body, turning him into a sieve.
"Hmph!"
Luo Zheng didn't care about these awls. With a thought, the Sealing Stone Solution had already accumulated on his back.
"Ding ding ding …"
The Heaven Crystals that could penetrate almost everything could not penetrate through Luo Zheng's back. Seeing this, Yao Yaoyin and Ling Kuang were even more shocked.
Ling Kuang gritted her teeth. She spread her wings and rapidly increased her altitude. But this struggle was clearly futile. Luo Zheng did not slow down after withstanding that wave of attacks from Yao Yaoyin. In the blink of an eye, he arrived beneath Ling Kuang's feet.
But at this moment, the black fog in the Chaotic Origin Great World started surging again.
Being Jinwu, Ling Kuang and Yao Yaoyin had a perception of wind that far surpassed other lifeforms. At the instant the black fog surged, the two girls revealed a joyous expression. That hurricane had started blowing again!
"Hu …"
Just as Luo Zheng was about to succeed, that irresistible force appeared once again.
This irresistible hurricane forced him and Ling Kuang away from each other, causing them to tumble forward.
"Ah! Big Brother Luo Zheng has been blown away! "
Lan Qing and Chou Xue were still at the bottom of the cavern. Even though the hurricane was pouring in from above the huge pit, it could not sweep them away.
Luo Zheng might not be able to resist this hurricane, but it did not mean others couldn't.
The two banshees fully spread their wings and flew with the wind. They flew steadily and quickly. This hurricane was their natural home ground!
"This hurricane came at the right time." Yao Yaoyin laughed.
"What should we do now?" Ling Kuang asked. She still had some lingering fear after being nearly caught by Luo Zheng.
"He can't resist at all. He is just a living target. Let this hurricane end his life!"
The two banshees lightly flapped their wings and changed their direction. They followed behind Luo Zheng at the same speed.
A huge transparent crystal stone appeared above Yao Yaoyin's head. Many Heaven Crystal spears were formed from that crystal stone.
"Xiu xiu …"
The Heaven Crystal spears shot out with the wind. They were much more powerful as they shot towards Luo Zheng.
Just like what Yao Yaoyin said, Luo Zheng could not adjust his body in the hurricane. Even if he could, there was a limit to it. He could only become a target and could not avoid those Heaven Crystal spears.
"Kuang kuang kuang …"
The Heaven Crystal spears struck Luo Zheng's back, shattering apart inch by inch. Under such circumstances, Luo Zheng could only choose to resist them head-on.
"Let me see how long you can last!" Yao Yaoyin revealed a vicious smile.
Yao Yaoyin found it hard to understand why Luo Zheng could resist the bombardment of her Heaven Crystals.
After all, he was not at the Chaotic Origin Realm. Even so, there was a limit to the Chaotic Origin Realm. Beyond this limit, the strength of the fleshy body would plummet. She did not believe that Luo Zheng could last forever!
Nevertheless, Luo Zheng's Sealing Stone Solution was fundamentally different from the Spirit of Chaotic Origin.
The Sealing Stone Solution did not have the property of absorbing energy like the Spirit of Chaotic Origin. But the Sealing Stone Solution would not become fatigued.
Luo Zheng stuck all the Sealing Stone Solution on his back. No matter how many times the Heaven Crystal spears struck him, they could not penetrate his back.
But every single Heaven Crystal spear that struck his back would release a powerful force. Under the impact of this force, Luo Zheng's blood and energy became turbulent. His organs were also shaken.
There was a limit to the Sealing Stone Solution. After protecting his back, he could not protect his other body parts. This could not go on.
On the other side, Ling Kuang waved her hand and brought out a Paramita Talisman.
That was a longbow flashing with blue light. She lightly pulled the bowstring with both hands, causing an aqua blue arrow to float on the bow.
"Sky South Bow!"
This longbow was a transcendent-grade Paramita Talisman. It was a sacred treasure of the Yu Nan Clan, a ruler superpower. It was also a unique and unique item. It was famous within the Paramita Realm.
This bow was the Paramita Talisman used by the previous Phoenix. It had always been kept as a treasure by the Jinwu. It was only when Ling Kuang appeared that the previous Phoenix passed it down to her. Many demonesses in the Phoenix Nest coveted this Paramita Talisman.
"Shoot the Sky South!"
Following Ling Kuang's sudden pull, an extremely sharp blue light shot out from the Sky South Bow.

Chapter 3035
In the Forbidden Zone of Wind.

Under the situation where he could not dodge, he could only resist it.

With a thought from Luo Zheng, the bowl-sized Stone Sealing Solution gathered at the center of his back.

Luo Zheng coughed and spat out a mouthful of golden blood.
The blood spread out rapidly as it was blown by the wind.

Fortunately, this injury was not serious. He could recover from it in an instant.

If he was allowed to grow in the future, he would be a huge threat to the Golden Crows. Ling Kuang was ready to risk everything. Even if she had to pay a huge price, she had to kill him now!

The Sealing Stone Solution could move at will. No matter where Ling Kui was shot, Luo Zheng could block it. At most, he would be injured.
When the hurricane stopped, he would naturally be able to take the initiative.

"Ling Kuang, use two arrows. One for the head, one for the dantian," Demon Drink suddenly suggested.
Demon Drink didn't understand the principle behind the Sealing Stone Solution, but she knew that if Luo Zheng could block the first arrow, he could also block the second. The hurricane could only blow for the time it took to burn a stick of incense. Once the wind stopped, they would be the ones to run.
Right now, they could only do their best and fight with everything they had.
Ling Kuang nodded. Her fingers pulled again, and another blue arrow was nocked on the longbow.
In the hurricane, wild energy flowed with the wind …
A member of the Liyuan Tribe was swept up into the sky. Seeing this scene in the black fog, his large eyes were wide open.
Naturally, he didn't think that there would be someone fighting in this damned hurricane.
Luo Zheng saw Ling Kuang use two arrows, and the corner of his mouth twitched.
It seemed that relying on the Sealing Stone Solution to block Ling Kuang's attack was somewhat unrealistic …
"You also have times when you're afraid." Demon Drink noticed Luo Zheng's expression.
Luo Zheng sighed softly. "Forget it. A lion uses its full strength to catch a rabbit. I shouldn't hold back against you guys."
"A lion catching a rabbit? You don't think you're that lion, do you? " Ling Kuang narrowed her eyes. The two arrows were already aimed at Luo Zheng's head and abdomen. With her strength, there was no possibility of missing at this distance.
Only the Hive Banshees and a few flying races could fight in the hurricane. Even if Luo Zheng had a trump card, it would be difficult to threaten them. Thus, Demon Drink and Ling Kuang didn't panic.
The moment Ling Kuang released the bowstring, a rhombus-shaped spiky ball appeared above Luo Zheng's head.

The feather seemed to be carved from feathers, vivid and lifelike.
The moment this feather appeared, two huge green wings appeared behind Luo Zheng's back.
The shadow of this wing, which was more than a thousand feet wide, flashed and disappeared …
It was just a glimpse, but Ling Kuang and Demon Drink could clearly feel its strength. This strength wasn't the difference in strength, but the difference in instincts. It was the difference between a lower level person and a higher level person!
The Golden Crow Clan was the king of birds. What in this world could frighten them to such an extent?
The two arrows from the South Sky Bow arrived in an instant. However, when they were inches away from Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng suddenly twisted his body and flew up with the wind. He easily dodged the two arrows, and then his whole body suddenly stopped in the air!

Because Luo Zheng suddenly stopped in the air, the two Banshees rushed toward Luo Zheng at an extremely high speed.
They didn't have time to react, nor did they have time to adjust their direction. Two pairs of frightened beautiful eyes reflected the sharpness of Luo Zheng's sword.
He pulled out his sword and swung it to the left and right.
The two Banshees had already passed by Luo Zheng.

The hurricane continued to blow wildly.

Chapter 3036
At the entrance of the Chaotic Origin Great World.

Eventually, everyone would have to enter the core region of the Chaotic Origin Great World. At that time, there would be another bloody storm.

…

This Paramita Token was much more formidable than any other movement technique. He was indeed the strongest expert of a Ruler superpower …

But just as she was puzzled, Luo Zheng went against the wind and flew towards her.

Only Chaotic Origin Realm experts were qualified to possess a strength of several thousand Divine Jun. Logically speaking, Chou Xing did not believe it. But looking at Lan Qing's confident expression, he was slightly puzzled.

Chapter 3037
Bloody

The hurricane in the Chaotic Origin Great World revolved from south to north. Hence, they naturally flew towards the west.

The hurricane came without any warning. Yet it was very punctual.
