"Dong!"
Under this ferocious smashing, a several meters deep hole was formed in the ground.
Using the method of digging a hole to hide from the hurricane was something Luo Zheng had thought of long ago. But previously, he had just entered the Chaotic Origin Great World and did not have a clear direction. Hence, he decided to just follow the wind.
The three of them tunneled into the hole to hide for an incense worth of time before exiting the hole and continuing their journey west.
After a period of grinding, the clansmen who entered the Chaotic Origin Great World gradually understood that the chances of digging out a Chaotic Origin Spirit from the periphery were too low. Hence, most of them started moving closer to the core region.
Some of them had traveled for half a day only to be blown further and further away by the hurricane.
After pondering for a while, they roughly thought of the same method as Luo Zheng. That was to dig a hole in the ground to hide temporarily. Once the hurricane was over, they would continue their journey.
Luo Zheng discovered three to four dug holes along the way. They did not even have to dig their own holes. Instead, they could directly enter and hide.
An hour later, a short mountain appeared below Luo Zheng.
This short mountain had been scorched by flames. The mountain was filled with sword scars. Some places were even riddled with holes. Clearly, an intense battle had occurred here.
"Let's go down and take a look." Luo Zheng said.
Luo Zheng's figure sank downwards. Lan Qing and Chou Xue followed closely behind.
Due to the hurricane, those scattered traces had been completely blown away. If someone had died, their corpse would have been swept away by the hurricane.
But with a slight sweep of his Spiritual Sense, he discovered an enormous cave behind this mountain.
The three of them climbed to the back of the mountain. Just as they arrived at the entrance of the cave, a thick stench of blood drifted over.
After entering the cave, the three of them were stunned by the scene before them.
Dozens of non-human corpses were strewn across the ground. Many of them were badly mutilated and died in a miserable manner.
Chou Xue swept his gaze twice before his eyebrows twitched, "It's Wang Kou from the Great Brightness Mountain!"
His figure flickered as he rushed towards the edge of the cave. The corpse of a Heavenly Palace disciple was embedded in a crack in the wall. Half of his body had been destroyed, but his facial features could still be vaguely discerned.
Chou Xue was also from the Great Brightness Mountain, so he was naturally acquainted with Wang Kou. Wang Kou was also an outstanding disciple of the Great Brightness Mountain. Chou Xue's heart ached when he saw his corpse.
"I don't know who did this, but their methods were so cruel …" Luo Zheng sized up the corpses. These people had all lost their spatial rings.
Not only had the culprit killed them, but they had also stolen their goods.
To be able to kill so many people, their strength had to be extremely outstanding. But those who could do this were all proud and aloof individuals.
Not only had they killed people on purpose, but they had also stolen their spatial rings like bandits …
The figure of Evil Deities appeared in Luo Zheng's mind. This was completely in line with his style of doing things.
Just as Luo Zheng was guessing, a pained groan came from not too far away.
Chou Xue was still collecting Wang Kou's corpse when Luo Zheng immediately rushed over. What he saw was the massive body of a Liyuan Tribe member.

Chapter 3038
Pursuit
The blood on the body of this Li Yuan Clan member had already stopped flowing. But there was still a finger-sized black four-legged snake sticking to the wound. This little snake was clearly formed from energy.
When he heard the footsteps of Luo Zheng, he turned over like a bird startled by the twang of a bow.
After seeing Luo Zheng, his eyes revealed a look of terror. His tall and sturdy body curled up into a ball.
The Li Yuan Clan was a relatively powerful superpower. This clan was extremely perfect, and they had the protection of the Spirit Abyss Clan in the Paramita World. They were the most likely superpower to become a Ruler civilization.
They looked down on all other races and were extremely proud.
But when this Li Yuan Clan member saw Luo Zheng, this human, he was actually terrified to such an extent. It was like a prey seeing a hunter.
"What happened here?" Luo Zheng asked.
The Li Yuan Clan member originally thought that Luo Zheng was here to take his life. But when he heard this question, he discovered that Luo Zheng did not have any huge enmity towards him. He was startled for a moment before replying: "That fellow … that human, that human who calls himself the Evil Deities, after entering this place, he started killing indiscriminately!"
It was the Evil Deities …
This answer indeed verified Luo Zheng's premonition.
Even though many races would slaughter each other in the Chaotic Origin Great World, everyone was scattered.
The majority of them would maintain reverence towards unfamiliar opponents. As long as the two races did not have a feud, they would even choose to work together.
The scale of this cavern was much bigger than the cavern Chou Xue and the others discovered. There were also many more races here.
Most people could not slaughter so many people …
"The Evil Deities are very strong, but he could not kill so many people at once. Why didn't you all escape?" Luo Zheng asked.
Even if a hurricane blew, these races could still escape.
Being brought away by the hurricane was still better than dying in the hands of the Evil Deities.
When this was mentioned, the Li Yuan Clan member's expression turned even more solemn. "His, his Paramita Token is too strange! That is a world! He is like a fish in water in that world. We can't escape at all. We can only be played to death by him! If not for my Paramita Token being the Fake Death Beast, I would have died without a doubt! "
The Fake Death Beast was a kind of Nirvana Realm creature that lived in the Eleventh Heaven.
The living creatures in the Nirvana Realm were difficult to tame, but the Abyss Spirit Clan controlled the Eleventh Heaven, so they naturally had their ways.
However, the Faking Death Beast itself was not powerful, and very few people would fuse with this Paramita Keepsake.
Back then, the Liyuan Clan had asked Ear Mouse to help them auction off the fake-dead beasts. Even though the price was quite reasonable, they didn't manage to sell many of them.
Some Paramita Realm experts would choose to fuse with a Fake Death Beast after cultivating the second type of True Dao Intent. Once this Paramita Realm Token was activated, the aura in the inner world and the aura in the dantian would be blocked, and the entire person would enter a state of fake death.
Regardless of whether he used his soul perception or other means to investigate, he was completely dead.
The Evil Deities had slaughtered so many clansmen at once that he didn't even bother to investigate them one by one. The Evil Deities didn't care if some escaped.
Thus, this Liyuan clansman managed to escape with his life ….
However, the black four-legged snakes stuck to his wounds were still tormenting him to the point where he wished he was dead. It was not an easy task to peel off these four-legged snakes.
"A world …."
Luo Zheng's pupils flickered slightly, and at the same time, he asked, "Brother Chou, have you heard of a similar Paramita Keepsake?"
The Instrument of Civilization wasn't a world. It represented a civilization and was connected to the entire Green Jade City.
With the help of the Green Jade Spirit, Luo Zheng could get the help of the entire Green Jade Civilization. Thus, in the hurricane, the Green Jade Spirit took out the Wing King's feather from the Instrument of Civilization.
"Blood Descends," Chou Xue spat out four words.
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"The Mountain Master of Taiming Mountain has a descend-type Paramita Keepsake, but what descends is not something, but a special world," Chou Xue continued, "Such a Paramita Keepsake can only be possessed by the strongest in the mother world. What kind of Evil Deities are these …."
"It's that black-clothed young man," Luo Zheng said.
Chou Xue stared blankly at Luo Zheng for a few breaths of time. In the end, that black-clothed young man didn't mention his name in public.
Thinking about how such a terrifying existence could be born in Luo Zheng's inner world, he was speechless. It was really a monster born of a monster.
"These Evil Deities are only active in the Thirteen Heavens of the Colored Realm. I'm afraid the possibility of obtaining such a Paramita Keepsake is not high," Chou Xue said, shaking his head.
Luo Zheng looked at the black four-legged snakes, and then asked, "Do you want me to help you get them off?"
"Don't! Don't touch them! I'll slowly refine them myself! "The Liyuan clansman's face darkened when he heard Luo Zheng's request.
The characteristics of these four-legged snakes were extremely strange. When they were peeled off, there would be unbearable pain. Even someone as strong as the Liyuan clansman couldn't bear it. He had only refined four of them gradually, but he still had more than ten four-legged snakes on him. He could only spend time refining them bit by bit.
If Luo Zheng were to forcefully pull them off, he would most likely faint from the pain.
Luo Zheng originally wanted to refine these four-legged snakes. It was indeed inconvenient for him to do so since the Liyuan clansmen were still on guard against him.
After shrugging his shoulders, Luo Zheng brought Lan Qing and Chou Xue out of the cave.
"Brother Luo Zheng, this Evil Deities is so talented. Why didn't you notice it in your inner world?" At the entrance of the Chaotic Origin Great World, this doubt had been hidden in the heart of Chou Xue. Now, he finally had the chance to ask.
Everyone had absolute control over their inner world. Luo Zheng was no exception.
"Of course I noticed it. Furthermore, he even schemed against me and tried to kill me." Luo Zheng chuckled.
Lan Qing and Chou Xue both widened their eyes. Such a fellow was truly daring. They had never seen such a weirdo in their inner worlds.
"However, he also helped me stabilize my inner world and helped me a lot." Luo Zheng said, "I fulfilled my promise and released him."
Luo Zheng did not expect that the innate nature of the Evil Deities did not change at all. After leaving his inner world, they became even more ferocious. Everything was fate.
Chou Xue did not continue asking.
He felt that it was definitely abnormal for the Evil Deities to be so strong. It should be related to Luo Zheng's inner world.
But if he continued asking, it might involve some of Luo Zheng's secrets. Luo Zheng might not be able to answer him.
After exiting the cave, the three of them continued heading towards the core region. They dug another hole in the ground and waited for the hurricane to pass.
When the hurricane stopped, the three of them crawled out of the cave.
At this moment, Lan Qing and Luo Zheng sensed something at the same time. They exchanged glances with each other.
In their senses, Feng Ge and Huo Ze appeared 250 kilometers to the south. They were currently rushing towards them.

Chapter 3039
Perilous Situation
The situation of Huo Ze and Feng Ge was worse than what Luo Zheng and Lan Qing had imagined.
When Feng Ge first entered the Chaotic Origin Great World, it was still relatively smooth.
After she was brought far away by the hurricane, she wanted to turn back to look for Luo Zheng. But as the hurricane blew on her again and again, the distance between her and Luo Zheng grew further and further.
In the end, she was blown more than half a million kilometers away by the hurricane. She was almost at the other end of the Chaotic Origin Great World.
Helpless, Feng Ge could only explore by herself.
Being the strongest person in the Supreme Lineage Palace, she was naturally not weak.
Along the way, she encountered several dangers. She even met a Jinwu Banshee.
Feng Ge was originally one of the two people targeted by You Xiong and the Golden Crow Clan. And Feng Ge was viewed as a traitor of the Phoenix Nest by the Banshee. She treated him like an enemy. Naturally, she would be enraged when she met him.
She did not spend much effort to kill that Banshee.
After exploring the Chaotic Origin Great World for several hours, Feng Ge even obtained three Spirits of Chaotic Origin.
According to the judgment of ordinary people, she should be heading towards the core area.
After all, the core area was much smaller than the outer area. Eventually, all the disciples of the great clans would gather there. Luo Zheng was no exception.
Yet she was thinking of meeting up with Luo Zheng. She made an extremely bold decision. She actually drifted along with the wind.
Every time a hurricane blew, she would be swept away by at least 60,000 to 70,000 kilometers away. During this process, she even utilized her movement technique to increase her speed!
After dozens of such movements, she actually circled around the entire Chaotic Origin Great World once …
And Huo Ze followed the rules. Just like the others, he would occasionally dig a hole to hide from the hurricane. Once the hurricane stopped, he would continue his journey.
He originally wanted to meet up with Lan Qing. But after using his soul imprint, he discovered that Lan Qing had met up with Luo Zheng. He was no longer in a rush. Under the protection of Luo Zheng, it should be the safest.
Not long after, Huo Ze ran into the disciples of the Heavenly Palace, Ye Shang, and the group led by Ye Shang grew larger and larger.
Ye Shang did not know where Huo Ze came from, but since he came on the airship of Greater One Mountain, and followed closely behind Luo Zheng, Ye Shang regarded him as a disciple of Greater One Mountain, and naturally invited Huo Ze to accompany him.
This group of disciples of the Heavenly Palace was very successful. Along the way, other disciples of the Heavenly Palace were constantly joining them. Gradually, the group grew to more than a dozen people.
Most of the clansmen and disciples of the other forces were acting alone. No one would dare to provoke such a team.
When they encountered some relatively powerful clansmen, these clansmen would avoid them …
Ye Shang's goal was clear. He did not wish to waste any time on the road. Instead, he focused on heading towards the core region of the Chaotic Origin Great World.
Although his rallying power in the Heavenly Palace was not as great as Chou Xue's, his strength was there, and the disciples of the Heavenly Palace listened to him.
However, when they were 50,000 kilometers away from the core area, they finally ran into trouble.
A team formed by the Divine Farmer Clan, the You Xiong Clan, and the Minyue Clan encountered them.
Ye Shang was also designated as a target to be eliminated. He was ranked third in the second tier!
When the two parties met, there was not much dispute before they started killing each other.
Ye Shang was extremely strong, but the other party had many elites. Three to four of them surrounded Ye Shang. Ye Shang was severely injured, and he created a hurricane. The other disciples of the Heavenly Palace also rode on the hurricane to escape.
The disciples of You Xiong and Shinno could only drill into the ground and wait for the hurricane to subside. But there were three elites who followed the hurricane.
After the hurricane stopped, the three elites landed on the ground and started chasing again. They really caught up with several unlucky disciples of the Heavenly Palace.
And Huo Ze was one of the unluckier ones …
The three elites of You Xiong and Shinno naturally treated Huo Ze as a disciple of the Heavenly Palace. Huo Ze ran all the way, but he did not know what to say. He could not possibly argue with the You Xiong Clan that he came from the Nine Li Clan right? That was an action of seeking death!
But not long after Huo Ze escaped, he coincidentally met Feng Ge who was passing by.
Feng Ge did not hesitate. He immediately helped Huo Ze deal with the three elites of You Xiong and Shinno.
With Huo Ze's help, Feng Ge became fiercer and fiercer as the fight went on. Eventually, Feng Ge was slightly stronger than the other three.
When the three of them were about to give up, another You Xiong Clan disciple rushed over. This one was way more powerful than his peers. With a pair of blades, he forced Feng Ge into a dangerous situation with a few moves.
At last, Feng Ge and Huo Ze had no choice but to turn around and run. Of course, they didn't run blindly.
Feng Ge took a long detour in order to approach Luo Zheng. They were running in opposite directions, and the distance between them was shortening!
Behind Feng Ge, a young man in a white robe was flying at a high speed. The pair of blades in his hands had been sparkling with a cold light. He said, "I have heard about Phoenix in You Xiong Family. Today's fight is indeed worthy of your reputation. Why do you still want to avoid the fight?"
This young man in a white robe was named Yu Yunze. He was not born in the main branch of You Xiong Family.
Although he was an outsider, he was still valued by the You Xiong Family because of his great talent. Among all You Xiong Family people, he was ranked in the top three, and had high hopes.
As a branch family member of You Xiong Family, Ji Hao needed to work harder and work harder to stand on the same starting line as You Xiong Family people.
Yu Yunze clearly understood that killing Feng Ge would be a great contribution!
Right now, he had a huge opportunity!
He had to act fast. Before long, the hurricane would rise, and by then, it wouldn't be so easy to catch up with Feng Ge.
"Buzz!"
Yu Yunze lightly patted his head, and a small paper boat appeared above his head. After this paper boat carried Yu Yunze, its speed increased greatly, leaving behind the few elite clansmen behind him. At the same time, it quickly closed the distance between Feng Ge and Huo Ze.
"No matter how hard you try, it's futile. Why do you have to …"
While speaking, he leaped off the paper boat. His pair of blades crossed in front and back, leaving shreds of afterimages in the air as he hacked towards Feng Ge's back.
Feng Ge frowned. The King of Stone Hill floating above her head slapped down with one hand!
As a descending Nirvana token, the King of Stone Hill was not to be underestimated.
How would Yu Yunze dare to take it head-on?
However, because of the paper boat, Yu Yunze's body movement was extremely powerful.
Facing the King of Stone Hill's attack, he didn't even try to dodge. Instead, his body swayed slightly and he was already in front of Feng Ge. The two blades cut towards Feng Ge's neck.
Feng Ge was shocked. She leaned back and barely dodged the attack. However, she had already lost her balance.
Yu Yunze wouldn't let go of this opportunity.
Huo Ze saw that Feng Ge was in danger. He turned around and wanted to help. However, the three elite warriors from You Xiong and Shinno also caught up and surrounded Huo Ze. Huo Ze couldn't even protect himself. How could he help Feng Ge?
"Swoosh! Swoosh! Swoosh! "
Yu Yunze's dual blades danced in the air as Feng Ge dodged in an extremely awkward manner, cutting a sorry figure.
How dangerous was it to pass through the blades?
After forcing Feng Ge to retreat a few hundred feet, Feng Ge finally made an irreversible mistake. Yu Yunze took advantage of this mistake and hacked down.
If this attack landed, Feng Ge would be cut in half.
At this moment, a rainbow-colored light shot out and crashed onto Yu Yunze's blade at a terrifying speed.
"Clang!"

Chapter 3040
Yu Yunze's palm turned into a claw and grabbed at the flying saber.
An unseen force was exerted on the saber, forcibly pulling it back into his hand.
But Feng Ge made use of this opportunity to retreat, pulling a distance away from Yu Yunze.
When Feng Ge saw Luo Zheng, her eyes turned red and she pounced forward.
At that moment, Feng Ge really thought that she was going to die.
She did not encounter any setbacks in the Heaven Palace. The first time was in the Hidden Domain. But her memory flame was disrupted in the Hidden Domain. By the time she recovered, everything was already in the past. Feng Ge was able to calm down and digest the memories in her mind.
To Feng Ge, such memories were not that deep …
After entering the Chaotic Origin Great World, she wanted to meet up with Luo Zheng. For this, she did not hesitate to waste time going around the Chaotic Origin Great World.
But in the end, she almost died …
She could not blame Luo Zheng for this, but she still blamed Luo Zheng for the grievances she felt in her heart.
Facing Feng Ge's pounce, Luo Zheng was also stunned.
But it was indeed a close shave. If he had been a little later, Feng Ge would have been sliced into pieces by those two sabers.
Luo Zheng hugged Feng Ge in his arms and patted her back, comforting her softly.
Yu Yunze held onto the two sabers. When he saw Luo Zheng, he sneered and shouted, "What a hero saving the beauty. You appeared at the right time!"
Luo Zheng pushed Feng Ge behind him and said coldly, "This is the second time …"
Yu Yunze and the others were stunned. They did not understand what Luo Zheng meant.
"This is the second time you have offended me." Luo Zheng continued, "Ling Kuang and Yao Yue from Jinwu were the first. You guys … are the second."
Hearing Luo Zheng's reply, Yu Yunze frowned slightly.
Ling Kuang and Yao Yue were the two strongest Banshees in Jinwu. They were not weak. At least, they were not weaker than Yu Yunze.
If Luo Zheng had encountered these two banshees before, he would have been a dead man …
How could he still be standing here perfectly fine?
"You encountered Ling Kuang and Monster Drink? Will they let you go? "Yu Yunze asked coldly. For some reason, Yu Yunze had a bad premonition.
Before entering the Chaotic Origin Great World, both You Xiong and Shinno treated the disciples of the Heaven Palace as prey. This time, these clansmen were not only aiming to break through to the Chaotic Origin Realm, they were also going to kill everyone on the hunting list!
Every time they killed a person, the clan would reward them handsomely.
Especially Luo Zheng and Feng Ge!
With Monster Drink and Ling Yi's personalities, they would never let Luo Zheng go so easily, unless … they were dead.
"Of course not." The corners of Luo Zheng's mouth curled up slightly. "They are already dead."
Hearing this answer, the elite clansmen of You Xiong and Shen Nong trembled slightly and retreated slightly, their vigilance increasing greatly.
To be able to kill Goblin Drinker and Ling Kuang …
To them, this was an extremely troublesome opponent.
Yu Yunze's eyebrows rose slightly. He sneered: "Aren't you afraid of blowing your own trumpet! You think you can scare us with just a sentence? Aren't you too naive? "
But at this moment, Feng Ge said: "So they are the ones who died … no wonder my bloodline sensed that two from the Jinwu had fallen!"
The banshees in the Phoenix Nest could sense each other's existence. Phoenix could sense Feng Ge, and Feng Ge could naturally sense Monster Drink and Ling Kuang too. Even though she could sense them, she did not put it to heart. After all, these banshees had nothing to do with her. Not only that, she even hated them. She hoped that they would all die.
The clansmen of You Xiong and Shen Nong knew that the Phoenix Nest Banshees' Golden Crow bloodline had this ability. Feng Ge's words had verified Luo Zheng's words. The elite disciples of the two clans took another step back.
Seeing his clansmen retreating, Yu Yunze revealed a look of anger.
Losing the battle before losing the person was a huge taboo! This group of fellows were simply useless!
"What are you afraid of? This Feng Ge is in cahoots with that Luo Zheng. How could you believe her words? You are all fools! " Yu Yunze turned to Luo Zheng and sneered: "This kind of person is just a coward. The more he brags, the more guilty he is. His goal is to make us retreat!"
Who would have thought that Luo Zheng would shake his head slightly: "That is not what I meant."
"You want to beg for mercy? Haha, I'm afraid that's impossible! "Yu Yunze laughed.
Everyone had seen it at the entrance of the Chaotic Origin Great World. Fuxi was protecting this brat from Heaven Palace. It was inconvenient for them to act outside.
Under the precondition of not falling out with him, it was the best opportunity to get rid of Luo Zheng in the Chaotic Origin Great World.
"No." Luo Zheng continued shaking his head. A strand of sharpness could be seen deep in his eyes, "I want to tell you that those who offend me must die. You Xiong, Shennong, Golden Crow, Min Yue, none from these four clans should think of reaching the Chaotic Origin Realm!"
Yu Yunze and the other elites were startled. As for Lan Qing, Chou Xue, Feng Ge and the others, they felt exceptionally delighted. They did not expect that Luo Zheng would have such a domineering side to him.
Domineering was supported by strength. Without sufficient strength, that would be arrogant!
Yu Yunze felt that something was amiss. His intuition told him that he had offended someone he should not have offended.
But given his position, he could not retreat. Regardless of strength or aura, he would not allow himself to lose. But at this moment, the black fog above the Chaotic Origin Great World started surging once again.
A hurricane rose. This battle was about to end.
Yu Yunze revealed a helpless smile: "It seems that the battle between us will have to be pushed back to the core zone of the Chaotic Origin Great World …"
The wind would soon blow them apart. They would not be able to fight this time.
By the time they reached the core zone of the Chaotic Origin Great World, Di An and Meng Hua would be waiting there. So what if this brat was strong? Wouldn't he die if he met them?
Yu Yunze let out a sigh of relief unknowingly …
Shennong, You Xiong and the other elite disciples were even worse. Some even let out a long sigh of relief.
But Luo Zheng suddenly turned his head and said: "Chou Xue, bring Feng Ge and the others to dig a hole and wait for me …"
Feng Ge and Huo Ze did not understand what was going on. It seemed that Luo Zheng did not intend to give up?
But the hurricane was about to arrive. What was he planning to do?
Lan Qing and Chou Xue immediately understood what Luo Zheng was planning to do.
He could move freely within the hurricane. He had a huge advantage. Killing these idiots would be easy!
Chou Xue did not hesitate. He directly created a huge hole in the ground.
But Feng Ge still said worriedly: "Luo Zheng, once the wind blows, you will run away again. This isn't too good …"
It was not easy for her to reunite with Luo Zheng. Naturally, she was not willing for Luo Zheng to leave.

Chapter 3041
Begging
Even though Feng Ge was filled with doubts, he still jumped into the pit with Chou Xue and the others.
Yu Yunze saw that Luo Zheng was still standing outside the huge pit. He felt puzzled.
What was this brat trying to do?
Was he planning to chase after him in the hurricane?
He glanced at Chou Xue's smiling face and felt even more panicked.
It was impossible to fight in the hurricane. Could it be that Luo Zheng had some special technique that allowed him to stand in the hurricane easily?
It wasn't just Yu Yunze. The other elites were also panicking.
As the black fog surged more and more violently, the hurricane started rolling again.
"Hu hu hu …"
Unsurprisingly, Yu Yunze and the others were dragged into the sky by the hurricane.
At this moment, Luo Zheng leaped lightly and was similarly swept away by the wind.
The hurricane was heading north, but Luo Zheng was actually rushing towards the west. He wasn't affected by the hurricane at all!
Seeing this, Yu Yunze's pupils shrank abruptly.
How could this be?
The elites from You Xiong and Shen Nong also felt their hearts sink.
This hurricane had the strength of 3,000 to 4,000 jun. None of the living beings who entered the Chaotic Origin Great World could resist it.
They would either hide in a cave, dig a hole in the ground, or be swept away by the hurricane. There were almost no exceptions.
Only a few winged living beings could control the direction of the wind. For example, the Golden Crow Clan. But they couldn't go against the wind!
"What is this brat trying to do?"
"Is he chasing after us?"
"He can't catch up. He definitely can't catch up!"
The hurricane was blowing at an extremely fast speed. In the blink of an eye, they were swept several kilometers away.
Luo Zheng wasn't affected by the hurricane. Hence, his speed wasn't fast.
But they had forgotten that Luo Zheng could freely control the hurricane!
After rushing towards the west for a distance, he happened to be behind these people. As the feathers on his head flashed with green light, an enormous green-winged illusion appeared faintly on his back.
"Hu!"
Luo Zheng also started moving with the wind.
Not only was he being pushed by the hurricane, his speed had also reached a terrifying level.
In just three to four breaths, he had already caught up with an elite from the Divine Farmer Clan.
Seeing Luo Zheng swooping down like an eagle, this elite's face was filled with horror.
He wanted to counterattack, but it was extremely difficult for him to maintain his posture in this wind. He suddenly turned around and shot two flying shuttles flickering with purple light at Luo Zheng.
This flying shuttle was moving against the wind, so its speed was extremely slow. Luo Zheng's figure slightly lowered and he was able to avoid it.
Just as this elite was about to hurriedly take out the Paramita Token, a sword gleam had already slashed across his body, and he was directly sliced into two halves.
When the other elites saw this scene, their faces were filled with despair.
"Let's split up …"
"If he catches up to us, we'll definitely die!"
They were all moving in the same direction, but the hurricane was blowing chaotically and would indeed scatter them. If he used his movement technique, he would indeed be able to widen the distance between them even faster.
However, Luo Zheng's explosive speed still exceeded their expectations.
Just as they made this decision, Luo Zheng had already appeared behind them. Before the second elite from the Divine Farmer Clan could react, he had already been killed by Luo Zheng's sword.
The remaining two elites realized that there was no way to escape, so they gave up the idea of escaping. They immediately took out their Paramita Tokens and launched a crazy counterattack against Luo Zheng.
But now Luo Zheng was fighting in the hurricane like a fish in water. Even if these two elites fought back with all their strength, it only took a few more breaths of time for Luo Zheng.
PUFF! PUFF! “
As several sword gleams flashed, the two elites were beheaded. Their blood was blown by the hurricane and quickly turned into a cloud of blood mist, which was quickly covered up by the black fog.
Now only Yu Yunze was left …
Yu Yunze had been full of confidence. If he was really confident that he could defeat Luo Zheng, he would have dug a hole in the same place and waited for the hurricane to pass before challenging Luo Zheng again.
However, after hearing Luo Zheng and Feng Ge's words, he had already thought of retreating.
When the hurricane started, he saw Luo Zheng's figure shuttling around casually. He was even more shocked.
Immediately, he activated his body movement technique and ran forward with the wind.
Yu Yunze's body movement technique was already very outstanding. Although it was not as good as the winged creatures like the Jinwu, it was not too far off. Therefore, he left the others far behind from the start.
After running for some distance, he looked back and saw that the few elites who were following him had already been killed. Luo Zheng was rushing towards him at full speed. His speed was one level faster than him!
"So fast …" Yu Yunze's heart skipped a beat.
Naturally, he would not surrender like the others. With a wave of his hands, the two swords connected in front and back. His entire body flipped in the wind.
"Hualala …"
Clusters of sawtooth swords spread out from his body. As he shouted, "Go!", the clusters of swords that were spinning at high speed burst out.
"Swoosh swoosh swoosh swoosh …"
Countless swords shrouded behind him. The power contained in each of the swords was extremely terrifying.
If it was an ordinary Nirvana Realm cultivator, he would definitely dodge if he did not want to be pierced by the swords.
That was Yu Yunze's goal. As long as he could reduce Luo Zheng's speed and get rid of him, it would be fine as long as he could escape.
From the beginning, he had treated the disciples of the Heavenly Palace as prey to hunt down. However, after meeting Luo Zheng, he realized that the roles had been reversed. He was the prey of Luo Zheng.
Moreover, according to Luo Zheng's tone, not only would he kill them, he would also kill the people of the Golden Crow, Shennong, You Xiong, and Minyue Clans …
"Ding ding ding …"
The scene behind him disrupted Yu Yunze's state of mind. What he saw made him suck in a breath of cold air.
Luo Zheng did not even use his sword to block the swords. He directly used his body to withstand the swords and forcefully charged over!
What was his body made of?! Even the elites of the Liyuan Clan could not do this!
"Go to hell!"
Yu Yunze spun even faster and shot out even more swords.
However, this level of attack was futile. It could not even slow down Luo Zheng's speed. Luo Zheng blocked all of them.
Luo Zheng quickly rushed to the top of Yu Yunze. He held a long sword in his hand and looked down at him.
No matter how strong the hurricane was, the hair on his head did not move. The feathers of the Wing King had already made him completely immune to the wind.
"Let me go! I can't die! "Yu Yunze shouted loudly.
In desperation, Yu Yunze could only beg like this.
He was really miserable in his heart …
If it was not for this hurricane, he really had the confidence to fight with Luo Zheng. How would he be at his mercy like this?
"You don't have the chance to regret …" Luo Zheng said indifferently.
He shook his long sword and slashed with the wind.
"Puff …"
As fresh blood bloomed in the sword light, Yu Yunze's corpse floated away with the wind.

Chapter 3042
Main Peak
Feng Ge was originally filled with worry.
After all, she had never seen how Luo Zheng stood in the wind. What if he did not return?
Fortunately, she, Huo Ze, and Lan Qing could sense Luo Zheng's position through their soul imprints.
Luo Zheng was initially traveling with the wind. He seemed normal.
But not long later, Luo Zheng actually started flying against the wind …
"This …" Feng Ge leaned against the corner of the huge pit. He covered his mouth as he looked at Huo Ze and Lan Qing.
There was no need to mention Huo Ze. He was just as shocked as Feng Ge.
Even those experts who had just stepped into the Chaotic Origin Realm would find it hard to withstand such a gale, much less travel against the wind.
How did Luo Zheng do it?
But the soul imprint would not make such a mistake. As the gale blew, Luo Zheng was indeed getting closer.
Lan Qing looked at the expression of her brother and Feng Ge before giggling. She then said, "Big Brother Luo Zheng should have killed those people and only returned now …"
Previously, when something like this happened, Lan Qing and Chou Xue had the same reaction as Feng Ge. Naturally, they could understand Feng Ge's current feelings.
Not long later, Luo Zheng stood above the huge pit. He lightly tunneled in.
Luo Zheng's lightly swaying figure contained an indescribable mystery. When Feng Ge saw this, his eyebrows twitched slightly. He asked, "Those people …"
"They have been killed by me." Luo Zheng nonchalantly said.
To Luo Zheng, the gale was a huge help.
One could say that there were no opponents who could fight against him within the gale.
It had only been half an incense of time since the gale started. Luo Zheng had to rush back and kill those people separately.
In such a short time …
Much less the strength of that Yu Yun Ze. Compared to Chou Xue, Feng Ge and the others were not weak.
Feng Ge and Huo Ze looked at Luo Zheng differently.
Previously, they also felt that Luo Zheng was extremely strong, but they also classified him as Chou Xue and Ye Shang. Perhaps Luo Zheng was a little stronger, but not by much.
And now, given the techniques Luo Zheng displayed, it was extremely easy for him to kill geniuses of this level. His strength had risen by an entire level and was comparable to a Chaotic Origin Expert …
Nevertheless, they were clear that Luo Zheng wasn't at the Chaotic Origin Realm.
Chou Xinghe smiled helplessly and rejoiced in his heart.
Fortunately, Luo Zheng stood on the side of the Heavenly Palace. If this brat was born in Jiuli, or even in Shennong, then he would be an extremely terrifying enemy. If he encountered him, then his ending would not be much better than Yu Yunze.
Previously, Feng Ge and Luo Zheng had fought in the Taishi Palace.
Feng Ge could roughly guess that Luo Zheng became stronger because of that 'Tool of Civilization' in the Dark Domain.
Later on, when Eastern Emperor summoned Feng Ge, he had asked Feng Ge to explain in detail what happened in the Dark Domain. After Feng Ge regained his memories, he remembered everything that happened in the Dark Domain clearly.
This Tool of Civilization chose Luo Zheng and not her.
Feng Ge did not feel any regret. If not for Luo Zheng, he would not have been able to walk out of the Dark Domain.
Furthermore, Feng Ge felt gratified when he saw Luo Zheng's growth.
After the group climbed out of the pit, they continued heading towards the core region.
Along the way, they met several other races. These races glanced at Luo Zheng and the others before carefully avoiding them.
The goal of the majority of living beings was to reach the Chaotic Origin Realm. They would not engage in meaningless battles. There were even some smaller races that sought protection from Luo Zheng, but they were rejected by Luo Zheng.
Some of the smaller races were closely related to Golden Crow You Xiong and the others. Luo Zheng did not wish to have an unstable factor by his side, just like that Mountain Tomb Clansman.
The core region of the Chaotic Origin Great World was a long ring-shaped mountain range.
The brown mountains were like sharp swords that pierced through the sky.
The tallest Main Peak was located at the center of the ring-shaped mountain range. It was more than 10 million meters tall and had almost reached the top of the Chaotic Origin Great World.
"There are several tens of thousands of mountain ranges in the core region. But the majority of the Chaotic Origin Spirits are gathered in the Main Peak and the four surrounding mountains. Of which, the Main Peak has the most Chaotic Origin Spirits." Chou Xue said.
Luo Zheng stood on a rock that was as tall as two people. He looked at that majestic Main Peak before saying: "Then let's directly enter the Main Peak."
Chou Xue shook his head: "Under normal circumstances, we should not consider entering the Main Peak."
"Why is that?" Luo Zheng asked.
Lan Qing and Huo Ze both looked at Chou Xue. Just like Luo Zheng, they knew very little about the Chaotic Origin Great World.
"Chaotic Origin Spirits are Nirvana Lifeforms. They are different from other lifeforms in the Mother World. They are not just independent entities. After devouring each other, they would grow. There is a mother body of a Chaotic Origin Spirit on the Main Peak." Feng Ge explained after Chou Xue.
The mother body was the first pair of Chaotic Origin Spirits that gave birth to the Chaotic Origin Spirits. The longer they stayed in the Mother World, the stronger they would become.
In the early days, there were still people who headed to the Main Peak to excavate the Chaotic Origin Spirits. But as the mother body grew stronger, the Main Peak became more dangerous.
In the recent few decades, very few people headed to the Main Peak to excavate the Chaotic Origin Spirits. After all, it was safer to excavate the Chaotic Origin Spirits from the four surrounding mountains.
"But the majority of the Chaotic Origin Spirits are in the Main Peak. If we don't enter it, wouldn't it be a waste?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
He only knew that there were 200,000 Chaotic Origin Spirits. Every time the Chaotic Origin Great World opened, there would not be enough Chaotic Origin Spirits to distribute.
Chou Xue chuckled: "It is indeed a waste. The Chaotic Origin Great World is so huge. It is impossible to dig out all the Chaotic Origin Spirits every single time. If we even kill the mother body, I'm afraid we would have to send experts to the Nirvana World to fuse with the Chaotic Origin Spirits before allowing them to descend. That is a very troublesome matter."
In reality, every time the Chaotic Origin Great World opened, the number of Chaotic Origin Spirits that were devoured would not exceed 100,000. Furthermore, the number was decreasing year by year.
But the fewer Chaotic Origin Spirits devoured, the faster the Chaotic Origin Spirits would recover to 200,000. That was why the Chaotic Origin Great World only opened once every 15 years. That was because too many Chaotic Origin Spirits had been devoured, causing the recovery speed to be too slow.
Luo Zheng glanced at the Main Peak that stood tall amidst the black fog. Since Chou Xue and Feng Ge had said so, he temporarily did not have any ideas on the Main Peak.
Other than the Main Peak, there were still four other peaks in the surroundings. And since they were heading from east to west, the first one they encountered would naturally be the East Peak.
"Alright! Our target is the East Peak! "Lan Qing extended her arm towards the East Peak and said.
Huo Ze and Lan Qing were confident of stepping into the Chaotic Origin Realm. After all, they had Luo Zheng standing behind them.
The group smiled knowingly. They then headed towards the East Peak.
But when the group of them headed towards the core region, they chose to take a detour towards the north.
Luo Zheng had already noticed that Ling Shuang's figure was initially drifting about. But after that, she remained fixed in the north-west direction. For several hours, Ling Shuang did not move at all. She should be hiding underground and waiting for him. Luo Zheng felt that this method was very smart. Naturally, he would not abandon Ling Shuang and enter the core region.

Chapter 3043
Provoke
After circling around to the north, it was easy to find Ling Shuang's position according to the soul imprint.
Surprisingly, it wasn't a big hole, but a naturally formed karst cave. There was even an underground water vein in the cave.
When they reached the entrance of the cave, Luo Zheng had a strange look on his face.
Logically speaking, Ling Shuang should have been able to sense his presence. After sensing Luo Zheng's soul imprint approaching, Ling Shuang shouldn't have stayed inside the cave. Now that there was no hurricane, she should have come out to meet him in advance.
However, the soul imprint showed that Ling Shuang had been staying in the cave without moving. This was very abnormal.
A bad premonition arose in his heart. Luo Zheng rushed into the cave first.
The moment he entered the cave, a strong smell of blood assailed his nostrils.
His eyebrows suddenly jumped, and he sped up.
The underground water vein was dyed red with blood. Some severed limbs sank into the water. Some belonged to humans, some belonged to aliens.
Chou Xinghe and the others followed behind. Seeing this scene, their faces were somewhat gloomy.
Before this, they had entered similar caves. Except for one member of the Liyuan Clan, the others had all died.
The only good news now was that Ling Shuang's soul imprint still existed. She was alive, but her condition was hard to say.
"Whoosh …"
Luo Zheng was like a bat shuttling through the night. Even though the cave was very complicated, he could still pass through it at an extremely fast speed.
Ling Shuang, please be safe …
Luo Zheng prayed silently in his heart.
After passing through the karst cave for more than a thousand feet, the scene in front of him made Luo Zheng even more frightened.
More than ten disciples of the Heavenly Palace and hundreds of aliens were piled together in a disorderly manner, forming a small mountain of corpses.
"Brother Gao …"
Luo Zheng saw the edge of the mountain of corpses. Gao Qizheng's eyes were wide open.
His body had already stiffened, and the fear on his face was fixed. He had died a miserable death.
"Ye Shang!"
Chou Xinghe, who was following closely behind Luo Zheng, found Ye Shang's body in the mountain of corpses.
Before Luo Zheng entered the Heavenly Palace, Ye Shang and Chou Xun were the two most talented disciples of the Heavenly Palace. Chou Xun and Ye Shang had a good personal relationship. Chou Xun's face was filled with grief when he saw this scene.
He did not expect that with Ye Shang's strength, he would die here before even entering the core area.
Lan Qing looked at Chou Xizhi's sorrowful expression and pursed her lips. She wanted to comfort Chou Xizhi, but she couldn't open her mouth.
Compared to the disciples of the Heavenly Palace, the six people from the Nine Li Tribe were considered lucky.
At the very least, their soul imprints did not disappear. They were still moving around the periphery of the Chaotic Origin Great World. Amongst them, Xian Shi was slowly moving towards them. It was just that his speed was slightly slower due to the hurricane.
Luo Zheng clenches his teeth and pulls out the corpses.
Luo Zheng crudely threw the corpses aside...
After Luo Zheng dug through the mountain of corpses layer by layer, a bloody figure was revealed.
This person was sitting in the same spot. Her long hair was dyed red with blood, and her eyes were tightly shut. She was Ling Shuang.
Above her head was a circle of four-legged snakes. These four-legged snakes formed a circle and occupied the top of her head.
"It's Evil Deities!" Chou Xizhi blurted out when he saw those four-legged snakes.
In another cave, they had seen the exact same four-legged snakes.
Luo Zheng nodded. After inspecting for a while, he let out a sigh of relief.
Ling Shuang was not injured. There were no wounds at all. These four-legged snakes had turned into a living seal, firmly sealing Ling Shuang in this place.
"But how do we remove these four-legged snakes …" Lan Qing frowned.
Previously, when that Li Yuan Tribe member removed the four-legged snakes, he appeared to be in extreme pain. He would rather refine them slowly than let others help him.
"Let me try …" Luo Zheng said.
The four-legged snakes on that Li Yuan Tribe member's body were lying on his wounds. Even though Ling Shuang looked miserable, she was not injured.
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and pinched the four-legged snake. With a light squeeze, this four-legged snake was crushed into pieces.
The circle formed by the black four-legged snakes was broken by Luo Zheng. These four-legged snakes immediately reacted and charged towards Luo Zheng's face.
Luo Zheng reacted quickly. He stretched out his hand and slapped out. Immediately, these four-legged snakes were smashed into pieces, turning into streams of energy.
Following that, Ling Shuang's body trembled. Only then did she wake up. Her breathing became hurried as she retreated. At the same time, she screamed: "You, don't come over. You can't kill me …"
Existences like Feng Ge and Ling Shuang had been bestowed with a powerful Hermit Divine Ability before entering the Chaotic Origin Great World.
But it would only be activated at a critical moment. Ling Shuang was no exception.
No matter how strong that fellow was, when the Hermit Divine Ability Grandma implanted in her was activated, that fellow could only flee miserably.
But Ling Shuang did not expect that that person had no intention of killing her at all.
He had only sealed Ling Shuang in place, and then used the corpses to cover her up.
"It's me! Ling Shuang! I am Luo Zheng! "Luo Zheng shouted.
After being sealed for a period of time, Ling Shuang's soul was suddenly opened. Her consciousness could not connect.
When she ascertained the identity of the person in front of her, she bit her lips and pounced towards Luo Zheng …
Feng Ge revealed a strange expression when he saw this. Compared to his encounter, Ling Shuang was clearly more unlucky.
"It's that fellow in your inner world." Ling Shuang said with an aggrieved expression.
"I know." Luo Zheng nodded.
After a long while, Ling Shuang finally calmed down.
"The Evil Deities killed so many people. Why did they spare Her Highness Ling Shuang?" Chou Xinghe asked in confusion.
That Li Yuan Clansman had relied on his own strength to escape. It was not because of the kindness of the Evil Deities.
Ling Shuang was also puzzled …
She had already used all her strength against the Evil Deities. She had even brought out the Paramita Talisman, the Twin Flowers, and executed her Taiyi Sword Technique to its limits. Yet, she was still not a match for this person.
Ling Shuang did not think that she could survive.
"It's a provocation." Luo Zheng stared at Ling Shuang.
At the entrance of the Chaotic Origin Great World, Ling Shuang stood beside Luo Zheng. Naturally, the Evil Deities could see everything clearly.
Perhaps it was because of this that the Evil Deities did not intend to kill Ling Shuang. Instead, they sealed her under this pile of corpses.
Such an action could only be a provocation …
"Truly perverted." Feng Ge said with a frown.
Luo Zheng's eyes were filled with killing intent. It was getting denser and denser. He silently helped Ling Shuang clean up, removing the bloodstains on her body.
Even though he did not say anything, those around him could feel his deep killing intent.
In this Chaotic Origin Great World, Luo Zheng would most likely have a battle with the Evil Deities.
But thinking of that fellow's strength, Chou Xinghe and the others felt deeply worried.
After cleaning up the bloodstains, Chou Xinghe kept the corpses of Ye Shang and the other Heaven Palace disciples into his spatial ring. Encountering an unreasonable existence like the Evil Deities was an unexpected calamity.

Chapter 3044
"Ding!"
The remaining four members of the Nine Li Tribe noticed that Luo Zheng, Huo Ze, and Lan Qing had gathered together before rushing towards the Eastern Peak.
Fortunately, most of them had safely passed through the outer perimeter of the Chaotic Origin Great World and were not far from the core region. Even Xian Shi, who was the furthest away from the Great World, only needed to go around the ring-shaped mountain range to reach the core region.
Currently, all the superpowers that entered the Chaotic Origin Great World were also gathering.
You Xiong, Shennong, Golden Crow, and other clans gathered at the Western Peak. The Li Yuan Clan gathered at the Northern Peak. Some smaller clans chose to gather at the Southern Peak.
When Luo Zheng brought Ling Shuang, Feng Ge, and others to the Eastern Peak, more than ten Ear Mice suddenly came out from a huge hole by the side.
These Ear Mice were half the height of a human. Every Ear Mouse had a thick aura. Their bean-sized black eyes were flashing with shrewdness.
"Sir Luo Zheng, this one is Hui Chen. We have been waiting for you for a long time." The leader of the Ear Mice came up. He raised his paw and cupped his hands towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng swept his gaze across these Ear Mice. These Ear Mice seemed to have come prepared. He then asked: "You are waiting for me here?"
"That's right." Hui Chen replied.
"How did you find my location?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Under the hurricane, everyone's location was constantly changing. Luo Zheng naturally felt puzzled.
"Zhi, this …" Hui Chen placed his paw by his mouth and blew a whistle. Not far away, tiny mice were popping their heads out from several fist-sized holes. The Ear Mice laughed, "The Chaotic Origin Great World is not a forbidden zone for living beings. Some Rock Mice have to listen to us."
Due to the nature of the Ear Mice, their individual strength might not be comparable to some of the bigger clans. But their intelligence gathering was unparalleled.
The Rock Mice in the Chaotic Origin Great World were also their eyes and ears.
"So that's the case …" Luo Zheng revealed a relieved expression. At the same time, he asked: "May I know why you are looking for me?"
Luo Zheng had also cooperated with the Ear Mice in the Nirvana Realm. Although the exploration of the Temple of Sky was a failure and even caused the Ear Mice to suffer a great loss, the responsibility was not Luo Zheng's. Moreover, Luo Zheng had even escorted the souls of the Ear Mice who had transformed into infant forms back.
"We would like to invite Sir Luo Zheng to explore the East Peak together," Hui Chen said unhurriedly.
In the Eared Mouse Clan, Luo Zheng was someone worth roping in.
Although they could not prove the relationship between "Tian Xing" and Luo Zheng, from the current evidence, the two were very likely to be the same person. Even if they were not, they should be inextricably linked.
Luo Zheng, Chou Xue, and Feng Ge looked at each other. Feng Ge, Chou Xue, and Ling Shuang nodded slightly.
The Ear Mouse was a race that could not be ignored. Moreover, the information they possessed was extremely useful. It would be much more convenient to travel with them.
But Hui Chen was slightly worried that Luo Zheng would reject them. Her pair of yellow bean eyes stared closely at Luo Zheng.
"It is my honor to travel with you all," Luo Zheng replied.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the Ear Mice heaved a sigh of relief.
The Rock Mice living in the Chaotic Origin Great World were very weak. When the hurricane blew, they did not dare to appear. When Luo Zheng killed the Beehive Demoness and You Xiong Tribe's Jade Cloud Ze, the Rock Mice could not see it with their own eyes.
But by comparing the clues before and after, the Ear Mice could easily make the correct judgment.
But this judgment left the Ear Mice puzzled. How did Luo Zheng kill them when the hurricane blew?
What made the Ear Mice even more inconceivable was that Luo Zheng could actually return to his original spot after killing them. That meant that Luo Zheng had returned while facing the hurricane.
This mystery made Hui Chen and the other Ear Mice feel an unbearable itch. But it was too rude to ask about such a secret directly. Luo Zheng might not be willing to tell them. Hui Chen could only endure it.
Perhaps they might have a chance to find out the reason behind it.
Just as the group was about to set off, Luo Zheng asked: "It is indeed convenient for you all to have the Rock Mice as your eyes and ears. I would like to inquire about the movements of two people. I wonder if it is possible for you to inform me?"
Hui Chen gave a 'zhi zhi' chuckle, "Sir Luo Zheng wants to investigate the movements of Luo Yan and Evil Deities, right?"
These Ear Mice were truly astute. They naturally knew where the two people Luo Zheng was most concerned about were. They even knew the black-robed young man's name, Evil Deities.
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded.
"Li Shan clearly knows about the anomaly in the Chaotic Origin Great World. He is prepared for this violent hurricane. They have already entered the Western Peak." Hui Chen answered, "That Evil Deities is in the North."
"He wants to enter the Northern Peak?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows.
Hui Chen's rat-like face revealed an indescribable expression, "No, he wants to kill."
The Ear Mice had never seen such a bloodthirsty person. Evil Deities was too lazy to excavate the Chaotic Origin Spirit. The only thing he did in the Chaotic Origin Great World was to kill. The problem was that no one could stop him.
Prince Wolf Gu Ke from the Celestial Wolf Clan, Princess Jiu Xuan from the Dream Ling Clan, Son of Heaven Han from the Color Eye Clan … all of them died in his hands.
These people were all top-notch geniuses from those huge clans. After killing so many of them, he did not stop. And now, he was slaughtering the Li Yuan Clansmen …
But to the Ear Mice, this was an advantage.
Evil Deities had killed many of his competitors. And the Ear Mice could avoid this fiend using the Rock Mice. They were safe.
"The Li Yuan Clansmen are extremely strong. This fellow …" Luo Zheng frowned.
Individually, the Li Yuan Clansmen had the best life force, strength, and fleshy body. This clan was tyrannical and powerful. It could not be ignored.
"But the current situation of the Li Yuan Clansmen is not good." Hui Chen shook her head.
…
…
Beneath the Northern Peak …
Fresh blood flowed along the cracks of the rocks like a small stream.
Every Li Yuan Clansman was 30 meters tall. They were like little giants. After the bodies of ten Li Yuan Clansmen were torn apart, their blood flowed like a river.
Halfway up the mountain, there were four Li Yuan Clansmen standing there. These four Li Yuan Clansmen were geniuses amongst geniuses. Regardless of whether it was the sacred arts they cultivated, their fleshy bodies, or their Paramita Talismans, they were all top-notch existences.
The four members of the Liyuan Clan stared fixedly at the Heretic God floating in mid-air. The leader of the four asked coldly, "Our Liyuan Clan has no enmity with you. Why are you slaughtering us like this?"
"Why?" Evil Deities's face was filled with killing intent. He smiled, "Because I want to hear a 'ding' sound. That sound is very beautiful, and it excites me."
What was a 'ding' sound?
The four Li Yuan Clansmen looked at each other. They could not understand Evil Deities's words.
"That is the sound of a reward, as well as the sound of becoming stronger! Hahaha … "As Evil Deities spoke, he suddenly waved his hands. A black world spread out, enveloping the four Li Yuan Clansmen within.
These four Li Yuan Clansmen were not to be underestimated. Ordinary methods were unable to deal with them. Evil Deities had no choice but to use his trump card.

Chapter 3045
Nothing.
The ring-shaped mountain range beneath the North Peak opened up to both sides, forming a natural mountain path.
On the mountain path, Luo Zheng stopped the team.
Through his soul imprint, he could sense Xian Shi and the other two Nine Li Tribesmen approaching.
About three incense sticks later, the three Nine Li Tribesmen joined the team. As for the final person, he did not get close. Instead, he seemed to have been blown away by the wind and was located 50,000 kilometers away.
"There's no need to wait for Huo Ting. Let's go." Huo Ze made his decision.
The Chaotic Origin Great World was a place where one could reach the Chaotic Origin Realm based on their capabilities. With so many of them, it was impossible for them to wait for a single person.
Hui Chen raised her claws and lightly blew out a whistle. Several hundred Rock Rats tunneled out from both sides of the mountain path. These Rock Rats obeyed and tunneled into the cavern beneath the North Peak.
With these Rock Rats scouting ahead, not only could they sense danger, they could also detect the location of the Chaotic Origin Spirits. It was extremely convenient.
Hence, all the great clans loved to travel with the Eared Rat Clan. But this time, the Eared Rat Clan had found Luo Zheng.
The cavern within the North Peak was very wide. There was a flat path that spiraled upwards.
Luo Zheng walked at the front. He extended his divine sense in all directions.
When he encountered some exposed rocks and stone pillars, Luo Zheng and the others would simply blast them away.
"Hong, hong …"
After several attempts, they were all blasted apart. There was no Chaotic Origin Spirit here.
"Zhi zhi … Sir Luo Zheng, the Rock Rats might seem weak, but they can bite through most rocks. Those that they can't bite through naturally contain Chaotic Origin Spirits." Hui Chen reminded.
Luo Zheng swept his gaze across the surrounding rock walls. Indeed, he discovered some tiny teeth marks. The rocks in this area should have been scoured by the Rock Rats.
Of course, Luo Zheng might not believe the words of the Eared Rat completely.
If they discovered the Chaotic Origin Spirit, they could simply keep it a secret and come back to take it away.
"Didn't someone say that most of the Chaotic Origin Spirits are gathered in the five mountain peaks?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Hui Chen revealed a puzzled look, "Logically speaking, that should be the case. Even at the entrance, there should be many Chaotic Origin Spirits occupying it. Yet this year, it seems like something is amiss …"
The speed at which the Rock Rats were searching for Chaotic Origin Spirits was very fast. They would move along the cavern and gnaw on the rocks. Very few fishes would slip through their net. According to logic, they should be facing some stronger Chaotic Origin Spirits by now. Yet until now, they had yet to find any.
The group spiraled upwards along the cave. Even after reaching a height of 30,000 meters, they still could not find the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
The situation was getting weirder and weirder …
Luo Zheng's team wasn't the only one who noticed this.
It was the same for the races that explored the North Peak, West Peak, and South Peak.
According to the usual practice, they should have a bountiful harvest after entering the four mountain peaks. Yet now, they did not even get a single Chaotic Origin Spirit. This was too abnormal.
When everyone climbed all the way to the top of the mountain through the caves, their gazes turned to the main peak in the center, which was like a sharp sword.
"The only possibility is that all the Chaotic Origin Spirits are gathered at the Master Peak." Hui Chen said to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng looked up at the incomparably tall Master Peak, "Then we can only enter the Master Peak?"
"During these years, very few people have stepped into the Master Peak. Currently, all the Chaotic Origin Spirits are hiding at the Master Peak. I wonder what kind of monster would be formed after fusing with them." Chou Xinghe said.
Even though Chou Xinghe had a frivolous temperament, he was a very cautious person.
Entering the Chaotic Origin Realm was a path that experts had to take. But it was unwise to throw away their lives for this. Given their lifespan, it was fine even if they had to wait another ten years.
"It is impossible for them to retreat just like that right?" Huo Ze frowned.
There were not many opportunities in the Chaotic Origin Great World. They had traveled far and wide before finally stepping into this place. Why would they be willing to give up so easily?
"I believe the other races won't give up so easily either." Luo Zheng looked up at the black fog in the sky. He then said: "Let's avoid the hurricane first. We'll know in a while when we enter …"
Those races had finally reached the core region. Naturally, they would not leave so easily. Even if the Master Peak was dangerous, they wanted to see what was inside.
Nevertheless, the outside of the Chaotic Origin Great World was in an uproar.
The core disciples and geniuses of these super organizations had absolute protection. Almost all of them had powerful hidden divine abilities. Yet these outstanding existences had all died in the Chaotic Origin Great World.
"Who killed our Crown Prince!" The leader of the Celestial Wolf Race had bloodshot eyes. He bared his teeth as if he was about to devour someone.
"The Son of Heaven Han is a genius that only appears once in ten thousand eras. How could he die!"
"Impossible …"
This time, too many clansmen had died in the Chaotic Origin Great World. Furthermore, all of them were peak existences. This did not make sense.
It was hard to say for the other clansmen. According to logic, these geniuses had a high chance of reaching the Chaotic Origin Realm. Yet two or three of them actually died …
The leader of the Celestial Wolf Race suddenly roared towards the sky, "Li Shan must give us an explanation!"
"Right! What exactly is going on with this hurricane in the Chaotic Origin Great World! "
"That time when we constructed the Chaotic Origin Great World, it was for the sake of competition. Since the casualties are so severe, we might as well split the Chaotic Origin Spirits one by one!"
The Chaotic Origin Spirits could be controlled. It was precisely because of the uneven distribution that the Chaotic Origin Great World was constructed. On one hand, it was to allow the clansmen to gain experience. On the other hand, they could distribute the Chaotic Origin Spirits according to their strength.
But this time, the number of people who died had exceeded what these leaders could bear.
"Zhi, those people were killed by that black-robed young man who came down from the black ship." The leader of the Eared Mouse Race said.
Other than Li Shan, the Eared Mouse Race was the clearest about the Chaotic Origin Great World. They had their own unique channels to obtain information.
"Black ship …"
"That woman Shen Tu is still outside the Drinking Snow Prefecture?"
"I'll go and negotiate with her!"
"What's the point of negotiating when they are already dead? Wait till that brat comes out. I'll tear him into pieces! "The leader of the Li Yuan Race said with widened eyes. He could not wait to crush Evil Deities into powder.
At this moment, a voice drifted over.
"There will only be more deaths. Those who survive will benefit immensely. You can only hope that the remaining clansmen can survive and reach the Chaotic Origin Realm." Fuxi's figure once again appeared at the center of the empty ground.
"Human Emperor, what do you mean by that?" The old man from the You Xiong Clan asked.
Yu Yun Ze's death had caused them great heartache. Even though the Eared Mouse Race knew that Yu Yun Ze died in the hands of Luo Zheng, they still had the intention to side with him. Hence, they directly blamed it on Evil Deities.
"You can go back and ask your Patriarch." Fuxi nonchalantly said: "This should be the last time the Chaotic Origin Great World opens."
The factions in the Mother World became clearer amidst the chaos.
The civilizations on the other shore were also in an uproar. The Primal Chaos Ancient Gods had also understood their standpoint after several hundred billion years of thinking.

Chapter 3046
Protective Array
Hearing Fuxi's words, the expressions of the leaders from the various great clans changed.
These people were the core strength of the various great clans, but they were ultimately not at the level of the spokesperson.
They were aware of the situation in the Mother World, but they did not expect it to happen so quickly …
Unless they could not even wait for the next ten years?
The speed at which the Sealed Stone was leaking out from the Hourglass of Time was getting faster and faster. Even Phoenix and the others were investigating this matter. But no matter how fast they were, it was impossible for them to compress the time within ten years.
It's just that they had forgotten that some things happened at an accelerated speed.
When it reached a critical point, it would naturally erupt …
And the recent events in the Paramita had triggered this critical point.
Phoenix and the others were still puzzled. Before they set off, Jinwu Patriarch Di Jun and You Xiong Clan Patriarch Di Hongshi had yet to return from the Paramita. They were completely unaware of what had happened in the upper echelons of the Paramita.
But Fuxi did not have the obligation to answer them. Many of these clans were standing against Li Shan.
He did not care about them. Instead, he disappeared in front of everyone.
At this moment, the leader of a great clan pointed to the image of the central array and said, "Someone has entered the core region. It is … someone from Li Shan!"
There was a vast region at the center of the ring-shaped mountain range. In this region, other than the central main peak, there were a hundred circular arrays scattered around.
In order to refine the Chaotic Origin Spirits, one had to enter the circular arrays. These arrays were set up by the experts from the Nine Li Tribe and other superpowers. The moment one stepped into the circular arrays, he would be in an absolutely safe region. No one could attack the person within the array.
If it was in the past, those living beings would think of ways to charge into the array after obtaining sufficient Chaotic Origin Spirits.
Entering the array meant that one could reach the Chaotic Origin Realm.
But it wasn't so easy to enter the array. Many experts had failed to obtain sufficient Chaotic Origin Spirits due to various reasons. Hence, they would guard by the side of the array and snatch the Chaotic Origin Spirits. Hence, during the process of charging into the array, a battle would definitely break out.
There were even some clans that worked together to form an impenetrable defense around the array. Even though such methods were despicable, they still complied with the rules.
The group of cultivators from Li Mountain were the most prepared. They were also the first batch of cultivators to enter the Chaotic Origin Great World. But their harvest was similarly negligible. After exploring the Western Summit for a while, they did not find anything. And now, they came down from the Western Summit and headed towards the Main Peak.
Luo Yan walked in the front. Before entering the main peak, she looked around the ring-shaped mountain range and a trace of disappointment appeared on her small face.
"Sister Yan 'er, why are you hesitating?" A woman beside Luo Yan asked.
This woman's beautiful hair was coiled up with a dozen golden hairpins. If one were to observe carefully, one would realize that these golden hairpins were actually Nirvana tokens!
The descent of the Nirvana Token consumed a lot of divine crystals, but these golden hairpins were in a state of descent all the time.
Her name was Ling Yun, and she was considered one of the most outstanding humans in Mount Li. She was the first friend Luo Yan made after entering Mount Li, and the two of them were as close as sisters.
"The Prime Peak has nine caves and 18 meridians. Each of them is dangerous. Even if we came prepared, it isn't easy for us to get a Chaotic Origin Spirit. Big Brother, he …" Luo Yan said with pursed lips.
After entering the Chaotic Origin Great World, Luo Yan had attempted to find Luo Zheng.
But the hurricane was too strong, and the Chaotic Origin Great World was so vast. It was truly hard to find traces of him.
If she had known, she would have left behind a soul imprint. Luo Yan was still too confident. She felt a strand of regret in her heart.
"Sister Yan 'er has always treated big brother as a deity. Yet now, you are starting to worry about him?" Ling Yun asked with a smile.
Luo Yan had mentioned Luo Zheng's glorious deeds many times. In front of Ling Yun, she had even described Luo Zheng as an existence that could do anything. But Ling Yun did not completely agree with it.
There were too many geniuses in this world. Just in Mount Li, the Nuwa Clan and the Human Race, there were geniuses who were unique in all aspects. And Luo Zheng was merely a person who walked out from some unknown corner.
Such a person might be number one in that small corner. He might be an unparalleled existence. But in the Origin World, he was not worth mentioning.
At the entrance of the Chaotic Origin Great World, Luo Zheng actually argued with Phoenix. That showed how confident he was. Indeed, he was worthy of Ling Yun's respect. But that did not represent his strength. After all, Luo Zheng had the backing of the Heavenly Palace. Whether it was Qiu Yinhe or Lin Zhanting, they were experts who had been famous for a long time.
But Fuxi had stepped out to protect that brat. He had even declared that Luo Zheng was his opponent. This left Ling Yun puzzled.
In the end, what made Ling Yun even more speechless was that the 'Soul Devouring Devil' that came down from the black ship was actually born in Luo Zheng's inner world. Ling Yun's heart was once again shaken. The image of Luo Yan's Big Brother became mysterious and lofty …
The only flaw was that that 'Soul Devouring Devil' did not listen to Luo Zheng. He even threatened to kill Luo Zheng.
"I am always afraid of accidents …" Luo Yan shook his head.
"Your Big Brother is such a formidable character. He will definitely have a way. He is not the only one entering the Main Peak. Everyone will eventually enter the Main Peak. Don't think too much." Ling Yun advised.
Even though the Main Peak had nine paths and 18 branched paths, there would definitely be many competitors entering the Main Peak. Their Li Mountain would naturally have the advantage of being the first to enter.
Waiting here would be a waste of time and a great opportunity.
"Mn …"
Luo Yan nodded reluctantly. Only then did he lead the living beings of Li Mountain into the Main Peak.
…
Not long after the people of Li Mountain entered the Main Peak, the hurricane started blowing again.
The hurricane blew across the entire Chaotic Origin Great World. It revolved around the core region and the wind became stronger.
When the hurricane stopped, several teams appeared. They were from You Xiong, Golden Crow, and Divine Farmer Clan. The leader had a head of silver hair. He carried a spear and walked at the front. That was the number one genius of You Xiong Clan, Di An.
They did not stay for long. They crossed the plains and found a cave beneath the Main Peak before entering it.
After Luo Zheng's group failed to explore the Eastern Peak, they retreated from the Eastern Peak with the Eared Mouse Clan. They crossed the plains from another side and headed towards the Main Peak.
As they passed by those circular arrays on the plains, Luo Zheng stopped and observed them. He asked: "These great arrays are used to refine the Chaotic Origin Spirit?"
"Zhi! Right! These great arrays can not only refine the Chaotic Origin Spirit, they can also be used as a temporary shelter. "Eared Mouse replied.
The moment they entered the great arrays, they would be absolutely safe. But every great array could only accommodate a limited number of people. Only about ten people could enter.
"Big Brother Luo Zheng, why don't you stay here and refine the Chaotic Origin Spirit!" Lan Qing suddenly suggested.
The Chaotic Origin Spirit they obtained from that cave was sufficient for one person to reach the Chaotic Origin Realm. Lan Qing was very willing to contribute her portion of the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
If Luo Zheng reached the Chaotic Origin Realm, his strength would increase tremendously too. He would not have to be afraid of that black-robed young man. This was what Lan Qing thought.

Chapter 3047
Stalagmite
These arrays had three functions. Other than helping to refine the Chaotic Origin Spirits, they could also protect some weaker beings. The final function was the teleportation array.
The strength of a Chaotic Origin Expert was on a completely different level from that of an ordinary Chaotic Origin Expert.
In order to prevent others from coming out after completing the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction, the moment they reached the Chaotic Origin Realm, they would be teleported out.
Hui Chen was aware that Luo Zheng had gathered enough Chaotic Origin Spirits. But she did not say anything. It was Luo Zheng's choice whether he was willing to leave or not.
"Naturally not." Luo Zheng shook his head.
Luo Zheng was the core member of this group. If he were to leave, relying on Chou Xue to lead the team to explore the Main Peak?
The risk was too great. It was impossible for him to do so …
Feng Ge and Ling Shuang had a look of understanding. Given their understanding of Luo Zheng, it was impossible for Luo Zheng to abandon these people.
Before the next hurricane arrived, Luo Zheng and the group arrived at the foot of the Main Peak.
This Main Peak looked like a slender mountain from afar. In reality, the area at the bottom of the mountain was not small. There were two caves facing the east of the Main Peak. One cave led upwards and the other led downwards.
Currently, at the entrance of the cave, Luo Zheng took a glance before choosing the path leading upwards.
The Eared Mouse Clan had always been cautious. At the moment they stepped into the Main Peak, Hui Chen once again drove those Rock Rats into the cave.
The cave in the Main Peak was narrow at first. But after walking for a few dozen meters or so, it opened up. The space within was much bigger than what they had imagined.
Everyone felt slightly nervous. They released their divine senses to scan the interior of the cave.
But this cave did not have any aura of life. It was like a barren land.
"This Main Peak doesn't seem to be any different from the East Peak." Huo Ze said gloomily.
Huo Ze walked to the side of several rocks. He tried smashing those rocks but to no avail.
Luo Zheng, Chou Xue, Ling Shuang, Feng Ge, and the others turned solemn. If they could not find any Chaotic Origin Spirits in the Main Peak, where else could they go?
Could it be that everyone had come here for nothing?
"There are still some differences." Hui Chen said.
"What's the difference?" Luo Zheng asked.
Hui Chen pointed to the depths of the cave and said, "The group of rock rats that I sent out to scout the way have all been wiped out."
"Did the Chaotic Origin Spirit devour those Rock Rats?" Chou Xue asked.
"Or did other species enter the main peak before us, and the rock rats were killed by them?" Feng Ge asked.
What they were most concerned about was whether there was a Chaotic Origin Spirit here.
"I'm not sure." Hui Chen shook her head. "There's a very strange power up there. When the rock rats enter the range of that power, they no longer listen to me. I have no way of knowing what they've discovered."
