The Rock Rats were not strong. Hence, it was normal for them to be devoured by some powerful Chaotic Origin Spirits.
Luo Zheng took the lead. The others and the Ear Mice lowered their footsteps and followed closely behind him. After traveling for several kilometers, Hui Chen reminded them: "The Rock Rats disappeared from here. Sir Luo Zheng, you must be careful."
Luo Zheng looked over. He saw many sharp stalagmites erected in the cave ahead. These stalagmites were densely packed together. They were like the fangs of some monster.
Luo Zheng squinted his eyes. His figure flew up and stuck close to one of the sharp stalagmites. He lightly placed his palm on the stalagmite. With a light push, a soft "bo" sound could be heard before this stalagmite turned into powder.
"Nothing." Luo Zheng shook his head. He then placed his palm on another sharp stalagmite. Using the same action, this stalagmite turned into powder. There was still no Chaotic Origin Spirit.
Huo Ze and the others were filled with disappointment. After ascertaining that there was no Chaotic Origin Spirit, they were no longer nervous. They followed behind Luo Zheng and started smashing those sharp stalagmites. But just like Luo Zheng, they did not find anything.
"Strange. If there is nothing here, why did the Rock Rats I released disappear?" Hui Chen said with a gloomy look.
There must be something strange about this cave. Other than exploring deeper, there was no other solution.
They followed behind Luo Zheng and bypassed those sharp stalagmites.
Just as they walked for a distance, a "rumbling" sound could be heard from behind them.
Everyone turned back. The sharp stalagmites that were smashed apart had regrown into small sharp stalagmites. Furthermore, these stalagmites were constantly growing. As they intertwined with each other, they completely sealed off their path of retreat. It was like a monster closing its fangs!
'Rocks can grow by themselves? What is going on? Let me go and have a look … 'Huo Ze decided to head over after seeing this scene.
Just as Huo Ze took a step forward, many fist-sized stalagmites sprouted out from all around the cave. These stalagmites were growing towards the center of the cave.
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly. With a flip of his hand, he sent a sword light towards those stalagmites that were growing.
"Ding!"
The stalagmites remained undamaged! Chou Xinghe and the others paled when they saw this!
"Huo Ze, come back! Everyone … run upwards! "
After realizing that something was amiss, Luo Zheng did not hesitate. He pulled towards Huo Ze, causing an unseen force to pull Huo Ze back from a distance of several dozen meters. Everyone began rushing forward.
If it were ordinary rocks, it would be fine even if they were sharper. Ordinary rocks could be smashed apart with a single slap. They could not harm them at all.
But if these stalagmites contained Chaotic Origin Spirits, it was different. They could not destroy these stalagmites in a short period. If they were drawn into them, the only outcome would be having their bodies pierced through by these stalagmites.
"Sou sou sou sou sou sou …"
Densely packed stalagmites sprouted out from all around the cave. Without a place to land, everyone could only run on the ground.

Over ten stalagmites had already grown half a chi tall at that narrow area. It would not take long for the cave to be completely sealed. They had to rush over before that happened!
"Sou!"
Feng Ge, Chou Xinghe, and Ling Shuang were the first to rush over. Following them were Luo Zheng, Hui Chen, and several other Nine Li Tribesmen.
Lan Qing, Huo Ze, and Xian Shi were slightly slower. Naturally, they fell behind.
When Luo Zheng arrived at this narrow area, he frowned. Both his hands and legs stepped on a sharp stalagmite. A vast energy burst out. He actually stopped these sharp stalagmites from extending further!

The stalagmites sprouted out from the stone wall. They exploded with a frightening strength. Luo Zheng could only slow them down slightly. He could not stop them completely.

Their bodies were small and nimble. Even if a stalagmite suddenly pierced through, they could easily pass through it.

This was equivalent to someone thrusting a sword at her with the strength of several thousand Godly Jun. It was just that the speed was not that fast.

Through normal means, the creatures of the Nirvana Realm could not descend into the mother world.
But the Chaotic Origin Spirit was unique. After it was discovered by Nüwa, she carefully nurtured it until it reached its current scale.

Chapter 3051

Furthermore, they seemed to have reached the end of this cavern. Even though there were eight more tunnels around this cavern, these eight tunnels were all leading downwards. Those tunnels should be connected to the other eight entrances at the bottom of the Main Peak.
"The Spirit of Chaotic Origin must be hidden above. We can break the walls and find a way out." Feng Ge said.
The Spirit of Chaotic Origin could freely cut through the rocks and enter and exit the rocks. With their strength, it was not hard for them to forcefully open a path. Feng Ge's suggestion was feasible.
Just as Luo Zheng, Chou Xue and the others were considering, another group of people walked in from another tunnel.
They were from You Xiong and Golden Crow Clans.
When they saw Luo Zheng's group, they were slightly startled. The silver-haired young man named Di An, who was carrying a long spear, narrowed his eyes slightly.
"Interesting, I was a little depressed along the way. Why didn't I meet the two of you? I didn't expect to meet you here, hehe …" Di An smiled faintly and gently squeezed the spear in his hand. The spear suddenly shook and wisps of white light curled up along the spear's body.
At the same time, Min Yue and the members of the Divine Farmer Clan also appeared in another tunnel.
The man named Shan from the Divine Farmer Clan was standing in the front, while the curly-haired woman named Mumu was still sitting on Shan's shoulder.
When they saw Luo Zheng and Feng Ge, they also showed a strong interest.
"Uncle, are we going to make a move now? Two people in the first gear appeared here at the same time … "Mumu said in a low voice. Her face was hidden behind her curly hair, so no one could see her expression clearly.
Shan Yi narrowed his eyes and sized up Luo Zheng and Feng Ge.
Other than fusing with the Spirit of Chaotic Origin, killing the prey in the first gear was also very important.
If they had obtained enough Spirits of Chaotic Origin, they would not hesitate to make a move on Luo Zheng immediately.
But their group was also searching for those Spirits of Chaotic Origin that had disappeared. When they arrived at the central region of the Main Peak, they also felt that this cave was weird.
"Shan" was a calm and prudent person. He would not act rashly before he was fully prepared.
"Let's wait a little longer. Everyone's target is still the Spirits of Chaotic Origin. We can't possibly return empty-handed," Shan Yi said while looking at the carvings around the cave. These strange carvings also attracted Shan Yi's attention.
"Even though the Spirits of Chaotic Origin are important, killing this group of trash from the Heavenly Palace is more important. If you are not willing to make a move, I'll do it." Di An was not as worried and patient as "Shan". His eyes were flashing with excitement. The spear on his shoulder was moving restlessly like a live snake.
When Feng Ge, Ling Shuang, Chou Xing and the others heard this, their expressions turned gloomy and vigilant.
They did not have the advantage in terms of numbers. The other party was also an outstanding expert among those superpowers. If they were to make a move, the Heavenly Palace would most likely suffer.
Luo Zheng slowly took a step forward, and his eyes became indifferent. "In my opinion, people who like to talk about trash are mostly trash that can't even withstand a single blow. If you want to prove yourself, then there's no need to be so quick with your words!"
Di An did not place Luo Zheng in his eyes. In the eyes of Di An, Shan and Mumu, the only person worthy of their vigilance was that black-robed young man. As for Luo Zheng and Feng Ge, they were merely their prey.
They originally thought that the disciples of the Great Yi Heavenly Palace were weak and did not dare to provoke them. They could only restrain themselves from ridiculing them.
Who would have thought that Luo Zheng would actually take the initiative to stand out.
"What a surprise. After all these years, the Great Yi Heavenly Palace has finally produced someone with backbone." Di An slowly lowered his white spear from his shoulder. With a light flick of his spear, the rocks on the ground were sliced apart like tofu. One could see how sharp it was.
"Backbone?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly, and the tip of the Snow Sword also slowly emerged from the Sumeru Ring. Luo Zheng held it in his hand and pointed it at You Xiong and the Golden Crow Clan, saying, "It has nothing to do with backbone. It's just that when I killed Yu Yunze, I said that You Xiong, Golden Crow, Minyue and Shennong, four clans.
Don't even think about leaving here alive! "
After Luo Zheng finished speaking, the Instrument of Civilization hidden in his inner world suddenly rotated. The fully unfolded Instrument of Civilization was a spiky ball. Although its appearance was not amazing, the unique aura of the Green Jade civilization was truly released. Everyone present was shocked.
"Arrogant!"
"You're courting death!"
"You're forcing us to attack!"
The clansmen of the four clans, including You Xiong and Golden Crow, retorted.
The expressions of Di An, Shan, Mumu and the other clansmen also changed slightly.
They still underestimated this guy!
After all, the black-robed young man was born from this person's inner world. How could this person's strength be underestimated?
But these proud sons of heaven had high aspirations and great luck. Even when facing all kinds of difficulties, they could overcome them with great perseverance and great opportunities. How could they be willing to back down?
Furthermore, they had more people. Their chances of winning were higher.
"Hu …"
Seeing that the battle was about to start.
"Da da da …"
Following a series of messy footsteps, another team came from the third passage.
"Brother!"
Luo Yan led the Nine Li Clansmen up from the bottom passage.
They were originally the first to enter the Main Peak. But Luo Yan had wasted quite a bit of time chasing after several Spirits of Chaotic Origin. Hence, they ended up lagging behind.
Seeing Luo Yan safe and sound, Luo Zheng revealed a joyous expression. He quickly went forward.
Li Shan had always been the one with the most number of people entering the Chaotic Origin Great World. And Li Shan had the advantage of being the first to enter. Even if they were torn apart by the hurricane, they could still quickly gather together.

Chapter 3052
Behind Luo Yan, six Nuwa Clan women stood out. They stood in a staggered formation facing You Xiong and the Divine Farmer Clan.
These six Nuwa Clan women had snake scales that flickered with a dark golden light. Their exquisite appearance revealed a hint of vigilance.
The position they stood in was filled with mysteries. They faintly formed the 'Six Emotions Snake Shadow Array'. They were the Six Snake Shadow Lodges that Li Shan nurtured in this generation.
Li Shan's Six Snake Shadow Lodges were very famous in the Mother World. They would select the most outstanding genius from every generation of Nuwa Clan and train them as one of the Six Snake Shadow Lodges.
This time, the Six Snake Shadow Lodges had not only to reach the Chaotic Origin Realm, they also had a mission to protect Luo Yan. Luo Yan was not weaker than the geniuses from the various superpowers. But Li Shan valued her too much and could not afford to lose her.
After Luo Yan met up with her brother, she immediately turned her head. Her face, which was originally full of smiles, was now covered with a layer of frost. Under her thin eyebrows, there was a kind of appearance that was not angry but dignified.
"You dare to act against my brother?" Luo Yan stared at Di An and questioned.
Di An snorted, "So what? Unless Li Shan wants to interfere too? "
The addition of Li Shan had made You Xiong and Shennong more vigilant. But it was not enough to make them cower. Nevertheless, the situation had become troublesome.
"Shan" frowned slightly.
He was not afraid of fighting with Li Shan and the disciples of the Heaven Palace. But this place was not suitable.
The sculptures in this cavern were emitting a strange aura. He did not know where the Chaotic Origin Spirit was hidden. Even if You Xiong and Shennong defeated the other party, it would be a pyrrhic victory. That Chaotic Origin Spirit might be snatched by other living beings first.
Luo Yan coldly glanced at Di An. Strands of red light revolved around her. Her long black hair danced in the wind, "Even though this place is not suitable for battle, if you really want to fight, Li Shan can only accompany you."
As soon as her voice faded, the bodies of the six snake shadows glowed with cyan light as well. The Nirvana power of the other Nuwa clansmen also surged out like tides.
Di An hadn't expected that the little girl from Li Mountain would be so decisive. He had no choice but to weigh the price in his heart …
"Pa."
Mountain walked to the side of Di An and lightly patted him. He then said: "Finding the Chaotic Origin Spirit is more important. Now is not the time to act."
Di An was not only strong, but he also cared a lot about his reputation. It was impossible for him to admit defeat in front of a group of women from Li Mountain. Under Luo Yan's pressure, he would probably choose to meet force with force.
Mountain saw this and gave Di An a way out of the embarrassing situation.
Di An coldly glared at Luo Zheng and Luo Yan, then turned to leave.
But the moment Di An turned around, his spear suddenly shook. A skull appeared in his right hand. This skull bit the shaft of the white spear. After a round, it went straight for Luo Zheng and Luo Yan.
"Resentful Soul Killing Spear!"
A black and gray spear radiance burst out from the tip of the spear.
Li Mountain and those from the Heaven Palace felt the aura of the spear radiance. Their expressions changed greatly.
The spear radiance seemed to contain some incomprehensible power. Even You Xiong and those from the Divine Farmer Clan instinctively felt disgusted. Especially "Mountain", whose expression was very ugly. He didn't expect Di An to attack as soon as he turned around.
Di An's attack seemed reckless, but in fact, he had planned it for a long time.
Luo Yan and Luo Zheng were the core disciples of the Heaven Palace and the Nuwa Clan. As long as these two were eliminated, the rest of the people were nothing to be afraid of.
His "Resentful Soul Killing Spear" was extremely insidious. No matter how powerful the opponent was, as long as they were stabbed, they would be killed in one blow.
The spear radiance turned into a sharp wind and crossed several people at an incredible speed. It also swept through the gaps of the Six Snake Shadow Clones and went straight for Luo Zheng and Luo Yan.
The Six Snake Shadow Clones didn't have time to react at all. This sharp wind had already passed through Luo Yan.
Luo Yan's strength was not weak, but at this moment, she obviously lacked vigilance. She only thought that Di An didn't have the courage to attack at all. She didn't expect him to suddenly attack.
After Luo Zheng felt the unique aura of the sharp wind, his pupils suddenly shrank.
Fortunately, the "Weapon of Civilization" was in an unfolded state. With a change of thought, an emerald-like feather floated out.
He reached out and grabbed Luo Yan. His figure seemed to fly and jump, and the two of them turned back and forth.
WHOOSH …
With this gentle turn, the sharp wind swept past Luo Zheng.
The clothes on his back were all rotten, and the surface of his body turned into a greenish-black color.
The sharp wind from the spear didn't contain poison, but Di An's Nirvana Realm Token was extremely sinister. The sharp wind contained a powerful corrosive power.
Luo Zheng's body could resist it. If it was an ordinary body, it would have turned into a pool of black water.
"You're courting death!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng was really furious.
He did want to punish the people of the four clans, including You Xiong, Golden Crow, and Shennong.
However, Luo Zheng and "Mountain" had the same idea. This damned place wasn't suitable for fighting. If there was any hatred, they could talk about it outside.
Di An's first attack was a sneak attack, and it was aimed at Luo Yan. This was the bottom line that Luo Zheng absolutely couldn't cross!
SWOOSH!
Luo Zheng's figure suddenly floated, and he went straight for Di An like a ghost.
Di An's attack didn't hit, and he knew that things were not good. He was even more confused.
The body movement that Luo Zheng displayed at that moment was completely beyond Di An's understanding.
In Di An's eyes, no one in the Nirvana Realm in the mother world could block a spear that suddenly stabbed out at this distance.
He was 100% sure …
However, Luo Zheng's body movement slightly twisted, and his "certainty" became 100% laughable.
Before he withdrew his spear, Luo Zheng had already closed in.
If he retreated at this moment, it would be taboo. Di An gritted his teeth, and the skull on his right hand let out a strange laughter. A brown light spun around the tip of the spear.
He was preparing for another attack, and it was even more powerful!
"Mountain" and Mumu saw Luo Zheng rushing over, and their faces became solemn.
They scolded Di An for being too impulsive in their hearts, but they had to make a move!
If Di An was defeated, You Xiong would lose a core person, and this was something that the others didn't want to see.
"Boom …"
Mountain's body suddenly shook, and a layer of golden light appeared on his bronze skin. A heavy aura came from inside out, and at this moment, he really turned into an unshakable mountain.
Mumu also flew up from Mountain's shoulder, and her right hand was strangely broken.
However, a small puppet floated at the joint of her right hand and right arm. This was her Nirvana token. The puppet seemed to connect her hand and arm through the air, and it looked quite strange.
Even if it was one against three, Luo Zheng was not afraid at all. He wanted to tear Di An into pieces.

Chapter 3053
The moment these huge eyes opened, everyone in the cavern was enveloped by an intense fear.
Some felt a sharp sword pressing against their forehead. As long as this sword was an inch closer, they would definitely die …
Some saw countless skeletons revolving around them. It was as if they had entered the Death Realm where there were no living beings …
The majority of them held onto their heads. Their faces were filled with terror. They instinctively felt that they would die at the next moment.
Even though most of them had experienced all sorts of dangers and life and death situations, this was the closest they had ever been to that moment.
Even existences like Luo Zheng and Di An who had a strong Sun Soul felt helpless in the face of this overwhelming fear. Luo Zheng had no choice but to retreat after landing on the ground. At the same time, he relied on his strong willpower to resist the fear of the Eye Devil.
Luo Zheng had experienced this feeling once in the Sky Sunflower Deity Temple. That time, he relied on 9527's reminder and the Severing Emotions Divine Dao to break free.
But now, this wave of fear was many times stronger than what he experienced in the Sky Sunflower Deity Temple. He could not calm himself down using the Severing Emotions Divine Dao …
"Heart Flow Only Me!"
At this moment, Luo Zheng circulated the cultivation technique from the < Heart Flow Sword Scripture >.
The Heart Flow Sword Scripture was considered an extension of the Severing Emotions Divine Dao. It focused more on the Sword Heart and had a better effect.
During this period of time in the Heart Flow Sword Sect, Luo Zheng had reached the Small Success Stage.
But even after Luo Zheng circulated his cultivation technique, he could only slightly suppress that fear. He could not completely erase it.
Comparatively, the others were in a worse state.
The majority of them were crawling on the ground. Some had ashen expressions and were trembling all over. Some were wailing.
Luo Yan stood at his original position. He seemed to be circulating some sort of cultivation technique to resist this fear. He did not move at all. His eyes were filled with confusion.
At the peak of the Main Peak.
The surface of the huge eye was flashing with a dark red light.
That wave of fear was naturally released by the huge eye …
Luo Zheng and Di An were both chosen by it and the Chaotic Origin Spirit. It did not wish for anything to happen to the two of them.
But Di An was not obedient the moment he entered. Just as the two of them were about to fight to the death, the huge eye had no choice but to intervene.
"If you make a move now, will it alert them?" the mother asked.
The mother was afraid that they would escape and decided to leave the Chaotic Origin Great World. Nobody was willing to let a cooked duck fly away.
"There should be no problem." The huge eye answered, "They will not rest until they have obtained enough Chaotic Origin Spirits!"
If it wasn't a last resort, the giant eye wouldn't be willing to reveal its ability now.
"Buzz buzz …"
The light in its eyes continued to flicker for ten breaths of time before it disappeared and returned to its original state.
Two rays of red and white light slowly revolved around its pupils.
In the middle of the main peak.
Many of the clansmen and disciples who thought they were about to die suddenly found that the terrifying pressure had disappeared. They crawled up with strange expressions.
"What was that just now …"
"You scared me to death!"
"I thought I was dead for sure!"
Experiencing such fear was like walking through hell. Most of them were sweating profusely.
"Uncle, the power of fear comes from the True Meaning of Good and Evil, but what is it? How can it affect people so much?" Mumu asked in confusion.
Mumu primarily cultivated the True Meaning of Good and Evil. Hence, she understood it very well.
After cultivating the True Meaning of Good and Evil to grand perfection, she could frighten all living beings within a region. Those with weak souls would be frightened to death.
But to Yang Souls who had stepped into the Nirvana Realm, that effect was negligible. Most people would not be affected by it.
Unless she could fuse with a related Nirvana Token or cultivate a sacred art in this aspect.
"Mountain" wasn't just strong. He was also very knowledgeable. In Mumu's eyes, there was almost nothing her uncle did not know.
"Devil Eye of Fear." Mountain nonchalantly answered, "It was a Ruler organization in the past. But it didn't do well in the Nirvana Realm and is almost extinct."
"Why would it appear here?" Mumu asked again.
In Mumu's opinion, regardless of whether it was a living being or not, they had to rely on a medium to descend.
And the medium was the inner world of the Nirvana Realm expert.
"I am not sure." Mountain frowned.
Actually, the Spirit of Chaotic Origin had broken the norm. It lived in a unique form within the Chaotic Origin Great World.
In essence, the Spirit of Chaotic Origin was also a Nirvana Lifeform.
Why would the Spirit of Chaotic Origin have such a unique interest? Many superpowers had researched it before. Yet they could not find the reason.
One could say that the current form of the Spirit of Chaotic Origin had violated the truth.
The moment the reason was found, those powerful existences hidden in the Nirvana Realm and the experts from the various Ruler civilizations would most likely return to their Mother World or even the Primal Chaos.
After Luo Zheng regained his consciousness, he glared at Di An coldly.
At the moment when he wanted to kill Di An, that fear descended upon him. This was definitely not a coincidence.
Based on this information, Luo Zheng could vaguely make a judgment in his heart.
What happened previously was clear. There must be a Devil Eye of Fear above the Main Peak. Furthermore, this Devil Eye of Fear was much stronger than the Mind Eye he fused with!
'Why would it stop me from killing Di An? Or is it purely trying to stop me from fighting with him? '
Luo Zheng leaned towards the latter conclusion.
This was also the first time Di An came to the Chaotic Origin Great World. And from his appearance, it seemed he did not know anything.
What was the purpose of that Devil Eye of Fear doing this?
Before figuring it out, Luo Zheng did not intend to act rashly. Together with Luo Yan, he led Li Shan and the members of the Heaven Palace to another side, pulling away from the You Xiong Clan.
At this moment, several dozen tall figures entered from another passage. They were members of the Li Yuan Clan.
The members of the Li Yuan Clan had gathered into three teams in the Chaotic Origin Great World. One of them had encountered the Evil Deities. The other two teams had also climbed up the Main Peak.
This trip to the Chaotic Origin Great World did not go smoothly for the Li Yuan Clan. Now, they had become much more restrained. Many members of the Li Yuan Clan who were over 30 meters tall glanced at the sculpture on the wall before gathering by the side.
From the tunnels around the cave, more and more non-humankind beings crawled out, and the number of living beings in the cave increased.
The majority of them were in groups of three to five. But there were also some who were alone.
For instance, Mo Yijian from the Heaven Palace had entered the Main Peak alone. After moving around the Chaotic Origin Great World for so long, he finally met up with Luo Zheng's group.
"The Spirit of Chaotic Origin must be hidden up there!"
"Let's directly open a path upwards and dig out the Spirit of Chaotic Origin!"
"Mn, other than the upper half of the Main Peak, there is no other place to hide!"
As the number of people in the cave increased, it became noisy. They were discussing how to continue ascending the mountain.
For instance, the members of the Li Yuan Clan were impatient. Two members of the Li Yuan Clan flew up and smashed their mountain-like bodies towards the top of the cave.

Chapter 3054
Everyone looked up at the ceiling of the cavern. The two Fear Eye Devil statues inlaid at the very top were flashing with red light.
After a long while, they disappeared completely.
"What is going on …"
"That seems to be the Malicious Thought Glance!"
"How could it be so strong?"
Those who entered the cavern later did not experience what happened previously. When they saw what happened to the Li Yuan Clansmen, their expressions changed.
"This is a statue of a Fear Eye Devil!"
"What a powerful Malicious Thought Glance … is there a powerful Fear Eye Devil on the Main Peak?"
"Then how are we going to go up?"
These people were ultimately experienced and knowledgeable. Not only did they recognize the statue on the wall, they even guessed that there was a Fear Eye Devil guarding the Main Peak.
After asking these questions, everyone turned their attention to the Nuwa Clansmen.
Even though the Drinking Snow Prefecture was a Gongli Prefecture, it was still under the control of Li Shan. The other superpowers might not know what happened in the Chaotic Origin Great World, but the people from Li Shan would definitely know something.
"What exactly happened in the Chaotic Origin Great World!"
"Li Shan should have informed all of us!"
"Please tell us!"
"If you don't tell us, don't blame us for being impolite!"
After wandering around the Chaotic Origin Great World for such a long time, they had explored the outer perimeter of the Chaotic Origin Great World and the four great summits. Yet, they did not get any Spirits of Chaotic Origin. It was no wonder these people were furious.
Facing so many outsiders, Li Shan's side would naturally bear a huge pressure.
Even if Li Shan was stronger, he would not be able to fight against so many outsiders.
At this moment, Luo Yan walked out unhurriedly. He swept his gaze across the crowd of outsiders before calmly saying: "The Chaotic Origin Sea has gone out of control. The Spirits of Chaotic Origin are no longer under our control."
Hearing Luo Yan's words, many outsiders revealed astonished expressions.
Some did not understand what he meant. Some made more connections.
"What exactly do you mean?" An outsider asked.
"This is the last time the Chaotic Origin Great World opens. This is the only chance to reach the Chaotic Origin Realm. If you miss it, it will never happen again." Luo Yan indifferently replied.
"What?"
"How could this be?"
"Impossible! The Chaotic Origin Great World has existed for so many years. How could it be so coincidental? "
The clansmen were in an uproar, their faces full of shock.
In their hearts, it was fine even if they did not succeed in fusing with the Chaotic Origin Spirit this time. The entrance ticket to the Chaotic Origin Great World might be precious to ordinary cultivators, but to these outstanding cultivators from the great clans, it was nothing much.
If he failed this time, he would try again next time. As long as the green hills are there, there is no need to worry about firewood. Under any circumstances, his life was the most important.
Luo Zheng was also filled with shock. He asked Luo Yan from the side, "Isn't the Chaotic Origin Spirit able to reproduce continuously?"
Luo Yan blinked his eyes and nodded, "That's true. But they belong to the Nirvana Realm after all. They still have their corresponding bodies in the Nirvana Realm."
In the past, the Chaotic Origin Spirits were strictly controlled in the Opposite Shore. And the location of control was the Chaotic Origin Sea.
Even if all 200,000 Chaotic Origin Spirits were wiped out, those experts could simply make a trip to the Chaotic Origin Sea and fuse with several Chaotic Origin Spirits as mother bodies. That would allow the Chaotic Origin Great World to prosper once again.
But now, after the Chaotic Origin Sea in the Nirvana Shore was occupied, their roots were severed.
Every time a Chaotic Origin Spirit fused with the Mother World, there would be one less.
After hearing Luo Yan's words, the expressions of many clansmen turned interesting. The final chance to break through to the Chaotic Origin Realm had actually been seized by them.
"Then what exactly happened on the Master Peak? Why are there these statues of Devil Eyes of Fear? "A Celestial Wolf Clansman asked.
Luo Yan shook his head and said: "I am not too sure about this. Regardless, I hope that everyone can show me your methods if you wish to break through to the Chaotic Origin Realm …"
Regardless of whether it was "Mountain" or Mu Mu, or the other geniuses from the various races, none of them would allow themselves to miss this opportunity. Being trapped in the Chaotic Origin Realm forever? That was worse than killing them!
Luo Yan's words had made them incomparably serious. They started giving their suggestions.
Originally, these superpowers were slightly wary of each other. But now, the atmosphere had become much more harmonious. After all, they had a common goal.
At this moment, another figure stealthily appeared at another cave entrance.
The moment this voice appeared, an unseen baleful aura spread out. It was like a huge beast baring its fangs and brandishing its claws.
All the other clansmen turned over. When they discovered that the one standing at the cave entrance was that black-robed young man, their expressions sank.
"Evil Deities! You still dare to appear here! "
A golden-armored Li Yuan Clansman took two steps forward. Two huge golden hammers appeared in his hands. His face was filled with fury.
The other Li Yuan Clansmen stepped out as well. They formed a crescent shape and blocked in front of Evil Deities.
When had the Li Yuan Clansmen ever suffered such a huge loss?
Close to one-third of the Li Yuan Clansmen had actually fallen in the hands of a human young man. This was a humiliation and a great enmity that they had to avenge!
It wasn't just the Li Yuan Clansmen. When the other great clans saw Evil Deities, they began glaring at him with hatred.
After Evil Deities entered the Chaotic Origin Great World, they had started a massacre for no reason. They had long angered many clans.
What this fellow did was no different from what he did in the Paramita World.
Even superpowers like You Xiong and Li Yuan could not escape from his vicious hands. This was exactly how Evil Deities did things in the Paramita World.
Someone had once declared that after discovering the identity of Evil Deities in the Mother World, they would grind his bones and scatter his ashes. Yet the Evil Deities in the Mother World were still so valiant.
Now that they had the advantage in numbers, the other clansmen's fear of Evil Deities was greatly reduced.
These people in the cave were all geniuses amongst geniuses from the various great clans. They did not believe that Evil Deities could fight against all of them by himself!
A careless smile appeared on the Evil God's face. The Fusion of Daos energy in his right hand slowly gathered and turned into a diamond-shaped gem, releasing a dangerous aura. Even in the face of so many powerhouses, his state of mind did not waver at all.
Some of the beings who were observing Evil Deities in the dark felt uneasy. What sort of trump card did he have?
Seeing the irritable Li Yuan Clansmen about to rush up, Di An from the You Xiong Clan suddenly reminded, "Brother Jin Sheng! If we fight here, I'm afraid that Evil Deities will be attracted to us! "
Jin Sheng was one of the strongest experts in this generation of the Li Yuan Clan.
He might not be willing to fight with Evil Deities, but the Li Yuan Clan could not take this lying down. He had no choice but to act.
"Oh?" Jin Sheng frowned slightly when he heard Di An's reminder.
"We've already experienced it once." Di An cupped his hands towards Jin Sheng before describing that terrifying energy.
The Li Yuan Clan was arrogant, and the You Xiong Clan did not have many interactions with the Li Yuan Clan.
But Di An still hoped to temporarily ally with the Li Yuan Clan. His reminder was naturally to rope in and curry favor with them.
After hearing Di An's words, Jin Sheng calculated in his heart. He glared fiercely at Evil Deities before leading the Li Yuan Clan to retreat to the side.

Chapter 3055
Evil Deities walked out of the passage with a faint smile on his face. Finally, his eyes stopped on Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng stood in place and looked at him with the same indifferent eyes.
As a member of his Inner World, Luo Zheng knew all about him.
From birth to growth, every detail …
With just a thought, Luo Zheng could gather all his information in his mind.
"You did something wrong," Luo Zheng's tone was a little cold.
Evil Deities looked at Luo Zheng indifferently and asked knowingly, "What is it?"
"You shouldn't have used Ling Shuang to provoke me!" Luo Zheng said in a low voice.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Ling Shuang pursed her lips.
Ling Shuang had fought with Evil Deities before and knew how terrible Evil Deities were.
Evil Deities spared her life at the last moment. Ling Shuang also knew that Evil Deities' purpose was to provoke Luo Zheng.
Ling Shuang didn't want Luo Zheng to fight Evil Deities for her. Although she believed in Luo Zheng's strength, from the current performance, it was obvious that Evil Deities were stronger.
In front of Evil Deities, the only result of defeat was death.
She was worried about Luo Zheng, so after Luo Zheng rescued her, she didn't show much excitement, and even looked a little indifferent …
But Luo Zheng's answer had a trace of determination. Looking at his reaction, he would not let Evil Deities go.
Ling Shuang felt a little sweet in her heart, but more than that, she was worried.
"Tsk, tsk, tsk …" Evil Deities smiled and shook his head, "It seems that you can't wait to destroy me."
"You shouldn't exist in the first place," Luo Zheng said in a low voice.
Evil Deities was an anomaly. Until now, Luo Zheng was very confused. How could such a character be born in his Inner World? Why didn't other people's Inner Worlds exist?
Could it be because of the Land of Origin?
Upon hearing Luo Zheng's words, Evil Deities laughed out loud. "My existence is not up to you. Even this round of Chaos has no say. I am destined to transcend all of this, but you …"
Evil Deities stopped smiling and stared at Luo Zheng with a pair of evil eyes. "You are destined to be my first stepping stone."
These words caused many of the Outsiders to frown.
This guy was getting more and more arrogant …
Even the Kurofune was not qualified to say such words.
What kind of backing did he have?
"Shut up!"
Luo Yan suddenly scolded coldly.
As she yelled, a beam of light that looked like a little fairy suddenly flashed past and swept past Evil Deities at an unavoidable speed.
That was the Language Spirit cultivated by Luo Yan!
The Saint Word Art, True Intent of Annihilation, was a very special true intent of the Dao.
Even in the Mother World, there were not many people who were suitable to cultivate this true intent of the Dao.
And Luo Yan was one of the best. Her talent in this true intent of the Dao was not inferior to Luo Zheng.
When the Saint Word Art, True Intent of Annihilation was cultivated to perfection, one could form their own Language Spirit.
The Language Spirit could be continuously strengthened through cultivation!
In Li Mountain, she was quite doted on by Nuwa. Later, a Fuxi came and under constant guidance, her Language Spirit had been strengthened to an outrageous level.
But she rarely used it.
A powerful Language Spirit could kill with a single word, and even destroy the heart with a single command.
An even more powerful Language Spirit was said to be able to become a prophecy. This was not a prophecy, but a living change of the future!
Evil Deities did not expect that Luo Yan would dare to act rashly. Under the "shut up", he was also attacked by the Language Spirit. For a moment, he was really unable to speak.
"You are merely a second-level lifeform in my big brother's inner world. A second-level lifeform should act like one. Do you really think that you are invincible just by relying on those people from the black ship? This world is not something a second-level lifeform like you can imagine … "
Taking advantage of the fact that Evil Deities could not speak, Luo Yan continued to criticize Evil Deities.
Evil Deities opened his mouth, yet he could not make a single sound.
He had a proud temperament. He had never placed anyone in his eyes. Even Shen Tu and the others from the black ship were the same.
Whoever dared to speak in such a manner in front of him, he would kill them with a flip of his hand.
After being ridiculed by Luo Yan, Evil Deities finally revealed a trace of anger on his frivolous face. He condensed a strand of killing intent and attacked!
But just as he released his killing intent, many bewitching red lights flashed above his head.
Luo Yan had long been chosen by the Chaotic Origin Spirit. At this moment, the huge eye was also evaluating the strength of Evil Deities. Naturally, it would not allow Evil Deities to kill people here.
Evil Deities was immediately enveloped by a terrifying energy …
Everyone had weaknesses and flaws in their soul. Evil Deities' weakness was indeed very small, but it still existed.
Under the eruption of this terrifying energy, his body became incomparably slow. His face revealed a look of pain.
Nevertheless, the soul energy of Evil Deities was far stronger than those of the same level. Even though he was in great pain, he was still qualified to resist.
"Yan 'er, move back." Luo Zheng took a step forward. He stood in front of Luo Yan.
"Big brother, I am not afraid of him." Luo Yan said with a straight face.
She wanted to face Luo Zheng head-on. She was already strong enough to face him alone.
Evil Deities bent over and silently endured that terrifying energy. The muscles on his face were twitching, making him look extremely sinister.
He was using his soul energy to resist the terrifying energy!
The members of the Li Yuan Clan, You Xiong Clan, Divine Farmer Clan, and other clans were all shocked when they saw this scene.
They had suffered losses in front of this Fearsome Demon before. They knew how terrifying this terrifying energy was.
Even though the terrifying energy would not harm people, it could create a huge pressure on one's soul.
Luo Zheng stared at Evil Deities silently. He was also quite surprised.
Just now, the hundred over statues in the cave were shining with red light. The terrifying energy was activated to the maximum. Even with his "Heart Flow", it was hard for him to resist it. Furthermore, You Xiong, the other four clans, Li Shan, and the members of the Heaven Palace were the ones bearing the terrifying energy together.
Even though there were only a dozen statues releasing the terrifying energy, Evil Deities could still resist the terrifying energy by himself. His soul was indeed unimaginably strong!
This kind of person must not be left alone outside …
This scene strengthened Luo Zheng's determination to take back the Evil Deities.
It wasn't just the people in the cave who were surprised. Even the huge eye at the ceiling was transmitting a consciousness of "surprise". "Interesting, interesting … Another unimaginable genius has come! He could actually resist my terrifying energy! "
The mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit was puzzled, "Resist your terrifying energy? If you can't do anything to him, wouldn't it be troublesome? "
During these ten years, under the arrangement of the mother body, the Chaotic Origin Spirits had become stronger by devouring each other.
However, their greatest reliance was still the giant eye.
Even the huge eye could not suppress this fellow. This was still a taboo for the mother body.
"Hmph." The huge eye transmitted a strand of disdain. The red light in its eyes overflowed.

Chapter 3056
"Hong!"
This Terror Energy was like a tide as it converged on the body of Evil Deities.
Evil Deities was originally barely holding on. But this full release of Terror Energy caused his expression to distort. He revealed a look of pain.
The Terror Energy was a unique form of soul attack. Even if one's soul was strong, as long as there was a flaw in one's heart, this energy would exploit that flaw to release the terror in one's heart.
Even if the Terror Energy was greatly boosted, Evil Deities could only hold on for ten breaths of time before he was suppressed to the ground.
The relationship between the tribes in the cavern was slightly complicated. But everyone had a hostile attitude towards Evil Deities.
Seeing Evil Deities suffering such a huge loss, everyone should be clapping their hands in joy. But this scene did not happen. On the contrary, many of them had solemn expressions.
Even an expert like Evil Deities with a strong soul could not resist against the Terror Energy. How could they head to the higher levels of the Main Peak?
After a long while, the red light finally dissipated.
After being punished by the Terror Energy, Evil Deities who crawled up from the ground finally stopped.
He did not say a word as he retreated to the side. His expression was gloomy as he glanced upwards from time to time.
Evil Deities had not suffered any huge losses ever since he entered the Origin World. He did not know who was the one who unleashed that Terror Energy. But Evil Deities would definitely remember this grudge!
"This Terror Energy is so strong. We aren't its opponent. How can we continue climbing up?" Someone asked softly.
"Mountain" had a solemn expression too, "Even if we climb up, if we are suppressed by this Terror Energy, it will be hard for us to defeat those Chaotic Origin Spirits."
The Main Peak was originally dangerous. After those Chaotic Origin Spirits devoured one another, they became much stronger. Entering the Main Peak was a huge risk for them.
"Unless we have to return empty-handed?" Another person asked.
Everyone turned silent …
Luo Yan had already said that if they missed this opportunity, no one would be able to step into the Chaotic Origin Realm in the future.
Who would be willing to stay at this place and not move forward?
Just as everyone was discussing softly, a soft sound came from the top of the cavern.
"Ka, ka …"
The carvings at the top of the cavern seemed to have loosened. When those carvings cracked open, three stairs extended out!

Clasping one's hands to welcome a guest, there must be a killing trap.

The Evil God sat motionless on the ground, a faint smile on his face as he coldly looked at these people. These people had all sorts of strange Paramita Tokens. Even if they met a Paramita Realm expert, they would still be able to fight back.

By combining the abilities of the Paramita Token, the power it could unleash could be increased by ten to a hundred times.

"It is naturally dangerous to go up from here. But what about the outside?" Evil Deities grinned.
The majority of them still had a fixed mindset. They wanted to charge up from the Main Peak.
But they could leap to the top of the Main Peak. That was another path.
Many people's eyes brightened.
"But the hurricane outside is very strong." Mu Mu said.
Evil Deities glanced at Mu Mu, "Have you all not dug holes in the ground before?"
Most of the living beings present had dug holes all the way from the boundary of the Chaotic Origin Great World. They naturally understood what Evil Deities meant.
"Then should we leave this place?" Di An asked.
Many nodded slightly. Since they had come up with a solution, they would follow it.
For the sake of obtaining the Chaotic Origin Spirit, everyone had formed a temporary team.
"But there is another method that is simpler. I am leaning towards this method. Hehe." Evil Deities chuckled.
Including Luo Zheng, everyone stared at Evil Deities. They did not know what other ideas this fellow had …
"This Main Peak is thin and long. Even though it is tall, its diameter is not long. How about we work together and break the Main Peak in half?" Evil Deities suggested.
"Break the Main Peak!"

If they entered the Main Peak from the outside, they would not be able to take the initiative. The situation was only slightly better than going up the stairs.
Directly cutting off the main peak was indeed a better method.
Many people turned towards the group from Li Shan. Luo Yan did not speak. Ling Yun shrugged by her side, "Li Shan is only asking us to act according to the circumstances."
"Since that's the case, we can indeed follow this method. But I wonder if there will be any unavoidable hurricanes." Jin Sheng said as he swayed his enormous body.
Breaking the Main Peak was something that Jin Sheng liked. But if they were blown away by the hurricanes after breaking the Main Peak, that would not be good.
"I have set up eyes outside." A green-skinned non-human said, "The hurricanes will stop soon."
Evil Deities chuckled when he saw how tempted everyone was: "Then let us begin now. There is only one chance …"
All the conversations above were conducted using True Essence Voice Transmissions. Hence, the mother body and enormous eye on the Main Peak could not hear them.
That enormous eye was faintly filled with doubt. The enormous eye asked using its consciousness, "Why are they not coming up? What exactly are they thinking of doing …"

At this moment …
The eyes of the enormous eye dilated.
"Not good!"

"Hong long long …"

Chapter 3057

The 300,000 meters tall Main Peak started tilting to one side. This process was not fast, but it was gradually accelerating. The peak of the Main Peak directly smashed onto the ring-shaped mountain range to the east, causing the entire mountain range to collapse.

The Chaotic Origin Reconstruct was similarly important to Evil Deities. Even though he was crazy, he was not a fool.

"Ka …"

Luo Yan placed both her hands together and gently raised them up. A blood-red Wah Shadow appeared behind her.
Red strands of energy extended out from the hands of Wah Shadow. They lashed downwards. Every time it lashed out, countless rock fragments would burst out. It was a frightening force.

Luo Yan's growth in Li Mountain was much faster than he imagined.

The women of the Nuwa Clan stood in midair. They brought out their Paramita Tokens, causing terrifying energy to fall on the main peak like raindrops.

For the sake of the Chaotic Origin Spirits, the group temporarily put aside their conflict.

Chapter 3058

Its efforts were very effective. Currently, almost no one dared to enter the Master Peak. Every ten years, it only had to sacrifice more than half of its Chaotic Origin Spirits to protect the remaining portion.
This time, not only did the giant eye's idea not work at all, it even overturned the entire main peak, which was the result it did not want to see.

The descent of the giant eye was a secret. Even the people of Mount Li did not notice it.

But they had been holed up in the Main Peak and could only passively take a beating.

Outside the Main Peak, many clansmen and disciples were frenziedly plundering the Chaotic Origin Spirits.

At this moment, the black fog in the sky started surging once again. The hurricane was about to blow.

On the other side, it was even more unfortunate. Two Divine Farmer Clan members were trying their best to dig out the Chaotic Origin Spirit. Before they could react, they were swallowed by the bloody mouth.

"Retreat!"
"The Chaotic Origin Spirit is coming out!"
"I told you not to get close to the mountain wall!"
The majority of the clansmen were still cautious. For instance, Luo Zheng, Luo Yan, Ling Shuang and the others merely slashed at the space. When the Chaotic Origin Spirit tunneled out from the mountain wall, they simply leaped up and pulled apart the distance between them.

"Ka …"
"Ka …"
Many huge Chaotic Origin Spirits tunneled out from the cracks in the mountain wall. These Chaotic Origin Spirits were emitting a murderous aura.

An even bigger crack exploded. The peak of the Main Peak was split into two.

Chapter 3059
Everyone's focus was not just on the main body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit. Instead, they were focused on that enormous eyeball.
Previously, they had seen the sculpture within the cavern. After experiencing the baptism of the Terror Energy, they knew that there should be a Fear Heart Eye Devil in the Main Peak.
Even though they were mentally prepared, they were still startled when they saw that enormous eyeball.

Regardless of which race they were, they were all retreating.
For instance, those non-humans who had originally hidden in the arrays and wanted to take a share of the loot. They were not confident in their strength. Hence, when they saw the enormous Chaotic Origin Spirit tunneling out, why would they dare to stay?
They turned around and tunneled into the nearest array.
"You still want to escape after forcing us out?"
The red light in the pupil of the enormous eye flashed. A circular blood mark spread out at a visible speed.
When the blood mark swept across the crowd, everyone felt their hearts palpitating.
In the blink of an eye, the blood mark had already enveloped an area of 30,000 meters!
The nearest array was right beside the blood mark. Several non-humans gritted their teeth and attempted to charge into the array.
This array was constructed by many powerful existences. As long as they hid within, they could preserve their lives.
But the moment they touched the blood mark, they felt as if they were struck by lightning!
The Terror Energy surged out and struck the weak point deep in their hearts. Some turned mad, and some wailed as they vented their emotions. Ultimately, they had no choice but to leave the blood mark.
Luo Zheng and the others felt their eyebrows twitching when they saw this blood mark.
The enormous eye had actually sealed everyone within!
This action revealed a piece of information. The Chaotic Origin Spirit and the enormous eye were confident of their strength. They were actually planning to capture everyone in one fell swoop!
Ever since the collapse of the Main Peak, Evil Deities had been leisurely moving around.
Seeing the appearance of the enormous eye and the Chaotic Origin Spirit, he revealed a look of intense interest.
But his position was slightly further away. Hence, the blood mark did not envelop him. Nevertheless, the Evil Deities had actually walked to the edge of the blood mark.
'The Terror Energy of that enormous eye is very strong. But it is impossible for this energy to extend without limit …' The Evil Deities looked at the blood mark beneath his feet. He revealed a look of interest. He then took a step towards the blood mark!
The moment the Evil Deities touched the blood mark, the power of fear invaded his mind.
The Heretic God frowned, and a strange color flashed in his eyes. He forcefully endured the invasion of the power of fear and stepped over the blood mark!
"That's pretty much what I've guessed …" The corners of Evil Deities' mouth curled up slightly, as if everything was under control. "The more powerful the Nirvana Token is, the weaker it is. You're too confident in yourself."
Evil Deities was a classic example of someone who did not want to suffer any losses. He wanted to make a move in the cave on the main peak, but he was stopped by the power of fear.
From that moment on, he was determined to find out who was behind the scenes.
After cutting off the main peak and forcing out the eyeball, the Evil Deities would definitely not let it go.
Evil Deities' analysis was not bad.
Some Nirvana Realm tokens had abnormal abilities, such as the mysterious type of Nirvana Realm tokens. The ability provided was often impossible to guard against. For example, Nirvana Realm tokens like the "Mind's Eye" and the "Heart of the Accomplice" had extremely abnormal abilities, but the Nirvana Realm tokens they contained were extremely weak.
That huge eyeball was only good at evil thoughts. It was not strong by itself.
Seeing the clansmen being trapped, the entrance of the Chaotic Origin Great World had long exploded.
"Why would a Mind's Eye appear in the Chaotic Origin Great World!"
"Li Shan, please give us an explanation!"
"Open the Chaotic Origin Great World and let us in!"
The vision of the leaders of these super organizations was clearly not something the younger generation could compare with.
Fear Eye Devils were extremely rare in the Nirvana World. But their origins were not mysterious. After all, the Fear Eye Devils were once part of a Ruler civilization.
Now that the mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit had charged out from the mountain, in addition to that Mind's Eye, how could the younger generation of clansmen resist against it?
Qiu Yin He, Lin Zhanting, and the others had solemn expressions.
The Chaotic Origin Great World was ultimately under the control of Li Shan. Was Li Shan aware of this? If he was, why would he let them go?
More importantly, the Nirvana Token had to be carried by the inner world of a lifeform before it could descend into the Nirvana World. The Chaotic Origin Spirit had become the only exception in the Nirvana World because of its unique form!
But what was that huge eyeball? Why would it descend into the Nirvana World like the Chaotic Origin Spirit? This went against common sense!
Phoenix lightly flapped her wings and charged towards the entrance of the Chaotic Origin Great World.
Lingering flames soared into the sky, releasing a scorching aura. One could hear Phoenix coldly saying: "Let me in."
The Godly Nest Demonesses had suffered a huge loss. Phoenix wanted to ensure that the remaining clansmen could obtain the Chaotic Origin Spirit.

Phoenix frowned and said: "Now that such an unforeseen event has occurred in the Chaotic Origin Great World, if we don't enter, they will definitely die!"
"Since ancient times, there have been many young cultivators who have died inside." The Li Clan woman replied: "The rules are not set by our Li Shan Clan alone. Your Golden Crow Clan's Di Jun is also involved. If you insist on barging in, don't blame our Li Shan for being impolite!"
"You …" Phoenix glared fiercely at the Li Clan woman. For a moment, she was speechless.
Even though Phoenix did not place the Li Clan woman guarding the entrance in her eyes, if she were to act against the Li Clan in the Snow Drinking Continent, she would be seeking trouble for herself. In the end, Phoenix could only give up.
Even the leaders of the other superpowers could not do anything. They could only place their hopes on their clansmen to survive before the mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit and safely undergo the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction.
On the peak of the snowy mountain.
Fuxi sat amidst the snow. He did not place his attention on the Chaotic Origin Great World.
His gaze swept across the entire Chaotic Origin Great World and reached the end of the Snow Drinking Continent.
Over there, there was a thin spatial tear …
Several sharp claws stealthily extended out from the spatial tear. Above the claws, there was a tiny blood eye.
The thing behind the spatial tear was extremely well-hidden. Even when space was torn apart, not a single trace of aura leaked out. It was as if it could naturally fuse with the space.
"Such a concealment sacred art is truly breathtaking. If not for me using the Eight Trigrams Plate to analyze the world, I would not have been able to deduce such a calamity …" Fuxi muttered, "Let me see what exactly you all are planning …"
Initially, Li Shan did not know about the changes happening within the Chaotic Origin Great World.
It was only until the changes occurred in the Chaotic Origin Sea that Fuxi discovered a strand of mystery after using the Eight Trigrams Plate. Naturally, he also discovered that Mind's Eye within the Main Peak of the Chaotic Origin Great World.
Being a Paramita Token, why did the Mind's Eye descend without any need for a vessel? This was something that Li Shan could not understand.

Chapter 3060
That enormous eye released a blood mark that enveloped everyone present. At the same time, it released a strand of consciousness.

The Chaotic Origin Spirit received a heavy blow from Jin Sheng and was smashed into the ground, forming a huge crater with a diameter of 30 meters.

This happened not just at Jin Sheng.
The situation of the Golden Crow, You Xiong, and Tian Lang clans was almost exactly the same!

Chou Xinghe was merely a marionette now. Even if he was struck by the Chaotic Origin Spirit, he would not be affected much …

During this process, the Heaven Palace, Nuwa Clan, Ear Rat, and even Golden Crow You Xiong's side were affected. In less than ten breaths of time, 20 to 30 people died!
That enormous eye remained floating in midair. Deep within its eyes, red light was continuously accumulating and being released.
According to this rhythm, it would not take long for it and the mother body to wipe out all these people.

But at this moment, the enormous eye suddenly sensed two auras coming from behind.

Chapter 3061
The giant eye did not know what this silk cloth was, but after it felt the green light emitted from the silk cloth, its huge pupils suddenly shrank.

The blood-red power of fear formed a spiral and directly covered the Luo siblings.

However, Luo Yan's face did not show the slightest fear. She gently raised the navy blue silk cloth, and the silk cloth expanded in the wind, becoming about ten feet in size.
After the silk cloth unfolded, it spun in front of Luo Yan, and circles of green light bloomed on its surface.
"Whoosh …"
The power of fear poured into the green light, and like snowflakes under the scorching sun, it instantly melted completely.
"The power of fear is indeed strong in some aspects. No matter how strong a person's mind is, there will always be things that they are afraid of. However, the more unorthodox the method, the easier it is to be restrained …" Luo Yan said calmly.
For example, the power of some evil methods was very terrifying. The reason why these methods were difficult to be elegant was not because of their improper origin.
The world was so big, and there were so many people with obsessions who would do anything to become strong.
However, although those evil methods were powerful, they were too easy to be restrained. If one wanted to become a true expert, one could only find a perfect path.
"Hero's Moment!"
Following Luo Yan's soft shout, the green light released from the silk cloth turned into green raindrops that filled the sky, and directly hit the giant eye.
When the giant eye saw those green raindrops, its pupils released a sense of panic.
It had two abilities, but they could not be used now.
The True Vision was an auxiliary ability that could help it distinguish the opponent's weakness more clearly and see through the illusory maze, but it could not help it directly defend against the enemy.
Although the power of fear was powerful, it was completely restrained by this silk cloth.
This inconspicuous silk cloth was one of the Nirvana Realm treasures of Mount Li.
Moment was originally meant as raindrops.
In the twenty-one heavens of the Nirvana Realm, there was a rainforest. Over the years, the rainforest was scattered with a type of green raindrops, known as Green Moment.
When a Yang Soul entered it and was drenched by the raindrops, the fear in its heart would be dispelled.
His entire body would be immersed in the feeling of blind confidence, and he would become incomparably heroic.
Some people would use the Green Flash as an auxiliary Nirvana Token to fuse with it. They would use it to fight against the fear in their hearts during crucial moments and make themselves braver.
The silk cloth was made from the smelting of the Green Flash.
Luo Zheng stood behind the silk cloth. Feeling the green light, he felt an irrepressible impulse in his heart. He was full of confidence.
"Such confidence is too blind …"
With a thought, Luo Zheng circulated the mental cultivation method of the Heart-Flow Sword Scripture and stifled this impulse in the depths of his heart.
"Da da da da da …"
Although the giant eye's speed was not slow, the area covered by the Green Flash was too large, and the giant eye could not avoid all the tiny water droplets.
When the green water droplets landed on the huge eye, a hissing sound could be heard.
The Eye Demon of Fear and the Green Flash were polar opposites. The water droplets had the effect of melting it!
The giant eye continued to transmit a painful consciousness as it quickly retreated.
"Brother, let's chase after it!"
Luo Yan's finger lightly tapped on the center of the silk cloth. Her figure quickly drifted forward. She was extremely nimble.
Luo Zheng's figure floated and followed closely behind her.
When the Main Peak was broken, Luo Zheng was slightly bewildered.
He himself had a Mind's Eye, so he knew how powerful it was.
Especially this giant eye at the peak of the Main Peak. It was much stronger than the one Luo Zheng had. If it could not counter the energy of fear, it would be an unsolvable existence.
But Luo Yan had secretly transmitted to him, saying that the Li Clan had made arrangements for everything.
Seeing how his little sister was behaving, Luo Zheng felt warmth in his heart. She was now an existence that could take charge of everything.
Those Chaotic Origin Spirits were still fighting against the other races. Luo Zheng was worried about her safety. Naturally, he had to protect her.
"Da da da …"
The silk cloth fluttered, causing green raindrops to scatter everywhere.
Some raindrops unintentionally landed on several Ear Mice. These Ear Mice who were originally cowering suddenly changed their nature. They became incomparably valiant as they charged towards those Chaotic Origin Spirits.
Under the constant erosion of the green raindrops, the surface of the giant eye that was originally as clear as a mirror was now filled with holes. The luster of the eye was also dimming.
"Sou …"
For the sake of evading these raindrops, the giant eye circled to the other side of the Main Peak.
Luo Yan lightly stepped on the surface of the Main Peak. Her figure fluttered as the raindrops continued to hit her to the side.
But at the moment she flew over, a voice abruptly sounded.
"This thing is very interesting. I want it!"
"Shua!"
A black figure charged towards Luo Yan at an extremely fast speed.
Luo Yan did not expect that there would be someone hiding at the side of the Main Peak. Her expression changed slightly. She lightly sucked in the silk cloth and spat out a single word, "Protect!"
An unseen light screen enveloped her.
Given Luo Yan's current sacred technique, she could already execute the law with her words.
The words he spat out were enough to protect her.
It would not be easy for prodigies of the same cultivation level to break through this light screen.
However, the power of the black figure far exceeded Luo Yan's expectations. He gently grabbed with one hand. Countless colors stacked together and erupted into a white light. Under the white light's annihilation, the light screen that Luo Yan had summoned shattered like glass.
"You …"
Only then did Luo Yan clearly see that the person who had just arrived was none other than the Evil Deities.
The Evil Deities smirked. Her smile carried a hint of evil.
The power of fear was what gave him a headache. If the giant eye continued to restrict him, he did not have a good way to resist.
As long as he had this silk cloth, nothing would be a problem.
Thus, he chose to make a move at this critical moment.
Just as the Evil Deities reached for the silk cloth, a longsword pierced towards Luo Yan from the side. The sharp sword projection extended endlessly and pierced towards the Evil Deities' palm.
The Evil Deities felt the power of this sword projection. She retracted her smile and her expression turned solemn.
A five-colored light condensed on her palm. That was the Dao Fusion Energy!
The rhombus-shaped Dao Fusion Energy had already been stacked by the Evil Deities to over 120 layers. Its power was frightening and was not any weaker than those Paramita Tokens from the 13 Levels of Heaven.
The Dao Fusion Energy had been condensed to its limit. It exploded the moment it clashed with the sword projection. A terrifying annihilation force spread out in all directions. It was like a blazing sun, attracting the attention of many people and even the Chaotic Origin Spirits.
If it were anyone else who fought with the Evil Deities, they might not be able to dodge in time and be swallowed by this blazing sun.
But Luo Zheng similarly understood the Dao Fusion Energy well!
The purpose of this sword strike was to force the Evil Deities back.
At the moment when the Dao Fusion Energy exploded, he grabbed Luo Yan with one hand. Luo Yan grabbed onto that piece of silk cloth and flew backwards, pulling a distance away from the Evil Deities.

Chapter 3062
That blazing sun bloomed like the sun, but withered at an extremely fast speed.
The light reflected in Luo Zheng's eyes quickly disappeared, and a strange expression flickered in his eyes.
Evil Deities gently raised his hand, and the rhombus-shaped fragments of Daofused energy gathered again, gently pulsing in his palm.
The light of these fragments was very beautiful, but also extremely dangerous.
"I have to thank you for being able to cultivate Daofused energy to such a level," Evil Deities said with a faint smile. "As far as I know, an ordinary person's inner world doesn't have so many Divine Dao existences."
Luo Zheng was silent for a while.
What he cared about wasn't Evil Deities' words, but Daofused energy.
Three thousand Divine Daos was an imaginary number, and some people had more than three thousand types of energy, while others had less than three thousand.

Both Luo Zheng and Evil Deities were extremely familiar with these three thousand types of Divine Daos.
But now, Luo Zheng discovered something unique within the Daofused energy. Within the fragments of Daofused energy, there was a faint white arc of light pulsing.
Because this white arc of light only made up one three-thousandth of the Daofused energy, Luo Zheng couldn't clearly perceive it …
What did he integrate into the Daofused energy?
Evil Deities noticed that Luo Zheng was staring at his Daofused energy, and the smile on his face deepened. "Are you puzzled as to why my Daofused energy is so powerful?"
"Yes," Luo Zheng replied honestly.

He spread out his hands, and rhombus-shaped Daofused energy spread out like wings.
The structure of this Daofused energy was extremely complex, and it was in an extremely unstable state, so the power contained within was naturally astonishing.

Those non-humans and disciples who were fighting with the Spirits of Chaotic Origin were bewildered when they heard the words of the Evil Deities.
Even though the power of the Dao Fusion Energy was extraordinary, what was this fellow so proud of?
There were so many powerful existences in the Mother World, and quite a few of them were researching on the Dao Fusion Energy. Yet in the end, didn't they choose to give up on it?
These non-humans and disciples admitted that the Evil Deities were formidable. However, they didn't think highly of the Dao Fusion Energy. Carrying the Nirvana Token and obtaining a stronger Nirvana Energy was the right path.
But it wasn't the Evil Deities who were observing this scene.
At the entrance of the Chaotic Origin Great World, the leaders of the various large clans could see what was happening through the arrays in front of the door.
Like Phoenix and the others, they were frowning. Similarly, they could not understand what the Evil Deities were talking about.
But Qiu Yinhe, Lin Zhanting, the leaders of the Li Yuan Clan, Eared Mouse Clan, and You Xiong Clan revealed solemn expressions.
"Brother Lin … there seems to be a unique energy within the Dao Fusion Energy of that black-robed young man?" Qiu Yinhe asked.
Qiu Yinhe and the others did not cultivate that many Deities. But they had some understanding of the energy that existed in this world.
Lin Zhanting nodded solemnly, "I've never seen such energy before …"
"It should be the energy of some Nirvana Token?" An Eared Mouse said.
"It is impossible for the Nirvana Energy to fuse so perfectly." Qiu Yinhe shook his head, "Truly strange …"
Their understanding of energy was ultimately flawed. The majority of them did not know that the Origin Source Energy of this world was not complete.
Fuxi sat quietly on the snowy mountain. He retracted his gaze from the periphery of the Drinking Snow Continent and landed on the core region of the Chaotic Origin Great World. Those blazing suns similarly entered his eyes.
When he noticed the white arc of light amidst this energy, Fuxi's eyes dilated slightly.
'How could this be!'
Fuxi had once fused 3,000 Deities. His understanding of the Dao Fusion Energy was much stronger than all the leaders below the mountain.
'Why would there be an additional energy out of nowhere? This energy … did not exist in the world in the past! That's right … no wonder the Dao Fusion Energy could not be fused completely. Without this strand of energy, it would be impossible to fuse it perfectly. Any effort would be wasted! But where did this brat find it?
Where did he come from? '
Huge waves surged in Fuxi's heart.
In the past, the Dao Fusion Energy was deemed as the most Origin Source Energy in the entire Primal Chaos. The moment it was fused completely, even the Truth Divine Ability could not compare with it.
But so many experts had spent countless years on it yet they could not achieve a breakthrough in the Dao Fusion Energy. In the end, even Fuxi, Donghuang Tai Yi, and others could only conclude that the Dao Fusion Energy could never be perfected.
Evil Deities stared intently at Luo Zheng.
He wanted to see astonishment, shock, disbelief, and other expressions on Luo Zheng's face.
But Evil Deities was disappointed. Luo Zheng remained calm as he looked at Evil Deities. Luo Zheng nonchalantly said: "If I were you, I would definitely not reveal this energy to the public."
"Do you think I am as cowardly as you?" Evil Deities mocked.
"After all, your Dao Fusion Energy cannot be perfected." Luo Zheng shook his head, "If you reveal it now, it will only attract the greed of others."
"Cannot be perfected?" Evil Deities smiled proudly, "I have already fused 2,999 Deities, including this one. Now, only the Mystic Thunder Divine Dao cannot be fused. I am only one step away from perfecting it!"
Evil Deities continued to stare at Luo Zheng as he spoke. But Luo Zheng's expression did not change.

Chapter 3063
Evil Deities had indeed gone further than Luo Zheng on the path of fusing Divine Daos.
Out of the three thousand Divine Daos, he was only missing the last one. Being able to do this with his cultivation was simply inconceivable.
If it had been in the past, Luo Zheng would have been looking forward to seeing what kind of profound meaning would be derived from the unification of Dao.
However, Luo Zheng had learned from the Spirit of Green Jade that the chaos was incomplete to begin with.
Luo Zheng had obtained one of the three flaws through the Green Jade Civilization, which was the Blood Origin Divine Dao. However, he had no clue about the other two flaws.
After deciphering the Sanskrit formed by the blood with Luo Nian's help, he had obtained a hint. The Spirit of Green Jade suspected that one of the flaws had something to do with the sacred fire of the second civilization, the Meta-Spirit Clan, but it still didn't know what it was exactly.
Luo Zheng hadn't expected that one of the flaws would be controlled by Evil Deities!
He didn't know if the arc-like energy was given to him by the black ship's powerhouse or if he had obtained it in the Paramita.
Evil Deities had been complacent ever since they obtained this unique energy.
In their eyes, the day when they completed the unification of Dao wasn't far away.
The only thing that made Evil Deities a little depressed was that this energy seemed to conflict with the Mystic Thunder Divine Dao!
Before this, Evil Deities had experimented with it millions of times!
If he took out the Mystic Thunder Divine Dao alone and inserted the arc of light into it, the 3,000 Divine Daos would be very stable.
If he took out the arc of light and inserted it into the Mystic Thunder Divine Dao, Evil Deities would also be able to maintain the stability of the fusion energy. However, the fusion energy would collapse the moment these two energies were added. There was no room for negotiation.
The arc of light itself was a type of thunderbolt. Thunderbolt energy was explosive and extremely difficult to control. When its characteristics clashed with the Mystic Thunder Divine Dao, the two thunderbolts were like fire and water.
Evil Deities had studied it countless times and even used different arrangements to fuse the Divine Dao, but they all failed …
He even wanted to use the power of the Paramita to suppress it and force the two thunderbolts to fuse the Divine Dao at the same time. However, this path still didn't work.
Luo Zheng's words were like a bolt of lightning that struck Evil Deities' heart.
"What? More than one flaw? "The Evil God stared at Luo Zheng in disbelief.
When the other cultivators in the Chaotic Origin Great World heard this, they did not express anything.
After all, the Dao-Melting energy was not something they had come into contact with, and they did not think that this thing would be very powerful after fusing. The only thing they noticed was the "mountain" not far away.
The Chaotic Origin Spiritual Mother was still rampaging through this region. If one was careless, one would be crushed by the mother body.
Just as everyone was chased around by the mother, "Mountain" and Mumu finally stood up.
Mumu's curly hair had extended to tens of thousands of feet long, and her slightly exotic little face hidden under the curly hair was completely exposed. Her blue eyes were full of seriousness as she controlled her hair to wrap around her mother's body.
Although the strength of this mother's body was far stronger than hers, in the face of those messy curly hair that couldn't be pulled out, she was actually somewhat helpless.
After Mumu restricted the movement of the mother body, it was up to "Mountain" to suppress the mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Mother.
During the process of suppressing the mother body, Mountain also heard Luo Zheng's words. After pondering for a moment, his tall and sturdy body suddenly trembled. He turned to look at Luo Zheng with astonishment.
And Fuxi who was outside the Chaotic Origin Great World also raised his eyebrows. He muttered: "There is more than one flaw in the Dao-Melting energy? Then how many are there exactly? "
If not for Fuxi being wary of that monster in the crack by the boundary of the Drinking Snow Prefecture, he would have charged in to interrogate Luo Zheng.
Fuxi was more curious about how Luo Zheng knew about it.
The 3,000 Deity Daos were formed from the Primal Chaos Energy. It was the Primal Chaos Energy that was divided into Yin and Yang. Yin and Yang transformed into the Five Elements, and the Five Elements gave birth to the myriad existences.
The pure energy that was continuously divided down was the Dao Accumulation of the 3,000 Deity Daos. It could also be seen as the foundation of the Primal Chaos Energy.
If there was truly a flaw, then the Primal Chaos Energy itself had a flaw. Or even the entire Primal Chaos World had a flaw?
Luo Zheng glanced at Evil Deities indifferently and said: "Yes."
"How did you know?" The eyes of Evil Deities flickered.
Evil Deities understood Luo Zheng's character. He would not casually fabricate a story to deceive him.
But Evil Deities had also experienced a huge fortuitous encounter when he discovered this secret. He had nearly died in the Paramita. He had used his life to exchange for this lump of arc light.
Unless Luo Zheng had discovered something?
Or perhaps he had grasped hold of other flaws?
"Why should I tell you?" Luo Zheng nonchalantly said.
Hearing Luo Zheng's answer, the eyes of Evil Deities flashed slightly. His eyes revealed a trace of haze.
Although he acted abnormally arrogant in front of Luo Zheng, deep in his heart, he still had a deep reverence for Luo Zheng.
Even though Shen Tu had helped him block the connection to the Soul Lodging Land and freed him from Luo Zheng's control, in the end, he was still a creature born from Luo Zheng's Inner World. The fact that Luo Zheng was his creator could not be erased.
This "creator" had many secrets, and Evil Deities was determined to obtain them.
"I don't need you to tell me. I just need to devour you!" Evil Deities grinned maliciously. His aura surged.
"Huala!"
Turbulent Dao Integration Energy spread out. This Dao Integration Energy flashed with magnificent colors and turned into a diamond-shaped giant sword. Evil Deities grasped this giant sword and his figure blurred as he charged straight at Luo Zheng.
"Brother! Be careful! "Luo Yan reminded from above.
"It's useless to be careful, hahaha …" Evil Deities laughed wildly as he waved his giant sword, drawing a colorful arc in the air and chopping down at Luo Zheng's head.
But at this moment, a red circle of light spread out. The fear contained in this circle of light attacked Evil Deities' heart.
