Under the highly condensed Dao Integration Energy, it was originally extremely unstable. After being affected by the fear, Evil Deities couldn't control the Dao Integration Energy and exploded, turning into a huge blazing sun!
With such a terrifying annihilation energy, even Evil Deities would suffer a great loss if he was caught in it. He suppressed the fear in his heart and instinctively flew back to avoid it.
Luo Yan held the silk cloth in one hand and gently dripped "Moment of Valor" on his brother to help Luo Zheng dispel the fear. At the same time, he said to Evil Deities, "The person I told my brother to be careful of wasn't you …"
Evil Deities sorted out his thoughts and turned to look at the giant eye on the other side.
This giant eye was only restrained by the silk cloth in Luo Yan's hand. Without that silk cloth, Evil Deities couldn't do anything to it.
Evil Deities had no chance of winning against Luo Zheng and the fear of the giant eye.
He gritted his teeth and dodged to the side of the main peak!
"Brother, should we chase after him?" Luo Yan looked at the black figure and asked.
"Do you think that eye will help us?" Luo Zheng asked with a smile.
"No." Luo Yan shook his head.

Chapter 3064
Facing Evil Deities in a chaotic battle wasn't a wise choice.
Evil Deities could slaughter their way through the Chaotic Origin Great World not just by fusing Dao Energy.
From what that Li Yuan Clansman said, Luo Zheng knew that Evil Deities seemed to be able to descend into a world. This trump card Evil Deities had never used before.
That huge eye would release its Terror Energy at an appropriate time. It was a threat to everyone. Hence, they had to eliminate this threat at this moment!
As for that arc of light in the hands of Evil Deities, it was also something that Luo Zheng had to get.
The huge eye felt extremely gloomy.
On one hand, it released its Terror Energy to help the Chaotic Origin Spirit. On the other hand, that huge sword formed by Evil Deities was incredibly powerful.
If Evil Deities were to land a hit, it would help it eliminate a formidable opponent.
Currently, the huge eye no longer thought of snatching the fleshy bodies of these people. It was already good enough for it to survive.
Yet it had never thought that Luo Yan's alertness was so high. He immediately used the Moment of Heroism to protect Luo Zheng. And Evil Deities was even more baffled. How could such a powerful huge sword explode in his hands?
Feeling helpless, the huge eye turned around and tunneled into the Main Peak.
Luo Zheng saw the huge eye tunneling into the Main Peak. He immediately changed his direction and punched out a huge hole in the wall of the Main Peak. He and Luo Yan then tunneled into this hole.
The Main Peak was currently in a horizontal position. The huge eye and Luo Zheng tunneled into the Main Peak at three-quarters of the height.
Previously, the huge eye had set up layers of mechanisms here. Especially the various Devil Eye Sculptures of Fear carved on the walls.
At the moment when Luo Zheng entered, the Terror Energy of the huge eye gushed out. At the same time, the eyeballs of the Devil Eye Sculptures of Fear flickered with strands of dark red light.
"Sou sou sou sou sou …"
These red lights came straight at Luo Zheng from all directions from different angles.
"Go crazy for me!"
At this moment, the giant eye also had a desperate attitude.
When his power of fear was squeezed to the limit, it was enough to drown the other party's mind, causing the other party's memories to become chaotic. It could even erase normal memories, leaving behind only endless fear, turning the other party into a lunatic who was constantly in fear.
Luo Zheng's expression changed when he saw the Fear power gathering from all directions.
Seeing this scene, Luo Yan coldly snorted and gently pulled Luo Zheng's arm. The silk cloth flickered with a blue light and turned into a huge cloak, covering both her and Luo Zheng.
No matter how powerful the power of fear was, it couldn't do anything to the two siblings, Luo Yan and Luo Zheng.
"Brother, I'll help you get there," Luo Yan whispered in his ear.
If the giant eye released an endless amount of Fear, they would probably be trapped inside forever.
Many clans were currently fighting with the Chaotic Origin Spirits outside. He did not know what kind of trouble the Evil Deities would cause. Hence, he had to end the battle as soon as possible.
"Alright." Luo Zheng nodded.
Strands of green light once again condensed on the surface of this green silk cloth. The green light transformed into strands of translucent Moment of Valor. At the moment Luo Yan pulled the silk cloth, the green light transformed into Moment of Valor and bathed Luo Zheng's body.
Under the protection of the Moment of Valor, Luo Zheng left behind a series of afterimages in the air. In the blink of an eye, he had charged towards the giant eye.
"Don't come over!"
The giant eye let out a hysterical consciousness. The red light in its eyes became denser and denser as it struck the surface of Luo Zheng's body.
But the Moment of Valor was too effective against the power of fear. This attack from the giant eye did not affect Luo Zheng at all.
"Hong!"
Luo Zheng's sword pierced through the pupil of the giant eye.
The eyes were practically the weakest part of all living beings. The Eye Devil Clan was no exception.
But in the chaos created by the Eye Devil Clan, no living being dared to face their eyes. Otherwise, they would only be met with endless fear.
Under this sword, the pupil of the giant eye was directly cut open.
The pitch-black pupil dispersed, soaking in the surrounding white. It was a tragic sight.
"Wu wu …"
Under the pain, the giant eye quickly wanted to roll back, trying to drill deeper into the main peak.
But Luo Zheng would not give it a chance. He raised his sword and a white sword light flashed.
His right eye emitted a white light. That was another ability unique to the Eye Devil Clan.
"Nine Flash Split Light!"
Luo Zheng's sword light was sharp and fast. In the blink of an eye, it was unknown how many sword strikes he had made.
Sword marks were left on the surface of the giant eye!
These sword marks were rapidly healing, but the old sword marks did not close. New sword marks appeared on the surface of the eyeball again.
"Puff puff puff puff …"
After Luo Zheng made hundreds of sword strikes in a row, the giant eye finally stopped struggling. It was fixed in place, motionless.
After observing for a moment, Luo Zheng slowly approached the giant eye.
Currently, he could not determine what kind of existence this giant eye was. If the Eye Devil Clan was a Paramita Lifeform, it was impossible for it to appear in the Chaotic Origin Great World unless it was a Paramita Token.
But if this thing was a Paramita Token, who was the one who sent it here?
Just as Luo Zheng approached the giant eye, a blood light burst out once again. This blood light seemed to be burning. Even though Luo Zheng had the protection of the Moment of Heroism, he could still feel this heart-palpitating energy.
"It is burning itself?"
The Paramita Token could be used to squeeze out the most terrifying power through burning.
But the bearer would also die. Furthermore, the bearer would have to suffer an even more intense pain. Most people would not choose to do so unless they were at the end of the road.
However, just as the eyeball began to burn, a green silk cloth covered the eyeball, wrapping it tightly.
Luo Yan lifted up the silk cloth wrapping the giant eye. His finger flicked on it several times. At the same time, he spat out a single word, "Seal!" The giant eye was completely sealed by the silk cloth.
Following which, she said to Luo Zheng, "Brother, I want to pass this thing to Li Shan."
Saying that, she gently extended her finger and tapped on her sumeru ring, sucking this giant eye into her sumeru ring.
This time, after entering the Chaotic Origin Great World, it was Luo Yan's mission to deal with this Eye Devil.
Li Shan was also interested in this eye. After subduing it, Luo Yan would naturally report back to Fuxi.
If it were anyone else, it would not be so easy to take away this giant eye in front of Luo Zheng. But since Luo Yan had spoken, Luo Zheng would naturally not say another word.
At this moment, several loud explosions could be heard coming from outside. Luo Zheng then said, "Let's go out!"
The 30,000 meters wide blood marks created by the giant eye using the energy of fear had completely disappeared. After everyone was no longer restricted by the blood marks, they became much more agile.
For instance, those few Ear Mice were chased by the Spirit of Chaotic Origin. Since they could not defeat the Spirit of Chaotic Origin, the Ear Mice could hide within the arrays. The Spirit of Chaotic Origin could not do anything to them.

Chapter 3065
Amidst the chaos, another Chaotic Origin Spirit fell. As the Chaotic Origin Spirits devoured each other, they had strengthened themselves to an extremely powerful level.
But the speed of the Chaotic Origin Spirit was not considered fast. Even though its defense was astonishing, it could only become a moving target when facing the outstanding experts and geniuses of the various races.
A Moon Spirit clansman was currently enveloped in a faint light screen.
This light screen could distort light, allowing her to conceal herself completely.
If one did not use their Divine Consciousness to probe, it was hard to discover her existence.
When she saw that Chaotic Origin Spirit collapsing, she immediately drew an arc in the sky. Extending her hand, she kept a handful of Chaotic Origin Spirits into her Sumeru Ring.
She estimated that there were about 300 Chaotic Origin Spirits. It was sufficient for her to reach the Chaotic Origin Realm.
Her little brother was still within the array. It was too dangerous outside. Hence, she decided to give the extra 100 Chaotic Origin Spirits to her little brother.
This Moon Spirit clansman was filled with joy. She immediately rushed towards an array not far away.
Entering the array meant that she was absolutely safe. Furthermore, no one could discover her existence amidst the chaos!
Just as she was about a hundred meters away from the array, an enormous black figure enveloped her.
The Moon Spirit clansman felt something amiss. She subconsciously raised her head.
A huge Li Yuan clansman descended from the sky. He was holding a brown great hammer that was releasing a powerful Paramita Energy.
"Hong!"
The fleshy body of this Moon Spirit clansman was directly smashed apart by the Li Yuan clansman. The Li Yuan clansman extended his hand and grabbed her Sumeru Ring, throwing all the miscellaneous items on the ground. He then took away those Chaotic Origin Spirits before entering the array 30 meters away.
The little brother of that Moon Spirit clansman was hiding within the array. After personally witnessing everything, he was stunned.
The Li Yuan clansman glared at this Moon Spirit clansman with disdain, "This Chaotic Origin Spirit is killed by our Li Yuan Clan. A mere Moon Spirit clansman dares to snatch it away? You are simply seeking death!"
If not for the protection of the array, the Li Yuan clansman would have killed this Moon Spirit clansman too.
In front of a superpower, ordinary small clans did not have much authority to speak.
On another side.
Huo Ze brought out a violet vine.
The vine grew and multiplied endlessly. It revolved around a Chaotic Origin Spirit.
These vines were extremely tough. Blades and swords could not harm them at all. Even if the Chaotic Origin Spirit tore apart the vines, they could still grow back rapidly.
That Chaotic Origin Spirit continued roaring and howling. Yet it had nowhere to vent its energy. It could only struggle futilely.
Behind the Spirit of Chaotic Origin, Ling Shuang and her avatar wielded their swords at the same time.
Buzz!
Those sword lights were like silver clouds floating in the air.
All the sword lights continued to merge, from ten thousand to five thousand, from five thousand to two thousand, one thousand, five hundred …
In the end, it turned into a highly condensed sword light.
"Taiyi Lingxi Sword Slash!"
"Hua!"
This sword light went straight for the Chaotic Origin Spirit, striking its back.
"Boom!"
Ling Shuang was not too surprised that the unbreakable Spiritual Rhino Sword Slash could not break through the defense of the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
Before the Chaotic Origin Spirit became fatigued, its defense was unbreakable.
But the energy of the Spirit of Chaotic Origin would be rapidly depleted. After slashing a few more times, the crystal of the Spirit of Chaotic Origin would become fatigued. Naturally, Xue Ying could easily kill this Spirit of Chaotic Origin.
"Hong!"
After a round of attacks from Ling Shuang, the Rainbow Stone followed closely behind, striking the back of this Spirit of Chaotic Origin.
Even though Lan Qing's attacks were not as sharp as Ling Shuang and Feng Ge's, the frequency of her attacks was extremely dense. That Rainbow Stone naturally disappeared the moment it struck the Spirit of Chaotic Origin. Once again, it appeared in Lan Qing's hand. Following that, Lan Qing lightly tossed it over again.
By the side, Feng Ge also brought out his King Stone Hill, slashing out countless blazing sword lights …
At this moment, Chou Xue was in a slightly awkward situation. Previously, he had once again used the Life Slaughter Chain. Hence, he could only watch by the side and occasionally remind the others.
Under the continuous attacks of the group, this Spirit of Chaotic Origin finally became fatigued. Its huge body was dismembered, and the Chaotic Origin Spirit that fused into its body flowed all over the ground.
Seeing this amber-colored crystal, everyone's face revealed a trace of joy.
A single Spirit of Chaotic Origin actually contained 2,000 Chaotic Origin Spirit Crystals. No wonder this thing was so powerful.
But the moment they killed this thing, they would gain quite a bountiful harvest. The crystal of this Spirit of Chaotic Origin was sufficient for ten people to reconstruct their Chaotic Origin!
At the moment when they killed the Spirit of Chaotic Origin, three figures flew over.
These three were actually from the Divine Farmer Clan. Judging from their attire and appearance, they should be three brothers.
The three of them had been observing in the dark for a long time. At the moment when they saw this Spirit of Chaotic Origin falling, they took advantage of the moment when the others were concentrating their attention to rush over to the pile of Chaotic Origin Spirit.
"Big Brother! Keep! "
One of them with a small mustache said.
Another muscular man with a bare upper body waved his hand lightly, keeping all the Chaotic Origin Spirit Crystals on the ground into his Sumeru Ring.
"Search! Die! "
Feng Ge's eyes turned red when he saw this.
For the sake of shattering this Spirit of Chaotic Origin, the group of them had fought bitterly for half a day. How could they allow others to snatch it away?
Her eyes flashed with golden light. King Stone Hill's thousand left hands swung their swords at the same time.
"Myriad Swords!"
The thousand swords intertwined with each other, forming a dense sword net.
"Return to One!"
King Stone Hill's left hand slashed out at the same time.
This three-dimensional sword net was like a cage, imprisoning an even more frightening sword light. That sword light was fused with the Golden Crow Divine Fire, emitting a scorching aura as it blasted towards the three brothers.
It was not just Feng Ge who attacked. Ling Shuang had also activated her Taiyi Sword Art. Huo Ze, Lan Qing, the other Nine Li Tribesmen, and some other Heavenly Palace disciples who joined later also attacked the three brothers.
These three brothers had quite a history in the Divine Farmer Clan. Even though they were not as strong as "Shan" and "Mu Mu", they were still existences close behind.
Since they dared to snatch the Spirit of Chaotic Origin in front of the Heavenly Palace, they were naturally confident in their own abilities.
"Realm of Dark Shadow!"
The leader of the three brothers patted lightly on the top of his head. A jet-black gemstone floated out from the top of his head and revolved non-stop.
As this gemstone revolved, the bodies of the three gradually turned illusory.
"Stone of Dark Realm?" Chou Xue raised his eyebrows when he saw this gemstone.
This gemstone was a mysterious Nirvana Token. Once it was activated, it would allow the user and those around him to enter a unique state. Under this state, all attacks would be rendered useless.
"Hua hua hua …"
Countless sword lights pierced through their bodies. The bodies of the three brothers of the Divine Farmer Clan were like ripples on the surface of water. They merely rippled for a while and were unharmed!
Seeing this, the expressions of the Heavenly Palace disciples turned gloomy.
The three brothers of the Divine Farmer Clan revealed a smug smile. The leader said, "Let's go! We will be safe once we enter the array! "

Chapter 3066
After the three brothers of the Divine Farmer Clan entered the array, the disciples of the Heavenly Palace could only watch helplessly.
They could safely refine these Spirits of Chaotic Origin. They could even give the excess Spirits of Chaotic Origin to the other members of the Divine Farmer Clan.
Under the state of the Realm of Shadows, the three brothers were moving very slowly.
But no matter how the disciples of the Heavenly Palace attacked, they could not affect the three of them at all. It was only a matter of time before they entered the array.
Ling Shuang, Feng Ge and the others attacked several times. They had used various methods to no avail. Their expressions turned unsightly.
"It's useless!" The tallest of the three brothers laughed heartily as he said with a proud expression.
"The level of energy isn't high enough. We can't break the Realm of Shadows." Chou Xinghe appeared relatively calm.
Feng Ge said with a straight face, "What is the level of energy?"
Chou Xinghe stared at the three Divine Farmer Clan members who were constantly moving. After pondering for a moment, he said, "All the energy in the Primal Chaos can be explained as the flow of energy. But there are different levels of energy …"
Chou Xinghe did not have a deep understanding of this knowledge. But he still had a general understanding of the classification of energy levels.
True Origin was the lowest level of energy flow. The mortal cultivators in the Mother World mostly used True Origin in their inner worlds.
When True Origin was condensed to the extreme, it would be the Divine Path. The Dao Connotation of the Divine Path was a type of energy. Its efficiency of flow was much higher than True Origin. It was the second level of energy.
And the energy of the Paramita Token belonged to the third level of energy. It was also the most widely used level by the experts in the Mother World.
However, the Nirvana Realm tokens themselves possessed all sorts of strange abilities, especially the energy contained in the mysterious Nirvana Realm tokens that could reach a higher level. For example, the Realm of Shadows was actually not difficult to break. As long as one could touch it or even affect them, the Realm of Shadows would automatically dispel itself.
Some of the Nirvana Realm tokens had unique abilities that could break through the Realm of Shadows.
But the disciples of the Heavenly Palace did not have such abilities. They could only watch as the three brothers slowly drifted away.
At this moment, not only were the disciples of the Heavenly Palace angry, Qiu Yinhe, Lin Zhanting and the leaders of the other mountains who were at the entrance of the Chaotic Origin Great World were also furious.
They had just gotten the Chaotic Origin Spirit, yet it was snatched away by the Divine Farmer Clan just like that. How could they not be infuriated?
The leader of the Divine Farmer Clan sneered at Qiu Yinhe. This time, they could protect ten members of the Divine Farmer Clan to undergo the Chaotic Origin Reconstruction. How could they not be happy?
Just as the three brothers had moved two zhang away, Luo Zheng and Luo Yan circled around from the other side.
He had just sealed the Mind's Eye with Luo Yan. Seeing the murderous and helpless expressions on the faces of the Heavenly Palace disciples, he was slightly taken aback and asked, "What happened?"
"They have snatched away our Chaotic Origin Spirits!" Feng Ge angrily said.
"Snatch it?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. He flicked his Snow Sword lightly, and a cold light shot towards the three brothers.
"Sou!"
The sword light instantly pierced through the three of them. The figures of the three men flashed slightly, and the three brothers actually laughed loudly at the same time.
"This brat thinks he can hurt us!"
"What a fool!"
"Most of the Heavenly Palace disciples are ignorant and ill-informed. Forgive them!"
The three brothers were even more delighted. Their floating speed was originally very slow. When they saw Luo Zheng, they actually turned a corner and moved towards Luo Zheng. Their provocation was self-evident.
"Brother Luo Zheng, ordinary methods can't break this Realm of Shadows." Chou Xinghe reminded, "But it's said that the Realm of Shadows is very fragile and can be easily disturbed. That's because the Stone of Shadows obtained from the Temple of Shadows in the 13th Heaven is of the lowest grade."
"Hmph, even if it's the lowest grade Stone of Shadows, it's not something that trash from the Heavenly Palace can break." One of the three Divine Farmer Clan brothers snorted coldly.
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly. His right eye flashed with a trace of red light. After releasing his Terror Energy, he charged towards the three brothers.
"Terror Energy?"
"This brat knows it too?"
The three Divine Farmer Clan brothers were similarly startled when they felt the Terror Energy.
But the Terror Energy still could not disturb the Realm of Shadows. It directly passed through the bodies of the three brothers.
Luo Yan frowned slightly by the side. The crimson Wah Shadow was released from her body. Many thin red tentacles also lashed out at the three brothers. But it was to no avail.
Chou Xinghe sighed before saying, "The difference in energy level is huge. This Realm of Shadows cannot be broken."
"Energy level …"
Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes.
This was not the first time Luo Zheng heard of this saying. Previously in the Deity Realm, he had used the Truth Square Crystal to break Dongfang Chunjun's Yin Yang Furnace.
The reason why the energy of the Truth Square Crystal could break the Yin Yang Furnace was because its energy level was higher than the Yin Yang Furnace.
"Give up, Luo Zheng. Let's find other Chaotic Origin Spirits." Luo Yan said.
The biggest Chaotic Origin Spirit was being held back by You Xiong, Golden Crow, Shen Nong, and the other clans. There were still many other smaller Chaotic Origin Spirits. Even though killing the Chaotic Origin Spirits was troublesome, it was still better than wasting time here.
Feng Ge's gaze swept to the other side as he searched for a new target.
Looking at the three Divine Farmer Clan brothers walking past him with complacent expressions, Luo Zheng gently extended his hand. A small green square crystal appeared in his palm.
The three Divine Farmer Clan brothers stared at the surface of this green square crystal.
This green square crystal could be used to defend against enemies when Luo Zheng was still at the True God Realm.
But Luo Zheng lacked the bloodline of a Chaotic Ancient God. Hence, he could not produce more Truth Square Crystals. The pure strength of the "Falling Star Hand" was not enough.
The three brothers did not recognize the Truth Square Crystal. But after observing it, they did not discover any powerful energy within it. Hence, they did not panic.
Then Luo Zheng waved his hand downwards.
A green maelstrom appeared above the heads of the three brothers. As that maelstrom spiraled, a speck of green starlight came crashing down.
Seeing the power of the Falling Star Hand, Luo Zheng smiled bitterly in his heart. It was indeed too weak to be condensed from a piece of Truth Square Crystal.
Chou Xue. Feng Ge and the others were disapproving when they saw that starlight. The strength of that starlight was not even one-ten-thousandth of their full strength.
But when the crowd at the entrance of the Chaotic Origin Great World saw this emerald green energy, their expressions turned solemn.
"This is …"
"Is this an energy at the Truth Level?"
"It's very weak. But it is valuable."
Many leaders could determine the level of energy contained in the "Falling Star Hand".
When Qiu Yinhe saw this thing, he was shocked once again. He subconsciously looked at Lin Zhanting by the side.
They did not expect Luo Zheng to have this hidden card.
What was his relationship with the Chaotic Ancient God?
Fuxi stood on the peak of the snow mountain. When he saw the green light emitted by the Falling Star Hand, his expression did not change. He already knew this outcome when he was at the bottom of the Chaotic Origin Great World.

Chapter 3067
The three brothers of the Divine Farmer Clan were originally in an illusory state, as if they were three ghosts floating in the air.
The previous methods that the disciples of the Heavenly Palace had tried had passed through them without affecting them at all.
However, the Realm of Shadows also had a great disadvantage. They relied on the Realm of Shadows to maintain a delicate balance. If they were careless, the balance would be broken. Therefore, the three of them were moving very carefully and extremely slowly.
The green starlight fell down like a small stone thrown into a lake. It was so weak that it couldn't cause any real damage to the Realm of the Nirvana.
However, the starlight didn't pass through their bodies like other people's attacks. It hit the three brothers without a doubt.
"Bo!"
The moment the starlight bloomed, the three figures were like a broken mirror, blooming with cracks and fragments.
After the fragments were quickly peeled off from the surface of their bodies, the three brothers of the Divine Farmer Clan stumbled out of the Realm of Shadows.
The three brothers obviously didn't expect that the seemingly ordinary starlight could cause such an effect.
Feng Ge, Ling Shuang, and the others also widened their eyes. After all, the power of the Falling Star Hand was so weak that it was negligible. It was ridiculous that it could break the Realm of Shadows just like that.
"It's the level of energy …" Chou Xizhi murmured.
He was more confused than the others.
When he saw Luo Zheng take out the starlight, he didn't feel the movement of the Nirvana Power. Instead, he condensed a green square crystal. This square crystal wasn't the Taoist Connotation of the Divine Path, and it couldn't be the True Core Visualization. The only possibility was the bloodline.
However, the energy level of the bloodline power could generally only reach about level three. Some weak bloodline power had the same energy level as the Taoist Connotation of the Divine Path. What kind of bloodline did Luo Zheng have?
Chou Xizhi's conjecture was completed entirely by intuition, but it was infinitely close to the truth.
However, in Chou Xizhi's memory, the bloodline power of many superpowers couldn't have such a high energy level.
The three brothers of the Divine Farmer Clan were also dumbfounded.
Although they weren't weak in the Divine Farmer Clan, how could the three of them fight against so many disciples of the Heavenly Palace?
However, these three brothers were also outstanding members of the Divine Farmer Clan. When the Dark Shadow Realm was broken, one of them turned his hands over, and two Ghost Head Machetes appeared in his hands. Then he slashed the machetes toward Luo Zheng with both hands.
At this distance, he didn't fear anyone!
However, just as the broadsword was about to chop down on Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng's hands had already transformed into two black hands, which simultaneously stretched upwards. Clang! Clang! Following two crisp sounds, the blade was unable to penetrate any deeper into the web between Luo Zheng's thumb and forefinger.
"These hands …" The man's eyes almost popped out of their sockets.
Luo Zheng stared coldly at the other party. His Nirvana Power surged violently. With a sudden twist of his hands, the two broadswords were twisted into fried dough twists. If this person did not withdraw his hands in time, his arms would also be twisted into fried dough twists!
"Retreat!"
The three of them didn't have the slightest bit of delusion after their first attack failed. They quickly retreated in another direction.
Currently, the members of the Divine Farmer Clan were working together with other great clans to deal with the mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit. As long as they regrouped with their clansmen, the disciples of the Heavenly Palace would not dare to touch them.
But how could the people of the Heavenly Palace allow these three people to escape?
Feng Ge, Ling Shuang, Mo Yijian and the others acted almost at the same time. Chaotic sword rays formed a wall that enveloped the three people.
When the three people of the Divine Farmer Clan saw these sharp and powerful sword rays, they were so frightened that their faces turned pale. One of them even shouted, "I, I will return the Chaotic Origin Spirit to you. Don't, don't kill me!"
The disciples of the Heavenly Palace weren't three-year-old children. They knew clearly how valuable the Chaotic Origin Spirit was.
This was something that they had risked their lives to obtain. These fellows had snatched it away. Now that they were forced to reveal their true forms by Luo Zheng, they wanted to return it and write off the debt? There was no such thing in the world.
"Since you dared to snatch the Chaotic Origin Spirit of our Heavenly Palace, you should be prepared to lose your life," Luo Zheng said calmly as he stood on the spot. Now, there was no need for him to act personally.
Just as those sword rays formed a net that surrounded the three people and closed in, a figure descended from the sky and landed beside the three people.
"Earth Fiend Protection!"
This person spread out his hands, and an earthen-yellow mountain illusion spread out from his body. This illusion immediately enveloped him and the three brothers of the Divine Farmer Clan.
Ding, ding, ding, ding, ding …
All kinds of sword rays slashed on the mountain illusion, giving off a series of crisp sounds. It was as if someone was striking a huge bell.
This person was the Divine Farmer Clan's "Mountain".
"Disciples of the Heavenly Palace, now that we are facing a great enemy, why should we kill each other?" Mountain said calmly.
The origin of "Mountain" was very mysterious. Other than Mu Mu, he was the most powerful person in the Divine Farmer Clan.
When he was fighting with the main body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit, he saw his clansmen being besieged by the disciples of the Heavenly Palace. He immediately rushed over and blocked the attacks of the disciples.
"Kill each other? Does your Divine Farmer Clan have the face to say such words? " Feng Ge scolded sternly, "Our Chaotic Origin Spirit has been snatched away by these three people. Since they are seeking death, they can't blame us."
After being scolded by Feng Ge, Mountain frowned. He didn't expect the three brothers would do such a thing. He said calmly, "Zhao Chen, return the Chaotic Origin Spirit to them."
When the three brothers saw "Mountain" appeared at the crucial moment, they felt they had a backer. Their originally weakened arrogance became arrogant again.
The Divine Farmer Clan wasn't afraid of the Great Yi Heavenly Palace. Moreover, the Divine Farmer Clan had the backing of the You Xiong Clan, the Golden Crow Clan, and other clans. These disciples of the Heavenly Palace were seeking death by bullying them.
When Zhao Chen, the oldest of the three brothers, was about to mock Feng Ge, "Mountain" actually asked them to return the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
"Why should we return it to them? Are we afraid of them? "Zhao Chen, the oldest of the three brothers, said unwillingly.
Hearing that, killing intent flashed in the eyes of Luo Zheng, Luo Yan, Feng Ge, Ling Shuang, and the others. It seemed it was useless to talk nonsense with these fellas.
"I'll just ask you to return it. Why are you spouting nonsense?" Mountain's eyes were also filled with anger.
The younger generation of the Divine Farmer Clan was afraid of "Mountain". However, "Mountain" had a gentle personality. Nobody had ever seen him getting angry before.
After sensing the anger of "Mountain", the hearts of Zhao Chen and his brothers trembled slightly. They hurriedly poured out the Chaotic Origin Spirits from their spatial rings.
That Zhao Chen personally returned so many Chaotic Origin Spirits. Naturally, he felt heartache. But he was helpless.
Just as Mountain was about to bring the three brothers away, Luo Zheng's indifferent voice sounded, "Are you treating it as if nothing happened after returning these Chaotic Origin Spirits?"

Chapter 3068
Ever since they entered the Chaotic Origin Great World, You Xiong, Shen Nong, Golden Crow, and the other great clans had been hostile towards the disciples of the Heavenly Palace.
Luo Zheng had been offended by these clans time and time again. It was simply a dream to let this matter pass so easily.
"What do you want?" Mountain stared at Luo Zheng calmly.
"Leave three lives behind or take out an equivalent amount of Chaotic Origin Spirits." Luo Zheng said.
After killing this Spirit of Chaotic Origin, they had obtained a total of over 2,000 pieces of Chaotic Origin Spirits. It was sufficient for ten people to reconstruct their Chaotic Origin.
What Luo Zheng meant was for the Divine Farmer Clan to take out another 2,000 pieces of Chaotic Origin Spirits.
When Zhao Chen and his brothers heard Luo Zheng's request, their expressions changed once again. Compensating them with an additional 2,000 pieces of Chaotic Origin Spirits was worse than killing them!
"You are asking for an exorbitant price!"
"If you want us to compensate with so many Chaotic Origin Spirits, why don't you just rob us?"
"Dream on!"
The three brothers said agitatedly.
The expression of "Mountain" sank slightly.
Previously, the Divine Farmer Clan members had worked together to kill a Spirit of Chaotic Origin and obtained more than 2,000 pieces of Chaotic Origin Crystals. Furthermore, all of them were in the hands of "Mountain". He did have the ability to compensate them.
But there were many members of the Divine Farmer Clan too. If he were to hand over these Chaotic Origin Spirits to Luo Zheng, ten members of the Divine Farmer Clan would lose the chance to reconstruct their Chaotic Origin. This was a huge loss.
Luo Zheng's expression did not change at all.
Currently, everyone was fighting to kill the Spirit of Chaotic Origin. They did not have much time to be in a stalemate here.
But words spoken were like water spilled. If the Divine Farmer Clan was unwilling, they could not blame him for being heartless.
Even though Luo Yan did not say a word, she was still leaning on Luo Zheng.
Her brother's enemy was her enemy. And she represented the Nuwa Clan of Li Shan.
If Mountain were to act here, he would definitely implicate Li Shan …
"Mountain" was ultimately not a person who would hesitate. In just three breaths of time, he had already seen through the situation.
But now was not the time to be troubled over this. He immediately made his decision. With a flip of his hand, his Sumeru Ring flashed with light. The Chaotic Origin Spirits poured out from the ring, forming a pile.
Luo Zheng didn't have time to count them carefully, but the pile of heads was similar to that of Zhao Chen and his two brothers.
"Let's go," Shan said.
When the three brothers saw the piles of Chaotic Origin Spirits on the ground, their hearts ached so much that their faces turned green. But they were helpless. They could only glare viciously at Luo Zheng before leaving.
Chou Xinghe looked at the back of "Mountain" and said softly, "This person is quite decisive..."
Although as a disciple of the Heavenly Palace, Chou Xue knew very well that the clansmen of Shennong and the Golden Crow were extremely weak in the face of Youxiong, Chou Xue faintly felt that if the "Mountain" did not admit defeat, he would probably suffer a great loss.
Luo Zheng lightly extended his hand, causing an unseen energy to stick to all the Chaotic Origin Spirits. With a hook of his finger, those Chaotic Origin Spirits tunneled into his Sumeru Ring.
"We will distribute these Chaotic Origin Spirits in a while." Luo Zheng said.
He originally wanted to let Lan Qing, Huo Ze and the other Nine Li Clansmen enter the array first. After all, there were only a few outstanding disciples left in the Nine Li Clansmen. But thinking that the other Heavenly Palace disciples would definitely not accept it, it was better to distribute them later.
At this moment, a series of screams could be heard not far away. A Chaotic Origin Spirit leaped up, pouncing on two Celestial Wolf Clansmen. Its huge body had already turned them into meat paste.
In the past, the Chaotic Origin Great World was relatively friendly to some of the smaller clans.
Even if they were to wander around the outer perimeter, they could dig out bits and pieces of Chaotic Origin Spirits that could allow them to reconstruct their Chaotic Origin.
But this time, with all the Chaotic Origin Spirits gathered at the Main Peak, the cruel side of the Heavenly Palace was revealed.
To a superpower like the Great Yi Heavenly Palace, these Chaotic Origin Spirits were prey. But the smaller clans did not have the strength to hunt them down. They might even die if they were careless.
"Let's go!" Feng Ge said as he took the lead to fly over.
When the disciples of the Heavenly Palace saw this Chaotic Origin Spirit, their eyes brightened. This Chaotic Origin Spirit represented two to three thousand Chaotic Origin Spirits.
Luo Zheng and Luo Yan's figures flashed. They also chased after that Chaotic Origin Spirit.
The body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit was tough and powerful. But its speed was absolutely inferior. It could not resist against all sorts of attacks.
If it was in the Main Peak, it would be unparalleled when it worked with the huge eye. But under the situation where the Main Peak was destroyed and the huge eye was sealed, the Chaotic Origin Spirit was not that terrifying.
That Chaotic Origin Spirit was constantly roaring and struggling. Yet it could not injure any of the Heaven Palace disciples. It was like a powerful beast that was trapped in an invisible space by the group of cultivators, slowly whittling away the energy of the Chaotic Origin Spirit Crystal within its body.
In less than 20 to 30 breaths of time, this Chaotic Origin Spirit let out an ear-piercing roar. After waving its four limbs in the air for a while, it fell to the ground with a 'putong'. Feng Ge and Ling Shuang took the lead. Two sword lights slashed at the abdomen of the Chaotic Origin Spirit, causing the Chaotic Origin Spirit within its body to be completely crystallized.
Many crystals flowed out.
Many great clans were currently attacking these twenty to twenty meters tall Chaotic Origin Spirits. Every clan had some gains and accumulated quite a number of Chaotic Origin Spirit crystals.
But there was ultimately a limit to the number of Chaotic Origin Spirits. When they could no longer find any new Chaotic Origin Spirits, everyone focused their attention on the main body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
This main body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit was more than 3,000 meters tall. It was just like a mountain.
One could imagine how many Chaotic Origin Spirits were hidden within it!
The Li Yuan Clan, You Xiong, Golden Crow, Shennong, Nuwa, and several hundred other clansmen were currently fighting against this main body.
Many Golden Crows had turned into golden flames that revolved around the main body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit. From time to time, one could see golden sparks bursting out from the body of the main body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
Even though it was being attacked by so many people, the main body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit did not show any signs of fatigue. It remained ferocious and extraordinary.
After so many years of recuperation, this main body had already grown to a terrifying stage!
"All of you, die!"
The eyes of the main body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit were filled with hatred. It could not wait to devour all of these outsiders alive.
These fellows might be weak, and their bodies were negligible. But all of them were as cunning as mosquitoes. They flew here and there. The main body of the main body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit chased after them, yet all of its attacks were nimbly dodged.
After Luo Zheng led the disciples from the Heaven Palace to kill two Chaotic Origin Spirits, he turned his attention to the final main body. They would definitely get a share of the Chaotic Origin Spirit within the main body of the main body.
At this moment, the black fog by the horizon started surging once again.
"The wind is rising again?" Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes.
When everyone saw the surging black fog, their expressions changed.
When the wind rose, who knew where they would be blown to?
And the main body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit was not afraid of the wind. If they dug into the ground to hide, they would definitely die.
"Hide in the array!"
Someone regained his senses.
He shouted.

Chapter 3069
At the moment they tunneled into the array, the inscriptions inscribed on the ground flashed with golden light. An oval-shaped light barrier covered the top of everyone's head.
Many disciples of the great clans could freely travel within the light barrier. But everything within the Chaotic Origin Great World would be blocked outside, be it the hurricanes or the Chaotic Origin Spirits.
"Hu …"
After the majority of them tunneled into the array, the hurricane in the Chaotic Origin Great World started blowing again.
Some of the non-humans who could not enter the array in time were directly swept away by the hurricane. This time, they were blown away by the hurricane for several tens of thousands of kilometers.
The mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit was still enraged. The intense hurricane could not blow it away at all.
The plan of that enormous eye was simply laughable. It had nearly caused the death of all its clansmen, leaving behind only it.
Currently, it only wanted revenge. It wanted to kill as many as it could.
But those fellows were like damnable rats. The moment the hurricane came, they would scatter and hide within the array.
It was not that the Chaotic Origin Spirit Clan had never researched this array. Over the long years, the Chaotic Origin Spirits had tried to destroy this array. But this array was inscribed in the void and could not be destroyed.
The moment the array was activated, the light barrier formed would be able to withstand a powerful force.
The mother body had once attempted to destroy this light barrier. Yet it ended in failure.
But during these ten years, the mother body had fused with the other Chaotic Origin Spirits, greatly increasing its strength.
It moved its feet and walked towards the nearest magic formation.
"The mother body is coming over!"
"It is coming over here!"
"What are you panicking for? This array is set up by a powerful existence in the Mother World. It is indestructible! "
This array gathered the Cangling Clan. This clan lived by the river. They had a half-human half-snake body just like the Nuwa Clan. But they had scales as their legs and tentacles growing out from their faces. They looked powerful.
The Cangling Clan was still very confident in their arrays. Over the years, these arrays had never encountered any problems.
The mother body walked to the nearest array. It abruptly lifted a thick and muscular leg and stomped down on the array.
Seeing that enormous leg stomping down, the Cangling Clan members still shrank back. All the clansmen squeezed together. They were all nervous.
"Kuang!"
Even the whistling of the wind could not cover up this loud noise.
The light barrier merely shook slightly before returning to its original state.
"It's fine …"
"It can't break the array!"
"I already told you!"
The Cangling clansmen breathed a sigh of relief.
All of the foreign beings in the other formations were also relieved to see this.
If the main body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit could break apart the light barrier, that would be troublesome.
"It's alright now …"
"Let's make use of this period to split the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystals!"
After the various great clans killed those Chaotic Origin Spirits, they obtained quite a number of crystals.
These Spiritual Crystals of Chaotic Origin were sufficient for many cultivators to reconstruct their Chaotic Origin. In any case, now that the hurricane had started, they had nothing to do within the array. Hence, they might as well distribute the crystals.
Everyone from the Heaven Palace was also looking at Luo Zheng, Feng Ge, and Ling Shuang with anticipation. The Spiritual Crystals they obtained from killing the other party were all in the hands of the three of them.
"Let's start distributing them too." Feng Ge retracted his gaze from the distance.
"Mn." Luo Zheng nodded. Just as he was about to shake out the Chaotic Origin Spirit from his Sumeru Ring, he saw the Chaotic Origin Spirit raising its enormous leg once again. It stomped down on the light barrier formed by the array.
"Clang!"
Another loud noise was heard.
This sound attracted the attention of the crowd once again.
This time, the light barrier trembled once more before regaining its original state. But this time, the trembling of the light barrier was much more intense. It seemed as though it could not withstand the impact any longer.
Seeing this scene, everyone's expression turned solemn.
This meant that this indestructible array could be destroyed by brute force.
When the Chaotic Origin Spirit stomped down a second time, its body that was more than 3,000 meters tall leaped up once again before stomping down heavily once more.
The Cangling Race cultivators within the array were extremely nervous. Seeing this stomp coming down, several of the more alert Cangling Race cultivators had already tunneled out from the light barrier.
The hurricane outside the array was very strong. Just as these few Cangling Race cultivators tunneled out, they were immediately swept away by the hurricane.
The remaining Cangling Race cultivators were in a dilemma. Even though their clan was not considered a superpower, they were still a huge clan. These Cangling Race cultivators had high hopes of breaking through to the Chaotic Origin Realm.
But this time, they had collected too few Chaotic Origin Spirits. Their final hope was placed on this mother body. After all, the mother body had absorbed several tens of thousands of Chaotic Origin Spirits.
If they were swept away by the hurricane, it would be equivalent to them being eliminated from the competition for the Chaotic Origin Spirit!
They could only place their hopes on this array!
"Hong!"
The mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit heavily landed. That extraordinarily tough light barrier shattered like a bubble with a 'bo' sound.
That leg of the mother body stomped down on the body of the Cangling Race cultivators. Under the immense pressure, everything within the array was crushed into meat paste.
"The array … has been broken!"
"This time, everyone is in trouble!"
"What should we do?"
The miserable state of the Cangling Race cultivators left everyone at a loss.
The array was their final shelter. But now, this array could no longer protect them.
"Hahahaha …"
The mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit let out a carefree laughter, "Naive ants. Do you think you are safe just by hiding in the array! I'll let you know what despair is! "
"Dong dong dong dong …"
The mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit took a step forward and charged towards the next array.
The people gathered in the magic array were from some of the smaller clans.
When those non-humans saw the mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit charging over, they turned pale with fright.
With the previous example, why would they still dare to stay within the light barrier?
They saw the mother body running a few steps before leaping up and descending towards that array.
All the non-humans within the array charged out of the light barrier at the same time!
"Hong!"
The light barrier was once again shattered by the mother body.
These non-humans were quite decisive. This time, none of them were trampled flat by the mother body.
But the mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit was not only huge, its movements were agile too. It had predicted that the non-humans within the array would escape. Nevertheless, they could only drift along with the wind after charging out of the array.
The mother body slapped down a palm in the direction of the hurricane. Over ten non-humans were swept along by the hurricane. They could not resist at all. They could only watch as the palm of the mother body slapped down and slapped them onto the ground.
"Dong dong dong dong …"
Following a series of dull thuds, several blood flowers bloomed on the ground.
Seeing the miserable state of those non-humans, the breathing of all the clansmen within the array hastened. They began discussing how to deal with the situation.

Chapter 3070
When the non-humans in the third array saw the mother body rushing over, why would they have any thoughts of staying behind?
Under such circumstances, survival was the only thing they could think of.
Those non-humans rushed out of the array almost immediately. At the same time, they executed their movement techniques and escaped with the wind. They were afraid that they would die in the hands of the mother body if they were too slow.
The mother body did not care whether those people escaped or not. It simply destroyed the third array.
At this moment, the few great clans were estimating their position and where the mother body would choose to hide.
The arrays in the core region were arranged densely like stars in the sky. But when the hurricane came, all the clansmen found a temporary array to protect themselves.
The Eared Mouse Clan, You Xiong, Golden Crow Clan, and other clans were further away from the mother body. But the arrays where the disciples of Great Yi Heaven Palace and the Nine Li Clansmen were located were closer to the mother body.
But the hurricane had just started. In the time it took for an incense stick to burn, the mother body had destroyed all the arrays that hid people!
For a moment, everyone became anxious. The leaders outside the Chaotic Origin Great World were also anxious.
"Li Shan still does not intend to act under such circumstances?"
"He even destroyed the arrays used to refine the Chaotic Origin Spirit. This, this is too unreasonable!"
"If they stay in the arrays, they would most likely die!"
Those leaders were clamoring. But Li Shan still did not appear. He did not intend to act against the mother body in the Chaotic Origin Great World.
In the blink of an eye, the fourth, fifth, and sixth arrays were destroyed.
With the precedent set by the Azure Spirit Clan, the non-humans behind became smarter. Knowing that they could not resist, they chose to retreat.
But those superpowers at the back were getting more and more anxious.
It was still fine if all the arrays were destroyed by the mother body.
But if the arrays they were in were destroyed and the hurricane stopped, the remaining clans would kill the mother body and divide it. That would be bad.
These great clans did not care about where they were. Their hearts were fluctuating intensely.
Seeing the mother body moving towards the Great Yi Heaven Palace, the disciples and Nine Li Clansmen were also in a mess.
"It seems we have to retreat from the arrays too …" Feng Ge frowned. His eyes were flickering.
"Don't panic. Perhaps the mother body will not come over to us." Chou Xue consoled.
The mother body was furious. It was stomping everywhere. Previously, it even missed an array.
However, the luck that he had thought he would die with did not appear …
When the mother body crushed the thirteenth array, it was already heading straight for the Greater One Heavenly Palace's array.
The Nuwa Clan of Li Mountain was in a formation nearby. Luo Yan looked at Luo Zheng from within the formation. Her face was filled with worry.
But under such circumstances, she couldn't help at all.
"Dong, dong, dong …"
The body of the mother stomped on the ground, causing the ground to tremble.
The members of the You Xiong Clan and the Divine Farmer Clan, who were further away, were also gloating.
"Hey! The Heavenly Palace is finished! "
"Be a good girl and be blown away by the hurricane! The crystallization within the Mother Chaotic Origin Spirit belongs to our Shennong! "
"After the disciples of the Heavenly Palace escape, it will be Li Shan's turn! The time it takes for half an incense stick to burn has already passed, and the situation is in our favor! "
You Xiong, Golden Crow, Shennong, and the other clans' disciples discussed.
This was especially true for the three Zhao brothers of the Divine Farmer Clan. Zhao Chen, who was their leader, said hatefully, "Is there any way to prevent them from leaving the Spell Array? It would be better if they were trampled to death by the mother body! "
"Shan" glanced at Zhao Chen calmly and replied, "There is no way."
Under such circumstances, it was an unsolvable trap. Other than escaping, there was no other way. As for trapping the disciples of the Heavenly Palace in the Spell Array, it was impossible.
Seeing the mother body getting closer and closer, Ling Shuang's pupils shrank slightly. She said in a low voice, "We have to retreat!"
"It will be too late if we don't leave now!" Huo Ze said anxiously.
They didn't want to repeat the same mistake.
When all the disciples of the Heavenly Palace were ready to rush out of the Spell Array, Luo Zheng said calmly, "Wait a little longer."
"Don't tell me you want to lure it away …" Chou Xue had already guessed Luo Zheng's plan.
Among all the clansmen in the core area, only Luo Zheng could avoid the horrifying hurricane.
If Luo Zheng could lure the mother body away, it would be a good choice.
"Yes," Luo Zheng nodded.
"How?" On the side, Mo Yijian looked puzzled. Even Ling Shuang's big eyes were staring at Luo Zheng, puzzled.
Ling Shuang didn't know that Luo Zheng could avoid the hurricane, nor did most of the disciples of the Heavenly Palace.
In the battle just now, it was Luo Zheng who led them all the way. Everyone had a natural trust in Luo Zheng, trusting that he could come up with a solution at the critical moment.
But the situation in front of them was simply impossible to deal with …
Many of the major clans were staring at the movements of the Heavenly Palace. According to their estimation, in one or two breaths of time, the disciples of the Heavenly Palace would rush out of the Spell Array, and they would be the first super force to be driven out of the Spell Array.
But after a breath of time, the disciples of the Heavenly Palace did not rush out of the Spell Array.
Only one person rushed out of the light shield, and that was Luo Zheng.
The other disciples of the Heavenly Palace were still standing in the same place, motionless.
"What is the Greater One Heavenly Palace doing?"
"Do they want to die?"
"Maybe they are unwilling? But what can they do when there is no other way? If they don't accept their fate, they will die! "
Amidst everyone's puzzlement, Luo Zheng had already passed through the light shield.
At the moment he rushed out of the light shield, a piece of feather appeared above his head. It was a green feather that looked like it was carved from jade.
"Hu …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng was not affected by the hurricane at all. He went against the wind and welcomed the incoming Chaotic Origin Spirit. The edge of his snow-white sword flashed with a sharp light.
"Shua!"
That sword light arced through a distance of several dozen meters before slashing down on the body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
The sword light could not injure the body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit at all. But it succeeded in attracting the attention of the body.
"Dong, dong."
The body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit actually stopped. Its pair of huge eyes stared at Luo Zheng.
It was slightly puzzled.
So many ants had been blown away by the hurricane. Why was this ant the only one who could move freely within the hurricane?
And he even dared to slash at it?
"Human little ant! Die! "
The body of the body of the mother twisted. Two enormous palms were slapping down on Luo Zheng's head.
But how could someone as agile as Luo Zheng be slapped by the body of the mother?
He was truly like a mosquito spreading its wings. With a slight push of his body, he dodged the two palms of the mother. Once again, he drew his sword and slashed at its head.
As for those clansmen from the superpowers within the array, they were all stunned when they saw this.
They had never imagined that Luo Zheng would use such a method to lure away the mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit!
Especially "Mountain", Di An and Mumu had an unprecedented solemn expression on their faces.

Chapter 3071
Under everyone's gaze, Luo Zheng's body was like a piece of paper. His speed was fast and light.
Even though the strength of the mother body was frightening, it was hard for even the experts of the other races to capture it, much less the nimble Luo Zheng.
Ling Shuang's eyes were shining. She looked gratified.
Luo Zheng's move was crucial. Otherwise, the Great Yi Heavenly Palace would have lost this opportunity.
Those leaders outside the Chaotic Origin Great World were also attracted by Luo Zheng's figure.
They had never experienced such a strong hurricane before. Hence, they did not know how frightening this hurricane was.
But even the banshees from the Divine Nest and those other races who were good at flying had to avoid it. One could see that this hurricane could not be disobeyed.
Luo Zheng could move so freely in the wind. It should be because of that jade-like feather. But no one could see the origin of that feather.
"Sou …"
As Luo Zheng was dealing with the mother body, a voice transmission entered his ears. It was Chou Xue.
"Brother Luo Zheng, lure the mother body to the You Xiong Clan." Chou Xue suggested.
You Xiong and the other three clans were targeting the disciples of the Heavenly Palace. Including Mo Yijian, almost all the remaining disciples of the Heavenly Palace had been attacked by the four clans. Many disciples of the Heavenly Palace had died in their hands. It was just that they did not witness it personally.
Now was the best time to take revenge. How could they let it go?
"That is my intention!"
Luo Zheng's figure turned around in the hurricane. As he drifted with the wind, he was already flying towards the You Xiong Clan.
The You Xiong Clan and the Min Yue Clan shared an array. As for the Golden Crow and the Divine Farmer Clan, they each occupied an array.
Seeing Luo Zheng rushing towards them, Di An and the others immediately understood Luo Zheng's intention.
He wanted to rely on the mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit to drive them away.
"Stinky brat from the Heavenly Palace, how dare you!"
"If you dare to come out, I'll definitely kill you!"
"Are you seeking death?"
You Xiong and the Divine Farmer Clan members scolded.
The nearby members of the Liyuan Clan all had mocking expressions on their faces. They were very happy to watch such a show.
Facing the threats from the few superpowers, Luo Zheng's look didn't change at all. Instead, he directly landed in the magic formation of You Xiong Family.
"Dong dong dong dong …"
The ground kept shaking as the mother body ran all the way. It also followed closely behind Luo Zheng.
"Stinking brat, get out of here!" Di An's face was cold, as if he wanted to eat Luo Zheng alive.
The other You Xiong Family people also pointed at Luo Zheng's nose and cursed.
However, Luo Zheng stood with his hands clasped behind his back, looking unmoved. He faced these humans with a faint smile on his face.
Unfortunately, they couldn't fight inside the array, so they couldn't do anything to Luo Zheng.
Seeing the mother getting closer and closer, Di An had no choice but to say, "Everyone, let's retreat!"
Since Luo Zheng intentionally lured the mother body over, this array would definitely be crushed by the mother body. In order to save his life, Di An could only disperse the people.
Even though the You Xiong Family people were unwilling, what choice did they have? They could only glare at Luo Zheng with hatred. They passed through the light barrier and were blown away by the hurricane.
Di An held onto his spear and leaped out of the light barrier.
But the moment he left the light barrier, the spear in his hand flashed and stabbed towards the ground.
"Ka!"
A sharp light shot out from the tip of his spear and penetrated the ground.
Di An was the strongest person below the Chaotic Origin Realm in You Xiong Family. He was arrogant and proud, and his strength couldn't be underestimated.
This sharp light was like an extension of his arm. The moment it penetrated the ground, it split into two, two into four, four into eight. It was like a constantly splitting tree root, extending endlessly underground.
In just a few breaths of time, it had turned into thousands of sharp lights rooted in the ground.
And these sharp lights were connected to the tip of his spear, firmly fixing him on the ground!
"Good technique!"
"He can actually resist the hurricane?"
"But he is still inferior to Luo Zheng …"
Even though the hurricane was strong, there were still some capable people who could find a way to evade it.
For instance, Di An was already outstanding. He could attract the attention of everyone.
But now, a Luo Zheng appeared and snatched away his limelight …
Luo Zheng did not need any external items. He could completely evade the hurricane. He was many times more brilliant than Di An.
"Dong!"
The mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit stepped down on the light barrier. At this moment, only Luo Zheng remained within the array.
Before the mother body stepped down, Luo Zheng's figure flashed and tunneled out of the array. At the same time, he exchanged glances with Di An, revealing a crafty smile.
Seeing Luo Zheng's smile, Di An's heart sank.
Even though he could barely stabilize his body with his spear, he could not move at all. This brat shouldn't be …
Halfway through his thoughts, Luo Zheng's figure lightly floated and once again charged towards Di An.
"Damn!"
Di An was extremely decisive. The moment he kept his spear, he turned around and pierced out a spear light at Luo Zheng.
This spear light was a helpless revenge. It was destined that it could not pierce through Luo Zheng. And he was also blown away by the hurricane.
But when Di An was blown away by the hurricane, he once again pierced his spear into the ground. The spear light extended endlessly and penetrated into the ground, fixing him once again.
Luo Zheng did not have the time to find trouble with Di An. He continued guiding the mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit to the next array.
When the demonesses of the Golden Crow Clan saw Luo Zheng rushing over, their expressions turned ugly. But they simply flapped their wings and flew away along with the hurricane …
Luo Zheng's figure suddenly stopped in midair. He looked to his left. There was only one Divine Farmer Clan member left.
Outside the Chaotic Origin Great World, there were Bears, Golden Crows, and the leaders of the Divine Farmer Clan. They were so angry that their noses were crooked.
They had made a plan and issued a name list to kill Luo Zheng and Feng Ge. In the end, not only did they not get anything, but they also suffered a great loss because of this brat.
Especially Phoenix, who was staring at Luo Zheng, who was flying nimbly, as if she wanted to swallow Luo Zheng alive.
"Mountain" stood in the array. When he saw Luo Zheng flying over, he closed his eyes slightly and said, "My fellow clansmen, please retreat first."
The clansmen of the Divine Farmer Clan nodded and left the array.
"Little Uncle, what about me?" Mumu, who was sitting on one side, asked.
"Just help me," Mountain said.
Hearing Mountain's words, Mumu's eyes lit up.
She knew her Little Uncle and Luo Zheng, as well as that guy called Evil Deities, would fight sooner or later.
But her Little Uncle's character was very steady. He usually put the overall situation first. When he was not sure of winning, he would choose to retreat.
Now, her Little Uncle could not stand it anymore …
"Okay!" Mumu showed a playful smile and reached out to gently pick up her hair. Her curly hair rose up in a circle.

Chapter 3072
Those steel needle-like curly hairs coiled around the body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
Previously, Mumu had used this method to pester the mother body.
These hairs were extraordinarily tough and could not be torn apart with brute force.
The attention of the mother body was no longer on Mumu and "Mountain". It was focused on smashing Luo Zheng into pieces and did not care about the two from the Divine Farmer Clan.
"It's done!"
As Mumu's hair coiled around the body of the mother body, some strands of hair coiled around the body of "Mountain".
"Let's go out."
After saying that, Mountain carried Mumu and walked out of the light barrier.
"Shua!"
A hurricane blew the two of them into midair.
The originally loose strands of hair were straightened under the force.
Under the force of inertia, they revolved around the mother body. With this swing, they charged towards Luo Zheng accurately.
As they approached Luo Zheng, a strand of golden light flashed in the eyes of "Mountain". A heavy aura burst out from him.
"Ho!"
As he roared, a pitch-black spiral appeared above his head.
"Extreme Nether Realm!"
As this pitch-black spiral rotated, it produced a strange gravitational force.
The gravitational force itself was not strong. But the laws contained within could directly fold the surrounding space!
Spatial techniques were forbidden in the Chaotic Origin Great World. Nobody could perform Greater Teleportation here. All spatial techniques could not be used here.
But "Mountain" had actually broken this rule!
"Hong!"
The space within a radius of three meters collapsed towards that pitch-black spiral.
Under this spatial folding of three meters, the surrounding space was affected radially.
The space 30 meters away from the black spiral was forcefully compressed by 30 meters. Similarly, the entire core region was compressed by one meter towards the spiral. And the entire Chaotic Origin Great World was also reduced by this one meter. With the black spiral as the center, it was shifted by one meter.
Even the entire Mother World, the entire Primal Chaos, was shifted!
This distance of three meters was negligible to the Chaotic Origin Great World. To the entire Mother World, it was impossible to measure.
In the entire Snow Drinking Continent, only Fuxi was sitting on the peak of the snow mountain. He could sense the spatial folding …
He stared at "Mountain" nonchalantly. A trace of disdain could be seen in his eyes.
"You can't hold it in anymore? Using a Truth Level technique on a junior who hasn't even reached the Chaotic Origin Realm, he is truly shameless … "Fuxi muttered.
Even so, Fuxi had no intention of lending a helping hand.
In Fuxi's eyes, everything was determined by fate.
If Luo Zheng died in the hands of "Mountain" and "Evil Deities", it could only be said that "Ruins" was blind.
The mission that he had been seeking for his entire life had actually fallen into the hands of such a little fellow. He was originally unwilling.
Many leaders standing at the entrance of the Chaotic Origin Great World revealed solemn expressions when they saw that black spiral.
"What kind of technique is that …" Phoenix exclaimed.
The innate talent of the Golden Crow Clan was the God Flame. But the demonesses born from the Divine Nest specialized in spatial techniques. For instance, Phoenix had cultivated the True Meaning of Void Illusory Extermination to the Paramita Realm. It was the same for the other demonesses. It was also because of this reason that the Golden Crow Clan's strength was reduced in the Chaotic Origin Great World.
Like Phoenix, who could use her Illusory Extermination Projection to kill her enemies in an extremely distant space, her understanding and attainments in the Dao of Space were definitely not something that ordinary people could compare with.
But when she saw that black spiral, her heart trembled violently.
She could not understand the profound mysteries of space condensed within!
The leader of the Divine Farmer Clan did not have much of a change in expression when he saw the technique of "Mountain".
"Mountain" was a unique existence in the Divine Farmer Clan. Even though he was considered a member of the younger generation, his status was extraordinary. Even Lie Shan Clan's Shennong had to be polite to "Mountain". It was not hard to guess what kind of existence such a character was.
"Luo Zheng, be careful!"
Qiu Yinhe, Lin Zhanting and the others were also shocked.
Previously, Luo Zheng could easily deal with all kinds of techniques.
But that technique of "Mountain" was something that even Qiu Yinhe could not understand. The power contained within was unimaginable.
Why would such a technique appear on a young clansman?
Luo Zheng was extremely close to this black spiral. His entire body was trapped within this 3 feet space.
Other than Luo Zheng, half of the hand of the Chaotic Origin Spirit's main body had been engulfed!
"Ka!"
That half of the hand was actually torn apart under the spatial folding!
The Li Yuan Clan, the Eared Mouse Clan, the disciples of the Heavenly Palace and the various other clans exclaimed when they saw this.
"Ah ah! How could this be possible? "
"The crystallization of the Chaotic Origin Spirit within the body of the mother has never entered a state of fatigue. How could it be torn apart? Impossible! "
"This is already defying common sense …"
The Chaotic Origin Spirit was an existence that was close to being indestructible.
In order to destroy a Chaotic Origin Realm expert, one had to cause the Chaotic Origin Spirit within the other party to enter a state of fatigue. Before that happened, all attacks would not be able to cause any damage to it.
The mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit still looked energetic and did not show any signs of fatigue.
Before this, the fleshy body of the mother body could not be damaged. Not even a tiny wound could be left behind.
However, the black spiral gently sucked half of the mother's hand off. Such a method could be said to have stunned every living being present.
Within one of the arrays, there was a pitch-black shadow standing behind an outsider.
This shadow stuck closely to the outsider. Yet no one within the arrays discovered it.
The Evil Deities were hidden here.
When he saw that black spiral, his eyes dilated slightly.
Before this, other than Luo Zheng, no one else in the entire Chaotic Origin Great World could enter his eyes. That included that little sister of Luo Zheng, Luo Yan.
Now that he saw the technique of this "Mountain", his expression turned solemn.
But this solemn expression only lasted for a breath of time before it was replaced by intense interest. He had discovered a new prey.
"Xiu xiu xiu xiu …"
The black spiral folding space did not last for long.
The spiral spiraled for two to three breaths of time. After folding the space of 3 meters, it completely disappeared. Be it the black spiral, that half of the hand of the mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit or Luo Zheng, it was as if they did not exist at all.
"Wu wu … ao!"
The mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit roared as it held onto half of its hand. It had never thought that anyone could injure it.
Under the pain, it turned its anger onto the "Mountain" and Mumu.
"Retreat."
The Mountain only said a single word.
The hair on Mumu's head began flashing with a faint luster. This hair was still tied to the body of the mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit. Yet it was rapidly extending.

Chapter 3073
Mountain and Mumu floated high up in the sky, dancing in the intense hurricane.
The Chaotic Origin Spirit tried to pull the two down from above, but every time it pulled Mumu's hair, Mumu's hair would become longer.
This way, the mother could not exert any strength and could not pull the two down.
"Hu hu …"
Within the hurricane, Mumu revealed a curious look and asked, "Little Uncle, is that guy dead?"
"Theoretically, he is dead," Mountain answered expressionlessly.
"What do you mean theoretically …" Mumu was puzzled.
"After that space was folded, he was completely exiled by me," Mountain answered.
Mumu pursed her lips and said, "Spatial exile is not a difficult technique, right? Can you kill him? "
The space in the Mother World was stable. True Gods who cultivated the Space Divine Dao might not be able to tear the space apart and exile their opponents. However, it was not difficult for Nirvana Realm experts who cultivated the True Meaning of Void and Illusory Destruction.
And existences at their level were not afraid of being exiled. As long as they had enough strength, they could tear apart the space that imprisoned them and easily get out.
"That's not what I meant," Mountain answered calmly and patiently, "After this space was folded by me, it completely disappeared. The entire chaotic space is actually missing this one zhang long and wide. Do you understand?"
Ordinary spatial divine abilities could tear apart a space. In reality, there was still a void in this space. Furthermore, the extensibility of space was very strong. Not long after, the surrounding space would be squeezed to the center and gradually recover. Sometimes, space was like water. It was constantly flowing.
But Mountain had directly deleted a space. This involved the laws and means at the bottom of the entire chaotic space. It was indeed difficult for existences like Mumu to understand.
"I don't understand." Mumu shrugged. She was confused but still asked, "Then this brat should not be able to come back, right?"
"Not necessarily," Mountain said again.
"…" Mumu was speechless.
She knew that her Little Uncle's strength was extremely terrifying. He would either not make a move or kill in one strike.
Just now, when her Little Uncle chose to make a move in the array, Mumu knew that Luo Zheng was doomed.
It seemed so now, but her Little Uncle actually said not necessarily. After going back and forth, Mumu felt dizzy.
"He's no ordinary man," Shan said.
"Of course, I know." Mumu cursed in his heart. His Little Uncle could deal with an ordinary person before he could do anything.
"He used [Falling Star Hand] just now" Mountain continued to explain: "That is a Truth level Thaumaturgy, although it is very weak, it is the real deal. Luo Zheng is an inheritor recognized by 'Ji'"
"What is Ji?" Mumu blinked.
"Primal Chaos Ancient God," Mountain replied.
Hearing this answer, Mumu raised his eyebrows, and there was a surprised look in his eyes.
"It's not easy to kill such a person," Shan continued.
In fact, "Shan" was very confident in his ability. In theory, Luo Zheng no longer existed, but he still wouldn't make a promise.
The faces of many disciples of the Heavenly Palace were full of anxiety.
"What's wrong with Luo Zheng? I can't even sense his soul imprint! "Feng Ge frowned.
"He should have used a space-related magical power to exile Luo Zheng," Ling Shuang deduced.
Ling Shuang didn't expect "Shan's" ability to be so unique. She was worried, but she was much calmer than Feng Ge.
Chou Xue shook his head and said, "No, that's not an ordinary method of exile! The Chaotic Origin Great World forbids all space-related magical powers. It's hard to imagine a space-related magical power that could bypass this restriction. "
With Chou Xue's experience, he naturally couldn't understand the origin of spatial folding. But this didn't stop him from making his own deductions.
"Then wouldn't Big Brother Luo Zheng …" Lan Qing suddenly had an ominous premonition. Tears were already rolling in her eyes.
"Little Sister Qing, don't be anxious. Let's just wait and see. Young Master isn't an ordinary person. He naturally has methods to return," Huo Ze consoled.
In the eyes of the Nine Li Tribe members, Luo Zheng was their only hope. This hope must not be extinguished.
Huo Ze naturally wasn't as knowledgeable as the Heavenly Palace disciples. But he was certain that Luo Zheng would return!
In another array, Luo Yan stood under the light barrier. His tea-colored eyes were filled with a complicated light. Worry, anger, sadness, and all sorts of emotions were mixed together.
"Little Sister Yan, your brother will be fine …" Ling Yun consoled by the side.
Luo Yan had always been cheerful in Li Mountain. This was the first time Ling Yun saw such an expression on Luo Yan's face.
"I hope …" Luo Yan muttered.
This trip to the Chaotic Origin Great World had been proceeding according to the script.
In Luo Yan's eyes, the only one who could have an accident was that Evil Deities. Hence, Luo Yan had always been wary of that fellow.
Others might not be able to detect the existence of Evil Deities, but Luo Yan could vaguely sense his location.
But man proposes, God disposes. This tall and sturdy young man from the Divine Farmer Clan had never revealed himself. But the moment he acted, he made her brother disappear. For a moment, she found it hard to accept …
What made Luo Yan even more worried was the terrifying destructive power of the folded space.
During the process of folding space, he actually ignored the existence of the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystal and 'bit' off half a hand of the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Mother. Was this an ordinary spatial divine ability?
"Hu …"
An incense's time was up.
The hurricane in the Chaotic Origin Great World gradually stopped.
That strand of hair that was several hundred thousand meters long wrapped around the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Mother let out a faint luster before dissipating silently.
Mu Mu and Shan descended from 30,000 meters high …
All the clansmen and disciples in the core region rushed out of the array at the first moment.
The Chaotic Origin Spiritual Mother was the final piece of fatty meat!
If it was a one-on-one battle, no one in the core region could defeat the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Mother. It was even hard for them to consume the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystal within it.
But it was different when they attacked together.
Some could delay the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Mother, making it less agile. Some could restrict its speed. There were even some who could weaken the strength of the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Mother. These endless methods made the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Mother less frightening.
Di An also pulled out his spear and charged towards the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Mother.
Even though Luo Zheng had disappeared because of 'Shan', he still had many competitors. Be it Ear Mouse, the Li Yuan Clan, or several other superpowers, they could not be underestimated.
More importantly, the members of the You Xiong Clan had been blown away by the hurricane. It would take some time for them to rush back from a distance of several tens of thousands of kilometers.
Just as Di An charged towards the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Mother, a petite figure blocked his path.
