Luo Yan's long hair fluttered in the wind. A layer of crimson energy floated around her black hair. That little face of hers was filled with coldness.
"Heh, little girl …" Di An did not place Luo Yan in his eyes.
But just as he spoke, he heard Luo Yan spitting out a single word angrily, "Scram!"
This word was like a huge hammer hammering down on Di An's chest.

Chapter 3074
No one dared to look down on Li Shan, but many people were surprised that Luo Yan sent Di An flying with only one move.
After all, Di An represented the most powerful person in the You Xiong Clan. Even if he was careless because he looked down on Luo Yan, it was still too unreasonable.
After repelling Di An, Luo Yan did not stop. She flipped in the air and flew straight upward, where Mumu and Mountain landed.
"This woman …"
A strange smile appeared on Mumu's face. The hair on her head coiled up and turned into a dark cloud, covering Luo Yan.
These curly hairs looked extremely soft, but in fact, they were full of killing intent.
Once caught by these hairs, Mumu had a hundred ways to twist the other party into pieces the size of a finger.
Facing the attack of this black cloud of hair, Luo Yan did not show the slightest fear on her face. She went forward to meet it.
"Holy Words!"
"Break!"
"Break!"
"Cut!"
When Luo Yan said the word "cut", the hair on Mumu's head was instantly cut into countless pieces and flew all over the sky.
How could Mumu's hair be so easy to be cut off?
A thought flashed through her mind. These broken hairs seemed to have spirituality. They kept connecting with each other and were about to return to their original state. However, with Luo Yan's "break" sound, the spirituality hidden in the hair was naturally destroyed.
When the sound of "slashing" was heard, Mumu's face trembled. She felt an extremely sharp slash coming from the void. If she was hit by the slash, she would definitely die.
While Mumu was still in a daze, Mountain grabbed Mumu and threw him backward. At the same time, he said, "You are no match for her. Let me do it."
Mountain stood in front of Mumu, so the invisible slash was naturally aimed at Mountain.
Mountain's face was calm. A burst of yellow light appeared on his body, and he punched at a strange angle in the air.
"Boom!"
As the muffled sound exploded in everyone's ears, they quickly regained their calm. It was very clear that Luo Yan's final invisible "slash" had been neutralized by the mountain.
"You are no match for me. Don't waste your time," Shan said, looking down at Luo Yan.
Within the Chaotic Origin Great World, the competition between the various great clans was intense. But most of the other races would intentionally or unintentionally avoid Li Shan.
This was ultimately the home ground of the Nuwa Clan. If they bullied the members of Li Shan too much, it would be hard for them to explain themselves after leaving the Chaotic Origin Great World.
But Mountain's vision was far beyond ordinary people. He could see that Luo Yan was not ordinary.
If he really killed Luo Yan here, he was afraid that it would cause some unnecessary trouble.
Mumu, who had been thrown out by "Mountain", turned back, and her face was full of hatred.
It was hard for any woman to accept losing to a woman who was more beautiful than herself. "Little Uncle, she attacked me. Kill her!"
Mumu thought that "Mountain" would definitely agree to her request. But Mountain shook his head and said, "I can't kill her. Before I kill her, the members of Li Shan will definitely barge into the Chaotic Origin Great World to interfere."
"Li Shan will break the rules for her?" Mumu said with a cold look.
"If it was in the past, he would not. But this time, he will." Mountain said with certainty.
After the Chaotic Origin Great World was established, Li Shan and the various clans in the Mother World had always obeyed the agreed rules. This was also the foundation that allowed the Chaotic Origin Great World to survive for so many years.
But this was the final opening of the Chaotic Origin Great World. Even if they broke the rules, they would not have any worries.
This was the logic of "Mountain".
What he wanted was to maximize the benefits. He was not like Mumu and Di An who were emotional.
Luo Yan would naturally not retreat just because of a single sentence from "Mountain". She said sternly: "Let go of my big brother. Otherwise, I will not let this matter rest!"
"Sou sou sou …"
The moment she finished speaking, dozens of figures flew over.
Ling Yun and the Six Snake Shadow Sons circled around Luo Yan with vigilant expressions. The other Nuwa Clan members were also glaring at "Mountain" carefully. This man might be powerful, but it was impossible for him to fight against the entire Nuwa Clan by himself!
"Sou!"
"Sou!"
Feng Ge, Ling Shuang, Lan Qing, Huo Ze, Chou Xinghe, and other disciples of the Heaven Palace also rushed over. They surrounded the other side of "Mountain".
Even though Feng Ge and Ling Shuang understood that it was hard to force "Mountain" to release Luo Zheng, they still had to try. Furthermore, You Xiong, Golden Crow, Shen Nong, and other clansmen had been blown away by the hurricane. Only "Mountain", Mumu, and the injured Di An were left here!
"My Heaven Palace will not back down either." Feng Ge's eyes were filled with determination.
Under this confrontation, the atmosphere became extremely tense.
Below, the Li Yuan Clan and the Eared Mouse Clan were leading the other clans to attack the main body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
Currently, the happiest clan was the Li Yuan Clan. Regardless of whether it was the Heaven Palace, Li Shan, You Xiong, Golden Crow, or Shen Nong, they were all contenders for the Chaotic Origin Spirit Crystals. Because of that brat Luo Zheng, these great clans had started fighting amongst themselves.
Making use of this opportunity to kill the main body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit, the Li Yuan Clan would be the biggest beneficiary.
Actually, the Eared Mouse Clan was concerned about the whereabouts of Luo Zheng. But the Eared Mouse Clan valued benefits greatly. Luo Zheng and them were merely using each other. They had not reached the stage where they would stand up for Luo Zheng.
Evil Deities stood in the shadow of a huge pit on the ground.
"Are they going to fight? Heh …" Evil Deities was filled with excitement. His fighting spirit was on the verge of bursting out from his face.
He had judged based on his instincts that "Mountain" was very powerful. Even if the Heaven Palace and Li Shan worked together, they could not take him down.
Evil Deities was not entirely certain if he could defeat "Mountain".
But during this period, he had been hiding in the dark. If he were to make use of the chaos, he might have a chance.
Mountain swept his gaze across the place. He shook his head and said to Luo Yan in a serious tone, "It's not that I don't want to release him. But he has completely disappeared …"
"That means you don't plan on releasing Luo Zheng?" Luo Yan narrowed her eyes. A strand of dangerous light was emitted from her eyes.
"I can't do it because he no longer exists." Mountain said.
"Since you can't do it, you should be prepared to die." Feng Ge's face was covered in a layer of frost. It was as if that cold nature of hers had returned to her body. This sentence of Mountain had already sentenced Luo Zheng to death. This was an outcome she could not accept.
The auras of the others also surged. Many Heaven Palace disciples and Nuwa Clan members brought out their Paramita Talismans.
The negotiation failed …
Mountain squinted his eyes. He looked down at everyone expressionlessly. He pointed at Luo Yan, "If you plan on acting against me, you should be prepared to die. Other than her, I'll kill everyone else."
This sentence of Mountain was a warning to everyone and also a message.

Chapter 3075
Fuxi, who was at the top of the snowy mountain, snorted coldly when he heard this.
No matter what happened, Fuxi did not intend to act.
Even when Luo Zheng, whom Fuxi valued the most, was sealed into a folded space by 'Mountain', he did not intervene. Furthermore, it was too late for him to intervene.
If 'Mountain' had acted against Luo Yan, Fuxi would have entered the Chaotic Origin Great World immediately.
Even though 'Mountain' had always been in the Chaotic Origin Great World, his judgment of Fuxi was extremely accurate!
"You are spouting nonsense!"
Being the inheritor of Eastern Emperor and an outstanding expert in the Tai Yi Palace, Feng Ge was naturally not one to be frightened.
A strand of golden light flashed in Feng Ge's eyes. It was flashing with a dangerous aura.
Strands of Golden Crow Divine Fire burst out from her hair. King Stone Mound floated above her head. More than ten thousand sword lights pierced towards' Mountain '.
At the same time, Luo Yan spread out her hands. A crimson energy surrounded her and started spreading. Her Wah Shadow was also revealed and rapidly expanded.
Chou Xue, Mo Yijian, Ling Shuang and even Lan Qing acted immediately!
"Mu Mu, retreat!" Mountain said.
Being able to fight against the enemy with his uncle here, Mumu felt that his blood was boiling.
She was not willing to miss this opportunity!
But this time, her little uncle's voice contained a strand of unprecedented seriousness. Clearly, her little uncle was not confident of winning against the Great Yi Heaven Palace and Li Shan. He had to go all out before allowing her to leave.
"I don't want …"
Just as Mu Mu opened her mouth to speak, she felt her vision blur and appeared on a mountain peak.
This was the side of the Northern Peak of the Core Region …
She could even sense the aura of her spatial sacred art. She had already penetrated through space.
Mu Mu angrily stomped her feet before flying back to the Core Region.
Mountain used both his hands to protect his front. With a light leap, he mysteriously disappeared in front of everyone.
At the next moment, he appeared behind Luo Yan. He stretched out his hand and lightly swiped.
A cube 10 meters tall, 10 meters wide and 10 meters long appeared in midair! The mountain even considered to accommodate Luo Yan's Wah Shadow. At the moment, Luo Yan's Wah Shadow had expanded to ten meters in radius.
However, this cubic space was an independent spatial cage. Even Luo Yan and her Wa-Ying were trapped in it.
"Break!"
Luo Yan's pupils flickered with a strange color.
Ordinary spatial barriers and spatial cages wouldn't be able to trap her.
As she let out a beast-like roar, the spatial cage didn't budge at all. It wasn't affected in the slightest.
"Hua, hua, hua …"
Nuwa's phantom, which had only grown to a height of ten meters, poured its energy onto the surface of the cube. However, each and every attack seemed to be hitting space itself!
After trapping Luo Yan, a heroic smile finally appeared on Mountain's face. "Since you insist on fighting, I can only accompany you …"
As his body shook violently, the clothes on his upper body were instantly torn apart, revealing a patch of bronze skin.
"Sixth Revolution: Taiyi Devil Shock!"
At this moment, two figures suddenly flew towards the "mountain". They were two Ling Shuangs.
Compared to Feng Ge, Ling Shuang was more rational.
Luo Zheng and Mountain didn't have a life-and-death grudge. If they could negotiate, she still hoped that Mountain would return Luo Zheng.
However, Mountain said that he couldn't do anything.
She didn't believe Mountain's words. Thus, she had to make him yield …
Ling Shuang and her doppelganger intertwined, each forming two shadows in midair.
The six silhouettes circled around the mountain like wavy lines, drawing a mysterious pattern in the air.
With Ling Shuang's current strength, it was quite difficult to perform this move.
If Granny was here, she would definitely not allow her to use this move.
Mountain stood in midair, motionless like a mountain. Facing the six dazzling figures, he suddenly stretched out his hand.
"Ka!"
This hand accurately grabbed one of the Ling Shuangs.
The other five Ling Shuangs all changed their expressions. They crossed their swords and went straight for Mountain in the center.
However, the five sword auras all hit nothing but air …
In the next moment, Mountain appeared on the other side. He gently twisted Ling Shuang's neck, and Ling Shuang's neck was broken.
"Buzz …"
Ling Shuang in Mountain's hand was like a creature in the desert that was quickly eroded by the wind. She quickly turned into a flower in his hand. That was Ling Shuang's Nirvana Realm token, the "Two Lives Flower".
The other five figures merged into one and returned to Ling Shuang's original body.
Ling Shuang's face was pale. The destruction of her doppelganger hurt her a lot …
If the 'mountain' had found her real body, she would have been dead by now!
Just as she was thinking about this, Mountain's voice came. "Next time, I will crush your original body's neck!"
"Whoosh!"
While 'Shan' was still talking, Chou Xinghe and Mo Yijian attacked him from both sides.
In the Heart-Flow Sword Sect, Luo Zheng had helped Mo Yijian before. Now that Luo Zheng was in trouble, Mo Yijian would not just stand by and watch.
Chou Xinghe straightened his sword and drew a strange line in the air, stabbing directly at Mountain.
His sword was very strange. It was wishful thinking to stab Mountain with such a simple move. However, halfway through the attack, Chou Xinghe suddenly turned around and flicked his sword behind him.
Mo Yijian, who was beside Chou Xinghe, suddenly flipped and slashed from top to bottom!
"Heart Quenching Stab!"
The direction of their attacks was somewhat baffling.
Mountain had strangely moved through space and appeared under Mo Yijian's sword.
Mo Yijian's sword was an absolute art from the Heart-Flow Sword. Even though it was not very powerful, it could make the opponent feel a great sense of danger!
Mountain's divine ability was indeed powerful. However, his soul was still at the Yang Soul Level of Level Thirteen. Under the pressure of the Heart Quenching Spike, his figure flashed once again and penetrated through space.
And Mountain's third landing point was behind Chou Xinghe. Chou Xinghe's sword pierced through his back!
"Da!"
Chou Xinghe's sword was very weak. It could not even pierce through Mountain's skin.
But after Chou Xinghe's sword stabbed out, he flew backwards with a satisfied expression. He rapidly pulled away from Mountain. Not only Chou Xinghe, even Mo Yijian did the same.
All spatial divine abilities were forbidden in the Chaotic Origin Great World. But Mountain could avoid this rule.
Just based on this point, Chou Xinghe and Mo Yijian were clear that even if Great Yi Heaven Palace and Li Mountain worked together, they would not be able to do anything to him.
Hence, the two were not in a rush to attack.
When Ling Shuang and Feng Ge attacked, they were already discussing how to deal with the situation.

Chapter 3076
Chou Xue told Mo Yijian that there was only one way to defeat the "Mountain", and that was to let him stab the "Mountain" once.
But it was extremely difficult to stab the "Mountain" who was constantly teleporting!
But Mo Yijian immediately told him to leave it to him.
Mo Yijian's comprehension of the Heart Sword was far beyond the reach of ordinary people.
The moment he attacked the "Mountain", he had entered the state of the Heart Sword, constantly guessing the "Mountain"'s possible attacks and making predictions.
This kind of prediction could not be completely accurate. After all, even Mo Yijian could not calculate what the "Mountain" was thinking.
But he could induce and force the opponent's thoughts.
Chou Xue attacked, Mo Yijian attacked, Chou Xue attacked again. Mo Yijian calculated the "Mountain"'s thoughts, and after three changes, Chou Xue finally stabbed the "Mountain" in the chest.
However, the power of this sword made Mo Yijian very disappointed.
If it was Mo Yijian, although it would be difficult to pierce through the "Mountain"'s body, he could still pierce through the "Mountain"'s skin.
In comparison, Chou Xue's attack was like scratching an itch!
But Chou Xue's expression of success made Mo Yijian even more confused. "What are you doing? Your sword is poisoned? I didn't even see blood. Even if there was poison, it wouldn't have any effect. "
"Look," Chou Xue said, pointing at the "Mountain" in the distance.
A small golden light appeared beside the "Mountain", and as the golden light continued to grow, a golden abacus appeared.
"What the hell is that?" Mo Yijian asked, puzzled.
Compared to countless mortals, the disciples of Great Yi Heavenly Palace were like immortals. It was normal that he had never seen an abacus.
"That's an abacus, used to calculate." Chou Xue glanced at Mo Yijian. This guy was too ignorant.
"Calculate … what's the use of it …" Mo Yijian still did not understand.
Not only Mo Yijian, but the disciples of Great Yi Heavenly Palace and Nuwa Clan around the "Mountain" were also staring at the golden object.
Even the Mountain himself was sizing up this little thing.
He gently extended his finger towards the golden abacus and lightly flicked it.
"Pa!"
The power of a casual flick of Mountain's finger was also extremely terrifying.
The golden abacus was flicked by his finger and instantly turned into golden powder, scattering in front of him …
But in the blink of an eye, the scattered gold powder reformed into a complete abacus.
"Indestructible?" Mountain raised his eyebrows slightly, pointed at the object and asked, "What is this?"
With Yishan's countless unknowns, he was actually unable to discern the origin of this golden abacus. It was indeed very strange.
Chou Xue stared at "Mountain" vigilantly without saying a word. Mo Yijian was not far from Chou Xue, and his expression was also tense.
"Answer," Shan ordered.
Seeing that Chou Xinghe still did not speak, Mountain's figure trembled slightly and strangely disappeared.
"Brother Chou, behind you, be careful!" Mo Yijian shouted.
The moment Chou Xue heard Mo Yijian's warning, he had already rushed forward, and "Mountain" had also strangely appeared behind Chou Xue, flashing a thick yellow light from the surface of his body. At the same time, he threw out a punch.
The power of this punch was extremely restrained, and there was not a trace of it being released outwards. But anyone could be sure that it was definitely more than 1,000 Godly Jun!
Because Mo Yijian's warning was timely, Chou Xue was able to dodge this punch.
Mountain silently glanced at Mo Yijian. In the two times they had fought, Chou Xue had been able to catch his figure, and it was probably because of Mo Yijian.
Mo Yijian could use "flow" to catch a person's subconscious reaction and strategy, but when "Mountain" became vigilant and began to deceive the subconscious, "flow" would naturally lose its effect.
Seeing that "Mountain's" figure was about to disappear in a flash, Mo Yijian reminded again, "Brother Chou Xue! Above! "
Chou Xue subconsciously lowered his body vertically, but the gradually disappearing "Mountain" did not use greater teleportation. Instead, he turned into a sharp arrow and shot towards Chou Xue.
Mountain's body contained a dull power. This power did not have any momentum, but once it hit, it would either kill or injure.
Chou Xue's eyes suddenly widened, and he used almost all of his strength to stab with his sword, at the same time turning his body and retreating madly.
Mo Yijian's wrong judgment had already caused Chou Xue to lose the chance to dodge …
Mountain stretched out his finger and lightly flicked Chou Xue's sword, which broke into countless pieces with a "ding" sound. Next, he threw a solid punch towards Chou Xue.
"Ka …"
When the fatal blow hit Chou Xue, the "Hermit" above Chou Xue's head suddenly shattered, and a snow-white mirror appeared in front of Chou Xue.
This mirror clearly reflected the shape of "Mountain", and the "Mountain" in the mirror also attacked the "Mountain" outside the mirror.
"Bang!"
The fists of the two "Mountains" collided, and the mirror shattered.
Chou Xue did not suffer any injuries, and the "Mountain" in the mirror was sent flying after clashing with the "Mountain" in the mirror.
"Jade Light Mirror!"
This was the Hermit divine ability bestowed to Chou Xue by the Mountain Master. At this crucial moment, it saved Chou Xue's life.
Under the situation where "Mountain" missed his attack, he turned around and prepared to charge towards Chou Xue again. But at this moment, the beads on the golden abacus following by the side of "Mountain" let out a "pa pa pa" sound.
Those beads kept moving up and down as if an invisible hand was fiddling with the abacus. Eventually, the beads stopped and revealed a number.
"2,400 Divine Jun …"
Chou Xue read out the number on the abacus, and his face turned slightly pale.
This "Mountain" could casually erupt with strength that far surpassed 1,000 Divine Jun. He was much stronger than ordinary Chaotic Origin Realm experts. How strong was his fleshy body? Was this person not a Chaotic Origin Realm expert?
If not for the Jade Light Mirror helping him block one round, he would have been blasted into a bloody pulp!
The Mountain glanced at the golden abacus by his side. He rapidly read out the number on it, "2,400 Divine Jun, this abacus isn't as simple as counting right …" "Of course not, your first attack is the upper limit of your strength. After that, every attack you make will be weakened by half." Chou Xue calmly said. He relied on the Hermit divine ability to avoid the most lethal attack. The golden abacus had started calculating, and he was no longer afraid of the Mountain.
Yes.
The Mountain stared at the number on the abacus and said, "In other words, my next attack can only unleash 1,200 Divine Jun strength?"
"Right." Chou Xue nodded.
Just as he nodded, the Mountain appeared in front of Chou Xue. Another fist came smashing down on Chou Xue.
This time, the Mountain was so fast that Mo Yijian did not have the time to remind him. Chou Xue reflexively raised his hands.
"Ka ka ka …"
How could Chou Xue's fleshy body withstand it? The bones in his arms and chest were broken into pieces. He vomited blood and fell down from the sky.
The Mountain looked at his fist and muttered, "I really only have half of my strength left …"

Chapter 3077
The abacus bead jumped and finally stopped at 600 Shen Jun.
When Mountain saw this number, he understood that he could only explode 600 Shen Jun for his next attack.
The strength of 600 Shen Jun would not have any advantage against the young geniuses.
Most of the people who stayed in the core area could easily explode the strength of 1,000 Shen Jun. Some of the descending Nirvana tokens could even exceed that.
Chou Xinghe was seriously injured and collapsed on the ground. Blood flowed out of his mouth.
When the disciples of the Heavenly Palace saw this, they descended from the sky and guarded him.
"Chou Xinghe, are you okay?" Lan Qing went to Chou Xinghe and helped him up.
Feng Ge stared at Chou Xinghe and took out a golden elixir. He stuffed it into Chou Xinghe's mouth and said, "You won't die."
After swallowing the elixir, Chou Xinghe still looked up at Mountain in the sky and said to everyone, "You can do it now. His strength has been greatly reduced. The second attack will only be 600 Shen Jun. The third attack will only be 300 Shen Jun …"
Chou Xinghe had merged two Nirvana tokens. He had used the Chain of Life several times before and could turn himself into a puppet of the Nirvana token to fight. In a short period of time, his strength had greatly increased, but the side effects were considerable.
The other Nirvana token was this golden abacus.
The origin of this abacus was very strange. Chou Xinghe had once wandered into a temple in the Nine Levels of Heaven and found a human in the temple!
This human was not in the form of a Yang soul, but in the form of a Nirvana creature.
Chou Xinghe had never heard of a Nirvana creature called the human race. Naturally, he felt very strange.
At that time, this human was dying and was captured by a golden line. Chou Xinghe released the human after unlocking the restriction in the temple. Chou Xinghe did this because they were of the same race.
This human was very grateful at that time and gave Chou Xinghe the Nirvana token when he left.
In the beginning, Chou Xinghe did not take this Nirvana token seriously. At that time, Chou Xinghe's goal was to enter the Thirteenth Level of Heaven. He wandered into the temple just to look for the soul crystal.
However, the Nirvana token could be merged and released at will. After Chou Xinghe merged the golden abacus into his body, he realized the magical effect of this thing.
This Nirvana Realm Token did not bestow power to oneself, but to impose it on the other party.
The condition wasn't difficult to fulfill. All he had to do was strike the other party once.
Therefore, Chou Xue had asked Mo Yijian to help him from the start to ensure that he could hit the "mountain".
When he hit the "mountain", the Nirvana token descended beside the "mountain". Once it descended, it couldn't be destroyed. Its function was the complete opposite of an ordinary Nirvana token.
Most of the Nirvana Realm Tokens could give their masters powerful Nirvana Realm Tokens. However, after the arrival of the golden abacus, the first step was to confirm the host's power.
Mountain's fatal blow just now had a terrifying strength of more than 2000 Shen Jun, but it was blocked by the Hermit's supernatural power of Martyrdom Chou.
After this attack, the power had been confirmed.
Then, the golden abacus would exert the reverse Nirvana Power. No matter how many Nirvana tokens the "mountain" could extract, the golden abacus would calculate all of them and only give half of the power of the first strike.
Following that, every strike of the "mountain" would be calculated in accordance with the multiplier until it was infinitesimally close to zero.
"But you have to be careful. His strength isn't terrifying …" Chou Xue said with difficulty.
Ling Shuang nodded.
The strength of the mountain was secondary. More importantly, he could casually use spatial divine techniques. That was a huge advantage.
"I, I'll bring you to the array …" Lan Qing carried Chou Xue and brought him to the array.
The array might not be able to withstand the attacks of the Chaotic Origin Spirit, but the mother was currently being held back by those from the Li Yuan Clan. It shouldn't be much of a threat.
The "mountain" remained standing in midair. He looked at the golden abacus by his side and fell into deep thought.
This unique Nirvana Realm Token was indeed out of his expectations. He felt that there was a special whirlpool in the world inside his body. Every time he started to extract the Nirvana Power, this whirlpool would absorb most of the power, leaving very little for him.
"Shua shua shua …"
During this process, those from the Heaven Palace once again gathered around him. Those from Li Mountain followed closely behind.
Chou Xue had risked his life for this opportunity. How could they give it up?
"I'll give you one more chance. Release Luo Zheng, and we'll let bygones be bygones." Feng Ge threatened with burning eyes.
The mountain remained silent. He was still thinking of a way to break out of this situation.
Feng Ge did not have that much patience. Seeing that the "mountain" did not speak, King Stone Mound floating above his head slashed his sword at him.
The moment Feng Ge acted, Ling Shuang and the other Heaven Palace disciples followed suit.
The Six Snake Shadow Sons of the Nuwa Clan and Ling Yun similarly released their Nirvana Power!
At the moment when many sword lights were applied on the body of the "mountain", the fleshy body of the mountain blurred slightly before easily penetrating through space.
But the moment he stepped out of space, Mo Yijian was already waiting by the side. He was thrusting his sword at the "mountain".
The mountain stared at Mo Yijian. He revealed a helpless smile. He did not like others guessing his thoughts. He was also tired of Mo Yijian's little tricks. Following that, his figure flashed and appeared behind Mo Yijian. He waved his hand and slapped at the back of Mo Yijian's neck.
The power of 600 Shen Jun would be fatal if it hit a vital part.
Mo Yijian was, after all, a rare genius of the Heart Flow Sword Sect. Almost by reflex, he retracted his sword behind his back and blocked Shan's attack.
"Bang …"
Without Luo Zheng's insane physique, Mo Yijian's result of resisting the power of 600 Shen Jun was obvious. His entire body was like a piece of lead as he smashed onto the ground.
Fortunately, he blocked the attack with his sword at the critical moment. Otherwise, this attack would have been fatal.
After this attack, the golden abacus floating beside the "mountain" jumped again. This time, the number displayed was only 300 Shen Jun.
"Whoosh whoosh …"
The Six Snake Shadow Sons formed a mysterious array that enveloped the mountain.
The mountain's figure kept flashing in the array. With another punch, he sent a Nuwa Clan member flying. The golden abacus started counting again. 150 Shen Jun!
As the mountain attacked again and again, the Nirvana Power he could obtain became weaker and weaker.
75 …
32 …
16 …
8 …
When the number of Shen Jun decreased to single digits, his attacks were no longer a threat.
Even the weakest disciple of the Heavenly Palace was much stronger than him.
Even so, there was no sign of panic on the Mountain's face. Just as Chou Xinghe had said, his greatest reliance was the space god art. This god art was fused into his bloodline, and this golden abacus could not calculate his bloodline god art at all.
Therefore, the "mountain" had been calmly experimenting. The abacus could not keep counting indefinitely.

Chapter 3078
Not only did Mountain possess powerful spatial powers, but his strength also made Evil Deities wary.

If one thought did not work, another thought appeared.

But the mother did not have any pity. That enormous foot of its stomped down without hesitation!
"Hong!"

Mountain calmly replied: "If we die together, I'll teleport away the moment the mother's foot stomps down."
If the mother's foot was just a few chi away from the ground, Mountain would teleport in with Evil Deities. Both of them would be crushed. No matter how fast Evil Deities was, he would not be able to escape.

Mountain would not truly die. He did not value his life that much. And Evil Deities only had one life.
Hence, the two of them were not on the same starting line when facing the test of fear of death.
Even though Evil Deities was incomparably arrogant, he was still afraid of death.
At the next moment, he would definitely let go of Mountain to prevent himself from being dragged into death. This was Mountain's judgment.
But Mountain had miscalculated something …

The eyes of Evil Deities flashed. The black light in his eyes started spreading once again.
"Dong dong, dong dong, dong dong …"
At this moment, all sounds disappeared.
Everyone could subconsciously hear their own heartbeats.

The snow-white skin turned dark. The dark sky flashed with blazing white light.
"What is going on!"
"What happened …"

A member of the Li Yuan Race was startled by the change in his environment. He was slightly distracted and was slapped by the mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit. The terrifying strength directly smashed him into pieces.
Mountain saw the change in his surroundings. His eyes dilated slightly. Finally, his expression changed greatly, "This is … the descent of a world. Which civilization bestowed it to you?"
Evil Deities narrowed his eyes as he stared at Mountain with a smile: "You truly have good eyesight. You can even see that there is a civilization standing behind me!"
There were many civilizations remaining in the Nirvana World. Every single Ruler civilization had once defined their own unique laws. Only those chosen as the spokesperson of the civilization would have the ability to descend into a world.
And in this world, the original laws would be redefined.
But there were strong and weak civilizations. Naturally, there would be strong and weak worlds too.
For instance, the 'Blood Descent' that the Mountain Master of the Grand Brightness Mountain had brought down was weaker by an entire level. That was because the civilization standing behind the Mountain Master of the Grand Brightness Mountain was not a truly powerful Ruler civilization.
"Even if I bring down a world, you might not be able to do anything to me!" The moment Mountain finished speaking, he once again formed a cube, enveloping him and Evil Deities within.

And since the mother body's foot had already stepped down, Evil Deities would definitely not be able to dodge.
But at the moment Evil Deities was thrown in, his figure similarly flashed. He had also executed a great teleportation, appearing in midair. He grinned: "What a mystical spatial sacred technique. Tsk tsk, it is truly convenient to use. Such an ability is destined to be mine …"

Chapter 3079

The bloodline ability of the Liyuan Race was rather special. It was divided into three stages, and each stage was vastly different.
After activating his bloodline, the strength of his physical body had greatly increased.
Just by relying on their own bloodline, the Liyuan Clan was able to unleash a strength that exceeded the limit of 1000 Shen Jun.
Of which the First Stage was Violet Gold Body Sculpting. The Second Stage was Refined Gold Body Sculpting. And the Third Stage was Chen Gold Body Sculpting. Only very few people in the Li Yuan Clan could use it. The Chen Gold Bloodline was the most noble bloodline. This time, only Jin Sheng was qualified to use it in this expedition to the Chaotic Origin Great World.

Previously, he discovered that Luo Zheng was too troublesome. Without any other choice, he could only erase Luo Zheng along with that space.
Compared to other people in the Chaotic Origin Great World, Luo Zheng was the greatest threat.
Mountain did not expect that after sending away Luo Zheng, an even more troublesome fellow would appear!
Many bloodline abilities appeared on the body of Evil Deities. His aura was also getting stronger.
Some bloodline abilities could increase the strength of the fleshy body. Some bloodline abilities could directly increase the strength of the fleshy body, speed, and other abilities.
Under the continuous combination of these bloodlines, he was getting closer and closer to a complete body!

As those bloodline abilities were activated, Evil Deities unleashed a domineering aura. Even the mother body that was chasing after them could feel the powerful aura of Evil Deities. It temporarily stopped in its tracks.
Outside the Chaotic Origin Great World …
When the leaders saw this scene, their expressions became very solemn.
In the final battle for the Chaotic Origin Spirit, they actually saw Evil Deities unleashing such an ability. On one hand, these leaders were envious. On the other hand, they were deeply worried for their own clansmen.

The other leaders nodded in agreement when they heard this.

"I'm honored to have obtained so many bloodline abilities! But these are only temporary. It's really regrettable … "Evil Deities shook his head regretfully. He then said," To make up for this regret, I can only let you sleep in this world. Only then can I have them forever.
Bloodline abilities, hahahaha! "
As Evil Deities let out a maniacal laugh, his figure disappeared mysteriously.

If someone with a bloodline ability appeared in this world, he would obtain the bloodline ability of the other party. But this was only temporary.

In this situation, he could only rely on the power of that person …
This human woman from Nuwa Clan was very important to Li Shan. That was why Mountain kept her trapped in the space cage. He had no intention of hurting her.

Chapter 3080
Mountain once again teleported and appeared behind Luo Yan.

Mountain stood behind Luo Yan and watched this scene calmly.
He did not care about the outcome of the battle between Nuwa's Apparitions.
As long as Evil Deities had the intention to kill Luo Yan, Fuxi would not hesitate to attack.
Swish swish swish …

Shan had thought that Evil Deities would take advantage of the situation and try to kill Luo Yan. He did not expect this arrogant guy to be so alert. He let go of Luo Yan at the crucial moment and continued to target himself!
Evil Deities looked wild and unrestrained, but they were actually very meticulous.
He had been punished by Fuxi before entering the Chaotic Origin Great World. He understood that the current him was not a match for Fuxi. Hence, he wisely chose to accept his fate.
At the very least, he could not kill this woman called 'Luo Yan'. Otherwise, he might not be able to walk out of the Drinking Snow Prefecture.
Evil Deities stood at a different height from 'Mountain'. Naturally, he was not as wise as Mountain who could see through the relationship between Luo Yan and Li Shan. He could also lure Fuxi into the Chaotic Origin Great World.
But his instincts and alertness allowed him to avoid this risk.
This time, he had no choice but to use Greater Teleportation once again.

Evil Deities once again accurately guessed the range of Mountain's teleportation. Before Mountain appeared, he had already arrived. Many blood-red energy beams intertwined, turning into many sharp spears that shot towards Mountain.
If Mountain could still use the Paramita Token and activate the Paramita Energy, he would not be afraid of this attack.
But if he were to receive this attack in his current state, it would signify the end of the battle.
Just as Evil Deities focused all his attention on Mountain, strands of soft hair coiled around Evil Deities' legs. With a sudden tug, Evil Deities was thrown out!
"Little Uncle, I'm back!" Mumu said with a brilliant smile.
Mumu had been teleported out of the core region by Mountain. Her heart was filled with resentment. Hence, she hurried back.
Seeing Evil Deities launching such an attack towards Little Uncle, she activated her long hair and coiled it, throwing Evil Deities out.
She had helped Little Uncle with great difficulty. Naturally, she felt delighted.
Ke Shan's expression changed greatly, "Mumu, be careful!"
Saying that, he rushed over and brought Mumu along with him using Spatial Teleportation.
Mumu was unaware of the coming danger. The Evil God, who had been thrown away, reached out and gently hooked the two spaces together. The blood-colored energy beams, which looked like spears, had passed through the space and directly drilled out of Mumu's body.
Puff! Puff! Puff!
When Mumu saw the energy beams coming out of her chest, she was shocked. She looked at Little Uncle in despair and didn't dare to move.
Crack ….
The next moment, Mumu's body was torn apart by the energy beams. Her broken body fell rapidly.
Exclamations could be heard outside the Chaotic Origin Great World.
The faces of the leaders of the Divine Farmer Clan turned pale.
Mumu was the No. 1 seed of this generation of the Divine Farmer Clan.
Although Mountain was powerful, he had a special origin. He was not the same as ordinary Divine Farmer Clan members.
Mumu's death was beyond their expectation. After returning to the Divine Farmer Clan, they would not be able to report back to the Divine Farmer Clan.
"Tsk! Tsk! Tsk! This is the fate of those who disturb my battle!" Evil Deities looked at the broken body on the ground and sneered.
Mountain was floating quietly in the same place as if he was a volcano that was about to erupt.
Thin, chain-like veins appeared on his bronze body. Those veins were shining with orange light. The surrounding space was also trembling faintly.
Swish ….
The space around Mountain was broken into countless pieces.
Those broken pieces of space didn't disappear. Instead, they were floating around him like sharp glazed tiles.
"Using the broken space as a weapon?" Evil Deities' eyes lit up.
The more powerful Bloodline Ability Mountain revealed, the more excited Evil Deities became.
Other people's Bloodline Ability could be maximized in the hands of Evil Deities. Some Bloodlines could even enhance other Bloodlines.
The reason why his Shadow Wa was more powerful than Luo Yan was that he had superimposed the Bloodlines of the Moon Spirit Clan and the Moran Clan. Evil Deities was probably the only one who had ever fused several Bloodlines together.
Among all the Bloodlines, Mountain's Bloodline was the most mysterious.
Evil Deities could only exert 30% of Mountain's Bloodline Ability. He wanted to see what would happen when Mountain's Bloodline Ability was maximized.
At this moment, everyone in the core area, including Li Shan and the disciples of the Heaven Palace, hoped Mountain could defeat Evil Deities.
…..
…..
In another part of the void, half a giant hand was floating quietly.
Luo Zheng's figure flickered and flew to the side of the giant hand.
He reached into the smooth and neat notch of the giant hand and took out amber-colored crystals one after another.
"There are over a thousand soul crystals in this giant hand. No wonder the body of the mother is so tough," Luo Zheng murmured.
After putting all the amber-colored crystals into his Sumeru Ring, he began to observe his surroundings.
This was a dark environment. It was as if sound didn't exist. It was terrifyingly quiet.
In the distance, there were some indistinct objects floating. Even further away, there were many thread-like objects that emitted a dim luster.
"Where is this?"

Chapter 3081
Luo Zheng was not unfamiliar with the Law of Space.
According to Luo Zheng's original thoughts, he should be trapped in a certain space or even deep within a spatial crack.
But the scene before him made Luo Zheng suspicious.
9527 went silent for a moment in his mind before saying, "This is not anywhere."
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng was startled.
"This is a Truth Divine Ability unique to another Primal Chaos Ancient God." 9527 explained.
Hearing 9527's explanation, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly, "You mean Mountain has the same Primal Chaos Ancient God bloodline as I do? No wonder. "
High-tiered energy could easily break free from the shackles of low-tiered energy.
The powerful existences who created the Primal Chaos World all had heaven-defying abilities. Logically speaking, it was simply wishful thinking for them to break free from the restrictions of the Chaotic Origin Great World.
But Mountain easily broke free. This could only mean one thing – the level of energy Mountain used was very high.
Luo Zheng immediately came to this conclusion. Mountain was the same as him and could use the Truth Divine Ability.
"He is different from you." 9527 denied.
"How is he different?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"He is originally an avatar of a Primal Chaos Ancient God. After fusing it into his own bloodline, he grew from an infant." 9527's voice was slightly gloomy. It seemed he was not in a good mood.
"He is a Primal Chaos Ancient God!" Luo Zheng was astonished. He took a moment to digest this information before asking again, "But you said this is not anywhere. What do you mean?" 9527 pondered for a moment before saying, "Mountain's Truth Divine Ability is the space itself. He controls the concept of space at the lowest level of the entire Primal Chaos. If we treat the Primal Chaos as a house, Mountain cut off a corner of the space in this house and threw this space, along with you and that hand, out.
In other words, he deleted this space from the Primal Chaos. "
Luo Zheng blinked his eyes and continued digesting 9527's words.
9527's description was very vivid and not hard to understand. It was just that it sounded unimaginable.
"Then you mean this place is outside the Primal Chaos?" Luo Zheng asked.
"I do not know." 9527 answered honestly.
"Even you don't know?"
Luo Zheng originally thought that it wouldn't be difficult to go back, but now he had a faint sense of crisis.
"What is beyond the chaos? I have no idea. Not only me, but even if I came here personally, I wouldn't be able to answer you," 9527 said.
Every living being had a different understanding of space.
For a small insect, the space it sensed was only the size of a few rooms. The world outside the room was unimaginable.
A mortal knows that beyond the mountains there is a sea, and beyond the sea there is a continent, but often that is all.
If the martial artists of the Origin World knew that this world had an end and that there was a giant crystal wall at the end of the world, that crystal wall could not be shaken. Only those who had reached the True Divinity realm had the qualifications to pass through.
As for the True Gods of the Homeworld, they understood that these crystal walls divided the Homeworld into many huge prefectures, and outside these prefectures was the boundless Primal Chaos.
The Primal Chaos also had a boundary. Whether it was the supreme experts of the Mother World or the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods, they were all very clear about the existence of this boundary.
For example, the bottom of the primordial chaos was the boundary of the lowest level of the primordial chaos.
However, until now, no one knew what was beyond the boundary. In terms of cognition, the world outside the Primal Chaos was in a state of incomprehension.
Luo Zheng pursed his lips. This time, it was not that 9527 did not want to tell him, but that he really did not know.
His gaze swept across the void, then he aimed at the objects in the distance whose original appearance could not be seen clearly and flew over.
The distance was not far, and Luo Zheng had arrived in about ten breaths of time.
This was the remains of a house.
Pillars carved out of white jade and broken stones lay still in the void. The pillars were severely damaged and had been floating here for who knows how many years.
"These remains should be the houses of a certain race in the Origin World," Luo Zheng analyzed.
"They also had the same fate as you. After being deleted, they were thrown out of the Primal Chaos," 9527 continued.
Hearing 9527's words, Luo Zheng was not discouraged.
He shuttled through the wreckage for a while, and after searching, he actually found two long swords and a hook. The grades were not bad, at least at the level of a Mystique Venerable Taoist Treasure.
However, these treasures were not of much use to Luo Zheng. After casually throwing them into the Sumeru Ring, he continued to patrol around the wreckage, carefully looking for some valuable clues.
This wreckage belt was about ten miles long. Luo Zheng searched from beginning to end, but did not find anything of significance.
Standing at the tail of the wreckage belt, Luo Zheng once again looked into the distance. Not far away, there was another wreckage belt.
In the other wreckage belt, there were all kinds of metals floating. These metals were twisted into all kinds of shapes. If one could vaguely identify them, one could even see the runes carved on the metals. Of course, these runes were very different from the runes in the Origin World.
When Luo Zheng passed by a huge "metal cup", his gaze swept across the cup, and his expression suddenly froze.
"Corpses."
There were dozens of corpses curled up in the cup. What made Luo Zheng feel strange was that these corpses were also emitting a brass-like luster. Even though they had been dead for countless years, they still looked like they had just died.
Luo Zheng pulled out a long sword and lightly tapped one of the corpses, and a crisp "ding" was heard.
"It seems like these corpses were originally metal bodies," Luo Zheng muttered.
Ever since he entered the Nirvana Realm and merged with the Instrument of Civilization, Luo Zheng had understood that the form of life was not just flesh and blood.
For example, the Green Jade civilization's body was a piece of jade. The entire civilization was born bit by bit from flying jade. It was fundamentally different from the structure of the living beings in the Origin World.
Since the Green Jade race used green jade as their body, it was not strange for a "metal" civilization to be born in the Chaos.
"These are not living beings from our civilization," 9527 also said.
In the past, there were other Chaos Ancient Gods in the Chaos who had mastered the magic of space at the lowest level. Naturally, like the "Mountain", they would delete some unnecessary space and throw it out of the Chaos.
Luo Zheng nodded. He was not very interested in these metal corpses. Standing on the edge of the metal cup, he sighed and said, "Is there no living thing in this damned place?"
There seemed to be no danger in this space. If some living beings had an infinite lifespan, they should be able to live for a long time after being sent into this space. But this damned place was dead silent, which seemed a bit strange.

Chapter 3082
Luo Zheng couldn't find any valuable clues in this piece of metal wreckage. Everyone, please search and see! Metal wreckage.
There were some unique words on this "metal wreckage," but these words weren't in Brahman, so it was impossible to decipher them in a short time.
After collecting a few pieces of special metals, Luo Zheng passed through the wreckage zone without consulting anyone. The wreckage ribbons were connected to each other and floated quietly in the void. Some of the wreckage ribbons had distinctive features and could be vaguely identified as belonging to Ming. However, most of the wreckage ribbons were mountain rocks and building fragments. In one of the ribbons, Luo Zheng discovered a …
A complete mountain.
"This mountain was actually cut in half …"
Standing at the top of the mountain, Luo Zheng released his divine sense and entered the mountain to look around.
There were a few caves in the mountain, and there were some small animal and insect corpses in the caves. It was hard to determine how long they had been dead.
"Strange …" Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
"What's wrong?" 9527 asked.
"Why are these small animal and insect corpses so well preserved?" Luo Zheng asked.
It was normal that the metal corpses hadn't rotted for countless years in this place.
But the ordinary insect corpses in the caves hadn't rotted after such a long time?
There was probably a problem …
"There's nothing strange about this," 9527 said lightly. "Time doesn't exist here. Even if it's a million years or ten billion years, it's still the same as when it came in …"
There was no concept of time in this space.
Hearing 9527's explanation, Luo Zheng felt a headache.
The moment Luo Zheng retracted his divine sense, he suddenly sensed something moving on the other side of the mountain!
The image scanned by his divine sense was a simple silhouette. Naturally, it couldn't be as clear as his eyesight, but something moving meant that it must be a living thing!
"Whoosh!"
Without saying anything, Luo Zheng shot toward the back of the mountain. Looking over, his eyes suddenly lit up. "There really is something!"
Soon after, a two-legged, big-eyed monster emerged from a small cave. The monster was holding the corpse of a small animal in its mouth.
The little monster was also very sharp. It discovered Luo Zheng almost at the same time. After staring at Luo Zheng for a breath's time, it suddenly stomped its shiny black feet along the mountain wall.
Its legs were very powerful. With each step it took, it directly jumped from the mountain to another ruin, constantly leaping forward.
After searching for such a long time, how could Luo Zheng give up?
He sped up and chased closely behind the little monster!
This little monster's body was only the size of a palm, but its speed was not slow at all. It easily jumped and shuttled through the wreckage.
Luo Zheng and the little monster chased each other like this for about the time it took for an incense stick to burn. They had traveled thousands of miles, and there were more and more wreckages floating around.
At first, these wreckages were just floating strips, but now, all kinds of wreckages were floating in the air in disorder. It was like a huge garbage dump, but also like a three-dimensional city.
If Luo Zheng's divine sense had not firmly locked onto the little monster, he might have lost it.
After chasing for another incense stick's time, Luo Zheng and the little monster were getting closer and closer. Seeing that it couldn't avoid Luo Zheng, the little monster drilled into the interior of a dilapidated house.
When Luo Zheng followed in, a blue ray of light shone directly at Luo Zheng.
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng didn't know what kind of supernatural power this ray of light was, but the energy contained in the ray of light was not weak.
His expression changed, and his body flipped over. The ray of light almost brushed past Luo Zheng's face, and penetrated the wreckage behind him, sending it into the boundless void.
This ray of light only swept past Luo Zheng's face once, and a finger-thick blood groove appeared on his face!
'What a terrifying power …'
Luo Zheng was inwardly startled. Looking in the direction where the ray of light came from, he discovered another little monster that looked exactly the same. It was just that this little monster's single eye was still emitting a blue glow.
Clearly, that terrifying blue ray of light was released by this little monster!
"Weng …"
The little monster's single eye condensed another blue light spot. At the same time, the surrounding wreckage also lit up with blue light. Many little monsters crawled out from the wreckage.
"Ji ji …" Not far away, that little monster that was holding a small animal in its mouth actually let out a weird laughter.
'Running for so long just to lure me into this trap?' Luo Zheng squinted his eyes slightly.
Luo Zheng did not have any malicious intent towards this little monster. He only wanted to understand this world and find a way to leave. The Chaotic Origin Great World could not do without him. After all, the Evil Deities were still lurking in the dark, eyeing him covetously.
Who would have thought that this little monster did not communicate with him at all and even wanted to kill him.
"Xiu xiu xiu xiu xiu …"
Blue light exploded from the eyes of those little monsters. Many thin and long blue light rays shot out. The blue light rays from different angles interweaved with each other, forming a dense net.
'You are too naive if you think you can defeat me with just this …'
A piece of illusory green-feathered figure appeared above Luo Zheng's head. His figure was constantly shuttling in and out of the blue light rays.
These blue light rays might be powerful, but Luo Zheng was like a piece of leaf that was as light as a feather. He moved around casually without being swept by these light rays.
After dodging continuously, Luo Zheng finally got close to the first little monster when it was unprepared. He suddenly reached out and grabbed it in his hand!
"Ji ji ji ji …"
The blue light rays shot out from the single eye of that little monster were terrifying. But the little monster itself was not strong. Even though it struggled with all its might, it could not escape from Luo Zheng's hand.
Seeing their comrade being caught, the other little monsters became even more excited. The blue light rays shot out from their eyes became denser and denser, forming an airtight net.
It seemed like there was nothing that could stop these light rays from penetrating through. Regardless of what material those floating ruins were made of, they were all penetrated by the light rays.
Luo Zheng carried that little monster and continued moving downwards. Just as he entered a wall-like ruin, this ruin was penetrated by the blue light rays and turned into ashes.
But at this moment, he discovered that one of the blue light rays was refracted. And this light ray had struck a three meter wide metal plate.
'This metal plate is able to block this light ray!'
With a thought, Luo Zheng charged towards the metal plate.

Chapter 3083
Luo Zheng sat on the metal plate. He revealed a helpless look.
The little monster in his hand let out a weird laughter. It seemed to be mocking Luo Zheng for falling into a trap.
This metal plate was originally a cage.
There was nothing in the ruins that could block those blue light rays. Regardless of who it was, they would find something to block the attack of these little monsters.
Those little monsters deliberately shot a blue light ray onto the metal plate. It was equivalent to reminding Luo Zheng that this metal plate could block the blue light rays …
And Luo Zheng was naturally trapped in this cage when he pounced onto the metal plate!
Luo Zheng touched this metal plate. He extended his hand and lightly knocked on it. A force of 800 Divine Jun burst out.
"Dong!"
A dull sound came from the metal plate. It did not move at all.
The toughness of this metal plate had exceeded his imagination. It was impossible for him to smash it apart.
As for the blue light rays surrounding the metal plate …
He took out an earthen-yellow shield from his Sumeru Ring. This shield was something dropped by an outsider from the Chaotic Origin Great World. It was a decent Nirvana Absolute Treasure. Grabbing onto the shield, Xue Ying lightly swept across the fenced-like blue light.
The shield could not block the blue light rays at all. A piece of it had been sliced apart by the edges.
Luo Zheng was confident of his fleshy body. But the faith treasure could not withstand a single blow. He did not dare to use his body to try again.
'Spatial teleportation …'
With a thought, Luo Zheng attempted to use Great Teleportation to teleport away.
But his Spatial Laws could not construct any spatial tunnel!
Spatial teleportation was forbidden in the Chaotic Origin Great World. It relied on powerful experts to seal the space. Ordinary spatial sacred techniques could not break through that level of restriction.
But this damnable place did not seem to have any Spatial Laws. Hence, it was impossible to teleport.
After trying several methods, Luo Zheng could only sit quietly on the metal plate.
That little monster by his hand was still letting out that weird laughter. He frowned. He grabbed onto that little monster and brought it towards the fence formed by the blue light rays.
"Ji ji ji!"
Even though the single eye of these little monsters could emit blue light rays, they were similarly afraid of the blue light rays.
It struggled madly in Luo Zheng's hands, twisting its body.
Luo Zheng was now a prisoner. Naturally, he wouldn't really kill this little monster. After scaring it, the little monster finally became obedient. After Luo Zheng let go of it, it lay on the side and didn't make a sound.
"If these little monsters can set up such a trap, it means that their intelligence is not bad. The key is how to communicate with them and see how they will deal with me …"
While Luo Zheng was thinking, the little monsters that were originally scattered all over the ruins jumped one after another and gathered below the metal plate.
They supported the metal plates and carried Luo Zheng, jumping continuously in the ruins and wreckage, lifting Luo Zheng all the way up.
After shuttling upwards for 30 minutes, there were more and more ruins and wreckage above. But as the direction turned, Luo Zheng saw a scene that surprised him.
The ruins and wreckage above had completely disappeared. In front of Luo Zheng, there was a city formed by ruins.
Because the whole city was formed by piles of debris, the connected houses were all different in material, color, size, shape, and style, giving a strange visual impact.
There were little monsters hiding in the crevices on the roofs, sides, and bottom of those houses. They were quietly sizing up Luo Zheng, this foreign prisoner.
After entering this city of ruins and wreckage, the little monsters threw the metal plates in the center of the city and retreated one after another.
The little monster that was locked in the cage with Luo Zheng made two "ji ji" sounds, as if it was begging its companions to take it away. But the other little monsters ignored it. Obviously, they didn't have any intention of getting it out of the cage.
Luo Zheng sat in the cage, sizing up this grotesque and variegated world.
This space was deleted from the entire chaos. In the process of deletion, it wasn't erased.
Just like Luo Zheng, after being exiled here, he probably felt lonely.
However, there were still living creatures in Luo Zheng's Inner World. Not only were there humans, there were also many other kinds of creatures. He could get these creatures out to accompany him, or even reproduce on their own, expanding into a city or a country.
"The origin of these little monsters is probably the same. I don't know what they plan to do with me …"
Luo Zheng had been thrown here for a long time. The little monsters that came and went showed a strong interest in Luo Zheng. Their big eyes showed curiosity, and there was even a trace of look in their eyes as if they were looking at a rare treasure.
At first, Luo Zheng didn't notice it. But almost every little monster looked at him like this. Luo Zheng felt a little creeped out in his heart.
"9527, what do their eyes mean?" Luo Zheng asked.
9527 was also quietly observing this world. It knew that there were some special spaces outside the chaos. For example, the Nirvana Realm was a special space at the top of the chaos. After the destruction of the past civilizations, they would escape to the Nirvana Realm. It was the last refuge, and it also contained the past civilizations of the chaos.
The secrets of the chaos.
Besides the Nirvana Realm, there was only this space.
But this space was a huge garbage dump. When some special situations couldn't be stopped, the chaos ancient god who controlled the space would delete it and throw it into this garbage dump.
9527's original owner wasn't the one who controlled the space. It didn't know what was going on in this world, so it was naturally very curious now.
"Maybe they're going to eat you as food," 9527 laughed.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng curled his lips and said, "It's not funny at all …"
The possibility that 9527 mentioned was real. The little monster that Luo Zheng first discovered was searching for the carcasses of small animals in the cave. It was obvious that they fed on meat.
"The possibility of eating you is really low. But from the expressions of these little monsters, you are very important to them, very important," 9527 analyzed seriously.
"I feel it too," Luo Zheng nodded in agreement.
But he was a human who was deleted from the mother world. What was the connection between him and the aborigines who lived in this damned place?
Luo Zheng even suspected that he was the first human to be deleted …
After staying in this cage for half a day, the door of the largest house in the distance opened. Dozens of little monsters climbed out.
These little monsters were obviously bigger than the average little monsters, and the little monster in the middle of them was wearing a delicate circular golden ring. Looking at this posture, the little monster with the golden ring had the highest status here.
"Da da da …"
These little monsters jumped a few times and approached the front of the cage.
Then there was another "chirp chirp chirp" jump, especially the little monster with the golden ring. It looked at Luo Zheng for a while and chirped to him again. Its voice was very soft, as if it was trying to comfort Luo Zheng.

Chapter 3084
Luo Zheng approached the fence formed by the blue light and said, "No matter what, can you let me out first?"
As he spoke, Luo Zheng pointed at the blue light and then pointed outside.
Since they couldn't speak, he could only rely on sign language.
He didn't know if the little monster with the golden hoop could understand him. After a while, it left with the other little monsters, leaving Luo Zheng alone in the cage. His face was full of helplessness, and the little monsters beside Luo Zheng were probably equally helpless.
After six to eight hours, some little monsters brought him some raw meat. They didn't know that Luo Zheng had fasted and thought that Luo Zheng needed to eat like them.
The raw meat was cut from the corpses. Even if it didn't rot, how could it be eaten?
He looked at it in boredom and saw that the little monster beside him was looking at him eagerly. He threw it over.
The little monster's single eye was full of excitement and gratitude. It ate the raw meat heartily.
"The blue light from the eyes of these little monsters is very powerful. It can directly hurt me, which means that their energy level is very high. But why didn't they fasted and rely on this raw meat for food?" Luo Zheng looked at the little monsters and thought.
Energy at the level of Divine Dao Taoist Connotation couldn't hurt Luo Zheng unless it was fused with Divine Dao.
Creatures who obtained Divine Dao Taoist Connotation had already entered the True God Realm. They needed to absorb the Spiritual Energy of Heaven and Earth to perform that kind of supernatural power.
As for the Nirvana Realm, they needed to absorb Divine Crystals to replenish their Nirvana Power.
The energy that the raw meat could replenish was so little that it wasn't worth mentioning. It was the most primitive and basic way. It was illogical to rely on eating these things to release the terrifying blue light.
Moreover, this space had existed for so many years. The corpses in the wreckage zone should have been dug out by these little monsters. But now, they could still dig out the corpses of small animals. It was very strange.
After being locked in the cage for two days, these little monsters finally moved.
All the little monsters in the wreckage city were gathered in this area and lined up neatly.
The little monster with the golden hoop stood on a high ground and used a high-pitched voice to call out loudly. The little monsters below responded with a uniform "Jiji" sound from time to time.
Luo Zheng didn't understand what they were saying, but he understood that it was some kind of oath.
For example, the start of a war, or some sort of encouragement before an operation …
What was he trying to do?
After the golden-ringed little monster spoke for a while, the strongest little monsters jumped towards Luo Zheng and lifted the metal plate again. It seemed that they were going to transport Luo Zheng to another place.
But just as they were about to set off, a bright red spot suddenly emerged from the ruins in the distance.
Those red dots were extremely fast. In the blink of an eye, they were above the ruined city. At this time, Luo Zheng could see clearly what those things were. They were a type of two-headed flying snake with bright red skin.
At the moment when the two-headed flying snake appeared, the aura of all the little monsters changed. Their single eye was flashing with hatred. Bits of blue light began brewing in their eyes.
"Xiu xiu xiu xiu …"
Many blue light rays shot upwards. There were many of them and they were densely packed!
Seeing these lethal blue light rays, Luo Zheng felt startled inwardly.
If he couldn't use Great Teleportation, not to mention him, even experts at the Chaotic Origin Realm or existences like Qiu Yinhe might not be able to survive!
But those two-headed flying snakes in the sky weren't afraid at all. They opened their snake heads and spat out many blood-red bubbles!
These bubbles were moving at an extremely high speed. If they were struck by the blue light rays, the two would rapidly be annihilated! But even more blood-red bubbles passed through the blue light rays and fell towards the ruined city below.
A blood-red bubble landed not far away from Luo Zheng. With a "bo" sound, it exploded. Strands of blood-red energy spread out like lightning, forming a sphere of annihilation about three meters wide.
Everything inside the ball had been completely melted away!
'The energy grasped by these two-headed strange snakes is terrifying …' Luo Zheng looked at the circular crater not far away. His eyelids were twitching continuously.
When the blood-red bubble landed, it immediately caused this ruined city to be riddled with holes. But the blue light rays also struck some of the two-headed flying snakes. Those two-headed flying snakes that were pierced through did not have any chance of surviving.
The hatred between these two races was extremely strong. Once they met, they would fight to the death.
"Ji ji ji …"
That little monster with the hoop let out a series of weird cries at Luo Zheng. The little monsters below the metal plate carried the cage and fled. These little monsters were extremely worried about Luo Zheng's safety. They brought Luo Zheng away from the battlefield where both parties were fighting.
But just as Luo Zheng moved a distance away from the cage, the two-headed flying snakes in the sky turned in a different direction and chased after Luo Zheng!
Now, Luo Zheng understood what was going on …
It seemed that these little monsters were not the only ones interested in him. Those two-headed flying snakes were also interested in him. They were coming for him!
"Huff huff huff …"
The little monsters escorted Luo Zheng through the ruins. The two-headed flying snakes were in hot pursuit. They looked like they would not give up until they reached their goal!
Under such a battle, the number of casualties was unknown.
But both sides were very "considerate" of Luo Zheng. Whether it was the bubbles spat out by the two-headed flying snakes or the blue light rays shot out from the eyes of the little monsters, they all carefully avoided the cage imprisoning Luo Zheng for fear of hurting Luo Zheng inside the cage!
The fierce battle lasted for a while. The little monsters carried Luo Zheng and retreated. They actually escaped into another wreckage zone.
Just as he jumped onto this wreckage zone, Luo Zheng's eyes lit up.
At the edge of the wreckage zone, there was actually a yellow planet floating!
This planet was extremely pocket-sized. It was only a few thousand feet in diameter. But what surprised Luo Zheng was that at the top of the planet stood a huge statue. It was actually a statue of a human!
Luo Zheng had previously wondered if he was the first human to be exiled to this place. Now, it seemed that his guess was very wrong.
It seemed that many years ago, there had been humans who had set foot here …
But from the traces in this space, many civilizations had existed for an extremely long time. There were even many former dominator level civilizations. Then, when was this human statue in front of him built?

Chapter 3085
The little monsters continued to carry Luo Zheng, walking along the surface of the pocket-sized planet.
After a while, they walked in front of the huge statue.
Luo Zheng looked up and carefully sized it up. This human statue had a beard, a rough face, and a pair of starry eyes that contained a unique power. The surface of the statue was covered with fine runes.
These runes extended all the way from the body of the statue to the bottom, divided into two branches, and finally extended to an altar.
When Luo Zheng's eyes rested on the altar, his heart slightly jumped, "Damn, these guys aren't planning to sacrifice me, are they?"
This space was completely different from what Luo Zheng had experienced before. Everything was grotesque and variegated, without any rules to speak of.
From the way the little monsters protected him, it was as if they were carefully protecting a sacrificial offering!
As soon as Luo Zheng finished his sentence, the little monsters carried Luo Zheng onto the altar and threw the metal plate onto the altar with a loud thud.
"Hey …" Luo Zheng shouted while sticking to the fence formed by the blue light.
"Ji ji ji ji ji …"
One of the little monsters faced Luo Zheng and made a strange noise. It seemed to be explaining to Luo Zheng, but what use was this explanation to Luo Zheng?
After the little monster's explanation, two unique triangular runes suddenly appeared in the eyes of the human statue. The triangular runes flipped, and the eyeballs began to roll, looking at Luo Zheng with a strange gaze.
Luo Zheng also looked at the pair of eyeballs with a complicated gaze.
After a while, a bright white light escaped from the eyeballs. This light spread down the statue's eyes, as if it was condensed into tears and flowed down. These two beams of light continued to flow, one on the left and one on the right, and quickly converged on the altar.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
The altar flashed with a strong light, wrapping Luo Zheng and the metal plate together.
When the light on the altar appeared, all the little monsters retreated. The feet on both sides of the eyeballs knelt on the ground, and their eyes revealed a strange look of respect.
The light that shrouded the sacrificial altar lasted for about the time it took for an incense stick to burn, and then gradually dissipated.
After the light dissipated, Luo Zheng breathed a sigh of relief. It seemed that they didn't intend to kill him …
But at this moment, a shadow suddenly appeared in front of the sacrificial altar. The shadow kept materializing and finally turned into an ancient door.
"A spatial gate … but there are no spatial fluctuations?"
The principle of the Space Gate was similar to that of the Transmission Array. Given enough time, Luo Zheng could also easily create one.
However, Luo Zheng had already tried, and the Space Laws in this space did not exist at all!
When the spatial door was formed, it didn't produce any spatial fluctuations. Naturally, he found it very strange.
"Kacha …"
The quaint door opened from the middle.
A man and a woman stepped out of the door and stood in the air.
When the little monsters saw the man and woman, they imitated the actions of humans and kept kowtowing. It was as if the man and woman were gods who had descended from the heavens and were worshipping them with extraordinary reverence.

The man was quite delicate and elegant, and the woman was beautiful and had a faint seductive aura.

Even he couldn't resist this blue light. The consequences of this delicate man touching it would be unimaginable.

He gently brushed his finger, and the blue light passed through his finger, but it didn't hurt him in the slightest!
With this wave of his hand, the blue rays of light were absorbed into his palm, and the entire fence formed by the blue rays of light disappeared without a trace!
"Ji ji ji …"
The little monster that had been trapped with Luo Zheng screamed a few times, and then jumped down from the sacrificial altar and knelt on the ground with the other little monsters. The seductive woman and the delicate man shook their heads at the same time, and then said, "We are not from the chaos."

Outside the chaos was an inexplicable concept. Even 9527 couldn't explain it. Perhaps he could find the answer from them.

Chapter 3089
No matter how prosperous a round of chaos was, in the end, it could not avoid that needle.
All souls were destroyed, and the living fell into eternal slumber.
This was not a catastrophe, but the fate of chaos since its birth.
In order to disobey this fate, there was only one way, and that was to transform into a Nirvana creature and hide in the Nirvana Realm.
Countless units of time had passed, and not a single living being had been spared.
Either enter the Nirvana Realm, die, or take the slim chance of becoming the next Child of Truth. But even after surviving two rounds of chaos, the outcome was still "death."
Countless creatures and geniuses had tried all kinds of methods.
Escaping from chaos was the simplest and most direct method.
But from the blossoming to the withering of civilizations, no one had ever succeeded.
Now, this "Ashoka King" was not only hiding in the "Recycle Bin" of this space, avoiding the doom of death after the arrival of the end, but even penetrating the chaos. The shock in 9527 and the Spirit of the Green Jade was almost indescribable.
Whether they believed it or not, now that the truth was in front of them, they naturally hoped that Luo Zheng would go and find out.
The handsome man and the seductive woman took the lead and went into the round hole, followed by Luo Zheng.
The wall of the round hole was made of an extremely dense brown material, not mud or rock. Luo Zheng reached out and gently pressed on the wall, and then burst out with the power of the Nirvana Realm.
But the wall did not react at all. The material of the wall was so solid that it was almost impossible to break …
After the handsome man noticed Luo Zheng's action, he smiled and said, "This wall is impossible to break. Master said that even the power of a hundred thousand or a million Godly Jun can't leave a mark on it …"
"…"
