"It's not a matter of firmness. Master said that this wall is absolutely indestructible," the seductive woman continued.
"Then how did your master dig through this thing?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Master naturally has his ways." The handsome man revealed a proud expression.
Almost every sentence of these two was related to Ashoka King …
Luo Zheng also gave up testing the strength of the cave wall and continued to drill forward with the other two.
The length of this cave was also beyond Luo Zheng's imagination. The cave was only half the height of a person, but it actually extended for dozens of miles. Climbing like this was not tiring, but it still took him four hours to reach the end.
When they reached the end of the cave, Luo Zheng, 9527, and the Green Jade Spirit all looked over.
"This is the world outside the Chaos?"
What appeared in front of Luo Zheng was a starry sky.
The dots of light, near and far, emitted a bright blue light.
Previously, the delicate man and the seductive woman had described it to be extremely mysterious. However, this was only a void filled with stars …
The handsome man smiled and said, "Outside the Chaos, it's not like what you see … Try stretching your hand out of this cave."
Luo Zheng looks at him strangely and reaches his hand out of the cave according to what the handsome man said.
Then, a strange scene appeared.
The moment his finger left the cave, it turned into countless dots of starlight and spread out in all directions!
The further Luo Zheng stretched his hand out, the more the dots of starlight separated from each other. If Luo Zheng slowly retracted his hand, all the dots of starlight would gather at the entrance of the cave again.
When Luo Zheng completely retracted his hand into the cave, the scattered dots of starlight returned to his hand.
"This … is so magical …"
From beginning to end, Luo Zheng did not feel the slightest difference. Although his hand turned into dots of starlight, it did not hurt at all. He even felt the sensation of touch!
Seeing Luo Zheng's reaction, the handsome man and the seductive woman looked as if they had already expected it.
The seductive woman also gently stretched out her hand and waved it continuously. The scattered dots of starlight also danced in the void as she waved her hand.
"I'll go out and take a look," Luo Zheng said.
Then he got up and wanted to get out of the cave.
"No!"
Unexpectedly, the handsome man and the seductive woman's expressions changed and they blocked Luo Zheng's way.
"What's wrong?" Luo Zheng asked with a strange expression.
The handsome man said, "Master said that this state is called the Mysterious State. It's okay if the rest of your body turns into the Mysterious State, but if your whole body goes in, you won't be able to find the way back. You'll be lost in this state forever!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng also took a breath of cold air. Outside the Chaos was indeed an unimaginable existence.
What appeared in front of them was not the void, but a special state where matter appeared in a different way. Space and time were also indescribable.
"Then how did your master and the others get in?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"Master has his own way … If you really want to see the outside, you can stick your head out. We'll pull you back after the time it takes to burn a stick of incense," the seductive woman suggested.
The two of them did not dare to enter the space outside the Chaos, but out of curiosity, they had used this method to see.
"That's one way!"
After Luo Zheng nodded, he leaned his body out of the cave. The handsome man grabbed Luo Zheng's legs from behind.
The moment Luo Zheng's head poked out of the cave, it also turned into countless bright blue starlights. As more and more of Luo Zheng's head poked out of the cave, the starlights began to spread rapidly.
Until now, Luo Zheng still did not feel anything strange.
Just as his whole head poked out, he felt his vision go dark and he could no longer see anything.
What replaced it was a new feeling!
Luo Zheng had never felt this before. In this void, everything around him became nothingness. He was one of the stars, and also all the stars!
Here, the distance became infinitely long and also infinitely short. He could reach out and touch the space that was farther away, but he could not reach the space that was closer.
Time accelerated with his thoughts. A hundred million years, ten billion years, an immeasurable number of years, but with a turn of his thoughts, only a moment passed.
Luo Zheng could not describe this strange feeling at all!
Just as the handsome man and the seductive woman said, in this state, he could not see the Chaos at all. Naturally, he could not find the cave. He even completely lost the perception of his own body.
If he rashly came in, he was afraid that he would be lost in this state forever.
Because he had lost the concept of time, Luo Zheng did not know how much time had passed. It seemed like a blink of an eye, but also like countless years.

Chapter 3090
After returning to the cave, Luo Zheng sat in the same place in a daze.
Switching back from the state of Xuan Liang, Luo Zheng was very uncomfortable.
"How was it?" The handsome man asked with a smile.
The seductive woman saw Luo Zheng's expression and looked as if she had expected it.
They had the same reaction when they first entered the space outside the Chaos.
After a while, Luo Zheng reacted.
He looked at the void outside the Chaos, and his expression became very complicated. "So, the light spots outside are actually other substances in the space?"
The handsome man nodded and said, "Master said that all substances are scattered. Maybe one of the light spots is part of Master …"
The void outside the Chaos was full of light spots, and substances were intertwined with each other. There was no distinction between you and me.
Luo Zheng had gone out once, and understood that it was meaningless to judge the size and distance of the void with his current eyesight.
"Luo Zheng, let me try!"
"I want to try too!"
9527 and the Spirit of the Green Jade said at the same time.
When Luo Zheng poked his head out, 9527 and the Spirit of the Green Jade were still lurking in his body, and did not follow Luo Zheng into the Chaos.
They were also very curious about this space.
Luo Zheng thought for a while and said with his consciousness, "Go into my arm."
After the Spirit of the Green Jade and 9527's consciousness quickly went into Luo Zheng's arm, Luo Zheng reached his right hand out of the Chaos.
Like before, his right hand turned into a light spot and quickly spread out. This time, it was the consciousness of 9527 and the Spirit of the Green Jade that spread out.
While letting the two consciousnesses observe the state of Xuan Liang, Luo Zheng said to the handsome man, "Since your Master has turned into the state of Xuan Liang, how can you send a message to him and let him return?"
The seductive woman pointed to the edge of the cave. There were slender ropes nailed to the edge of the cave, and complex runes were drawn on the surface of the ropes.
There were a total of thirteen ropes. Because most of the ropes extended out of the chaos, only a small section the length of a finger was left in the cave. Luo Zheng had not noticed it before.
"Master and the other senior brothers have tied the Heaven Unraveling Rope around their bodies. Through the Heaven Unraveling Rope, they can spread the news," said the seductive woman.
"Thirteen ropes. There should be a total of thirteen people … Using the ropes to secure themselves? This King Ashoka sure has some ideas," Luo Zheng thought to himself.
After the seductive woman explained, she gently raised her jade-like hand, and red, yellow, and blue energy beams spiraled from her palm.
Among the three energy beams, Luo Zheng had seen two of them before. They were the blue light beam emitted by the Big Eye Monster and the red bubble emitted by the Two-Headed Flying Snake. As for the third energy beam, it was the first time that Luo Zheng had seen it.
They had also said that King Ashoka had raised three races in the void. Presumably, the last race used the yellow energy.
"Hum …"
After the three energy beams formed a ball in the seductive woman's hand, she gently patted it on the runes on one of the ropes. The runes on the rope suddenly lit up and extended out of the chaos, turning into a few spots of light that quickly disappeared into the distance.
From the looks of it, the mysterious space outside the chaos was not untraceable. At the very least, King Ashoka had found the rules within.
At this time, Luo Zheng also withdrew his right hand from outside the cave. 9527 and the Green Jade Spirit that had been hidden in his right hand naturally returned as well.
"…" 9527.
"…" The Green Jade Spirit.
One was the former Scion of Truth, the other was the split soul of the current Chaos Elder God.
What kind of grand spectacle had they not seen? What kind of secrets had they not known?
But after entering the state of Profound Quantity, they were still deeply shocked.
Especially the Green Jade Spirit. It really did not expect that the space it had once controlled could extend out of the chaos and present such an inconceivable scene.
Luo Zheng did not ask how they felt. Instead, he asked, "How long do we have to wait to pass the message to your master?"
The handsome man thought for a moment before replying, "It's hard to say. Sometimes, master is in the space for too long and returns slowly. Sometimes, he returns very quickly. It depends on his mood." The seductive woman also said, "In the past, a golden-faced ghost was banished here. That golden-faced ghost had a very unique Nirvana token. Senior Brother and I couldn't beat it. In the end, we could only escape into this cave and call for Master. That time, Master only took a few breaths to come back.
In just a few breaths, the golden-faced ghost was subdued. "
"It depends on whether master is willing to return. If he values it very much, I'm afraid that in an instant …" As the handsome man spoke, the stars in the distance constantly fluctuated and gathered in the direction of the cave entrance.
The seductive woman pursed her lips and said, "It seems that master values you very much."
As disciples of King Ashoka, King Ashoka only appeared in moments of life and death.
Now that the seductive woman had just passed on Luo Zheng's message, master began to return. The seductive woman was actually a little jealous.
"Buzz …" More and more light spots gathered at the cave entrance. These light spots kept shrinking until they finally outlined a blurry human silhouette. The moment this human silhouette entered the cave, a curly-bearded man appeared in front of Luo Zheng and the others. This curly-bearded man and Luo Zheng were on the pocket-sized planet.
The statues they saw were exactly the same.
"Master!"
"Master!"
The seductive woman and the handsome man cupped their fists at the curly-bearded man and bowed respectfully.
After the curly-bearded man nodded slightly, his gaze fell on Luo Zheng. A pair of thick eyebrows suddenly rose as he sized him up very carefully.
"Greetings, senior." Luo Zheng also cupped his fists at the curly-bearded man and bowed.
King Ashoka did not respond. His gaze finally fell on Luo Zheng's right hand. He said, "There are two foreign race souls in your body. Why don't you show yourself? If you want to continue hiding and secretly observe me, don't blame me for erasing your consciousness! "
Hearing their master mention the foreign races, the seductive woman and the handsome man's faces immediately tensed up.
Under any circumstances, King Ashoka only recognized the human race and was extremely repulsive of other foreign races.
Some of the creatures who were removed from this space were extremely innocent. In order to survive, many of them had knelt and begged for mercy.
However, their master showed no mercy to the foreign races who entered this space. He did not leave a single one alive. Not only that, but their master also ordered his disciples to do the same. Over time, the seductive woman and the handsome man, like King Ashoka, had killed countless foreign races and developed an instinctive repulsion towards them. Luo Zheng was the only one who had survived after so many units of time.

Chapter 3091
Hearing King Ashoka's unfriendly tone, Luo Zheng's expression stiffened.
"Senior, these two are my companions …"
Luo Zheng was about to explain when King Ashoka cut him off. "No matter who you are, please reveal your true appearance. Otherwise, don't blame me for being impolite."
If it were any other situation, King Ashoka would have killed them without hesitation.
However, Luo Zheng was still a human. Since they were brought here by Luo Zheng, King Ashoka restrained himself. Still, he could not tolerate 9527 and the Green Jade Spirit spying on him in secret.
The Green Jade Spirit and 9527 did not hesitate. They transformed into two human soul forms, one green and one brown, and appeared before King Ashoka. At the same time, they cupped their hands in greeting.
"We have been residing in Luo Zheng's body for a long time. We have never shown our true appearances to anyone, nor do we have any intention of spying on you. We hope you can forgive us," said the Green Jade Spirit.
King Ashoka sized up the Spirit of the Green Jade, and a strange look appeared in his eyes. However, he smiled and said, "I'm familiar with your aura. You once controlled this space. You're the Child of Truth of the Green Jade civilization, right?"
The Green Jade Spirit nodded and smiled helplessly. "You are so powerful that I did not realize you were hiding here."
"If you had, I would not have lived to this day." King Ashoka glanced at the Green Jade Spirit.
Every once in a while, all matter within this space would be completely erased. This was the backyard of the Child of Truth, who controlled the truth of space. No matter which Child of Truth it was, they couldn't tolerate a single human living here.
"You must be joking," the Green Jade Spirit replied. "You have such great power. You can pass through the Wall of Chaos and travel beyond the chaos. If you can uncover the mysteries within, our Green Jade civilization would be more than happy to coexist with you. Why would we be your enemy?"
King Ashoka's expression did not change. "Even if you are not willing to be my enemy, the civilization behind you may not be. The situation on the other side is extremely complicated. How can a Child of Truth like you make decisions?"
For a moment, the jade spirit didn't know how to respond to King Ashoka's words. Speaking of which, if the jade spirit had sensed King Ashoka's existence back then, it would most likely have thought of a way to eliminate him.
"Forget it …" Duke Ashoka suddenly let out a long sigh. "At least I've survived. Now, even if those Scions of the Truth control the truth of space, they can forget about touching this space!"
Hearing King Ashoka's words, a strange expression appeared on the Green Jade Spirit's face. "Are you saying that even the Scion of Truth is unable to control this space?"
"Of course," Duke Ashoka said proudly. "They can only delete the space here, but they can't retrieve it. They can't even see what's inside this space. I've taken away the rights of the Scion of Truth"
Depriving the Child of Truth of his rights …
King Ashoka spoke calmly, but Luo Zheng, the Green Jade Spirit, and 9527 felt as if thunder had exploded in their hearts.
Such a rule was defined by the truth. Who could easily break the truth of the entire Chaos?
Amidst his shock, Luo Zheng's eyes lit up slightly. "Senior, since you've taken away the rights of the Scion of Truth, can you send me out?"
King Ashoka glanced at Luo Zheng and asked, "Are you in a hurry to go back?"
"Yes," Luo Zheng pursed his lips and nodded.
King Ashoka knew that most of the creatures sent into this space had offended the Scion of Truth.
However, the Scion of Truth's opponents were all exceptionally strong. Luo Zheng did not have such strength.
"Don't worry," King Ashoka said again. "I can help you find the time node of this space. No matter how much time is wasted, I'll send you back at that time node."
"Locate the time node at will!"
9527 and the Green Jade Spirit were shocked again.
The smaller the world, the easier it was to control it.
If it was one's own Inner World, as the omnipotent creator, controlling the flow of time and even reversing time would not be difficult.
However, an Inner World with a chaotic timeline was not a good thing for the creator either.
Usually, unless it was absolutely necessary, no one would mess up the flow of time in their Inner World too much.
In the universe, the Saints and even the Realm Masters could manipulate time and even change the flow of time. However, in the Divine Realm, such abilities were extremely rare and precious.
When it was expanded to the Mother World and the Chaos, it was basically impossible to directly cultivate supernatural powers to manipulate time and manipulate it.
Apart from a very small number of unique Nirvana tokens, almost no one could surpass the truth of time!
As for the truth of time, even the Chaos Ancient Gods could not grasp it!
Therefore, when King Ashoka said that he could locate the time node at will and send Luo Zheng back, they were extremely shocked.
"That's for the best!" Luo Zheng's face was full of joy.
King Ashoka glanced outside the cave. This time, he returned alone. The other twelve disciples were still floating outside the Chaos. He then said, "Let's return to the palace first."
Not long after, the group returned to the palace built on the Pillar of Ji's Deletion.
Along the way, King Ashoka asked about the situation in the Chaos. Luo Zheng told him about the situation of the several major human clans. At the same time, he also mentioned some of the superpowers in the Mother World.
When he heard that the You Xiong Clan and the Divine Farmer Clan had joined forces to exterminate the Jiuli Tribe, King Ashoka frowned and sighed, "I didn't expect that there would be a day when the Human Clan would kill each other!"
In King Ashoka's era, the Human Clan was a whole. As a dominating force, they occupied the entire Chaos.
Although they could not defeat that needle in the end, the Human Clan was extremely united from beginning to end.
From the way King Ashoka ostracized and hated the Alien Clan, it could be seen that he was always concerned about his own clan. He did not expect that when the Human Clan was reborn in the Chaos, they would be in such a complicated situation. He actually felt heartache.
"Senior, with your supernatural powers, if you can return to the Chaos, you can end that conflict," Luo Zheng suddenly said.
"Return?" King Ashoka shook his head.
"Senior, aren't you in control of this space? Can you reclaim everything in this space?" Luo Zheng asked.
King Ashoka revealed a helpless smile. At the same time, he pointed at 9527's soul and said, "He can return to the Chaos. You can also return to the Chaos. But can he?"
King Ashoka pointed at the Green Jade Spirit and said.
Although they were both souls, the Green Jade Spirit and 9527 were fundamentally different.
9527 was a genuine Divided Soul. The Green Jade Spirit relied on the "Instrument of Civilization" to carry Luo Zheng and appear in the Chaos. In fact, the Green Jade Spirit's body was still in the Nirvana Realm. He was in a descending state.
"Senior, you mean …" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.

Chapter 3092
After a chaotic world was destroyed, the new world's rules would completely suppress everything in the previous world.
Living beings, matter, and even consciousness would be rejected.
Other than the chaotic ancient gods who had comprehended the truth, all living beings who wanted to live could only escape to the other side.
The space that King Ashoka was in was independent of the chaotic world. Although he could avoid the deletion of the Pillar of Time and control this space, he could only hide in this space like the other creatures on the other side.
"Another Paramita …" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
9527 and the Green Jade Spirit were also surprised. They only saw this space as a "recycle bin," but from another perspective, this space could avoid the extinction of the chaotic world. It could indeed be called another Paramita!
King Ashoka smiled and said, "Actually, I also intend to turn this place into another Paramita, a Paramita that belongs only to us humans. The other races can forget about getting a share of it!"
The situation on the other side was very complicated. Not only were there countless Yang souls, but there were also many kinds of Paramita creatures …
If one race could monopolize the other side, how terrifying would that be?
King Ashoka was very ambitious!
This news was naturally good for the human race. Luo Zheng thought for a moment and asked, "But how do the creatures of the chaotic world enter this space?"
The entrance to this space was controlled by the "mountain" and the chaotic ancient god behind it. It was difficult for King Ashoka to ensure that the ancient god was on his side.
King Ashoka asked calmly, "How do the creatures of the chaotic world enter the other side?"
"By cultivating the True Meaning of Dao, of course," Luo Zheng answered without thinking.
When Luo Zheng answered, the Green Jade Spirit and 9527 exchanged glances. They already had a feeling in their hearts.
Luo Zheng immediately reacted. "Is there a True Meaning of Dao that can lead to this place?"
King Ashoka's face revealed a meaningful expression. He reached out and three energies of red, yellow, and blue spiraled up. "This True Meaning of Dao is my life's work. I call it the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction."
"You created a True Meaning of Dao!" 9527 felt that his brain could not wrap around it.
"How is this possible …" The Green Jade Spirit's eyes were also filled with confusion.
What they had seen and heard today had completely overturned their understanding.
The Green Jade Spirit understood everything more thoroughly than 9527. All the Sanskrit was passed down by it through the Nirvana Realm, and the thirteen True Meanings of Tao recorded should have been created by the No.1 Civilization.
There were always people who found special Nirvana Realm tokens in the Nirvana Realm, but no one had ever created the True Meaning of Dao!
King Ashoka fused the three types of energy into one, and the three colors merged into a ball of black energy.
As the black energy continued to roll, it finally turned into a stone statue. This statue was actually a horse's head!
"Creating things out of nothing …" Luo Zheng's eyes kept twitching.
The ability to create things out of thin air could only be completed in one's own inner world.
But Duke Ashoka had actually used the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction to casually create a stone sculpture …
This action also shocked the three of them.
"Crack!"
As King Ashoka gently squeezed, the horse's head was crushed into powder. He said calmly, "The thirteen True Meanings of Tao were indeed created by the Instant Civilization, but they were all castrated by the Elemental Spirit Civilization and lost their original abilities. Back then, our human civilization fought a bloody battle with the Elemental Spirit Civilization in the Thirty-second Heaven, and the Elemental Spirit Civilization suffered a great loss. Since then, all the True Meanings of Tao have lost their original powers …"
"The human race was once so powerful in the bloody battle with the Elemental Spirit Civilization?" The Green Jade Spirit was even more shocked.
Even a civilization as powerful as the Green Jade Spirit Civilization couldn't make an enemy of the Elemental Spirit Civilization. The Green Jade Spirit didn't expect the human race to be so glorious in the Nirvana Realm!
It was just that there was a long gap between the 77th and 749th civilizations. When the human race retreated in defeat and chose to go into hiding, the legends of the human race disappeared without a trace. It was normal that the Green Jade Spirit hadn't heard of them.
King Ashoka's expression was proud, and the corners of his mouth curled up slightly. He ignored the Green Jade Spirit. The human race of the past was certainly not a nameless race!
Luo Zheng was concerned about another question. "Has the True Meaning of Dao been castrated?"
King Ashoka looked at Luo Zheng with a smile and asked, "Why? You noticed? "
"No," Luo Zheng shook his head and said, "I cultivated the True Meaning of the Eternal Sword Fate and entered the Dao. However, I unintentionally obtained two Sanskrit characters from the True Meaning of Enlightenment. These two Sanskrit characters seem to have completed the True Meaning of the Eternal Sword Fate. I believe the castrated character that King Ashoka spoke of should be referring to the two extra Sanskrit characters?"
King Ashoka looked at Luo Zheng and said, "Demonstrate it for me."
Luo Zheng nodded, closed his eyes and meditated. He began to recite silently.
Not long after, a white cyclone formed in his mind. Although he didn't comprehend the True Meaning of the True Meaning of Enlightenment, it was enough for him to easily form a cyclone of this level.
The moment he saw the cyclone of True Meaning, King Ashoka nodded and said, "It's done."
"Does this count as … a part of the castrated character?" Luo Zheng asked.
King Ashoka nodded and said, "Yes. The True Meaning of Enlightenment that you speak of should have been deliberately released by someone."
Donghuang Taiyi obtained that yellow paper. It translated the True Meaning of the Eternal Sword Fate and formed the foundation of Taiyi Heavenly Palace.
As for whether Donghuang Taiyi obtained it by chance or was given to him by someone, only Donghuang Taiyi himself would know …
"Other than this True Meaning of Enlightenment, are there other castrated characters of the True Meaning of Dao?" King Ashoka asked again.
Luo Zheng shook his head and said, "I've never heard of them."
King Ashoka looked at 9527. 9527 also denied it and said, "I don't think so."
"The True Meaning of Thirteen Gates of Dao in the 749th Civilization should also be the castrated part that you speak of," the Green Jade Spirit replied.
With that said, Luo Zheng thought of something important and said, "Not only that, the True Meaning of Strength Crusher Divine Physique can't enter the Dao and can't use the physical body to enter the Nirvana Realm …"
"The True Meaning of Strength Crusher Divine Physique is also banned?" King Ashoka thought quietly after hearing this news. Then, a smile of understanding appeared on his face and he said, "As expected, those cowering in the Nirvana Realm are starting to feel afraid …"
The human race had never been a weak race. They had been immersed in it for so many years, all for this day.
"But senior, as you said, if we turn this place into a Nirvana Realm, where can we find the Nirvana Realm Token? And how can we obtain the power of the Nirvana Realm? "Luo Zheng asked again.
King Ashoka pointed at the void and said, "Aren't the Nirvana Realm Tokens all there?"
In the distant void, streaks of wreckage floated quietly.
"…"
Luo Zheng, 9527, and the Green Jade Spirit were speechless.

Chapter 3093
With King Ashoka's ability to control this space, it was not difficult to clean up the wreckage.
He left the wreckage here for a purpose, which was to keep it as a Nirvana Token.
"But these wreckage ruins themselves have no energy, no Nirvana Power, and should not have those complicated abilities …" Luo Zheng said after thinking for a while.
Whether a Nirvana Token was powerful or not could be seen by dividing it into two.
The strength of the Nirvana Token's Nirvana Power, and whether the Nirvana Token had any unique abilities.
The stronger the Nirvana Power, the stronger the bearer's power.
Although the mysterious Nirvana Realm Token's Nirvana Power wasn't strong, it often possessed an extremely abnormal ability. For example, the Heart of an Accomplice could forcibly end one's life and that of another powerhouse. Such an ability was actually so heaven-defying that it was almost unreasonable.
King Ashoka took a few steps, looked at the void, and said, "According to their thinking, it is almost impossible to complete the Path of the End by relying on the Nirvana Token. Back then, our human race fell into this nest."
9527 and Luo Zheng did not understand, but the Green Jade Spirit's eyes suddenly flashed.
After all, the Green Jade Civilization was once a dominating force, and had also fought against that needle, and wanted to complete the Path of the End!
"Senior, do you mean that it is impossible to complete the Path of the End by relying on the power of the Nirvana Token?" asked the Green Jade Spirit.
For the first time, King Ashoka looked at the Green Jade Spirit with an admiring gaze, and then said, "Among so many civilizations, our human race should have been the closest at that time. We almost completed it …"
"Almost?" A strange look flashed in the Green Jade Spirit's eyes. "Our Wing Monarch had fused with the Spiritless Grass of the 33 Levels of Heaven, and then used the Lamp of Judgment to reflect a thousand Spiritless Grass. After extracting the Nirvana Power, the power of withering was multiplied by thirty times, and combined with the Nether Thunder Shield, but …
It was a failure, and it should have been almost … "
As the strongest powerhouse of the Green Jade Civilization, the Wing Monarch was naturally chosen as the Person of the End.
The Green Jade Civilization gathered the power of an entire civilization and combined the Nirvana Realm tokens they searched for together. They unleashed the most terrifying power to fight against the needle.
For example, the Supreme Spirit Grass was a type of rootless grass that grew in the depths of the Thirty-third Heaven. It looked inconspicuous, but in reality, the Paramita Power it contained was the strongest among all the Paramita Tokens!
The Lamp of Judgment was also a rare Nirvana Realm Token. It was considered a supreme Nirvana Realm Token within the Thirty-two Levels of Heaven. Using the Lamp of Judgment to reflect the shadow of the Spiritless Grass, one could inherit half of the Nirvana Power of the Spiritless Grass. Reflecting a thousand Spiritless Grass was equivalent to increasing the power of the Spiritless Grass by five times.
A hundred times!
Luo Zheng had used the power of withering before. It was a Hermit Supernatural Power passed down to him by the Jiuli Tribe. However, the power of the Hermit Supernatural Power itself was far less than when used by the original body. When used by the Yi Monarch and the strong cultivators around him, the effect was naturally very different.
By multiplying it by 30 times on the basis of 500 times, the Paramita Power that was obtained was a number that would make one shudder.
This was the Yi Monarch's Path of the End!
When Luo Zheng heard the Green Jade Spirit's words, he was already very shocked. How terrifying was the power of the Yi Monarch's Path of the End?
Even with such a terrifying method, it still could not fight against the needle.
The Yi Monarch was the king of the Green Jade Civilization. Although the Green Jade Civilization had already been destroyed, the Green Jade Spirit was still quite proud when mentioning him.
Especially the Green Jade Civilization's Path of the End. It was already quite impressive to be able to reach that step …
Therefore, the Green Jade Civilization was not weak in the Nirvana Realm. It was also a powerful civilization that was once ranked in the top ten.
After hearing the Green Jade Spirit mention the Path of the End, King Ashoka shook his head and said, "The Green Jade Civilization's collecting ability is not bad. You were even able to find the Lamp of Judgment and the Netherworld Thunder Shield."
"Your Excellency is too kind." The Green Jade Spirit's face showed a trace of relief. At least this proved that the Green Jade Civilization did not do badly back then, although they still could not avoid failure.
Unexpectedly, King Ashoka changed the topic and said, "I'm not praising you. You are not just a little bit away from completing the Path of the End … There are no spiritual herbs carrying the Nirvana Realm token, and then the Lamp of Judgment and the power of withering to enhance it. This method is only one of our alternatives, and we are the most powerful.
We are not using the Netherworld Thunder Shield, but the Illusory Void Shield with stronger defense. "
"At that time, we chose a total of ten people to be the Person of the End. The person who used this plan was only ranked eighth …" King Ashoka shook his head as he spoke.
"Eighth …" The Green Jade Spirit's eyes widened, and his face was full of disbelief. "How is that possible! This is already the best plan chosen by the Yi Monarch! "
The Green Jade Spirit felt like he had been dealt a blow.
He originally thought that the Green Jade Civilization's Path of the End was already very close to success. However, it was only one of their alternatives, and its ranking was so low.
Dissatisfied in his heart, he quickly asked, "Then what did the first person use …"
"The person in first place …" A hint of sorrow appeared in King Ashoka's eyes. "That is our Human Ancestor. He inherited the Heart of Limits and the Great Overdraft Stone. The Great Overdraft Stone cannot be illuminated by the Lamp of Judgment, but he overdrew on everything he had, including his life force.
The power of the Nirvana Realm that was overdrafted from the Heart of Limits was stronger than the power that your Green Jade Civilization obtained. Then, with the power of withering to enhance it, and then … "
"Sigh, unfortunately, it still failed in the end, but it was really just a little bit away." King Ashoka revealed a regretful expression.
Perhaps he realized that he had dealt a blow to the Green Jade Spirit, so he added, "Actually, you can't be blamed. After our human race almost completed the Path of the End, those fellows hiding in the Nirvana Realm were frightened. They set the rules, and the Nirvana Realm was designed by them. They had no intention of letting us do as we pleased.
He Wenming completed the Path of the End. I estimate that after the 77th civilization, the Heart of Limits and the Great Overdraft Stone, the two Nirvana Realm tokens, were hidden by them, or even directly destroyed … "
9527 also nodded. "We have indeed never heard of these two Nirvana Realm tokens."
"According to their rules, the Path of the End is destined to fail. There is no possibility of success," King Ashoka said firmly. "So we cannot rely on that Nirvana Realm. We have to create our own power!"
He glanced at Luo Zheng and continued, "Their power also comes from outside the Chaos. We can also obtain this power."
As he spoke, King Ashoka reached out and gently tapped the pile of fragments on the ground. The broken stone statue of the horse's head reformed on its own, returning to its original state. However, the restored statue of the horse's head seemed to be somewhat different.
King Ashoka asked, "What did you see?"
